namespace DevExpress.XtraVerticalGrid.Data
{
	public class RootDescriptorContext : DescriptorContext
	{
		private Lazy<object> multiInstance;

		public override bool CanExpand => true;

		public override bool IsLoaded => visibleHandles != null;

		public override object MultiInstance => multiInstance.Value;

		public RootDescriptorContext(object instance, IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
			: base(serviceProvider)
		{
		}

		protected internal override void ContextAdded()
		{
			base.ContextAdded();
			SetIsExpanded(isExpanded: true);
		}

		protected internal override void SetIsExpandedInternal(bool? isExpanded)
		{
			base.SetIsExpandedInternal(isExpanded);
			if (isExpanded.HasValue)
			{
				InitializeChildren();
			}
		}

		protected override void ResetCore()
		{
			base.ResetCore();
			multiInstance = new Lazy<object>(GetMultiInstance);
		}

		private object GetMultiInstance()
		{
			object sourceInternal = base.DataController.SourceInternal;
			if (base.IsMultiSource)
			{
				(sourceInternal as INotifyCollectionChanged).Do(delegate(INotifyCollectionChanged x)
				{
					x.CollectionChanged += ((WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler>)new INotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler(this, delegate(DescriptorContext d, object o, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
					{
						d.DataController.Invalidate(d.RowHandle);
					})).Handler;
				});
			}
			return sourceInternal;
		}

		protected override void AfterReset()
		{
			base.AfterReset();
			InitializeChildren();
		}

		protected override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetPropertiesInternal(object source, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			if (!IsGetPropertiesSupported)
			{
				return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(source, attributes, noCustomTypeDesc: false);
			}
			return base.GetPropertiesInternal(source, attributes);
		}

		protected override object GetValue()
		{
			return Instance;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<string> GetValidationError()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override void InitializeChildrenInternal()
		{
			base.InitializeChildrenInternal();
			base.CategoryHandles.Any();
		}
	}
}
namespace DevExpress.PropertyGrid.Internal

	public class PropertyDescriptorWrapper : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		private readonly PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute wrapper;

		private readonly PropertyDescriptor baseDescriptor;

		public override AttributeCollection Attributes => baseDescriptor.Attributes;

		public override Type ComponentType => wrapper.ComponentType.Return((Type x) => x, () => baseDescriptor.ComponentType);

		public override bool IsReadOnly => wrapper.IsReadOnly.Return((bool? x) => x.Value, () => baseDescriptor.IsReadOnly);

		public override Type PropertyType => wrapper.PropertyType.Return((Type x) => x, () => baseDescriptor.PropertyType);

		public PropertyDescriptorWrapper(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute wrapper, string name)
			: base(name, null)
		{
			this.wrapper = wrapper;
			baseDescriptor = wrapper.GetBasePropertyDescriptor();
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			return wrapper.CanResetValue.Return((Func<object, bool> x) => x(component), () => baseDescriptor.CanResetValue(component));
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			return wrapper.GetValue.Return((Func<object, object> x) => x(component), () => baseDescriptor.GetValue(component));
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
			wrapper.ResetValue.Return<Action<object>, Action<object>>((Action<object> x) => x, () => baseDescriptor.ResetValue)(component);
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
			wrapper.SetValue.Return<Action<object, object>, Action<object, object>>((Action<object, object> x) => x, () => baseDescriptor.SetValue)(component, value);
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			return wrapper.ShouldSerializeValue.Return((Func<object, bool> x) => x(component), () => baseDescriptor.ShouldSerializeValue(component));
		}
	}
	public class NullPropertyDescriptor : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		public static readonly PropertyDescriptor Instance = new NullPropertyDescriptor();

		public override Type ComponentType => typeof(object);

		public override bool IsReadOnly => true;

		public override Type PropertyType => typeof(object);

		private NullPropertyDescriptor()
			: base("EmptyDescriptor", null)
		{
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute : Attribute
	{
		public Func<PropertyDescriptor> BasePropertyDescriptor { get; set; }

		public Func<object, bool> CanResetValue { get; set; }

		public Func<object, bool> ShouldSerializeValue { get; set; }

		public Func<object, object> GetValue { get; set; }

		public Action<object> ResetValue { get; set; }

		public Action<object, object> SetValue { get; set; }

		public Type ComponentType { get; set; }

		public bool? IsReadOnly { get; set; }

		public Type PropertyType { get; set; }

		internal PropertyDescriptor GetBasePropertyDescriptor()
		{
			return BasePropertyDescriptor.Return((Func<PropertyDescriptor> x) => x(), () => NullPropertyDescriptor.Instance);
		}
	}
	public class PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> : NestedBuilderBase<PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute, PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder>, TParentBuilder> where TParentBuilder : PropertyMetadataBuilderBase<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder>
	{
		internal PropertyDescriptorBuilder(TParentBuilder parent)
			: base(parent)
		{
			ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.PropertyType = typeof(TProperty);
			});
			ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.ComponentType = typeof(T);
			});
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> CanResetValueRule(Func<T, bool> canResetValueRule)
		{
			return ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.CanResetValue = (object c) => canResetValueRule((T)c);
			});
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> ShouldSerializeValueRule(Func<T, bool> shouldSerializeValue)
		{
			return ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.ShouldSerializeValue = (object c) => shouldSerializeValue((T)c);
			});
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> SetValueRule(Action<T, TProperty> setValue)
		{
			return ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.SetValue = delegate(object c, object v)
				{
					setValue((T)c, (TProperty)v);
				};
			});
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> GetValueRule(Func<T, TProperty> getValue)
		{
			return ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.GetValue = (object c) => getValue((T)c);
			});
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> ResetValueRule(Action<T> resetValue)
		{
			return ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.ResetValue = delegate(object c)
				{
					resetValue((T)c);
				};
			});
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, TParentBuilder> IsReadOnly(bool isReadOnly)
		{
			return ChangeAttribute(delegate(PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute x)
			{
				x.IsReadOnly = isReadOnly;
			});
		}

		public TParentBuilder EndPropertyDescriptor()
		{
			return EndCore();
		}
	}
	public static class PropertyDescriptorBuilderExtensions
	{
		public static PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, PropertyMetadataBuilder<T, TProperty>> PropertyDescriptor<T, TProperty>(this PropertyMetadataBuilder<T, TProperty> builder)
		{
			return new PropertyDescriptorBuilder<T, TProperty, PropertyMetadataBuilder<T, TProperty>>(builder);
		}
	}
	public static class PropertyDescriptorAttributeExtensions
	{
		public static PropertyDescriptor WrapPropertyDescriptor(this PropertyDescriptorWrapperAttribute wrapper, string name)
		{
			return new PropertyDescriptorWrapper(wrapper, name);
		}
	}
}
namespace DevExpress.Xpf.Core.Native
{
	public class PreloadProvider : IPreloadSupport
	{
		public IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GetConfiguredTree()
		{
			PropertyGridControl propertyGridControl = new PropertyGridControl
			{
				ShowProperties = ShowPropertiesMode.WithPropertyDefinitions,
				AllowExpanding = AllowExpandingMode.Force
			};
			propertyGridControl.BeginInit();
			propertyGridControl.SelectedObject = (object)new PreloadDataItem
			{
				Item = "First"
			};
			propertyGridControl.PropertyDefinitions.Add(new DevExpress.Xpf.PropertyGrid.PropertyDefinition
			{
				Path = "Item"
			});
			propertyGridControl.Expand("Item");
			propertyGridControl.EndInit();
			yield return propertyGridControl;
		}
	}
}

		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			ShowCategoriesButton = GetTemplateChild("PART_ShowCategories") as System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton;
			HideCategoriesButton = GetTemplateChild("PART_HideCategories") as System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton;
			UpdateButtons();
		}

		protected internal void OnSelectViewCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((ApplyingMode)e.Parameter == ApplyingMode.WhenGrouping)
			{
				ShowCategories = CategoriesShowMode.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				ShowCategories = CategoriesShowMode.Hidden;
			}
			UpdateButtons();
		}

		protected internal void OnSelectViewCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowCategoriesChanged(CategoriesShowMode oldValue)
		{
			UpdateButtons();
		}

		private void UpdateButtons()
		{
			((ShowCategories == CategoriesShowMode.Visible) ? ShowCategoriesButton : HideCategoriesButton).Do(delegate(System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton x)
			{
				x.IsChecked = true;
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseUpCore(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs args)
		{
			PropertyGrid.With((PropertyGridControl x) => x.View).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.CellEditorOwner).Do(delegate(CellEditorOwner x)
			{
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)x).ProcessIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged();
			});
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator.Create(this);
		}

	public class MergedEnumerator<T> : IEnumerator<T>, IDisposable, IEnumerator where T : class
	{
		private IEnumerator<IEnumerator<T>> sourceEnumerator;

		public T Current => sourceEnumerator.Current.With((IEnumerator<T> x) => x.Current);

		object IEnumerator.Current => Current;

		public MergedEnumerator(params IEnumerator<T>[] args)
		{
			sourceEnumerator = args.ToList().GetEnumerator();
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			sourceEnumerator.Dispose();
		}

		public bool MoveNext()
		{
			if (sourceEnumerator.Current == null)
			{
				sourceEnumerator.MoveNext();
			}
			if (sourceEnumerator.Current == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!sourceEnumerator.Current.MoveNext())
			{
				sourceEnumerator.MoveNext();
				if (sourceEnumerator.Current == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return sourceEnumerator.Current.MoveNext();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			sourceEnumerator.Reset();
			while (sourceEnumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				sourceEnumerator.Current.Reset();
			}
			sourceEnumerator.Reset();
		}
	}
	public class SingleElementEnumerator<T> : IEnumerator<T>, IDisposable, IEnumerator where T : class
	{
		private T source;

		private bool useSource;

		public T Current
		{
			get
			{
				if (!useSource)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return source;
			}
		}

		object IEnumerator.Current => Current;

		public SingleElementEnumerator(T source)
		{
			this.source = source;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			source = null;
			useSource = false;
		}

		public bool MoveNext()
		{
			if (useSource || source == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			useSource = true;
			return true;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			useSource = false;
		}
	}
	public static class EnumeratorExtensions
	{
		private class GenericEnumeratorWrapper<T> : IEnumerator<T>, IDisposable, IEnumerator where T : class
		{
			private IEnumerator source;

			public T Current => (T)source.Current;

			object IEnumerator.Current => source.Current;

			public GenericEnumeratorWrapper(IEnumerator source)
			{
				this.source = source;
			}

			public void Dispose()
			{
				source = null;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				return source.MoveNext();
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				source.Reset();
			}
		}

		private class EmptyEnumerator<T> : IEnumerator<T>, IDisposable, IEnumerator where T : class
		{
			T IEnumerator<T>.Current => null;

			object IEnumerator.Current => null;

			void IDisposable.Dispose()
			{
			}

			bool IEnumerator.MoveNext()
			{
				return false;
			}

			void IEnumerator.Reset()
			{
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerator<T> OfType<T>(this IEnumerator enumerator) where T : class
		{
			if (enumerator != null)
			{
				return new GenericEnumeratorWrapper<T>(enumerator);
			}
			return new EmptyEnumerator<T>();
		}
	}

	public class PropertyGridSearchControl : SearchControl
	{
		static PropertyGridSearchControl()
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(PropertyGridSearchControl);
			NavigationManager.NavigationModeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeFromHandle, new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(NavigationMode.Auto));
		}

		protected internal virtual bool GetIsPopupOpened()
		{
			ButtonEdit editorControl = ((SearchControl)this).GetEditorControl();
			PopupBaseEdit val = (PopupBaseEdit)(object)((editorControl is PopupBaseEdit) ? editorControl : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val.IsPopupOpen;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator.Create<PropertyGridSearchControl>(this);
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridVirtualizationModeExtension : MarkupExtension

			mode = (Net45Detector.IsNet45() ? VirtualizationMode.Recycling : VirtualizationMode.Standard);
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return mode;
		}
	}

		static PropertyGridView()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<PropertyGridView>();
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<PropertyGridView>.New().OverrideMetadata(NavigationManager.NavigationModeProperty, (object)NavigationMode.Auto, (Action<PropertyGridView>)null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.None).Register<RowData>("SelectedItem", ref SelectedItemProperty, (RowData)null, (Action<PropertyGridView, RowData, RowData>)delegate(PropertyGridView x, RowData oldValue, RowData newValue)

				.RegisterReadOnly<PropertyGridControl>("PropertyGrid", ref PropertyGridPropertyKey, ref PropertyGridProperty, (PropertyGridControl)null, (Action<PropertyGridView, PropertyGridControl, PropertyGridControl>)delegate(PropertyGridView x, PropertyGridControl oldValue, PropertyGridControl newValue)
				{
					x.OnPropertyGridChanged(oldValue, newValue);

				.RegisterReadOnly<bool>("HasCellEditorError", ref HasCellEditorErrorPropertyKey, ref HasCellEditorErrorProperty, false, (Action<PropertyGridView, bool, bool>)delegate(PropertyGridView x, bool oldValue, bool newValue)
				{

				.RegisterReadOnly<IEnumerable>("CategoriesSource", ref CategoriesSourcePropertyKey, ref CategoriesSourceProperty, (IEnumerable)null, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<CategoriesShowMode>("ShowCategories", ref ShowCategoriesProperty, CategoriesShowMode.Visible, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null);
			CommonThemeHelper.TreeWalkerOverrideProperty<PropertyGridView>((Action<PropertyGridView, CommonThemeTreeWalker>)delegate(PropertyGridView x, CommonThemeTreeWalker newValue)
			{
				x.OnTreeWalkerChanged(newValue);
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedItemChanged(RowData oldValue, RowData newValue)
		{
			if (PropertyGrid != null)
			{
				PropertyGrid.UpdateSelection(newValue);
				InplaceEditorBase currentCellEditor = ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor;
				if (!ClearAutomationEventsHelper.IsEnabled && currentCellEditor != null && AutomationPeer.ListenerExists(AutomationEvents.AutomationFocusChanged))
				{
					UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)(object)currentCellEditor)?.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.AutomationFocusChanged);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void ShowEditor(bool selectAll)

			});
		}

			((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.Do(delegate(InplaceEditorBase x)

			((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.Do(delegate(InplaceEditorBase x)

			});
		}

		public PropertyGridView()
		{
			//IL_0045: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
			CellEditorOwner = new CellEditorOwner(this);
			ImmediateActionsManager = new ImmediateActionsManager((FrameworkElement)this);
			ValidationErrors = new Dictionary<string, Tuple<BaseValidationError, ValidationErrorSource>>();
			BarItems = new BarItems(this);
			base.LayoutUpdated += OnLayoutUpdated;
			base.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(PropertyGridCommands.Refresh, OnRefreshCommandExecuted, OnRefreshCommandCanExecute));
			base.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(PropertyGridCommands.Reset, OnResetCommandExecuted, OnResetCommandCanExecute));
			base.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(PropertyGridCommands.AddItem, OnAddItemCommandExecuted, OnAddItemCommandCanExecute));
			base.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(PropertyGridCommands.RemoveItem, OnRemoveItemCommandExecuted, OnRemoveItemCommandCanExecute));
			ResizingStrategy = new PropertyGridResizingStrategy(this);
			UpdateResourceProvider();
			MenuHelper = new PropertyGridMenuHelper(this);
			CollectionHelper = new ModifyCollectionHelper(this);
			PropertyGridHelper.SetView(this, this);
			ItemsControlHelper.SetScrollUnit((DependencyObject)this, (ScrollUnit)0);
			base.ItemContainerGenerator.StatusChanged += ItemContainerGenerator_StatusChanged;
			base.ItemContainerGenerator.ItemsChanged += ItemContainerGenerator_ItemsChanged;
			rowMenuControllerLazy = new Lazy<BarManagerMenuController>(() => GridMenuInfoBase.CreateMenuController((PopupMenu)(object)MenuHelper.Menu));
		}

		private void ItemContainerGenerator_ItemsChanged(object sender, ItemsChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateAutomationTree();
		}

		private void ItemContainerGenerator_StatusChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated && !SelectionStrategy.Updating)
			{
				SelectionStrategy.Invalidate();
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateAutomationTree()
		{
			if (IsAutomationTreeValid)
			{
				IsAutomationTreeValid = false;
				InvalidateVisual();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateAutomationTree()
		{
			if (IsAutomationTreeValid)
			{
				return;
			}
			AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(this);
			if (automationPeer != null)
			{
				automationPeer.ResetChildrenCache();
				if (AutomationPeer.ListenerExists(AutomationEvents.StructureChanged))
				{
					automationPeer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.StructureChanged);
				}
			}
			IsAutomationTreeValid = true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnTreeWalkerChanged(CommonThemeTreeWalker newValue)
		{
			UpdateResourceProvider();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateResourceProvider()
		{
			ResourceProvider = PropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.From(this);
		}

		public void ClearValidationError(RowHandle handle)
		{
			ClearValidationError(PropertyGrid.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle));
		}

		public void ClearValidationError(string fieldName)
		{
			SetValidationError(fieldName, null, ValidationErrorSource.Unknown);
		}

		[Obsolete("Use the SetValidationError(RowHandle, BaseValidationError, ValidationErrorSource) method instead")]
		public void SetValidationError(RowHandle handle, BaseValidationError error)
		{
			SetValidationError(handle, error, ValidationErrorSource.Unknown);
		}

		[Obsolete("Use the SetValidationError(string, BaseValidationError, ValidationErrorSource) method instead")]
		public void SetValidationError(string fieldName, BaseValidationError error)
		{
			SetValidationError(fieldName, error, ValidationErrorSource.Unknown);
		}

		public void SetValidationError(RowHandle handle, BaseValidationError error, ValidationErrorSource source)
		{
			if (handle != null)
			{
				SetValidationError(PropertyGrid.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle), error, source);
			}
		}

		public void SetValidationError(string fieldName, BaseValidationError error, ValidationErrorSource source)
		{
			if (fieldName != null)
			{
				if (error == null)
				{
					if (ValidationErrors.ContainsKey(fieldName))
					{
						ValidationErrors.Remove(fieldName);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ValidationErrors[fieldName] = Tuple.Create<BaseValidationError, ValidationErrorSource>(error, source);
				}
			}
			UpdateHasCellEditorError();
		}

		private void UpdateHasCellEditorError()
		{
			bool hasCellEditorError = HasCellEditorError;
			HasCellEditorError = ValidationErrors.Count != 0;
			if (hasCellEditorError == HasCellEditorError)
			{
				OnHasCellEditorErrorChanged(hasCellEditorError, HasCellEditorError);
			}
		}

		[Obsolete("Use the GetValidationError(RowHandle, out ValidationErrorSource) method instead")]
		public BaseValidationError GetValidationError(RowHandle handle)
		{
			ValidationErrorSource source;
			return GetValidationError(handle, out source);
		}

		[Obsolete("Use the GetValidationError(string, out ValidationErrorSource) method instead")]
		public BaseValidationError GetValidationError(string fieldName)
		{
			ValidationErrorSource source;
			return GetValidationError(fieldName, out source);
		}

		public BaseValidationError GetValidationError(RowHandle handle, out ValidationErrorSource source)
		{
			return GetValidationError(PropertyGrid.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle), out source);
		}

		public BaseValidationError GetValidationError(string fieldName, out ValidationErrorSource source)
		{
			source = ValidationErrorSource.Unknown;
			if (fieldName == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (ValidationErrors.TryGetValue(fieldName, out var value))
			{
				source = value.Item2;
				return value.Item1;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public virtual void ClearNestedValidationErrors(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (ValidationErrors.Count != 0)
			{
				ClearNestedValidationErrors(PropertyGrid.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ClearNestedValidationErrors(string fieldName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fieldName))
			{
				ValidationErrors.Clear();
				UpdateHasCellEditorError();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnRefreshCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = SelectedItem.If((RowData x) => !x.IsCategory).With((RowData x) => x.Handle) ?? GetRowHandleForCommandEventArgs(e.OriginalSource, e.Parameter);
			if (rowHandle != null && PropertyGrid != null)
			{
				string fieldNameByHandle = PropertyGrid.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(rowHandle);
				PropertyGrid.DataView.Invalidate(rowHandle);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnResetCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = SelectedItem.If((RowData x) => !x.IsCategory).With((RowData x) => x.Handle) ?? GetRowHandleForCommandEventArgs(e.OriginalSource, e.Parameter);
			if (CellEditorOwner.CanHideEditor && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor != null)
			{
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.HideEditor(true);
			}
			if (rowHandle == null || PropertyGrid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			object value = PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetValue(rowHandle);
			CellValueChangingEventArgs e2 = PropertyGrid.RaiseCellValueChanging(rowHandle, value, value, isReset: true);
			if (((CancelRoutedEventArgs)e2).Cancel)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e2).Handled)
			{
				Exception ex = PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView.ResetValue(rowHandle);
				if (ex != null)
				{
					CellValidationError error = new CellValidationError(ex.Message, ex, (ErrorType)1);
					SetValidationError(rowHandle, (BaseValidationError)(object)error, ValidationErrorSource.Internal);
					(((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor as CellEditor).Do(delegate(CellEditor x)
					{
						x.SetValidationErrorInternal((BaseValidationError)(object)error);
					});
				}
			}
			PropertyGrid.UpdateData();
			object value2 = PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetValue(rowHandle);
			if (value != value2)
			{
				PropertyGrid.RaiseCellValueChanged(rowHandle, value, value2, isReset: true);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAddItemCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (OnAddItemCommandCanExecuteCore())
			{
				RowData rowData = SelectedItem.If((RowData x) => !x.IsCategory);
				if (rowData != null && rowData.Handle != null && PropertyGrid != null && PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator != null && PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView != null)
				{
					PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView.SetValue(rowData.Handle, ((EditableDataObject)rowData).Value);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnRemoveItemCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void EnqueueShowEditor(string fullPath)
		{
			ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)delegate
			{
				ShowEditor(selectAll: false);
			});
		}

		protected internal void OnAddItemCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		private bool OnAddItemCommandCanExecuteCore()
		{
			RowData rowData = SelectedItem.If((RowData x) => x.IsCategory);
			if (rowData == null || rowData.Handle == null || PropertyGrid == null || PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator == null || PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.DataView.IsNewInstanceInitializer(rowData.Handle))
			{
				return ((EditableDataObject)rowData).Value != null;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected internal void OnRemoveItemCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected internal void OnRefreshCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		protected internal void OnResetCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RowHandle handle = SelectedItem.If((RowData x) => !x.IsCategory).With((RowData x) => x.Handle) ?? GetRowHandleForCommandEventArgs(e.OriginalSource, e.Parameter);
			if (handle != null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = PropertyGrid.With((PropertyGridControl x) => x.RowDataGenerator).If((RowDataGenerator x) => x.DataView.CanResetValue(handle)).ReturnSuccess();
			}
		}

		private RowHandle GetRowHandleForCommandEventArgs(object sender, object parameter)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = null;
			RowHandle rowHandle2 = (parameter as string).With((string x) => PropertyGrid.With((PropertyGridControl y) => y.RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(x)));
			if (sender is FrameworkElement)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)sender;
				RowHandle rowHandle3 = (frameworkElement.DataContext as RowData).With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
				return rowHandle2 ?? rowHandle3;
			}
			return rowHandle2;
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!firstInitialized)
			{
				firstInitialized = true;
				FirstInitialized();
			}
			PropertyGrid.Do(delegate(PropertyGridControl x)
			{
				if (x.DataController.VisualClient == DataController.NullClient)
				{
					x.DataController.VisualClient = this;
					x.UpdateDataControllerSource();
				}
				x.UpdateData();
				x.RowDataGenerator.UpdateDataViewChange();
				PropertyGrid.PropertyBuilder.ClearUnlinkedDefinitions();
			});
			ImmediateActionsManager.ExecuteActions();
			if (base.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated)
			{
				UpdateAutomationTree();
				SelectionStrategy.UpdateSelection();
			}
		}

		private void FirstInitialized()
		{
			PropertyGrid.Do(delegate
			{
				SelectionStrategy.OnInitialized();
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyGridChanged(PropertyGridControl oldValue, PropertyGridControl newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowMinHeightChanged(double oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator.Create(this);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			ScrollViewer = GetTemplateChild("PART_ScrollViewer") as ScrollViewer;
			ItemsPresenter = GetTemplateChild("PART_ItemsPresenter") as ItemsPresenter;
			ScrollViewer.Do(delegate(ScrollViewer x)
			{
				VirtualizingPanel = (PGVirtualizingStackPanel)LayoutHelper.FindElementByType((FrameworkElement)x, typeof(VirtualizingPanel), (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null);
			});
		}

		protected override bool IsItemItsOwnContainerOverride(object item)
		{
			return base.IsItemItsOwnContainerOverride(item);
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new RowControl();
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			RowControlBase rowControlBase = element as RowControlBase;
			RowData rowData = item as RowData;
			if (rowControlBase != null && rowData != null)
			{
				rowControlBase.IsCategory = rowData.IsCategory;
				rowControlBase.IsLastElement = base.Items.IndexOf(rowData) == base.Items.Count - 1;
				rowControlBase.RowData = rowData;
				rowControlBase.OwnerView = this;
			}
			if (rowControlBase is RowControl rowControl)
			{
				rowControl.CategoriesShowMode = PropertyGrid.ShowCategories;
			}
		}

		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			if (element is RowControlBase rowControlBase)
			{
				rowControlBase.RowData = null;
			}
		}

		public bool CommitEditing()
		{
			return !HasErrorInActiveEditor;
		}

		public bool PerformNavigationOnLeftButtonDown(DependencyObject originalSource)
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected internal virtual bool CheckCanNavigate(bool shouldCommitEditor = true)
		{
			if (shouldCommitEditor && CellEditorOwner.InternalCanCommitEditing && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor != null)
			{
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.CommitEditor(false);
			}
			return ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.Return((InplaceEditorBase x) => !x.Edit.IsValueChanged || !HasErrorInActiveEditor, () => true);
		}

		protected override void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(e);
			if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).IsActiveEditorHaveValidationError())
			{
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).ProcessIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged();
			}
		}

		public void ProcessMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			HandleMouseInputIfHasValidationError(e, shouldCommitEditor: false, setKeyboardFocus: false);
		}

		public void ProcessMouseButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			HandleMouseInputIfHasValidationError(e, shouldCommitEditor: false, setKeyboardFocus: false);
		}

		protected virtual bool HandleMouseInputIfHasValidationError(RoutedEventArgs e, bool shouldCommitEditor, bool setKeyboardFocus)
		{
			if (!CheckCanNavigate(shouldCommitEditor))
			{
				if (setKeyboardFocus)
				{
					((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.With((InplaceEditorBase x) => x.Edit).Do(delegate(IBaseEditWrapper x)
					{
						x.SetKeyboardFocus();
					});
				}
				if (((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor == null || !TreeHelper.GetParents(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, true, true).Contains((DependencyObject)(object)((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void ProcessMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RowData rowData = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).With((DependencyObject x) => PropertyGridHelper.GetRowData(x));
			RowData rowData2 = ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.With((InplaceEditorBase x) => PropertyGridHelper.GetRowData((DependencyObject)(object)x));
			if (HandleMouseInputIfHasValidationError(e, rowData != rowData2, setKeyboardFocus: true) || e.ChangedButton != MouseButton.Left)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = LayoutHelper.FindLayoutOrVisualParentObject(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, (Predicate<DependencyObject>)((DependencyObject dobj) => PropertyGridHelper.GetView(dobj) == this), false, (DependencyObject)null);
			CellEditorPresenter cellEditorPresenter = ((dependencyObject == null) ? null : PropertyGridHelper.GetEditorPresenter(dependencyObject));
			RowControlBase rowControlBase = ((dependencyObject == null) ? null : PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl(dependencyObject));
			if (rowControlBase != null)
			{
				SelectionStrategy.SelectViaPath(rowControlBase.RowData.FullPath);
				SelectionStrategy.Refresh();
				if (cellEditorPresenter != null && cellEditorPresenter.RowControl == rowControlBase)
				{
					rowControlBase.EnsureSelection(cellEditorPresenter);
				}
			}
			((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).ProcessMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnGotFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnGotFocus(e);
			UpdateSelectedRow();
			if (((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor is CellEditor { IsEditorFocused: not false } cellEditor)
			{
				cellEditor.RefreshFocus();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLostFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnLostFocus(e);
			UpdateSelectedRow();
		}

		protected void UpdateSelectedRow()
		{
			if (SelectedItem != null)
			{
				(base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(SelectedItem) as RowControlBase).Do(delegate(RowControlBase x)
				{
					x.UpdateState();
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		protected internal virtual bool ShouldPassKeyDownInLookUpMode(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			return ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).ProcessKeyForLookUp(e);
		}

		protected internal void ProcessKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (CanProcessNavigation())
			{
				PropertyGrid.NavigationManager.ProcessNavigation(e);
			}
			MenuHelper.ProcessKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected bool CanProcessNavigation()
		{
			return !MenuHelper.MenuIsOpen;
		}

		public virtual bool ShouldProcessCollectionChangedAsynchronously()
		{
			return true;
		}

		internal RowControlBase GetRowControl(ItemContainerGenerator generator, RowData item)
		{
			if (item == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return generator.ContainerFromItem(item) as RowControlBase;
		}

		protected virtual void OnHasCellEditorErrorChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (HasErrorInActiveEditor)
			{
				(((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor as DependencyObject).Do((Action<DependencyObject>)MouseEventLockHelper.SubscribeMouseEvents);
			}
		}

		bool IVisualClient.AllowCommitOnValidationAttributeError()
		{
			return PropertyGrid.Return((PropertyGridControl p) => p.AllowCommitOnValidationAttributeError, () => false);
		}

		AllowExpandingMode? IVisualClient.GetAllowExpandingMode(RowHandle handle)
		{
			PropertyDefinition definitionFor = GetDefinitionFor<PropertyDefinition>(handle);
			if (definitionFor == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!definitionFor.AllowExpanding.HasValue)
			{
				return PropertyGrid.AllowExpanding;
			}
			return definitionFor.AllowExpanding.Value;
		}

		void IVisualClient.Invalidate(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!suppressInvalidations)
			{
				Invalidate(handle);
			}
		}

		void IVisualClient.Invoke(Delegate method, params object[] args)
		{
			base.Dispatcher.Invoke(method, args);
		}

		public Type GetConvertToType(RowHandle handle, Type defaultValue)
		{
			return GetDefinitionFor<PropertyDefinition>(handle).Return((PropertyDefinition x) => (!x.UseTypeConverterToStringConversion) ? defaultValue : typeof(string), () => defaultValue);
		}

		IInstanceInitializerImplementer IVisualClient.GetInstanceInitializer(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == RowHandle.Root)
			{
				return null;
			}
			IInstanceInitializer instanceInitializer = GetDefinitionFor<PropertyDefinitionBase>(handle)?.InstanceInitializer ?? GetStandardDefinitionFor<PropertyDefinitionBase>(handle)?.InstanceInitializer;
			if (instanceInitializer != null)
			{
				return new InstanceInitializerImplementer(instanceInitializer);
			}
			return null;
		}

		IInstanceInitializerImplementer IVisualClient.GetListItemInitializer(RowHandle handle)
		{
			IInstanceInitializer instanceInitializer = GetDefinitionFor<CollectionDefinition>(handle)?.NewItemInitializer ?? GetStandardDefinitionFor<CollectionDefinition>(handle)?.NewItemInitializer;
			if (instanceInitializer != null)
			{
				return new InstanceInitializerImplementer(instanceInitializer);
			}
			return null;
		}

		bool? IVisualClient.AllowInstanceInitializer(RowHandle handle)
		{
			PropertyDefinition definitionFor = GetDefinitionFor<PropertyDefinition>(handle, getStandard: false);
			PropertyDefinition standardDefinitionFor = GetStandardDefinitionFor<PropertyDefinition>(handle);
			return (definitionFor?.AllowInstanceInitializer ?? standardDefinitionFor?.AllowInstanceInitializer) ?? PropertyGrid.AllowInstanceInitializer;
		}

		bool IVisualClient.AllowListItemInitializer(RowHandle handle)
		{
			CollectionDefinition definitionFor = GetDefinitionFor<CollectionDefinition>(handle, getStandard: false);
			CollectionDefinition standardDefinitionFor = GetStandardDefinitionFor<CollectionDefinition>(handle);
			return (definitionFor?.AllowNewItemInitializer ?? standardDefinitionFor?.AllowNewItemInitializer) ?? PropertyGrid?.AllowListItemInitializer ?? true;
		}

		bool IVisualClient.AllowCollectionEditor(RowHandle handle)
		{
			CollectionDefinition definitionFor = GetDefinitionFor<CollectionDefinition>(handle, getStandard: false);
			CollectionDefinition standardDefinitionFor = GetStandardDefinitionFor<CollectionDefinition>(handle);
			return (definitionFor?.UseCollectionEditor ?? standardDefinitionFor?.UseCollectionEditor) ?? PropertyGrid.If((PropertyGridControl x) => !x.UseCollectionEditor.HasValue || x.UseCollectionEditor.Value).ReturnSuccess();
		}

		bool IVisualClient.IsExpanded(RowHandle handle, Func<bool?> getDefault)
		{
			return PropertyGrid.RaiseCustomExpand(handle, (RowHandle h) => getDefault().Return((bool? x) => x.Value, () => PropertyGrid.DataView.IsGroupRowHandle(h) && PropertyGrid.ExpandCategoriesWhenSelectedObjectChanged));
		}

		private TPropertyDefinition GetDefinitionFor<TPropertyDefinition>(RowHandle handle, bool getStandard = true) where TPropertyDefinition : PropertyDefinitionBase
		{
			if (PropertyGrid == null || handle == null || handle.IsRoot || handle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return null;
			}
			RowData rowData = PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.RowDataFromHandle(handle);
			if (rowData != null && rowData.Definition != null && !rowData.IsDirty)
			{
				return rowData.Definition as TPropertyDefinition;
			}
			PropertyDefinitionBase definition = PropertyGrid.PropertyBuilder.GetDefinition(PropertyGrid.DataView, handle, getStandard: false);
			if (definition != null)
			{
				return definition as TPropertyDefinition;
			}
			return GetStandardDefinitionFor<TPropertyDefinition>(handle);
		}

		private TPropertyDefinition GetStandardDefinitionFor<TPropertyDefinition>(RowHandle handle) where TPropertyDefinition : PropertyDefinitionBase
		{
			if (PropertyGrid == null || handle == null || handle.IsRoot || handle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return PropertyGrid.PropertyBuilder.GetStandardDefinition(PropertyGrid.DataView, handle, addToLogicalTree: false) as TPropertyDefinition;
		}

		private void Invalidate(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (firstInitialized)
			{
				PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.Invalidate(handle);
			}
			InvalidateVisual();
		}

		private bool IsRootFieldName(string name)
		{
			if (name == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return PropertyGrid.DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(name).If((RowHandle x) => x.IsRoot).ReturnSuccess();
		}

		public override void BeginInit()
		{
			suppressInvalidations = true;
			base.BeginInit();
		}

		public override void EndInit()
		{
			base.EndInit();
			suppressInvalidations = false;
			PropertyGrid.InvalidateData();
		}

		internal void AddChildCore(object child)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(child);
			logicalChildren.Add(child);
		}

		internal void RemoveChildCore(object child)
		{
			RemoveLogicalChild(child);
			logicalChildren.Remove(child);
		}

		void ILogicalOwner.AddChild(object child)
		{
			AddChildCore(child);
		}

		void ILogicalOwner.RemoveChild(object child)
		{
			RemoveChildCore(child);
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.ProcessNavigation(NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			return false;
		}

		IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> INavigationSupport.GetChildren()
		{
			return base.Items.Cast<RowData>().Select((RowData item, int index) => (base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(index) as FrameworkElement) ?? new FakeRowControl(index).Do(delegate(FakeRowControl x)
			{
				x.OwnerView = this;
				x.RowData = item;
				PropertyGridHelper.SetPropertyGrid(x, PropertyGrid);
			}));
		}

		FrameworkElement INavigationSupport.GetParent()
		{
			return PropertyGrid;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetSkipNavigation()
		{
			if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				return SelectedItem == null;
			}
			return true;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetUseLinearNavigation()
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected string GetEditorFieldName(CellEditor editor)
		{
			return editor.With((CellEditor x) => x.RowData).With((RowData x) => x.FullPath);
		}

		PropertyDescriptor IVisualClient.GetPropertyDescriptor(object instance, string fieldName, DescriptorContext parentContext)
		{
			return null;
		}

		[SpecialName]
		bool ILogicalOwner.get_IsLoaded()
		{
			return base.IsLoaded;
		}

		[SpecialName]
		void ILogicalOwner.add_Loaded(RoutedEventHandler value)
		{
			base.Loaded += value;
		}

		[SpecialName]
		void ILogicalOwner.remove_Loaded(RoutedEventHandler value)
		{
			base.Loaded -= value;
		}

		[SpecialName]
		double ILogicalOwner.get_ActualWidth()
		{
			return base.ActualWidth;
		}

		[SpecialName]
		double ILogicalOwner.get_ActualHeight()
		{
			return base.ActualHeight;
		}
	}
	public enum CellEditorPresenterPathMode
	{
		Absolute,
		Relative
	}
	public class CellEditorPresenter : System.Windows.Controls.Panel, INavigationSupport
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsSelectedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PathModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PathProperty;

		private Locker initializationLocker = new Locker();

		private RowData rowData;

		private RowControlBase rowControl;

		private PropertyGridView ownerView;

		private RowData actualRowData;

		private RequestPresenterWeakEventHandler requestPresenterHandler;

		private FieldInvalidatedEventHandler fieldInvalidatedHandler;

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsSelectedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsSelectedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public RowData RowData
		{
			get
			{
				return rowData;
			}
			set
			{
				if (rowData != value)
				{
					rowData = value;
					OnRowDataChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public RowControlBase RowControl
		{
			get
			{
				return rowControl;
			}
			set
			{
				if (rowControl != value)
				{
					RowControlBase oldValue = rowControl;
					rowControl = value;
					OnRowControlChanged(oldValue, value);
				}
			}
		}

		public PropertyGridView OwnerView
		{
			get
			{
				return ownerView;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ownerView != value)
				{
					ownerView = value;
					OnOwnerViewChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public RowData ActualRowData
		{
			get
			{
				return actualRowData;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != actualRowData)
				{
					actualRowData = value;
					OnActualRowDataChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public string Path
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PathProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PathProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public CellEditorPresenterPathMode PathMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (CellEditorPresenterPathMode)GetValue(PathModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PathModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal CellEditor CellEditor { get; private set; }

		protected internal bool IsEditorEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (CellEditor != null)
				{
					return CellEditor.EditCoreIsEnabled;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		static CellEditorPresenter()
		{
			PropertyGridHelper.RowControlProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CellEditorPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				(d as CellEditorPresenter).Do(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
				{
					x.RowControl = e.NewValue as RowControlBase;
				});
			}));
			PropertyGridHelper.RowDataProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CellEditorPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				(d as CellEditorPresenter).Do(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
				{
					x.RowData = e.NewValue as RowData;
				});
			}));
			PropertyGridHelper.ViewProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CellEditorPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				(d as CellEditorPresenter).Do(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
				{
					x.OwnerView = e.NewValue as PropertyGridView;
				});
			}));
			NavigationManager.NavigationModeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CellEditorPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(NavigationMode.Auto));
			IsSelectedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSelected", typeof(bool), typeof(CellEditorPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditorPresenter)d).OnIsSelectedChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			PathModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("PathMode", typeof(CellEditorPresenterPathMode), typeof(CellEditorPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(CellEditorPresenterPathMode.Relative, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditorPresenter)d).OnPathModeChanged((CellEditorPresenterPathMode)e.OldValue);
			}));
			PathProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Path", typeof(string), typeof(CellEditorPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditorPresenter)d).OnPathChanged((string)e.OldValue);
			}));
		}

		public CellEditorPresenter()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			requestPresenterHandler = new RequestPresenterWeakEventHandler(this, delegate(CellEditorPresenter d, object o, RequestPresenterEventArgs e)
			{
				e.Register(d);
			});
			fieldInvalidatedHandler = delegate(object o, FieldInvalidatedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnFieldInvalidated(e.FullPath);
			};
			PropertyGridHelper.SetEditorPresenter(this, this);
		}

		protected virtual void OnFieldInvalidated(string fieldName)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Path))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fieldName) || fieldName == RowData.With((RowData x) => x.FullPath))
				{
					UpdateActualRowData();
				}
				else if (PathMode == CellEditorPresenterPathMode.Absolute && fieldName == Path)
				{
					UpdateActualRowData();
				}
				else if (string.Format("{0}.{1}", RowData.With((RowData x) => x.FullPath) ?? "", Path) == fieldName)
				{
					UpdateActualRowData();
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowDataChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualRowData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnOwnerViewChanged()
		{
			InternalUpdateCellEditor();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowControlChanged(RowControlBase oldValue, RowControlBase newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.RequestPresenter -= ((WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler>)requestPresenterHandler).Handler;
				oldValue.With((RowControlBase x) => x.OwnerView).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).Do(delegate(PropertyGridControl x)
				{
					x.RowDataGenerator.FieldInvalidated -= fieldInvalidatedHandler;
				});
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.RequestPresenter += ((WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler>)requestPresenterHandler).Handler;
				newValue.With((RowControlBase x) => x.OwnerView).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).Do(delegate(PropertyGridControl x)
				{
					x.RowDataGenerator.FieldInvalidated += fieldInvalidatedHandler;
				});
				newValue.UpdateCellEditorPresentersSelection();
			}
			InternalUpdateCellEditor();
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualRowDataChanged()
		{
			InternalUpdateCellEditor();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsSelectedChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			if (IsSelected)
			{
				RowControl.EnsureSelection(this);
			}
			InternalUpdateCellEditor();
		}

		protected virtual void OnPathModeChanged(CellEditorPresenterPathMode oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualRowData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnPathChanged(string oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualRowData();
		}

		public override void BeginInit()
		{
			initializationLocker.Lock();
			base.BeginInit();
		}

		public override void EndInit()
		{
			base.EndInit();
			initializationLocker.Unlock();
			InternalUpdateCellEditor();
		}

		private void UpdateActualRowData()
		{
			if (RowData == null)
			{
				ActualRowData = null;
				return;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Path))
			{
				ActualRowData = RowData;
				return;
			}
			RowDataGenerator rowDataGenerator = RowData.RowDataGenerator;
			DataViewBase dataView = rowDataGenerator.DataView;
			RowHandle rowHandle = null;
			if (PathMode == CellEditorPresenterPathMode.Relative)
			{
				string fieldNameByHandle = dataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(RowData.Handle);
				if (fieldNameByHandle == null)
				{
					ActualRowData = null;
					return;
				}
				rowHandle = ((!dataView.IsGroupRowHandle(RowData.Handle)) ? dataView.GetHandleByFieldName(fieldNameByHandle + "." + Path) : dataView.GetHandleByFieldName(Path));
			}
			if (PathMode == CellEditorPresenterPathMode.Absolute)
			{
				rowHandle = dataView.GetHandleByFieldName(Path);
			}
			ActualRowData = (rowHandle.IsInvalid ? null : rowDataGenerator.ForceGetRowDataForHandle(rowHandle));
		}

		private void InternalUpdateCellEditor()
		{
			initializationLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
			{
				UpdateCellEditor();
			});
		}

		protected virtual void ClearCellEditor(CellEditor editor)
		{
			if (editor != null)
			{
				editor.IsEditorFocused = false;
				editor.RowControl = null;
				editor.OwnerView = null;
				editor.RowData = null;
				base.InternalChildren.Remove((UIElement)(object)editor);
			}
		}

		protected virtual CellEditor CreateCellEditor()
		{
			return new CellEditor();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCellEditor()
		{
			if (object.Equals(null, ActualRowData) || object.Equals(null, RowControl) || object.Equals(null, OwnerView))
			{
				ClearCellEditor(CellEditor);
				CellEditor = null;
				return;
			}
			if (CellEditor == null)
			{
				CellEditor = CreateCellEditor();
				base.InternalChildren.Add((UIElement)(object)CellEditor);
			}
			CellEditor.RowData = ActualRowData;
			CellEditor.RowControl = RowControl;
			CellEditor.OwnerView = OwnerView;
			CellEditor.IsEditorFocused = IsSelected;
		}

		protected internal void ShowEditor()
		{
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((InplaceEditorBase)CellEditor).ShowEditor();
			OwnerView.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((IAction)new SelectAllAction((InplaceEditorBase)(object)CellEditor));
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (CellEditor != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)CellEditor).Measure(availableSize);
				return ((UIElement)(object)CellEditor).DesiredSize;
			}
			return default(Size);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			if (CellEditor != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)CellEditor).Arrange(new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), finalSize));
				return finalSize;
			}
			return default(Size);
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.ProcessNavigation(NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			return false;
		}

		IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> INavigationSupport.GetChildren()
		{
			return new FrameworkElement[0];
		}

		FrameworkElement INavigationSupport.GetParent()
		{
			return RowControl;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetSkipNavigation()
		{
			return !((INavigationSupport)RowControl).GetSkipNavigation();
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetUseLinearNavigation()
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal class RequestPresenterEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private List<CellEditorPresenter> presenters = new List<CellEditorPresenter>();

		public void Register(CellEditorPresenter presenter)
		{
			presenters.Add(presenter);
		}

		public IEnumerable<CellEditorPresenter> GetPresenters()
		{
			return presenters.ToArray();
		}
	}
	internal delegate void RequestPresenterEventHandler(object source, RequestPresenterEventArgs args);
	internal class RequestPresenterWeakEventHandler : WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler>
	{
		private static Action<WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler>, object> onDetachAction = delegate(WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler> handler, object rowControl)
		{
			((RowControlBase)rowControl).RequestPresenter -= handler.Handler;
		};

		private static Func<WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler>, RequestPresenterEventHandler> createHandlerFunction = (WeakEventHandler<CellEditorPresenter, RequestPresenterEventArgs, RequestPresenterEventHandler> h) => h.OnEvent;

		public RequestPresenterWeakEventHandler(CellEditorPresenter owner, Action<CellEditorPresenter, object, RequestPresenterEventArgs> onEventAction)
			: base(owner, onEventAction, onDetachAction, createHandlerFunction)
		{
		}
	}
	public class PopupBrushEditSettings : PopupBrushEditSettingsBase
	{
		static PopupBrushEditSettings()
		{
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			EditorSettingsProvider.Default.RegisterUserEditor(typeof(PopupBrushEdit), typeof(PopupBrushEditSettings), (CreateEditorMethod)(() => (IBaseEdit)(object)new PopupBrushEdit()), (CreateEditorSettingsMethod)(() => (BaseEditSettings)(object)new PopupBrushEditSettings()));
		}
	}

		public double HeaderPercent => 0.5;

		event EventHandler IPropertyGridResizingStrategy.HeaderPercentChanged

			}
			remove
			{
			}
		}

		{
		}

		}

		{
		}

		public void SetInfiniteWidth(double width)

		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridResizingStrategy : IPropertyGridResizingStrategy
	{
		private readonly PropertyGridView view;

		private double? infiniteWidth;

		private double headerPercent = 0.5;

		private double maxHeaderPercent = 1.0;

		private double minHeaderPercent;

		private double? rowWidth;

		private int currentRowHC = -1;

		private bool dragging;

		private HeaderShowMode headerShowMode = HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader;

		private bool mouseOverSplitter;

		private double valueMaxWidth = double.PositiveInfinity;

		private double valueMinWidth;

		private GridLength valueWidth = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star);

		private double headerMinWidth;

		private double headerMaxWidth = double.PositiveInfinity;

		private GridLength headerWidth = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star);

		private readonly Locker headerPercentLocker = new Locker();

		private WeakList<EventHandler> headerPercentChangedList = new WeakList<EventHandler>();

		protected PropertyGridView View => view;

		protected RowDataGenerator Generator => view.PropertyGrid.With((PropertyGridControl x) => x.RowDataGenerator);

		protected RowsCollectionView RowsCollectionView => Generator.With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.ItemsSource);

		public double HeaderPercent
		{
			get
			{
				return headerPercent;
			}
			protected set
			{
				if (!(Math.Abs(headerPercent - value) < 0.0001))
				{
					headerPercent = value;
					OnHeaderPercentChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected double RowWidth
		{
			get
			{
				double? num = rowWidth;
				if (!num.HasValue)
				{
					double? num2 = (rowWidth = View.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.ItemsPresenter).Return((ItemsPresenter x) => x.ActualWidth, () => 0.0));
					return num2.Value;
				}
				return num.GetValueOrDefault();
			}
		}

		protected bool MouseOverSplitter
		{
			get
			{
				return mouseOverSplitter;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mouseOverSplitter != value)
				{
					mouseOverSplitter = value;
					OnMouseOverSplitterChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool Dragging
		{
			get
			{
				return dragging;
			}
			set
			{
				if (dragging != value)
				{
					dragging = value;
					OnDraggingChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double ValueMaxWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return valueMaxWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (valueMaxWidth != value)
				{
					valueMaxWidth = value;
					OnValueMaxWidthChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double ValueMinWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return valueMinWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (valueMinWidth != value)
				{
					valueMinWidth = value;
					OnValueMinWidthChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public GridLength ValueWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return valueWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(valueWidth == value))
				{
					valueWidth = value;
					OnValueWidthChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double HeaderMinWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return headerMinWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (headerMinWidth != value)
				{
					headerMinWidth = value;
					OnHeaderMinWidthChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double HeaderMaxWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return headerMaxWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (headerMaxWidth != value)
				{
					headerMaxWidth = value;
					OnHeaderMaxWidthChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public GridLength HeaderWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return headerWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(headerWidth == value))
				{
					headerWidth = value;
					OnHeaderWidthChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler HeaderPercentChanged
		{
			add
			{
				headerPercentChangedList.Add(value);
			}
			remove
			{
				headerPercentChangedList.Remove(value);
			}
		}

		public PropertyGridResizingStrategy(PropertyGridView propertyGridView)
		{
			//IL_0075: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			view = propertyGridView;
			view.MouseMove += OnViewMouseMove;
			view.SizeChanged += OnViewSizeChanged;
			view.MouseLeftButtonDown += OnViewMouseLeftButtonDown;
			view.MouseLeftButtonUp += OnViewMouseLeftButtonUp;
		}

		public void SetInfiniteWidth(double width)
		{
			infiniteWidth = width;
		}

		public bool AdjustAvailableSize(ref Size availableSize)
		{
			if (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width))
			{
				SetInfiniteWidth(Math.Max(RowWidth, CalcInfiniteHeaderWidthImpl() / 0.5 + 1.0));
				return UpdateAvailableSize(ref availableSize);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool UpdateAvailableSize(ref Size availableSize)
		{
			if (infiniteWidth.HasValue)
			{
				availableSize.Width = infiniteWidth.Value;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private double CalcInfiniteHeaderWidthImpl()
		{
			int i = 0;
			double expanderWidth = View.GetResourceProvider().GetExpanderWidth();
			double num = 0.0;
			double headerColumnMinWidth = View.PropertyGrid.HeaderColumnMinWidth;
			for (; i < 10 && i < RowsCollectionView.Count && RowsCollectionView.GetItemAt(i) is RowData rowData; i++)
			{
				FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(rowData.Header, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture, View.FlowDirection, GetTypeFace(View), TextElement.GetFontSize(View), View.Foreground);
				num += Math.Max(formattedText.Width + expanderWidth * (double)(rowData.Level + 1), headerColumnMinWidth);
			}
			if ((double)i == 0.0)
			{
				return headerColumnMinWidth;
			}
			return num / (double)i;
		}

		private Typeface GetTypeFace(DependencyObject element)
		{
			FontFamily fontFamily = (FontFamily)element.GetValue(TextElement.FontFamilyProperty);
			FontStyle style = (FontStyle)element.GetValue(TextElement.FontStyleProperty);
			FontWeight weight = (FontWeight)element.GetValue(TextElement.FontWeightProperty);
			FontStretch stretch = (FontStretch)element.GetValue(TextElement.FontStretchProperty);
			return new Typeface(fontFamily, style, weight, stretch);
		}

		private void OnHeaderPercentChanged()
		{
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = View.PropertyGrid;
			if (propertyGrid != null)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (HeaderWidth.IsStar && ValueWidth.IsStar)
				{
					flag = Math.Abs(HeaderPercent - HeaderWidth.Value / (HeaderWidth.Value + ValueWidth.Value)) < double.Epsilon;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					propertyGrid.HeaderColumnWidth = new GridLength(HeaderPercent, GridUnitType.Star);
					propertyGrid.ValueColumnWidth = new GridLength(1.0 - HeaderPercent, GridUnitType.Star);
				}
			}
			headerPercentChangedList.CleanResolvedReferences();
			List<EventHandler> list = ((IEnumerable<EventHandler>)headerPercentChangedList).ToList();
			foreach (EventHandler item in list)
			{
				item?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueMinWidthChanged()
		{
			RecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueMaxWidthChanged()
		{
			RecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueWidthChanged()
		{
			RecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderWidthChanged()
		{
			RecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderMaxWidthChanged()
		{
			RecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderMinWidthChanged()
		{
			RecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void CheckRecalcHeaderPercent()
		{
			if (!WidthLimited())
			{
				RecalcHeaderPercent();
			}
		}

		private bool WidthLimited()
		{
			if (!double.IsPositiveInfinity(HeaderMaxWidth) && !double.IsPositiveInfinity(ValueMaxWidth) && ValueMinWidth != 0.0)
			{
				return headerMinWidth != 0.0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void RecalcHeaderPercent()
		{
			if (Locker.op_Implicit(headerPercentLocker) || RowWidth < double.Epsilon)
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = headerPercentLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				double num = HeaderPercent;
				if (!HeaderWidth.IsAbsolute && !ValueWidth.IsAbsolute)
				{
					double num2 = HeaderWidth.Value + ValueWidth.Value;
					num = HeaderWidth.Value / num2;
					if (WidthLimited())
					{
						maxHeaderPercent = 1.0;
						minHeaderPercent = 0.0;
						HeaderPercent = num;
						return;
					}
				}
				double num3 = RowWidth;
				if (double.IsNaN(num3) || num3 <= HeaderMinWidth + ValueMinWidth + 0.5 || !View.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).Return((PropertyGridControl x) => x.ShowGridLines, () => true))
				{
					rowWidth = null;
					HeaderPercent = num;
					return;
				}
				double num4 = -1.0;
				double num5 = -1.0;
				if (HeaderWidth.IsAbsolute)
				{
					num4 = HeaderWidth.Value;
					if (!ValueWidth.IsAbsolute)
					{
						num5 = num3 - num4;
					}
				}
				if (ValueWidth.IsAbsolute)
				{
					num5 = ValueWidth.Value;
					if (!HeaderWidth.IsAbsolute)
					{
						num4 = num3 - num5;
					}
				}
				if (!HeaderWidth.IsAbsolute && !ValueWidth.IsAbsolute)
				{
					num4 = num3 * num;
					num5 = num3 * (1.0 - num);
				}
				double num6 = num3;
				if (ValueMinWidth < num6)
				{
					num6 -= ValueMinWidth;
				}
				double num7 = HeaderMinWidth;
				if (num7 > num6)
				{
					num7 = num6;
				}
				num6 = Math.Min(HeaderMaxWidth, num6);
				maxHeaderPercent = num6 / num3;
				minHeaderPercent = num7 / num3;
				num = Math.Min(maxHeaderPercent, Math.Max(minHeaderPercent, num4 / num3));
				HeaderPercent = num;
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewSizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			rowWidth = null;
			CheckRecalcHeaderPercent();
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewMouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateMouseOverSplitter();
			if (!Dragging)
			{
				return;
			}
			double x = Mouse.GetPosition(View.ItemsPresenter).X;
			Locker val = headerPercentLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				if (x > RowWidth)
				{
					HeaderPercent = maxHeaderPercent;
				}
				else if (x <= 0.0)
				{
					HeaderPercent = minHeaderPercent;
				}
				else
				{
					HeaderPercent = Math.Min(Math.Max(x / RowWidth, minHeaderPercent), maxHeaderPercent);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewMouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Dragging = false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (MouseOverSplitter)
			{
				Dragging = true;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateMouseOverSplitter()
		{
			if (!Dragging)
			{
				if (headerShowMode != HeaderShowMode.Left || !View.PropertyGrid.ShowGridLines)
				{
					MouseOverSplitter = false;
					return;
				}
				double num = headerPercent * RowWidth;
				double num2 = View.GetResourceProvider().GetSplitterWidth() / 2.0;
				double num3 = num - num2;
				double num4 = num + num2;
				Point position = Mouse.GetPosition(View.ItemsPresenter);
				MouseOverSplitter = position.X >= num3 && position.X <= num4;
			}
		}

		private void OnMouseOverSplitterChanged()
		{
			if (MouseOverSplitter)
			{
				View.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeWE;
			}
			else
			{
				View.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Arrow;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDraggingChanged()
		{
			if (Dragging)
			{
				Mouse.Capture(View, CaptureMode.SubTree);
			}
			else
			{
				View.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			}
		}

		public void SetCurrentRow(RowControl row)
		{
			currentRowHC = row.GetHashCode();
			headerShowMode = row.HeaderShowMode;
		}

		public void ResetCurrentRow(RowControl row)
		{
			if (currentRowHC == row.GetHashCode())
			{
				currentRowHC = -1;
				headerShowMode = HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader;
				UpdateMouseOverSplitter();
			}
		}
	}
	public enum RowControlVisualStates
	{
		Normal = 0,
		Inactive = 1,
		Selected = 2,
		ReadOnly = 4,
		SelectedInactive = 3,
		SelectedReadOnly = 6,
		SelectedInactiveReadOnly = 7
	}

		static RowControlBase()
		{
			CanShowCollectionButtonProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CanShowCollectionButton", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnCanShowCollectionButtonPropertyChanged));
			ShowCollectionButtonProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowCollectionButton", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnShowCollectionButtonPropertyChanged));
			IsCollectionRowProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsCollectionRow", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnIsCollectionRowPropertyChanged));
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(RowControlBase);
			NavigationManager.NavigationModeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeFromHandle, new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(NavigationMode.Auto));
			RowDataProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("RowData", typeof(RowData), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnRowDataChanged((RowData)e.OldValue);
			}));
			ActualIndentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualIndent", typeof(double), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ExpandButtonWidthProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ExpandButtonWidth", typeof(double), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnExpandButtonWidthChanged((double)e.OldValue);
			}));
			OwnerViewProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OwnerView", typeof(PropertyGridView), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnOwnerViewChanged((PropertyGridView)e.OldValue, (PropertyGridView)e.NewValue);
			}));
			DesiredHeaderWidthProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DesiredHeaderWidth", typeof(double), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnDesiredHeaderWidthChanged((double)e.OldValue);
			}));
			StateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("State", typeof(RowControlVisualStates), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(RowControlVisualStates.Normal));
			RenderReadOnlyProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("RenderReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnRenderReadOnlyChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			IsReadOnlyProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnIsReadOnlyChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			UIElement.IsEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeFromHandle, new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnIsEnabledChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			IsSelectedProperty = Selector.IsSelectedProperty.AddOwner(typeof(RowControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnIsSelectedChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnIsExpandedChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			DescriptionPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("Description", typeof(object), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnDescriptionChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
			}));
			DescriptionProperty = DescriptionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsCategoryPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("IsCategory", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnIsCategoryChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			IsCategoryProperty = IsCategoryPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CellTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CellTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnCellTemplateSelectorChanged((DataTemplateSelector)e.OldValue, (DataTemplateSelector)e.NewValue);
			}));
			CellTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CellTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).OnCellTemplateChanged((DataTemplate)e.OldValue, (DataTemplate)e.NewValue);
			}));
			ActualCellTemplateSelectorPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualCellTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ActualCellTemplateSelectorProperty = ActualCellTemplateSelectorPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CustomShowCommandButtonProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CustomShowCommandButton", typeof(bool?), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).UpdateActualShowCommandButton();
			}));
			ShowCommandButtonProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowCommandButton", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((RowControlBase)d).UpdateActualShowCommandButton();
			}));
			ActualShowCommandButtonProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualShowCommandButton", typeof(bool), typeof(RowControlBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			RowControlProperty = PropertyGridHelper.RowControlProperty.AddOwner(typeof(RowControlBase));
		}

		private void OnToolTipOpening(object sender, ToolTipEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource == this && !new Rect(0.0, 0.0, base.RenderSize.Width * OwnerView.ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercent, base.RenderSize.Height).Contains(Mouse.GetPosition(this)))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnRowDataIsExpandedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (setIsExpandedLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = setIsExpandedLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				IsExpanded = RowData.IsExpanded;
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}
