		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualDescription()
		{
			//IL_0123: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0128: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0140: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0142: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0142: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0149: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0152: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0159: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (RowData == null || OwnerView == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Style style = OwnerView.PropertyGrid.GetDescriptionContainerStyleSelector(RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Definition)).SelectStyle(RowData.Description, null);
			bool isSuperTipStyle = style?.TargetType.IsAssignableFrom(typeof(SuperTipControl)) ?? true;
			Func<bool> func = () => OwnerView.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).If((PropertyGridControl x) => (x.ShowDescriptionIn & DescriptionLocation.ToolTip) == DescriptionLocation.ToolTip).ReturnSuccess();
			Func<bool> func2 = () => Description != null && Description.GetType() == typeof(SuperTipControl);
			if (func())
			{
				if (Description == null || !(Description.GetType() == typeof(PropertyDescriptionPresenterControl)))
				{
					Description = CreateTooltipDescriptionControl();
				}
			}
			else if (OwnerView.ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercent * base.ActualWidth < DesiredHeaderWidth + (double)(RowData.Level + 1) * OwnerView.GetResourceProvider().GetExpanderWidth())
			{
				if (!func2())
				{
					SuperTipControl val = new SuperTipControl();
					((FrameworkElement)val).Style = style.If((Style x) => isSuperTipStyle);
					SuperTipControl val2 = val;
					SuperTip val3 = (val2.SuperTip = new SuperTip());
					SuperTipItem val5 = new SuperTipItem();
					val3.Items.Add((SuperTipItemBase)(object)val5);
					val5.Content = RowData.Header;
					((ContentElement)(object)val5).IsEnabled = !RenderReadOnly && !IsReadOnly;
					Description = val2;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Description = null;
			}
		}

		private PropertyDescriptionPresenterControl CreateTooltipDescriptionControl()
		{
			PropertyDescriptionPresenterControl propertyDescriptionPresenterControl = new PropertyDescriptionPresenterControl
			{
				SelectedRow = RowData,
				ContentTemplateSelector = OwnerView.PropertyGrid.GetDescriptionTemplateSelector(RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Definition)),
				IsInTooltip = true,
				IsEnabled = (!RenderReadOnly && !IsReadOnly)
			};
			propertyDescriptionPresenterControl.Style = OwnerView.PropertyGrid.GetDescriptionContainerStyleSelector(RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Definition)).SelectStyle(RowData.Description, propertyDescriptionPresenterControl);
			return propertyDescriptionPresenterControl;
		}

		public override void BeginInit()
		{
			if (!initPending)
			{
				initPending = true;
				base.BeginInit();
			}
		}

		public override void EndInit()
		{
			if (initPending)
			{
				base.EndInit();
				initPending = false;
				UpdateActualCellTemplateSelector();
			}
		}

		}

		protected virtual void OnRowDataChanged(RowData oldValue)
		{
			BeginInit();
			Description = null;
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.ShouldHighlightValueChanged -= OnRowDataShouldHighlightValueChanged;
				oldValue.CommandChanged -= OnRowDataCommandChanged;
				oldValue.IsReadOnlyChanged -= OnRowDataIsReadOnlyChanged;
				oldValue.IsExpandedChanged -= OnRowDataIsExpandedChanged;
				oldValue.IsSelectedChanged -= OnRowDataIsSelectedChanged;
				oldValue.IsCollectionRowChanged -= OnRowDataIsCollectionRowChanged;
				oldValue.IsModifiableCollectionItemChanged -= OnRowDataIsModifiableCollectionItemChanged;
				oldValue.ShowMenuButtonChanged -= OnRowShowMenuButtonChanged;
				oldValue.RenderReadOnlyChanged -= OnRowDataRenderReadOnlyChanged;
				oldValue.HeaderChanged -= OnRowDataHeaderChanged;
			}
			if (RowData != null)
			{
				RowData.CommandChanged += OnRowDataCommandChanged;
				RowData.ShouldHighlightValueChanged += OnRowDataShouldHighlightValueChanged;
				RowData.IsReadOnlyChanged += OnRowDataIsReadOnlyChanged;
				RowData.IsExpandedChanged += OnRowDataIsExpandedChanged;
				RowData.IsSelectedChanged += OnRowDataIsSelectedChanged;
				RowData.IsCollectionRowChanged += OnRowDataIsCollectionRowChanged;
				RowData.IsModifiableCollectionItemChanged += OnRowDataIsModifiableCollectionItemChanged;
				RowData.ShowMenuButtonChanged += OnRowShowMenuButtonChanged;
				RowData.RenderReadOnlyChanged += OnRowDataRenderReadOnlyChanged;
				RowData.HeaderChanged += OnRowDataHeaderChanged;
				UpdateActualIndent();
				OnRowDataShouldHighlightValueChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataIsReadOnlyChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataIsExpandedChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataIsSelectedChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataIsCollectionRowChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataIsModifiableCollectionItemChanged(null, null);
				OnRowShowMenuButtonChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataRenderReadOnlyChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataHeaderChanged(null, null);
				OnRowDataCommandChanged(null, null);
			}
			UpdateCellTemplateAndSelector();
			UpdateActualDescription();
			PropertyGridHelper.SetRowData(this, RowData);
			UpdateState();
			EndInit();
			if (this.RowDataChanged != null)
			{
				this.RowDataChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		private void OnRowDataCommandChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateActualShowCommandButton();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowDataShouldHighlightValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)

		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCellTemplateAndSelector()
		{
			if (RowData != null && !IsCategory)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, CellTemplateProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Definition.CellTemplate")
				{
					Source = RowData,
					Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
				});
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, CellTemplateSelectorProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Definition.CellTemplateSelector")
				{
					Source = RowData,
					Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
				});
			}
			else
			{
				ClearValue(CellTemplateProperty);
				ClearValue(CellTemplateSelectorProperty);
			}
		}

		private void OnRowDataIsCollectionRowChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			IsCollectionRow = RowData.IsCollectionRow;
			this.RowDataIsCollectionRowChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

			IsReadOnly = RowData.IsReadOnly;
		}

		}

			CustomShowCommandButton = RowData.CanShowMenu;
		}

		private void OnRowDataRenderReadOnlyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RenderReadOnly = RowData.RenderReadOnly;
		}

		private void OnRowDataHeaderChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			string text = ((RowData == null) ? null : RowData.Header);
			if (text != null)
			{
				Typeface typeface = new Typeface(TextElement.GetFontFamily(this), TextElement.GetFontStyle(this), TextElement.GetFontWeight(this), TextElement.GetFontStretch(this));
				FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(text, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, base.FlowDirection, typeface, TextElement.GetFontSize(this), Brushes.Black, new NumberSubstitution
				{
					Substitution = NumberSubstitution.GetSubstitution(this)
				}, TextOptions.GetTextFormattingMode(this));
				DesiredHeaderWidth = formattedText.WidthIncludingTrailingWhitespace;
			}
			else
			{
				DesiredHeaderWidth = 0.0;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRenderReadOnlyChanged(bool oldValue)

		}

		}

		protected virtual void OnIsEnabledChanged(bool oldValue)

		protected override void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(e);
			UpdateState();
			if (base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && OwnerView != null)
			{
				RowControlBase rowControlBase = (Keyboard.FocusedElement as DependencyObject).With(PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl);
				PropertyGridControl propertyGridControl = rowControlBase.With((RowControlBase x) => x.OwnerView).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid);
				if (rowControlBase == this || (propertyGridControl != OwnerView.PropertyGrid && PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl(VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(rowControlBase)) == this))
				{
					OwnerView.SelectionStrategy.SelectViaHandle(RowData.Handle);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnRowDataIsSelectedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (changeIsSelectedLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = changeIsSelectedLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				IsSelected = RowData.IsSelected;
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsSelectedChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateCellEditorPresentersSelection();
		}

		public virtual void UpdateCellEditorPresentersSelection()
		{
			if (!changeIsSelectedLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				Locker val = changeIsSelectedLocker.Lock();
				try
				{
					RowData.IsSelected = IsSelected;
				}
				finally
				{
					((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
				}
			}
			if (!IsSelected)
			{
				Navigating = false;
				GetPresenters().ForEach(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
				{
					x.IsSelected = false;
				});
			}
			else
			{
				(from x in GetPresenters()
					orderby NavigationManager.GetNavigationIndex(x)
					select x).If((IOrderedEnumerable<CellEditorPresenter> x) => x.All((CellEditorPresenter p) => !p.IsSelected)).With((IOrderedEnumerable<CellEditorPresenter> x) => x.FirstOrDefault()).Do(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
				{
					x.IsSelected = true;
				});
			}
			if (IsSelected && RowData.Return((RowData x) => x.IsCategory, () => false) && !GetPresenters().Any())
			{
				BringIntoView();
			}
			UpdateState();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			UpdateState();
		}

		protected internal IEnumerable<CellEditorPresenter> GetPresenters()
		{
			if (this.RequestPresenter == null)
			{
				return new CellEditorPresenter[0];
			}
			RequestPresenterEventArgs e = new RequestPresenterEventArgs();
			this.RequestPresenter(this, e);
			return e.GetPresenters();
		}

		protected internal void EnsureSelection(CellEditorPresenter cellEditorPresenter)
		{
			foreach (CellEditorPresenter presenter in GetPresenters())
			{
				presenter.IsSelected = presenter == cellEditorPresenter;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			UpdateState();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateShowCollectionButton()
		{
			ShowCollectionButton = CanShowCollectionButton;
		}

		}

		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)

		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnContextMenuOpening(e);
			PropertyGridView ownerView = OwnerView;
			if (ownerView != null && ownerView.CellEditorOwner.With((CellEditorOwner x) => ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)x).CurrentCellEditor).If((InplaceEditorBase x) => x.IsEditorVisible) == null)
			{
				ShowMenuMode showMenuMode = OwnerView.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).Return((PropertyGridControl x) => x.ShowMenu, () => ShowMenuMode.OnMenuButtonClick);
				if ((showMenuMode & ShowMenuMode.OnRightClick) != 0)
				{
					ownerView.MenuHelper.ShowCommandsMenu(RowData, (RowControl)this, PlacementMode.MousePoint);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateActualIndent()
		{
			if (RowData != null)
			{
				ActualIndent = (double)RowData.Level * ExpandButtonWidth;
			}
		}

		public virtual void UpdateState()
		{
			RowControlVisualStates rowControlVisualStates = RowControlVisualStates.Normal;
			if (!base.IsEnabled || (RenderReadOnly && !IsCategory))
			{
				rowControlVisualStates |= RowControlVisualStates.ReadOnly;
			}
			if (IsSelected)
			{
				rowControlVisualStates |= RowControlVisualStates.Selected;
				if (!OwnerView.Return((PropertyGridView x) => x.IsKeyboardFocusWithin, () => false) && (!PopupMenuManager.IsAnyPopupOpened || PopupMenuManager.TopPopup.With((Func<BarPopupBase, PropertyGridView>)PropertyGridHelper.GetView) != OwnerView))
				{
					rowControlVisualStates |= RowControlVisualStates.Inactive;
				}
			}
			State = rowControlVisualStates;
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
		{
			UpdateActualCellTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			UpdateActualCellTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualShowCommandButton()
		{
			bool actualShowCommandButton = (CustomShowCommandButton.HasValue ? CustomShowCommandButton.Value : ShowCommandButton);
			ShowMenuMode showMenuMode = OwnerView.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).Return((PropertyGridControl x) => x.ShowMenu, () => ShowMenuMode.OnMenuButtonClick);
			if (showMenuMode == ShowMenuMode.OnRightClick && RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Command) == null)
			{
				actualShowCommandButton = false;
			}
			ActualShowCommandButton = actualShowCommandButton;
		}

		protected internal void UpdateActualCellTemplateSelector()
		{
			if (!initPending && RowData != null)
			{
				ActualCellTemplateSelector = new PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector
				{
					CustomTemplate = CellTemplate,
					CustomTemplateSelector = CellTemplateSelector
				};
			}
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.ProcessNavigation(NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			if (direction == NavigationDirection.Right && RowData.CanExpand && !RowData.IsExpanded)
			{
				RowData.IsExpanded = true;
				return true;
			}
			if (direction == NavigationDirection.Left && RowData.IsExpanded)
			{
				RowData.IsExpanded = false;
				return true;
			}
			if (direction == NavigationDirection.Enter)
			{
				return NavigationManager.GetChildren(this).FirstOrDefault().If((FrameworkElement x) => !x.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					.Return((FrameworkElement x) => x.Focus(), () => false);
			}
			return false;
		}

		IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> INavigationSupport.GetChildren()
		{
			if (!HasCustomTemplate)
			{
				return GetPresenters();
			}
			List<FrameworkElement> children = new List<FrameworkElement>();
			ConditionalVisualTreeEnumerator conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator = new ConditionalVisualTreeEnumerator(this, (DependencyObject x) => NavigationManager.GetNavigationMode(x) == NavigationMode.None);
			while (((NestedObjectEnumeratorBase)conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator).MoveNext())
			{
				if (!object.Equals(((VisualTreeEnumerator)conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator).Current, this))
				{
					(((VisualTreeEnumerator)conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator).Current as FrameworkElement).If((FrameworkElement x) => NavigationManager.GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None).Do(delegate(FrameworkElement x)
					{
						children.Add(x);
					});
				}
			}
			return children;
		}

		FrameworkElement INavigationSupport.GetParent()
		{
			return OwnerView;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetSkipNavigation()
		{
			if (!HasCustomTemplate)
			{
				if (!IsCategory && GetPresenters().Any((CellEditorPresenter x) => x != null && x.IsEnabled && x.IsEditorEnabled))
				{
					if (!HasActiveEditor && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)OwnerView.CellEditorOwner).EditorWasClosed && !OwnerView.PropertyGrid.NavigationManager.OpenEditorOnSelection)
					{
						return OwnerView.SelectedItem.With((RowData x) => OwnerView.GetRowControl(x)).If((RowControlBase x) => x.NavigatingInsideHeader).ReturnSuccess();
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetUseLinearNavigation()
		{
			if (((INavigationSupport)this).GetSkipNavigation())
			{
				return !HasCustomTemplate;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

	public class BrushTypeCheckedConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value.Equals(parameter);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(bool)value)
			{
				return "None";
			}
			return parameter;
		}
	}
	public class PopupMenuActualWidthToHorizontalOffsetConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			double num = (double)value;
			double num2 = (double)parameter;
			return num2 - num;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return Convert(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
		}
	}
	public class GridLengthToDoubleConverterExtension : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			GridLength gridLength = (GridLength)value;
			if (!gridLength.IsAbsolute)
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			return gridLength.Value;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			double num = (double)value;
			if (double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				return new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star);
			}
			return new GridLength(num, GridUnitType.Pixel);
		}
	}
	public class CheckEqualityConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			TypeConverter converter = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(targetType);
			flag = flag && converter != null && parameter != null;
			if (flag)
			{
				flag &= converter.CanConvertFrom(parameter.GetType());
			}
			return !object.Equals(value, parameter) && flag && object.Equals(value, converter.ConvertFrom(parameter));
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(value is bool) || !(bool)value)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return parameter;
		}
	}
	public class EnumToCollectionExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		public Type EnumType { get; set; }

		public EnumToCollectionExtension()
		{
		}

		public EnumToCollectionExtension(Type enumType)
		{
			if (enumType != null && !enumType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(Enum)))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
			EnumType = EnumType;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			if (!(EnumType == null))
			{
				return Enum.GetValues(EnumType);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class RowStateCheckerConverterExtension : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public RowControlVisualStates ExistingStates { get; set; }

		public RowControlVisualStates MissingStates { get; set; }

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return ((RowControlVisualStates)value & ExistingStates) == ExistingStates && ((RowControlVisualStates)value & MissingStates) == 0;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return RowControlVisualStates.Normal;
		}
	}
	public class DescriptionLocationConverterExtension : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object TrueValue { get; set; }

		public object FalseValue { get; set; }

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public DescriptionLocationConverterExtension()
		{
			TrueValue = true;
			FalseValue = false;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			DescriptionLocation descriptionLocation = (DescriptionLocation)value;
			DescriptionLocation descriptionLocation2 = (DescriptionLocation)parameter;
			if ((descriptionLocation & descriptionLocation2) == 0)
			{
				return FalseValue;
			}
			return TrueValue;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value;
		}
	}
	internal class TypeInfoToStringValueConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (value as DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo).With((DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo x) => x.Name);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}

		public static RowHandle Root => root;

		public static RowHandle Invalid => Incorrect;

		protected internal bool IsRoot => this == Root;

		protected internal bool IsInvalid => this == Invalid;
	}

	internal class SelectSearchControlCommand : ICommand
	{
		private PropertyGridControl owner;

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged
		{
			add
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested -= value;
			}
		}

		public SelectSearchControlCommand(PropertyGridControl owner)
		{
			this.owner = owner;
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (owner.ShowSearchBox && owner.SearchControl != null)
			{
				return !((UIElement)(object)owner.SearchControl).IsKeyboardFocusWithin;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			NavigationManager.FocusElementOrFirstAvailableChild((FrameworkElement)(object)owner.SearchControl, NavigationDirection.Next);
		}
	}
	internal class BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem : BarSplitButtonItem
	{
		private IEnumerable<ITypeInfoImplementer> typeInfos;

		private ITypeInfoImplementer currentTypeInfo;

		private bool dataInitialized;

		protected string NullCaption { get; private set; }

		public bool IsCollectionInitializer { get; private set; }

		protected Dictionary<ITypeInfoImplementer, BarItem> Items { get; private set; }

		protected RowDataGenerator RowDataGenerator { get; private set; }

		protected DataViewBase DataView => RowDataGenerator.DataView;

		protected RowHandle Handle { get; private set; }

		internal PopupMenu Menu { get; set; }

		protected internal ITypeInfoImplementer CurrentTypeInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return currentTypeInfo;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != currentTypeInfo)
				{
					ITypeInfoImplementer oldValue = currentTypeInfo;
					currentTypeInfo = value;
					OnCurrentTypeInfoChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		protected IEnumerable<ITypeInfoImplementer> TypeInfos
		{
			get
			{
				return typeInfos;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != typeInfos)
				{
					IEnumerable<ITypeInfoImplementer> oldValue = typeInfos;
					typeInfos = value;
					OnTypeInfosChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool HasItems => Items.Count != 0;

		public bool HasCommonValue => Items.Count == 1;

		static BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem()
		{
			BarItemLinkCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem), typeof(BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink), (CreateObjectMethod<BarItemLink>)((object i) => (BarItemLink)(object)new BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink()));
		}

		public BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem(RowDataGenerator generator, RowHandle handle)
		{
			Handle = handle;
			RowDataGenerator = generator;
			Items = new Dictionary<ITypeInfoImplementer, BarItem>();
		}

		protected internal virtual void InitializeData()
		{
			if (!dataInitialized)
			{
				dataInitialized = true;
				NullCaption = PropertyGridControlLocalizer.GetString(PropertyGridControlStringID.NewItemInitializerButtonContent);
				IsCollectionInitializer = DataView.IsCollectionHandle(Handle);
				IEnumerable input = (IsCollectionInitializer ? DataView.GetCollectionNewItemValues(Handle) : DataView.GetTypeInfos(Handle));
				TypeInfos = input.With((IEnumerable x) => x.OfType<ITypeInfoImplementer>());
				((BarItem)this).IsVisible = HasItems;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLinkControlLoaded(object sender, BarItemLinkControlLoadedEventArgs e)
		{
			((BarItem)this).OnLinkControlLoaded(sender, e);
			InitializeData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnTypeInfosChanged(IEnumerable<ITypeInfoImplementer> oldValue)
		{
			UpdateItems();
			if (TypeInfos == null || !TypeInfos.Any())
			{
				CurrentTypeInfo = null;
			}
			else
			{
				if (IsCollectionInitializer)
				{
					CurrentTypeInfo = DataView.GetSelectedCollectionNewItem(Handle) as ITypeInfoImplementer;
				}
				CurrentTypeInfo = CurrentTypeInfo ?? TypeInfos.ElementAt(0);
			}
			UpdatePopupMenu();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCurrentTypeInfoChanged(ITypeInfoImplementer oldValue)
		{
			if (CurrentTypeInfo == null)
			{
				((BarItem)this).Content = NullCaption;
				((BarItem)this).Command = null;
				return;
			}
			BarItem val = Items[CurrentTypeInfo];
			((BarItem)this).Content = val.Content;
			((FrameworkContentElement)this).ToolTip = CurrentTypeInfo.Description;
			((BarItem)this).Command = val.Command;
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateItems()
		{
			if (TypeInfos == null)
			{
				Items.Clear();
				return;
			}
			foreach (ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfo in TypeInfos)
			{
				Items[typeInfo] = (BarItem)(object)new BarInstanceInitializerItem(RowDataGenerator, Handle, typeInfo, this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdatePopupMenu()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (Menu == null)
			{
				Menu = new PopupMenu();
				((BarSplitButtonItem)this).PopupControl = (IPopupControl)(object)Menu;
			}
			((Collection<BarItemLinkBase>)(object)Menu.ItemLinks).Clear();
			foreach (BarItem value in Items.Values)
			{
				Menu.ItemLinks.Add(value);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink : BarSplitButtonItemLink
	{
		static BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink()
		{
			BarItemLinkControlCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink), typeof(BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLinkControl), (CreateObjectMethod<BarItemLinkControlBase>)((object li) => (BarItemLinkControlBase)(object)new BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLinkControl()));
		}
	}
	internal class BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLinkControl : BarSplitButtonItemLinkControl
	{
		protected BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink InitializerLink => ((BarItemLinkControlBase)this).Link as BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItemLink;

		protected BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem InitializerItem => ((BarItemLinkControlBase)this).Item as BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem;

		protected override void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((BarSplitButtonItemLinkControl)this).OnLoaded(sender, e);
			if (InitializerItem != null)
			{
				InitializerItem.InitializeData();
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateLayoutPanelArrowAlignment()
		{
			((BarSplitButtonItemLinkControl)this).UpdateLayoutPanelArrowAlignment();
			UpdateLayoutPanelArrowAndSecondBorderByItems();
		}

		protected override void UpdateLayoutPanelShowSecondBorder()
		{
			((BarSplitButtonItemLinkControl)this).UpdateLayoutPanelShowSecondBorder();
			UpdateLayoutPanelArrowAndSecondBorderByItems();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateLayoutPanelArrowAndSecondBorderByItems()
		{
			if (InitializerItem != null && ((BarItemLinkControlBase)this).LayoutPanel != null && InitializerItem.HasItems)
			{
				BarItemLayoutPanel layoutPanel = ((BarItemLinkControlBase)this).LayoutPanel;
				layoutPanel.ShowArrow &= !InitializerItem.HasCommonValue;
				BarItemLayoutPanel layoutPanel2 = ((BarItemLinkControlBase)this).LayoutPanel;
				layoutPanel2.ShowSecondBorder &= !InitializerItem.HasCommonValue;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class BarInstanceInitializerItem : BarButtonItem
	{
		protected DataViewBase DataViewBase => RowDataGenerator.With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.DataView);

		protected RowDataGenerator RowDataGenerator { get; set; }

		protected RowHandle Handle { get; set; }

		protected ITypeInfoImplementer Info { get; set; }

		protected BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem Owner { get; set; }

		static BarInstanceInitializerItem()
		{
			BarItemLinkCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(BarInstanceInitializerItem), typeof(BarInstanceInitializerItemLink), (CreateObjectMethod<BarItemLink>)((object i) => (BarItemLink)(object)new BarInstanceInitializerItemLink()));
		}

		public BarInstanceInitializerItem(RowDataGenerator generator, RowHandle handle, ITypeInfoImplementer info, BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem owner)
		{
			Handle = handle;
			RowDataGenerator = generator;
			Info = info;
			Owner = owner;
			InitializeData();
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeData()
		{
			string format = PropertyGridControlLocalizer.GetString(Owner.IsCollectionInitializer ? PropertyGridControlStringID.NewItemInitializerStringFormat : PropertyGridControlStringID.InstanceInitializerStringFormat);
			((BarItem)this).Content = Info.Name ?? string.Format(format, Info.Type.Name);
			((FrameworkContentElement)this).ToolTip = Info.Description;
			((BarItem)this).Command = DelegateCommandFactory.Create(OnCommandExecuted, () => true, useCommandManager: false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandExecuted()
		{
			if (Owner.IsCollectionInitializer)
			{
				RowDataGenerator.View.CollectionHelper.AddCollectionItem(RowDataGenerator.RowDataFromHandle(Handle), Info);
			}
			else
			{
				DataViewBase.SetValue(Handle, Info);
			}
			Owner.CurrentTypeInfo = Info;
		}
	}
	internal class BarInstanceInitializerItemLink : BarButtonItemLink
	{
	}

		protected internal void ExecuteMenuCustomizations(ILinksHolder holder)
		{
			//IL_0094: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			DataViewBase dataView = RowDataGenerator.DataView;
			PropertyGridView view = RowDataGenerator.View;
			((Collection<IBarItem>)(object)holder.Items).Add((IBarItem)(object)view.BarItems.CreateRefreshItem());
			((Collection<IBarItem>)(object)holder.Items).Add((IBarItem)(object)view.BarItems.CreateResetItem());
			CollectionDefinition collectionDefinition = Definition as CollectionDefinition;
			CollectionDefinition collectionDefinition2 = StandardDefinition as CollectionDefinition;
			if (dataView.IsNewInstanceInitializer(Handle) && (!IsCollectionRow || ((collectionDefinition == null || collectionDefinition.AllowAddItems) && (collectionDefinition2 == null || collectionDefinition2.AllowAddItems))))
			{
				((Collection<IBarItem>)(object)holder.Items).Add((IBarItem)new BarItemSeparator());
				((Collection<IBarItem>)(object)holder.Items).Add((IBarItem)(object)new BarInstanceInitializerSplitButtonItem(RowDataGenerator, Handle));
			}
		}

		public RowData()
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			initializationLocker = new Locker();
			initializationLocker.Unlocked += OnInitialized;
			canShowCollectionButton = new Lazy<bool>(CalcCanShowCollectionButton);
			ForcedChildren = new RowDataCollection(this);
			Column = new EditorColumn(this);
			IsReady = false;
		}

		private void OnInitialized(object sender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (!IsDirty)
			{
				ApplyDefinition(Definition);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void EditableObjectChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)

				RowDataGenerator.SetValue(Handle, newValue);
			}
		}

		public object CoerceIsExpanded(bool value)

			{
				bool isExpanded = RowDataGenerator.Return((RowDataGenerator x) => x.SetExpanded(Handle, IsExpanded), () => IsExpanded);
				IsExpanded = isExpanded;
			});
			RaiseIsExpandedChanged();
		}

			RowDataGenerator.SetViewValidationError(Handle, ValidationError, source);
		}

		public void SetValidationError(BaseValidationError value, ValidationErrorSource source)
		{
			try
			{
				validationErrorSource.Push(source);
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ValidationErrorPropertyKey, (object)value);
			}
			finally
			{
				validationErrorSource.Pop();
			}
		}

		}

		protected internal virtual bool SetValueInternal(object value)
		{
			Locker val = valueChangedLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				object value2 = ((EditableDataObject)this).Value;
				((EditableDataObject)this).Value = value;
				return !object.Equals(value2, ((EditableDataObject)this).Value);
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected override object CoerceValue(object newValue)
		{
			PropertyGridControl propertyGridControl = RowDataGenerator.With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.View).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid);
			if (!valueChangedLocker.IsLocked && propertyGridControl != null && !IsDirty && Handle != null && RowDataGenerator != null && RowDataGenerator.DataView != null && !propertyGridControl.RaiseCellValueChanging(this, ((EditableDataObject)this).Value, newValue))
			{
				return ((EditableDataObject)this).Value;
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		protected override void OnValueChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue == ((EditableDataObject)this).Value)
			{
				return;
			}
			((DataObjectBase)this).RaiseContentChanged();
			PropertyGridControl propertyGridControl = RowDataGenerator.With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.View).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid);
			if (propertyGridControl != null && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)propertyGridControl.View.CellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor == null && !valueChangedLocker.IsLocked && !IsDirty && Handle != null && RowDataGenerator != null && RowDataGenerator.DataView != null)
			{
				Exception ex = propertyGridControl?.RaiseValidateCell(this, oldValue, ((EditableDataObject)this).Value);
				ex = ex ?? RowDataGenerator.DataView.SetValue(Handle, ((EditableDataObject)this).Value);
				string text = ex?.Message;
				text = propertyGridControl.RaiseInvalidCellException(this, ex);
				if (ex == null)
				{
					RowDataGenerator.DataView.Update();
					propertyGridControl.RaiseCellValueChanged(this, oldValue, RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetValue(Handle));
				}
				else
				{
					SetValueInternal(RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetValue(Handle));
				}
			}
		}

		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnEditSettingsChanged(BaseEditSettings oldValue)

			if (Definition == null || !Definition.isStandardDefinition)
			{
				Column.RaiseContentChanged(null);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateEditSettingsSource()
		{
			bool useTypeConverter = (Definition as PropertyDefinition).Return((PropertyDefinition x) => x.UseTypeConverterToStringConversion, () => false);
			StandardValuesSource currentEditSettingsSource = GetCurrentEditSettingsSource(EditSettings);
			if (currentEditSettingsSource != null)
			{
				currentEditSettingsSource.UseTypeConverter = useTypeConverter;
			}
		}

		private StandardValuesSource GetCurrentEditSettingsSource(BaseEditSettings settings)
		{
			return ((LookUpEditSettingsBase)(object)((settings is LookUpEditSettingsBase) ? settings : null)).With((LookUpEditSettingsBase x) => x.ItemsSource as StandardValuesSource);
		}

		protected internal bool ShouldConvertEditableValue(BaseEditSettings settings)
		{
			if (ShouldConvertValue)
			{
				return GetCurrentEditSettingsSource(settings).Return((StandardValuesSource x) => !x.UseTypeConverter, () => true);
			}
			return false;
		}

		}

		protected virtual void OnActualIsReadOnlyChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefinitionChanged(PropertyDefinitionBase oldValue, PropertyDefinitionBase newValue)
		{
			UnsubscribeDefinitionEvents(oldValue);
			SubscribeDefinitionEvents(newValue);
			if (!Initializing && !IsDirty)
			{
				UpdateEditSettingsSource();
				if ((newValue == null || !newValue.isStandardDefinition) && RowDataGenerator != null && RowDataGenerator.DataView != null && Handle != null)
				{
					RowDataGenerator.DataView.Invalidate(Handle);
				}
				ApplyDefinition(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ApplyDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase newValue)
		{
			RaiseShowMenuButtonChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void UnsubscribeDefinitionEvents(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null)
			{
				definition.RequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties -= OnDefinitionRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties;
			}
			if (definition is PropertyDefinition)
			{
				((PropertyDefinition)definition).ShowMenuButtonChanged -= OnDefinitionShowMenuButtonChanged;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SubscribeDefinitionEvents(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			UnsubscribeDefinitionEvents(definition);
			if (definition != null)
			{
				definition.RequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties += OnDefinitionRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties;
			}
			if (definition is PropertyDefinition)
			{
				((PropertyDefinition)definition).ShowMenuButtonChanged += OnDefinitionShowMenuButtonChanged;
			}
		}

		private void OnDefinitionRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties(object sender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			RecalcCanShowCollectionButtonForSubtree();
		}

		protected void RecalcCanShowCollectionButtonForSubtree()
		{
			RecalcCanShowCollectionButton();
			if (IsCollectionRow && RowDataGenerator != null && !IsDirty && !Handle.IsInvalid)
			{
				(from x in RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetChildren(Handle).Select(RowDataGenerator.GetRowDataForHandle)
					where x != null
					select x).ForEach(delegate(RowData x)
				{
					x.RecalcCanShowCollectionButton();
				});
			}
		}

		protected internal void RecalcCanShowCollectionButton()
		{
			canShowCollectionButton = new Lazy<bool>(CalcCanShowCollectionButton);
			((DependencyObject)this).CoerceValue(IsModifiableCollectionItemProperty);
		}

		private object CoerceIsModifiableCollectionItem(object baseValue)
		{
			return CanShowCollectionButton;
		}

		protected virtual bool CalcCanShowCollectionButton()
		{
			if (Definition == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			Func<bool> func = delegate
			{
				bool? flag = (bool?)Definition.GetValue(CollectionDefinition.HideCollectionButtonProperty);
				if (flag.HasValue)
				{
					return !flag.Value;
				}
				if (IsCollectionRow)
				{
					if (CheckCustomNonCollectionDefinition(Definition))
					{
						return false;
					}
					return (Definition as CollectionDefinition).GetValue(StandardDefinition as CollectionDefinition, (CollectionDefinition collectionDefinition) => collectionDefinition?.AllowAddItems ?? RowDataGenerator.DataView.CanAddCollectionNewItem(Handle), (bool v1, bool v2) => v1 && v2);
				}
				if (!IsCollectionRow && Parent != null)
				{
					if (Parent.Definition != null)
					{
						if (CheckCustomNonCollectionDefinition(Parent.Definition))
						{
							return false;
						}
						CollectionDefinition d = Parent.Definition as CollectionDefinition;
						CollectionDefinition d2 = Parent.StandardDefinition as CollectionDefinition;
						if (!d.GetValue(d2, (CollectionDefinition collectionDefinition) => collectionDefinition?.AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializer ?? false, (bool v1, bool v2) => v1 || v2))
						{
							if (RowDataGenerator.DataView.CanAddCollectionNewItem(Parent.Handle))
							{
								return d.GetValue(d2, (CollectionDefinition collectionDefinition) => collectionDefinition?.AllowRemoveItems ?? true, (bool v1, bool v2) => v1 && v2);
							}
							return false;
						}
						return true;
					}
					return RowDataGenerator.DataView.CanAddCollectionNewItem(Parent.Handle);
				}
				return true;
			};
			bool defaultValue = func();
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = RowDataGenerator.View.PropertyGrid;
			CollectionButtonsVisibilityEventArgs e = new CollectionButtonsVisibilityEventArgs(PropertyGridControl.CollectionButtonsVisibilityEvent, Handle, RowDataGenerator, defaultValue, (!IsCollectionRow) ? CollectionButtonKind.Remove : CollectionButtonKind.Add);
			propertyGrid.RaiseEvent(e);
			return e.IsVisible;
		}

		private static bool CheckCustomNonCollectionDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (!definition.isStandardDefinition)
			{
				return !(definition is CollectionDefinition);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDefinitionShowMenuButtonChanged(object sender, EventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseShowMenuButtonChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnStandardValuesChanged(IEnumerable oldValue, IEnumerable newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsReadOnlyChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			RaiseIsReadOnlyChanged();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateIsReadOnly()
		{
			if (Definition != null)
			{
				Definition.Apply(this);
			}
		}

		public void BeginInit()
		{
			initializationLocker.Lock();
		}

		public void EndInit()
		{
			initializationLocker.Unlock();
		}

		public void SetEditableObject(EditableObjectWrapper wrapper)
		{
			valueChangedLocker.DoLockedAction<object>((Func<object>)(() => EditableObject = wrapper));
		}

		public void UpdateValue()
		{
			object value = RowDataGenerator.DataView.GetValue(Handle);
			if (!object.Equals(((EditableDataObject)this).Value, value))
			{
				SetValueInternal(value);
			}
		}
	}
	public class EditorColumn : IInplaceEditorColumn, IDefaultEditorViewInfo
	{
		[ThreadStatic]
		private static BaseEditSettings cachedEditSettings;

		private WeakReference ownerWR = new WeakReference(null);

		private ColumnContentChangedEventHandler contentChanged;

		public RowData Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return (RowData)ownerWR.Target;
			}
			set
			{
				ownerWR = new WeakReference(value);
			}
		}

		ControlTemplate IInplaceEditorColumn.DisplayTemplate => null;

		BaseEditSettings IInplaceEditorColumn.EditSettings
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_003c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0042: Expected O, but got Unknown
				object obj = Owner.With((RowData x) => x.EditSettings);
				if (obj == null)
				{
					obj = cachedEditSettings;
					if (obj == null)
					{
						TextEditSettings val = new TextEditSettings();
						cachedEditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)val;
						obj = (object)val;
					}
				}
				return (BaseEditSettings)obj;
			}
		}

		ControlTemplate IInplaceEditorColumn.EditTemplate => null;

		DataTemplateSelector IInplaceEditorColumn.EditorTemplateSelector => Owner.With((RowData x) => x.CellTemplateSelector);

		System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment IDefaultEditorViewInfo.DefaultHorizontalAlignment => System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;

		bool IDefaultEditorViewInfo.HasTextDecorations => false;

		event ColumnContentChangedEventHandler IInplaceEditorColumn.ContentChanged
		{
			add
			{
				//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
				contentChanged = (ColumnContentChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine((Delegate)(object)contentChanged, (Delegate)(object)value);
			}
			remove
			{
				//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
				contentChanged = (ColumnContentChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove((Delegate)(object)contentChanged, (Delegate)(object)value);
			}
		}

		public EditorColumn(RowData owner)
		{
			Owner = owner;
		}

		protected internal void RaiseContentChanged(DependencyProperty property)
		{
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (contentChanged != null)
			{
				contentChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ColumnContentChangedEventArgs(property));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowMenuChanged(ShowMenuMode oldValue, ShowMenuMode newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdateActualShowCommandButton();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueColumnMinWidthChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			View.ResizingStrategy.ValueMinWidth = newValue;
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueColumnMaxWidthChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			View.ResizingStrategy.ValueMaxWidth = newValue;
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueColumnWidthChanged(GridLength oldValue, GridLength newValue)
		{
			View.ResizingStrategy.ValueWidth = newValue;
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderColumnMinWidthChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			View.ResizingStrategy.HeaderMinWidth = newValue;
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderColumnMaxWidthChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			View.ResizingStrategy.HeaderMaxWidth = newValue;
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderColumnWidthChanged(GridLength oldValue, GridLength newValue)
		{
			View.ResizingStrategy.HeaderWidth = newValue;
		}

		protected virtual void UseOptimizedEditorsChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			useOptimizedEditor = newValue;
			DataView.Do(delegate(DataViewBase x)
			{
				x.Invalidate(RowHandle.Root);
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnExpandButtonsVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdateExpandButtonVisibility();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowEditorButtonsChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdateShowEditorButtons();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHighlightingModeChanged(HeaderHighlightingMode oldValue, HeaderHighlightingMode newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdateHighlightingMode();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowGridLinesChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdateShowGridLines();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowPaddingChanged(Thickness oldValue, Thickness newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdatePadding();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SelectedPropertyPathChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.SelectViaPath(newValue);
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(SelectionChangedEvent));
		}

		protected virtual void SelectedPropertyValueChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionTemplateSelectorChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			RootPropertyDefinition.SetValue(PropertyDefinitionBase.PropertyDefinitionTemplateSelectorProperty, args.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionTemplateChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			RootPropertyDefinition.SetValue(PropertyDefinitionBase.PropertyDefinitionTemplateProperty, args.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionStyleChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			RootPropertyDefinition.SetValue(PropertyDefinitionBase.PropertyDefinitionStyleProperty, args.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			RootPropertyDefinition.SetValue(PropertyDefinitionBase.PropertyDefinitionsSourceProperty, args.NewValue);
		}

		protected internal void ExecuteMenuCustomizations(DependencyObject context)
		{
			menuCustomizations.Execute(context);
		}

		public PropertyGridControl()
		{
			//IL_012d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0137: Expected O, but got Unknown
			View = InitializeView();
			View.BeginInit();
			selectSearchControlCommand = new SelectSearchControlCommand(this);
			SelectionStrategy = new SelectionStrategy(this);
			NavigationManager = new NavigationManager(this);
			DataController = new DataController();
			DataController.RowHandlesInvalidated += OnRowHandlesInvalidated;
			PropertyBuilder = new PropertyBuilder(this);
			RowDataGenerator = CreateRowDataGenerator();
			RowDataGenerator.BeginInit();
			RowDataGenerator.PropertyBuilder = PropertyBuilder;
			RowDataGenerator.View = View;
			UpdateDataViewByShowCategories();
			RowDataGenerator.EndInit();
			PropertyBuilder.BeginInit();
			RootPropertyDefinition = new RootPropertyDefinition();
			RootPropertyDefinition.Builder = PropertyBuilder;
			SetValue(PropertyDefinitionsPropertyKey, RootPropertyDefinition.PropertyDefinitions);
			PropertyBuilder.Definitions = RootPropertyDefinition.PropertyDefinitions;
			PropertyBuilder.EndInit();
			menuCustomizations = new CustomizationActionsField((DependencyObject)this, false);
			InitializeInputBindings();
			AddLogicalChild(PropertyBuilder);
			AddLogicalChild(RootPropertyDefinition);
			PropertyGridHelper.SetPropertyGrid(this, this);
			View.EndInit();
			About.CheckLicenseShowNagScreen(typeof(PropertyGridControl));
			stringComparer = new Lazy<IComparer<string>>(() => CreateStringComparer());
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CloseEditor();
			HideEditor();
			DataController.VisualClient = null;
			DataController.Source = null;
			DataController.Update();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowHandlesInvalidated(IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles)
		{
			PropertyBuilder.HandlesInvalidated(handles);
		}

		public string GetParentPath(string fullPath)
		{
			return FieldNameHelper.GetParentFieldName(fullPath);
		}

		public void ShowPropertyMenu(string propertyPath)
		{
			RowHandle handleByFieldName = DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(propertyPath);
			View.MenuHelper.ShowCommandsMenu(handleByFieldName);
		}

		public void ShowNewItemMenu(string propertyPath)
		{
			RowHandle handleByFieldName = DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(propertyPath);
			View.MenuHelper.ShowCollectionMenu(handleByFieldName);
		}

		public void HideMenu()
		{
			((Popup)(object)View.MenuHelper.Menu).IsOpen = false;
		}

		public virtual void Expand(string propertyPath)
		{
			DataView.MakeRowVisible(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(propertyPath), withChildren: true);
		}

		public virtual void Collapse(string propertyPath)
		{
			DataView.SetIsExpanded(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(propertyPath), isExpanded: false);
		}

		public object GetRowValueByRowPath(string propertyPath)
		{
			return DataView.GetValue(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(propertyPath));
		}

		public Exception SetRowValueByRowPath(string propertyPath, object value)
		{
			return DataView.SetValue(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(propertyPath), value);
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView(string path)
		{
			RowDataGenerator.ScrollToAsync(path);
		}

		public void ShowEditor(bool selectAll = false)
		{
			View.ShowEditor(selectAll);
		}

		public void HideEditor()
		{
			View.HideEditor();
		}

		public void CloseEditor()
		{
			View.CloseEditor();
		}

		public void PostEditor()
		{
			View.PostEditor();
		}

		protected virtual RowDataGenerator CreateRowDataGenerator()
		{
			return new RowDataGenerator();
		}

		protected internal virtual void InitializeInputBindings()
		{
			base.InputBindings.Add(new InputBinding(selectSearchControlCommand, new KeyGesture(Key.F, ModifierKeys.Control)));
		}

		protected internal virtual PropertyGridView InitializeView()
		{
			PropertyGridView propertyGridView = CreatePropertyGridView();
			propertyGridView.PropertyGrid = this;
			return propertyGridView;
		}

		protected virtual PropertyGridView CreatePropertyGridView()
		{
			return new PropertyGridView();
		}

		protected internal virtual CategoriesShowMode OnShowCategoriesChanging(CategoriesShowMode newValue)
		{
			if (View == null || View.CheckCanNavigate())
			{
				return newValue;
			}
			return ShowCategories;
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowCategoriesChanged(CategoriesShowMode oldValue)
		{
			UpdateDataViewByShowCategories();
			View.ShowCategories = ShowCategories;
		}

		protected virtual void SelectedObjectsChanged(IEnumerable oldValue)
		{
			UpdateDataControllerSource();
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedObjectChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			UpdateDataControllerSource();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateDataControllerSource()
		{
			if (SelectedObject != null)
			{
				DataController.Source = SelectedObject;
				DataController.IsMultiSource = false;
			}
			else
			{
				DataController.Source = SelectedObjects;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowExpandingChanged(AllowExpandingMode? oldValue)
		{
			InvalidateData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnTrimDisplayTextChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			InvalidateData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnFilterModeChanged(PropertyGridFilterMode oldValue)
		{
			foreach (EventHandler filterModeChangedHandler in filterModeChangedHandlers)
			{
				filterModeChangedHandler(this, new EventArgs());
			}
			InvalidateData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnSortModeChanged(PropertyGridSortMode oldValue, PropertyGridSortMode newValue)
		{
			InvalidateData();
		}

		protected internal virtual bool CanShowEditor(CellEditor cellEditor)
		{
			if (RowDataGenerator.With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.ItemsSource).Return((RowsCollectionView x) => x.AsyncUpdatePending, () => false))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return cellEditor.With((CellEditor x) => x.RowData).With((RowData x) => x.Handle).Return(RaiseShowingEditorEvent, () => false);
		}

		protected internal virtual bool RaiseShowingEditorEvent(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return !((CancelRoutedEventArgs)new ShowingPropertyGridEditorEventArgs(handle, RowDataGenerator)
			{
				RoutedEvent = ShowingEditorEvent
			}.Do((Action<ShowingPropertyGridEditorEventArgs>)RaiseEvent)).Cancel;
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseShownEditorEvent(RowHandle handle, IBaseEdit edit)
		{
			RaiseEvent(new PropertyGridEditorEventArgs(ShownEditorEvent, handle, RowDataGenerator, edit));
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseHiddenEditorEvent(RowHandle handle, IBaseEdit edit)
		{
			RaiseEvent(new PropertyGridEditorEventArgs(HiddenEditorEvent, handle, RowDataGenerator, edit));
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseSortEvent(PropertyGridSortingEventArgs args, PropertyGridSortMode sortMode)
		{
			sortMode = ((sortMode != PropertyGridSortMode.Unspecified) ? sortMode : (sortMode = ((SortMode == PropertyGridSortMode.Unspecified) ? PropertyGridSortMode.NoSort : SortMode)));
			switch (sortMode)
			{
			case PropertyGridSortMode.Ascending:
				args.ResultCollection = args.SourceCollection.OrderBy((RowInfo x) => x.Header ?? "", StringComparer);
				break;
			case PropertyGridSortMode.Descending:
				args.ResultCollection = args.SourceCollection.OrderByDescending((RowInfo x) => x.Header ?? "", StringComparer);
				break;
			case PropertyGridSortMode.Definitions:
				args.ResultCollection = args.SourceCollection.OrderBy((RowInfo x) => PropertyDefinitionBase.GetIndexInCollection(x.Definition));
				break;
			case PropertyGridSortMode.Custom:
				args.RoutedEvent = SortEvent;
				RaiseEvent(args);
				break;
			}
		}

		protected virtual IComparer<string> CreateStringComparer()
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			return (IComparer<string>)new NaturalStringComparer();
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseCellValueChanged(RowData data, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (data != null && data.FullPath != null && data.RowDataGenerator != null && !data.IsDirty)
			{
				RaiseCellValueChanged(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(data.FullPath), oldValue, newValue);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseCellValueChanged(RowHandle handle, object oldValue, object newValue, bool isReset = false)
		{
			RaiseEvent(new CellValueChangedEventArgs(handle, RowDataGenerator, oldValue, newValue, isReset)
			{
				RoutedEvent = CellValueChangedEvent
			});
		}

		protected internal virtual CellValueChangingEventArgs RaiseCellValueChanging(RowHandle handle, object oldValue, object newValue, bool isReset = false)
		{
			CellValueChangingEventArgs e = new CellValueChangingEventArgs(handle, RowDataGenerator, oldValue, newValue, isReset)
			{
				RoutedEvent = CellValueChangingEvent
			};
			RaiseEvent((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e);
			return e;
		}

		protected internal virtual bool RaiseCellValueChanging(RowData data, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (data == null || data.FullPath == null || data.RowDataGenerator == null || data.IsDirty)
			{
				return true;
			}
			CellValueChangingEventArgs e = RaiseCellValueChanging(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(data.FullPath), oldValue, newValue);
			return !((CancelRoutedEventArgs)e).Cancel;
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseValidateCell(RowData data, object oldValue, object newValue, out Exception exception, out bool isValid, out object errorContent, out ErrorType errorType)
		{
			//IL_00a3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a9: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			exception = null;
			isValid = true;
			errorContent = null;
			errorType = (ErrorType)0;
			if (data == null || data.FullPath == null || data.RowDataGenerator == null || data.IsDirty)
			{
				return;
			}
			ValidateCellEventArgs e = new ValidateCellEventArgs(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(data.FullPath), data.RowDataGenerator, oldValue, newValue);
			PropertyDefinitionBase definition = data.Definition;
			try
			{
				if (definition != null)
				{
					e.RoutedEvent = PropertyDefinition.ValidateCellEvent;
					definition.RaiseEvent(e);
					if (e.Handled)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				e.RoutedEvent = ValidateCellEvent;
				RaiseEvent(e);
			}
			finally
			{
				exception = e.ValidationException;
				isValid = e.IsValid;
				errorContent = e.ErrorContent;
				errorType = (ErrorType)(int)e.ErrorType;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual Exception RaiseValidateCell(RowData data, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			RaiseValidateCell(data, oldValue, newValue, out var exception, out var _, out var _, out var _);
			return exception;
		}

		protected internal virtual string RaiseCustomDisplayText(RowData data, string displayText)
		{
			if (data == null || data.FullPath == null || data.RowDataGenerator == null || data.IsDirty)
			{
				return displayText;
			}
			CustomDisplayTextEventArgs e = new CustomDisplayTextEventArgs(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(data.FullPath), data.RowDataGenerator, displayText)
			{
				RoutedEvent = CustomDisplayTextEvent
			};
			RaiseEvent(e);
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				return displayText;
			}
			return e.DisplayText;
		}

		protected internal bool RaiseCustomExpand(RowHandle handle, Func<RowHandle, bool> getDefaultValue)
		{
			if (handle == null || handle.IsRoot || handle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return true;
			}
			CustomExpandEventArgs e = new CustomExpandEventArgs(handle, DataView, RowDataGenerator, getDefaultValue)
			{
				RoutedEvent = CustomExpandEvent
			};
			if (!e.Row.IsVisible)
			{
				return false;
			}
			RaiseEvent(e);
			return e.IsExpanded;
		}

		protected internal virtual object RaiseInvalidCellException(RowData data, Exception exception, object errorContent)
		{
			if (data == null || data.FullPath == null || data.RowDataGenerator == null || data.IsDirty || exception == null)
			{
				return exception?.Message;
			}
			InvalidCellExceptionEventArgs e = new InvalidCellExceptionEventArgs(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(data.FullPath), data.RowDataGenerator, exception, errorContent)
			{
				RoutedEvent = InvalidCellExceptionEvent
			};
			RaiseEvent(e);
			return e.Message;
		}

		protected internal string RaiseInvalidCellException(RowData data, Exception exception)
		{
			return Convert.ToString(RaiseInvalidCellException(data, exception, null));
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator.Create(this);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (ViewPresenter != null)
			{
				ViewPresenter.Content = null;
			}
			ViewPresenter = GetTemplateChild("PART_ViewPresenter") as ContentPresenter;
			if (ViewKindSelector != null)
			{
				ViewKindSelector.PreviewKeyDown -= ViewKindSelector_PreviewKeyDown;
			}
			ViewKindSelector = GetTemplateChild("PART_Selector") as PropertyGridViewKindSelectorControl;
			if (ViewKindSelector != null)
			{
				ViewKindSelector.PreviewKeyDown += ViewKindSelector_PreviewKeyDown;
			}
			if (ViewPresenter != null)
			{
				ViewPresenter.Content = View;
			}
			DescriptionPresenter = GetTemplateChild("PART_DescriptionPresenter") as PropertyDescriptionPresenterControl;
			IList<string> input = null;
			if (SearchControl != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)SearchControl).PreviewKeyDown -= SearchControl_PreviewKeyDown;
				input = ((SearchControl)SearchControl).MRU;
			}
			SearchControl = GetTemplateChild("PART_SearchControl") as PropertyGridSearchControl;
			if (SearchControl != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)SearchControl).PreviewKeyDown += SearchControl_PreviewKeyDown;
				input.Do(delegate(IList<string> x)
				{
					x.ForEach(delegate(string el)
					{
						((SearchControl)SearchControl).MRU.Add(el);
					});
				});
			}
			UpdateActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector();
			UpdateActualDescriptionTemplateSelector();
		}

		private void ViewKindSelector_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)View.CellEditorOwner).ProcessKeyDown(e);
		}

		private void SearchControl_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!SearchControl.GetIsPopupOpened() && e.Key != Key.Home && e.Key != Key.Left && e.Key != Key.Right && e.Key != Key.End)
			{
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)View.CellEditorOwner).ProcessKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
			oldFocusWithin = base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin;
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				oldFocusWithin = false;
			});
		}

		protected override void OnGotFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnGotFocus(e);
			if (!oldFocusWithin)
			{
				View.Focus();
			}
		}

		public override void BeginInit()
		{
			View.BeginInit();
			base.BeginInit();
		}

		public override void EndInit()
		{
			base.EndInit();
			View.EndInit();
		}

			if (sender is PropertyGridControl { View: not null } propertyGridControl)
			{
				propertyGridControl.View.ProcessMouseButtonUp(e);
			}
		}

		{
			if (sender is PropertyGridControl { View: not null } propertyGridControl)
			{
				propertyGridControl.View.ProcessMouseWheel(e);
			}
		}

		private static void ProcessPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is PropertyGridControl { View: not null } propertyGridControl)
			{
				propertyGridControl.View.ProcessMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateDataViewByShowCategories()
		{
			switch (ShowCategories)
			{
			case CategoriesShowMode.Hidden:
				DataView = new DataView(DataController);
				break;
			case CategoriesShowMode.Visible:
				DataView = new CategoryDataView(DataController);
				break;
			case CategoriesShowMode.Tabbed:
				DataView = new TabbedDataView(DataController);
				break;
			}
			UpdateDataViewFilterCriteria();
			RowDataGenerator.DataView = DataView;
		}

		protected virtual void OnUserFilterCriteriaChanged(CriteriaOperator oldValue, CriteriaOperator newValue)
		{
			UpdateDataViewFilterCriteria();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowPropertiesChanged(ShowPropertiesMode oldValue, ShowPropertiesMode newValue)
		{
			UpdateDataViewFilterCriteria();
		}

		protected virtual void OnFilerCriteriaChanged(CriteriaOperator oldValue, CriteriaOperator newValue)
		{
			UpdateDataViewFilterCriteria();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateDataViewFilterCriteria()
		{
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (DataView != null)
			{
				GroupOperator val = new GroupOperator((GroupOperatorType)0, (CriteriaOperator[])(object)new CriteriaOperator[1] { CriteriaOperator.Parse("[IsVisible] = true", new object[0]) });
				CriteriaOperatorCollection operands = val.Operands;
				if (ShowProperties == ShowPropertiesMode.WithPropertyDefinitions)
				{
					((List<CriteriaOperator>)(object)operands).Add(CriteriaOperator.Parse("[HasPropertyDefinition] = true", new object[0]));
				}
				if (!object.Equals(FilterCriteria, null))
				{
					((List<CriteriaOperator>)(object)operands).Add(FilterCriteria);
				}
				DataView.FilterCriteria = (CriteriaOperator)(object)val;
				DataView.SearchCriteria = UserFilterCriteria;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHighlightNonDefaultValuesChanged(bool newValue)
		{
			InvalidateData();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDescriptionContainerStyleSelectorChanged(StyleSelector oldValue, StyleSelector newValue)

			UpdateActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector();
		}

		{
			UpdateActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDescriptionTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)

		}

		protected virtual void OnDescriptionTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)

			UpdateActualDescriptionTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelectorChanged(StyleSelector oldValue, StyleSelector newValue)
		{
			RowData input = ((View == null) ? null : View.SelectedItem);
			ActualDescriptionContainerStyle = ((ActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector == null) ? null : ActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector.SelectStyle(input.With((RowData x) => x.Description), DescriptionPresenter));
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowDescriptionInChanged(DescriptionLocation oldValue, DescriptionLocation newValue)
		{
			foreach (RowControl item in TreeHelper.GetChildren<RowControl>((DependencyObject)this, (Func<RowControl, bool>)null).ToList())
			{
				item.UpdateActualDescription();
			}
		}

		protected internal void UpdateActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector()
		{
			PropertyDefinitionBase definition = View.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.SelectedItem).With((RowData x) => x.Definition);
			ActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector = GetDescriptionContainerStyleSelector(definition);
		}

		protected internal void UpdateActualDescriptionTemplateSelector()
		{
			PropertyDefinitionBase definition = View.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.SelectedItem).With((RowData x) => x.Definition);
			ActualDescriptionTemplateSelector = GetDescriptionTemplateSelector(definition);
		}

		protected internal PrioritizedStyleSelector GetDescriptionContainerStyleSelector(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			return new PrioritizedStyleSelector
			{
				DefaultStyle = DescriptionContainerStyle,
				DefaultStyleSelector = DescriptionContainerStyleSelector,
				CustomStyle = definition.With((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.DescriptionContainerStyle),
				CustomStyleSelector = definition.With((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.DescriptionContainerStyleSelector)
			};
		}

		protected internal PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector GetDescriptionTemplateSelector(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			return new PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector
			{
				DefaultTemplate = DescriptionTemplate,
				DefaultTemplateSelector = DescriptionTemplateSelector,
				CustomTemplate = definition.With((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.DescriptionTemplate),
				CustomTemplateSelector = definition.With((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.DescriptionTemplateSelector)
			};
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateSelection(RowData rowData)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.SelectViaHandle(rowData.With((RowData x) => x.Handle));
			UpdateActualDescriptionContainerStyleSelector();
			UpdateActualDescriptionTemplateSelector();
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.ProcessNavigation(NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			return false;
		}

		IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> INavigationSupport.GetChildren()
		{
			List<FrameworkElement> list = new List<FrameworkElement>();
			if (ShowToolPanel)
			{
				list.Add(ViewKindSelector);
			}
			if (ShowSearchBox && ShowCategories != CategoriesShowMode.Tabbed)
			{
				list.Add((FrameworkElement)(object)SearchControl);
			}
			list.Add(View);
			return list;
		}

		FrameworkElement INavigationSupport.GetParent()
		{
			return null;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetSkipNavigation()
		{
			return true;
		}

		bool INavigationSupport.GetUseLinearNavigation()
		{
			return false;
		}
