	public class EditableObjectConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public IValueConverter Converter { get; set; }

		object IValueConverter.Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is EditableObjectWrapper)
			{
				value = ((EditableObjectWrapper)value).Source;
			}
			if (Converter != null)
			{
				return Converter.Convert(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
			}
			return Convert(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
		}

		object IValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (Converter != null)
			{
				return Converter.ConvertBack(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
			}
			return ConvertBack(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
		}

		protected virtual object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value;
		}

		protected virtual object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value;
		}
	}
	public class EditableObjectConverterExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		private static EditableObjectConverter instance;

		public IValueConverter Converter { get; set; }

		public EditableObjectConverterExtension()
		{
			if (instance == null)
			{
				instance = new EditableObjectConverter();
			}
		}

		public EditableObjectConverterExtension(IValueConverter converter)
		{
			Converter = converter;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			if (Converter != null)
			{
				return new EditableObjectConverter
				{
					Converter = Converter
				};
			}
			return instance;
		}
	}
	internal enum RowInfoColumns
	{
		Header,
		Description,
		HasPropertyDefinition,
		IsCategory,
		FullPath,
		Path,
		Type,
		EditableObject,
		IsVisible,
		IsAttached,
		IsReadOnly
	}
	public class RowInfo
	{
		public const string DisplayName = "Header";

		public const string DisplayText = "EditableObject";

		public const string DescriptionColumn = "Description";

		internal static string[] columns;

		protected DataViewBase view;

		protected RowHandle handle;

		protected RowDataGenerator generator;

		private string fullPath;

		private string header;

		private string path;

		private Lazy<object> description;

		private Type type;

		private object editableObject;

		private bool isCategory;

		private bool isCategoryInitialized;

		private bool hasCustomDefinition;

		private bool hasCustomDefinitionInitialized;

		private bool? isVisible;

		private bool? isAttached;

		private bool? isReadOnly;

		private Lazy<PropertyDefinitionBase> definition;

		internal static string[] Columns
		{
			get
			{
				if (columns == null)
				{
					columns = (from x in Enum.GetValues(typeof(RowInfoColumns)).OfType<RowInfoColumns>()
						select x.ToString()).ToArray();
				}
				return columns;
			}
		}

		internal RowHandle Handle => handle;

		protected internal PropertyDefinitionBase Definition => definition.Value;

		public string Header
		{
			get
			{
				if (header == null)
				{
					InitializeHeader();
				}
				return header;
			}
		}

		public object Description => description.Value;

		public bool HasPropertyDefinition
		{
			get
			{
				if (!hasCustomDefinitionInitialized)
				{
					InitializeHasPropertyDefinition();
				}
				return hasCustomDefinition;
			}
		}

		public bool IsCategory
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isCategoryInitialized)
				{
					InitializeIsCategory();
				}
				return isCategory;
			}
		}

		public string FullPath
		{
			get
			{
				if (fullPath == null)
				{
					InitializeFullPath();
				}
				return fullPath;
			}
		}

		public string Path
		{
			get
			{
				if (path == null)
				{
					InitializePath();
				}
				return path;
			}
		}

		public object EditableObject
		{
			get
			{
				if (editableObject == null)
				{
					InitializeEditableObject();
				}
				return editableObject;
			}
		}

		public Type Type
		{
			get
			{
				if (type == null)
				{
					InitializeType();
				}
				return type;
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isReadOnly.HasValue)
				{
					InitializeIsReadOnly();
				}
				return isReadOnly.Value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsAttached
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isAttached.HasValue)
				{
					InitializeIsAttached();
				}
				return isAttached.Value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsVisible
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isVisible.HasValue)
				{
					InitializeIsVisible();
				}
				return isVisible.Value;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<RowInfo> Children => view.Return((DataViewBase x) => from h in x.GetAllChildren(Handle)
			select new RowInfo(view, h, generator), () => Enumerable.Empty<RowInfo>());

		internal static FilterColumn GetColumn(RowInfoColumns column)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_004b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0055: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0068: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0079: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_008c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0096: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00c6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00d0: Expected O, but got Unknown
			FilterColumn val = new FilterColumn();
			switch (column)
			{
			case RowInfoColumns.Header:
			case RowInfoColumns.Description:
			case RowInfoColumns.FullPath:
			case RowInfoColumns.Path:
			case RowInfoColumns.Type:
				val.ColumnType = typeof(string);
				val.EditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)new TextEditSettings();
				break;
			case RowInfoColumns.HasPropertyDefinition:
			case RowInfoColumns.IsCategory:
				val.ColumnType = typeof(bool);
				val.EditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)new CheckEditSettings
				{
					IsThreeState = false
				};
				break;
			case RowInfoColumns.EditableObject:
				val.ColumnType = typeof(object);
				val.EditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)new TextEditSettings();
				break;
			case RowInfoColumns.IsVisible:
			case RowInfoColumns.IsAttached:
			case RowInfoColumns.IsReadOnly:
				val.ColumnType = typeof(bool);
				val.EditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)new CheckEditSettings();
				break;
			default:
				val.ColumnType = typeof(string);
				val.EditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)new TextEditSettings();
				break;
			}
			val.ColumnCaption = column.ToString();
			val.FieldName = column.ToString();
			return val;
		}

		protected virtual bool CheckValidSource()
		{
			if (view != null && handle != null)
			{
				return generator != null;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeIsAttached()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				isAttached = view.IsAttachedProperty(handle);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeIsReadOnly()
		{
			if (!CheckValidSource())
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsCategory)
			{
				isReadOnly = false;
				return;
			}
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = (PropertyDefinition)Definition;
			if (propertyDefinition != null && (propertyDefinition.IsReadOnly.Return((bool? x) => x.Value, () => false) || propertyDefinition.EditSettings.Return((Func<BaseEditSettings, bool>)PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.GetIsReadOnly, (Func<bool>)(() => false))))
			{
				isReadOnly = true;
				return;
			}
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition2 = (PropertyDefinition)generator.GetStandardDefinition(handle, addToLogicalTree: false);
			if (propertyDefinition2.IsReadOnly.Return((bool? x) => x.Value, () => false) || propertyDefinition2.EditSettings.Return((Func<BaseEditSettings, bool>)PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.GetIsReadOnly, (Func<bool>)(() => false)))
			{
				isReadOnly = true;
			}
			else
			{
				isReadOnly = view.ShouldRenderReadOnly(handle) || view.IsReadOnly(handle);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeFullPath()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				fullPath = view.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle) ?? "";
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeType()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				type = view.GetPropertyType(handle);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeIsVisible()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				isVisible = !Definition.If((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.Visibility != Visibility.Visible).ReturnSuccess();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeEditableObject()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				editableObject = view.GetValue(handle);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializePath()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				path = view.GetNameByHandle(handle) ?? "";
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeIsCategory()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				isCategory = view.IsGroupRowHandle(handle);
				isCategoryInitialized = true;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeHasPropertyDefinition()
		{
			if (!CheckValidSource())
			{
				return;
			}
			hasCustomDefinition = Definition.If((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => !x.isStandardDefinition || x.isResourceGeneratedDefinition).ReturnSuccess();
			if (!hasCustomDefinition)
			{
				RowHandle parent = view.GetParent(handle);
				if (parent != null && !parent.IsRoot && (GetDefinitionIfNonStandard(parent) as CollectionDefinition).ReturnSuccess())
				{
					hasCustomDefinition = true;
				}
			}
			hasCustomDefinitionInitialized = true;
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeHeader()
		{
			if (CheckValidSource())
			{
				header = GetDefinition().With((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.GetMergedHeader(view, generator.GetStandardDefinition(handle, addToLogicalTree: false), Handle));
			}
		}

		internal RowInfo()
			: this(null, null, null)
		{
		}

		internal RowInfo(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle, RowDataGenerator generator)
		{
			this.view = view;
			this.handle = handle;
			this.generator = generator;
			definition = new Lazy<PropertyDefinitionBase>(GetDefinitionIfNonStandard);
			description = new Lazy<object>(GetDescription);
		}

		internal RowInfo(RowHandle handle, RowDataGenerator generator)
			: this(generator.DataView, handle, generator)
		{
		}

		private object GetDescription()
		{
			if (!CheckValidSource())
			{
				return null;
			}
			return GetDefinition().With((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.GetMergedDescription(view, generator.GetStandardDefinition(handle, addToLogicalTree: false), Handle));
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase GetDefinition()
		{
			return Definition.Return((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x, () => generator.GetStandardDefinition(Handle, addToLogicalTree: false));
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase GetDefinitionIfNonStandard()
		{
			return GetDefinitionIfNonStandard(handle);
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase GetDefinitionIfNonStandard(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			RowData rowData = generator.RowDataFromHandle(rowHandle);
			if (rowData != null && !rowData.IsDirty && rowData.Definition != null)
			{
				return rowData.Definition.If((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => !x.isStandardDefinition || x.isResourceGeneratedDefinition);
			}
			return generator.PropertyBuilder.GetDefinition(view, rowHandle, getStandard: false) ?? generator.PropertyBuilder.GetStandardDefinition(view, rowHandle, addToLogicalTree: false).If((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.isResourceGeneratedDefinition);
		}
	}
	public class FilterColumnProvider : Decorator, ISearchPanelColumnProvider, ISearchPanelColumnProviderBase, IFilteredComponent, IFilteredComponentBase
	{
		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ColumnsPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColumnsProperty;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey AvailableColumnsPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableColumnsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CustomColumnsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OwnerProperty;

		private CriteriaOperator rowCriteria;

		private FilterCondition filterCondition;

		private string searchText;

		private FilterByColumnsMode filterByColumnsMode;

		private EventHandler rowFilterChanged;

		private EventHandler propertiesChanged;

		private EventHandler filterModeChanged;

		public ObservableCollection<string> CustomColumns
		{
			get
			{
				return (ObservableCollection<string>)GetValue(CustomColumnsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CustomColumnsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> AvailableColumns
		{
			get
			{
				return (ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>)GetValue(AvailableColumnsProperty);
			}
			protected internal set
			{
				SetValue(AvailableColumnsPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> Columns
		{
			get
			{
				return (ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>)GetValue(ColumnsProperty);
			}
			protected internal set
			{
				SetValue(ColumnsPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public PropertyGridControl Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return (PropertyGridControl)GetValue(OwnerProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OwnerProperty, value);
			}
		}

		IEnumerable<string> ISearchPanelColumnProvider.Columns => Columns;

		CriteriaOperator IFilteredComponentBase.RowCriteria
		{
			get
			{
				return rowCriteria;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!object.Equals(value, rowCriteria))
				{
					CriteriaOperator oldValue = rowCriteria;
					rowCriteria = value;
					OnRowCriteriaChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		event EventHandler IFilteredComponentBase.PropertiesChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertiesChanged = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(propertiesChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				propertiesChanged = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(propertiesChanged, value);
			}
		}

		event EventHandler IFilteredComponentBase.RowFilterChanged
		{
			add
			{
				rowFilterChanged = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(rowFilterChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				rowFilterChanged = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(rowFilterChanged, value);
			}
		}

		static FilterColumnProvider()
		{
			ColumnsPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("Columns", typeof(ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>), typeof(FilterColumnProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ColumnsProperty = ColumnsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			AvailableColumnsPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("AvailableColumns", typeof(ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>), typeof(FilterColumnProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			AvailableColumnsProperty = AvailableColumnsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CustomColumnsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CustomColumns", typeof(ObservableCollection<string>), typeof(FilterColumnProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FilterColumnProvider)d).OnCustomColumnsChanged((ObservableCollection<string>)e.OldValue, (ObservableCollection<string>)e.NewValue);
			}));
			OwnerProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Owner", typeof(PropertyGridControl), typeof(FilterColumnProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FilterColumnProvider)d).OnOwnerChanged((PropertyGridControl)e.OldValue, (PropertyGridControl)e.NewValue);
			}));
		}

		public FilterColumnProvider()
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			filterByColumnsMode = (FilterByColumnsMode)0;
			filterModeChanged = OnOwnerFilterModeChanged;
		}

		public virtual void UpdateColumns(FilterByColumnsMode mode)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0060: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			filterByColumnsMode = mode;
			AvailableColumns = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(Owner.Return((PropertyGridControl x) => x.GetAvailableColumns(), () => new ObservableCollection<string>()));
			if ((int)mode != 0)
			{
				if ((int)mode == 1)
				{
					Columns = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(CustomColumns.Return((ObservableCollection<string> x) => x, () => new ObservableCollection<string>()));
				}
				return;
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				if ((Owner.FilterMode & PropertyGridFilterMode.ByHeader) == PropertyGridFilterMode.ByHeader)
				{
					observableCollection.Add("Header");
				}
				if ((Owner.FilterMode & PropertyGridFilterMode.ByValue) == PropertyGridFilterMode.ByValue)
				{
					observableCollection.Add("EditableObject");
				}
				if ((Owner.FilterMode & PropertyGridFilterMode.ByDescription) == PropertyGridFilterMode.ByDescription)
				{
					observableCollection.Add("Description");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				observableCollection.Add("Header");
			}
			Columns = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(observableCollection);
		}

		bool ISearchPanelColumnProviderBase.UpdateFilter(string searchText, FilterCondition filterCondition, CriteriaOperator filterCriteria)
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			this.searchText = searchText;
			this.filterCondition = filterCondition;
			((IFilteredComponentBase)this).RowCriteria = filterCriteria;
			return true;
		}

		string ISearchPanelColumnProviderBase.GetSearchText()
		{
			return searchText;
		}

		IEnumerable<FilterColumn> IFilteredComponent.CreateFilterColumnCollection()
		{
			return CreateFilterColumnCollectionOverride();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowCriteriaChanged(CriteriaOperator oldValue)
		{
			PerformUpdate();
			RaiseRowFilterChangedChanged();
		}

		protected internal void RaiseRowFilterChangedChanged()
		{
			if (rowFilterChanged != null)
			{
				rowFilterChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		protected internal void RaiseFilterColumnsChanged()
		{
			if (propertiesChanged != null)
			{
				propertiesChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<FilterColumn> CreateFilterColumnCollectionOverride()
		{
			return (from x in Enum.GetValues(typeof(RowInfoColumns)).OfType<RowInfoColumns>()
				select RowInfo.GetColumn(x)).ToArray();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCustomColumnsChanged(ObservableCollection<string> oldValue, ObservableCollection<string> newValue)
		{
			PerformUpdate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnOwnerChanged(PropertyGridControl oldValue, PropertyGridControl newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.FilterModeChanged -= filterModeChanged;
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.FilterModeChanged += filterModeChanged;
			}
			PerformUpdate();
		}

		protected virtual void PerformUpdate()
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				Owner.UserFilterCriteria = ((IFilteredComponentBase)this).RowCriteria;
			}
			UpdateColumns(filterByColumnsMode);
		}

		protected void OnOwnerFilterModeChanged(object sender, EventArgs args)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UpdateColumns(filterByColumnsMode);
		}
	}
	public class FilterColumnProviderExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return new FilterColumnProvider();
		}
	}
	public class RowDataCollection : ObservableCollection<RowData>
	{
		private RowData owner;

		public RowData Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return owner;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != owner)
				{
					RowData oldValue = owner;
					owner = value;
					OnOwnerChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal ReadOnlyCollection<RowData> UnsortedItems { get; internal set; }

		public RowDataCollection(RowData owner)
		{
			Owner = owner;
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				ForceUpdateChildren(e.NewItems, delegate(RowData rb)
				{
					rb.Level = Owner.Level + ((!Owner.IsCategory && !Owner.Handle.IsRoot) ? 1 : 0);
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOwnerChanged(RowData oldValue)
		{
			ForceUpdateChildren(this, delegate(RowData rb)
			{
				rb.Level = Owner.Level + ((!Owner.IsCategory && !Owner.Handle.IsRoot) ? 1 : 0);
			});
		}

		public virtual void ForceUpdateChildren(IList list, params Action<RowData>[] updateActions)
		{
			list = list ?? this;
			if (Owner == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (RowData item in list)
			{
				foreach (Action<RowData> action in updateActions)
				{
					action(item);
				}
			}
		}
	}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SourceBindingProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("SourceBinding", typeof(object), typeof(PropertyWrapper), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnSourceBindingPropertyChanged));

		public static object GetSourceBinding(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return obj.GetValue(SourceBindingProperty);
		}

		public static object GetTargetBinding(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return obj.GetValue(TargetBindingProperty);
		}

		public static void SetSourceBinding(DependencyObject obj, object value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(SourceBindingProperty, value);
		}

		public static void SetTargetBinding(DependencyObject obj, object value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(TargetBindingProperty, value);
		}

		protected static void OnSourceBindingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetTargetBinding(d, e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class PGVirtualizingStackPanel : VirtualizingStackPanel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsStandardPanelProperty;

		private static readonly Action<VirtualizingStackPanel, bool> set_InRecyclingMode;

		public bool IsStandardPanel
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsStandardPanelProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsStandardPanelProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static PGVirtualizingStackPanel()
		{
			IsStandardPanelProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsStandardPanel", typeof(bool), typeof(PGVirtualizingStackPanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((PGVirtualizingStackPanel)d).OnIsStandardPanelChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			if (Net45Detector.IsNet45())
			{
				set_InRecyclingMode = ReflectionHelper.CreateInstanceMethodHandler<VirtualizingStackPanel, Action<VirtualizingStackPanel, bool>>((VirtualizingStackPanel)null, "set_InRecyclingMode", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic, (int?)null, (Type[])null, true);
			}
			else
			{
				set_InRecyclingMode = delegate
				{
				};
			}
			PropertyGridHelper.ViewProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PGVirtualizingStackPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((PGVirtualizingStackPanel)d).OnViewChanged((PropertyGridView)e.OldValue, (PropertyGridView)e.NewValue);
			}));
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewChanged(PropertyGridView oldValue, PropertyGridView newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null && oldValue.VirtualizingPanel == this)
			{
				oldValue.VirtualizingPanel = null;
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.VirtualizingPanel = this;
			}
		}

		public PGVirtualizingStackPanel()
		{
			base.CanHorizontallyScroll = false;
			base.SizeChanged += OnSizeChanged;
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void OnSizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateViewportSize();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsStandardPanelChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateViewportSize();
		}

		public new void BringIndexIntoView(int index)
		{
			base.BringIndexIntoView(index);
		}

		private void UpdateViewportSize()
		{
			if (IsStandardPanel)
			{
				PropertyGridHelper.SetViewportSize(this, base.RenderSize);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCleanUpVirtualizedItem(CleanUpVirtualizedItemEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.UIElement is RowControlBase { IsSelected: not false })
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
			}
			base.OnCleanUpVirtualizedItem(e);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			ClearAutomationEventsHelper.ClearAutomationEvents();
			set_InRecyclingMode(this, ItemsControl.GetItemsOwner(this).Return(VirtualizingPanel.GetVirtualizationMode, () => VirtualizationMode.Standard) == VirtualizationMode.Recycling);
			PropertyGridHelper.GetView(this).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.ImmediateActionsManager).If((ImmediateActionsManager x) => x.FindActionOfType(typeof(NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateActionBase)) != null)
				.Do(delegate(ImmediateActionsManager x)
				{
					x.ExecuteActions();
				});
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}
	}
	public class PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public DataTemplate DefaultTemplate { get; set; }

		public DataTemplateSelector DefaultTemplateSelector { get; set; }

		public DataTemplate CustomTemplate { get; set; }

		public DataTemplateSelector CustomTemplateSelector { get; set; }

		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			if (CustomTemplate != null)
			{
				return CustomTemplate;
			}
			if (CustomTemplateSelector != null)
			{
				return CustomTemplateSelector.SelectTemplate(item, container);
			}
			if (DefaultTemplate != null)
			{
				return DefaultTemplate;
			}
			if (DefaultTemplateSelector != null)
			{
				return DefaultTemplateSelector.SelectTemplate(item, container);
			}
			return base.SelectTemplate(item, container);
		}
	}
	public class PrioritizedStyleSelector : StyleSelector
	{

		public StyleSelector DefaultStyleSelector { get; set; }

		public Style CustomStyle { get; set; }

		public StyleSelector CustomStyleSelector { get; set; }

		public override Style SelectStyle(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			if (CustomStyle != null)
			{
				return CustomStyle;
			}
			if (CustomStyleSelector != null)
			{
				return CustomStyleSelector.SelectStyle(item, container);
			}
			if (DefaultStyle != null)
			{
				return DefaultStyle;
			}
			if (DefaultStyleSelector != null)
			{
				return DefaultStyleSelector.SelectStyle(item, container);
			}
			return base.SelectStyle(item, container);
		}
	}

		protected virtual void OnCustomCellTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)

			((DependencyObject)this).CoerceValue(IsCellTemplateOverriddenProperty);
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateData();
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateContent(true);
		}

			((DependencyObject)this).CoerceValue(IsCellTemplateOverriddenProperty);
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateData();
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateContent(true);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCellTemplateOverriddenChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorSourceType = (BaseEditSourceType)(newValue ? 1 : 0);
		}

		static CellEditor()
		{
			CustomCellTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CustomCellTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(CellEditor), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnCustomCellTemplateChanged((DataTemplate)e.OldValue, (DataTemplate)e.NewValue);
			}));
			CustomCellTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CustomCellTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(CellEditor), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnCustomCellTemplateSelectorChanged((DataTemplateSelector)e.OldValue, (DataTemplateSelector)e.NewValue);
			}));
			IsCellTemplateOverriddenPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("IsCellTemplateOverridden", typeof(bool), typeof(CellEditor), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnIsCellTemplateOverriddenChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
			}, (DependencyObject d, object e) => ((CellEditor)(object)d).UpdateIsCellTemplateOverridden(e)));
			IsCellTemplateOverriddenProperty = IsCellTemplateOverriddenPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(CellEditor);
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeFromHandle, new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsEditorFocusedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsEditorFocused", typeof(bool), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnIsEditorFocusedChanged((bool)e.NewValue);
			}));
			RowDataProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("RowData", typeof(RowData), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnRowDataChanged((RowData)e.OldValue);
			}));
			OwnerViewProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OwnerView", typeof(PropertyGridView), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnOwnerViewChanged((PropertyGridView)e.OldValue);
			}));
			RowControlProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("RowControl", typeof(RowControlBase), typeFromHandle, (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnRowControlChanged((RowControlBase)e.OldValue);
			}));
			DataValidationErrorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DataValidationError", typeof(BaseValidationError), typeof(CellEditor), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
				((CellEditor)(object)d).OnDataValidationErrorChanged((BaseValidationError)e.OldValue);
			}));
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsEditorFocusedChanged(bool newValue)
		{
			if (newValue)
			{
				IBaseEdit editCore = base.editCore;
				if (editCore != null)
				{
					editCore.UpdateToolTip();
				}
			}
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnIsFocusedCellChanged();
			FrameworkElement input = base.editCore as FrameworkElement;
			if (ValidationService.GetAllowAccessibilityAlerts((DependencyObject)(object)this) && newValue)
			{
				UpdateCellStatus("PropertyGridCell_IsEditorFocusedChanged");
				input.Do(delegate(FrameworkElement x)
				{
					ToolTipService.AddToolTipOpeningHandler(x, TooltipOpeningHandler);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				AutomationProperties.SetItemStatus((DependencyObject)(object)this, string.Empty);
				input.Do(delegate(FrameworkElement x)
				{
					ToolTipService.RemoveToolTipOpeningHandler(x, TooltipOpeningHandler);
				});
			}
		}

		private void TooltipOpeningHandler(object sender, ToolTipEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateCellStatus("PropertyGridCell_EditCoreTooltipOpening");
		}

		private void UpdateCellStatus(string sourceId)
		{
			AccessibilityValidationHelper.AddAlert((Func<FrameworkElement>)(() => (FrameworkElement)(object)this), (Func<BaseValidationError>)(() => DataValidationError), (Func<string>)null, true, false, (Func<AutomationPeer>)null, sourceId);
		}

		public void RefreshFocus()
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnIsFocusedCellChanged();
		}

		protected override void SetKeyboardFocus()
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.SetKeyboardFocus();
			if (((UIElement)this).IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement child in TreeHelper.GetChildren<FrameworkElement>((DependencyObject)(object)this, (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)((FrameworkElement x) => x.Focusable)))
			{
				if (KeyboardHelper.Focus((UIElement)child))
				{
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnEditorActivated()
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.IsValueChanged = !object.Equals(((InplaceEditorBase)this).GetEditableValue(), ((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.EditValue);
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			if (propertyGrid != null && RowData != null)
			{
				propertyGrid.RaiseShownEditorEvent(RowData.Handle, base.editCore);
			}
		}

		protected sealed override bool RaiseShowingEditor()
		{
			return PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid((DependencyObject)(object)this)?.CanShowEditor(this) ?? false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowControlChanged(RowControlBase oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator.Create<CellEditor>(this);
		}

		protected override void OnEditValueChanged(object sender, EditValueChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnEditValueChanged(sender, e);
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = RowData?.Definition as PropertyDefinition;
			if (isUpdating || propertyDefinition == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			BaseEditSettings val = ((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorColumn.With((IInplaceEditorColumn x) => x.EditSettings);
			if (propertyDefinition.PostOnEditValueChanged || PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.GetPostOnEditValueChanged(val))
			{
				if (OwnerView != null)
				{
					OwnerView.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)PostEditorAction);
				}
				else
				{
					PostEditorAction();
				}
			}
			else if (PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.GetPostOnPopupClosed(val) && PopupBaseEditHelper.GetIsValueChangedViaPopup(val))
			{
				PostEditorAction();
			}
		}

		private void PostEditorAction()
		{
			if (((InplaceEditorBase)this).IsEditorVisible)
			{
				((InplaceEditorBase)this).PostEditor(true);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowDataChanged(RowData oldValue)
		{
			if (RowData == null)
			{
				((InplaceEditorBase)this).SetEdit((IBaseEdit)null);
				((DependencyObject)this).ClearValue(CustomCellTemplateSelectorProperty);
				((DependencyObject)this).ClearValue(CustomCellTemplateProperty);
				((DependencyObject)this).ClearValue(DataValidationErrorProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!RowData.IsCategory)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(CustomCellTemplateProperty, (BindingBase)new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Definition.CellTemplate")
					{
						Source = RowData
					});
					((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(CustomCellTemplateSelectorProperty, (BindingBase)new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Definition.CellTemplateSelector")
					{
						Source = RowData
					});
				}
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(DataValidationErrorProperty, (BindingBase)new System.Windows.Data.Binding("ValidationError")
				{
					Source = RowData
				});
			}
			((DependencyObject)this).CoerceValue(IsCellTemplateOverriddenProperty);
			EditorColumn editorColumn = oldValue.With((RowData x) => x.Column);
			EditorColumn editorColumn2 = RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Column);
			if (editorColumn != editorColumn2)
			{
				((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnOwnerChanged((IInplaceEditorColumn)(object)editorColumn);
			}
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateEditorDataContext();
		}

		protected override void UpdateEditorDataContext()
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateEditorDataContext();
			if (base.editCore != null && ((int)((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorSourceType == 1 || ((InplaceEditorBase)this).IsProperEditorSettings()))
			{
				((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateEditValue(base.editCore);
			}
		}

		protected override bool IsProperEditorSettings()
		{
			return EditorSettingsProvider.Default.IsCompatible(base.editCore, ((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorColumn.EditSettings);
		}

		protected override DataTemplate SelectTemplate()
		{
			if (((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorColumn != null && ((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorColumn.EditorTemplateSelector != null)
			{
				return ((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorColumn.EditorTemplateSelector.SelectTemplate(RowData, (DependencyObject)(object)this);
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual void OnOwnerViewChanged(PropertyGridView oldValue)
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnOwnerChanged((IInplaceEditorColumn)(object)RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Column));
		}

		public virtual object GetEditableValueForExternalEditor()
		{
			if (RowData == null)
			{
				return ((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditableValue;
			}
			if (RowData.ShouldConvertValue)
			{
				return ((EditableDataObject)RowData).Value;
			}
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.DoValidate();
			return ((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditableValue;
		}

		public virtual void SetEditableValueFromExternalEditor(object value)
		{
			if (RowData != null)
			{
				((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditableValue = value;
				if (RowData.ShouldConvertValue)
				{
					((InplaceEditorBase)this).CommitEditor(true);
					OwnerView.EnqueueShowEditor(RowData.FullPath);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override object GetEditableValue()
		{
			if (RowData != null)
			{
				if (RowData.ShouldConvertEditableValue(base.editCore.With((IBaseEdit x) => x.Settings)) && ((EditableDataObject)RowData).Value != null)
				{
					return RowData.RowDataGenerator.DataView.ConvertToType(RowData.Handle, ((EditableDataObject)RowData).Value);
				}
				return ((EditableDataObject)RowData).Value;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override EditableDataObject GetEditorDataContext()
		{
			return (EditableDataObject)(object)RowData;
		}

		protected override bool IsInactiveEditorButtonVisible()
		{
			if (OwnerView != null && !OwnerView.PropertyGrid.ShowGridLines)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (RowData == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (RowControl is RowControl && ((RowControl)RowControl).ShowEditorButtons)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (base.editCore != null && PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.GetIsStandardEditSettings((DependencyObject)(object)RowData.EditSettings))
			{
				return RowData.If((RowData x) => x.Handle != null).With((RowData x) => x.RowDataGenerator).With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.DataView)
					.If((DataViewBase x) => x.IsNewInstanceInitializer(RowData.Handle))
					.ReturnSuccess();
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual bool PostEditorCore(object editableValue)
		{
			//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_014f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (((InplaceEditorBase)this).HasAccessToCellValue && RowData != null && RowData.Handle != null)
			{
				object value = ((EditableDataObject)RowData).Value;
				if (PropertyGrid != null && !PropertyGrid.RaiseCellValueChanging(RowData, value, editableValue))
				{
					return true;
				}
				ValidationErrorSource source;
				BaseValidationError validationError = OwnerView.GetValidationError(RowData.Handle, out source);
				if (validationError != null)
				{
					RowData.SetValidationError(validationError, source);
					return false;
				}
				DataViewBase dataView = OwnerView.PropertyGrid.DataView;
				source = ValidationErrorSource.Unknown;
				RowData.SetValidationError(null, source);
				Exception exception = null;
				bool isValid = true;
				object errorContent = null;
				ErrorType errorType = (ErrorType)1;
				if (PropertyGrid != null)
				{
					PropertyGrid.RaiseValidateCell(RowData, value, editableValue, out exception, out isValid, out errorContent, out errorType);
					if (exception != null || !isValid)
					{
						source = ValidationErrorSource.User;
					}
				}
				if (isValid)
				{
					exception = exception ?? dataView.SetValue(RowData.Handle, editableValue);
				}
				errorContent = ((PropertyGrid == null) ? (errorContent ?? exception.Message) : PropertyGrid.RaiseInvalidCellException(RowData, exception ?? (isValid ? null : new Exception(Convert.ToString(errorContent))), errorContent));
				if (exception != null || !isValid)
				{
					if (source != ValidationErrorSource.User)
					{
						source = ValidationErrorSource.Internal;
					}
					CellValidationError value2 = new CellValidationError(errorContent, exception, errorType);
					RowData.SetValidationError((BaseValidationError)(object)value2, source);
					return false;
				}
				if (PropertyGrid != null)
				{
					((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.IsValueChanged = false;
					dataView.Update();
					PropertyGrid.RaiseCellValueChanged(RowData, value, dataView.GetValue(dataView.GetHandleByFieldName(RowData.FullPath)));
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override bool PostEditorCore()
		{
			if (((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.IsValueChanged)
			{
				return PostEditorCore(((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditableValue);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void InitializeBaseEdit(IBaseEdit newEdit, BaseEditSourceType newBaseEditSourceType)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).InitializeBaseEdit(newEdit, newBaseEditSourceType);
			if (RowControl is RowControl { ShowGridLines: false })
			{
				BaseEditHelper.SetShowBorder(newEdit, true);
			}
			newEdit.InvalidValueBehavior = (InvalidValueBehavior)0;
		}

		protected override bool ShowEditorCore(bool selectAll)
		{
			return ((InplaceEditorBase)this).ShowEditorInternal(selectAll);
		}

		protected override void OnShowEditor()
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnShowEditor();
			InplaceEditorBase currentCellEditor = ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)OwnerView.CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor;
			if (!object.Equals(currentCellEditor, this) && !object.Equals(currentCellEditor.With((Func<InplaceEditorBase, CellEditorPresenter>)PropertyGridHelper.GetEditorPresenter), PropertyGridHelper.GetEditorPresenter((DependencyObject)(object)this)))
			{
				currentCellEditor.With((Func<InplaceEditorBase, CellEditorPresenter>)PropertyGridHelper.GetEditorPresenter).Do(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
				{
					x.IsSelected = false;
				});
			}
			PropertyGridHelper.GetEditorPresenter((DependencyObject)(object)this).Do(delegate(CellEditorPresenter x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = true;
			});
			OwnerView.PropertyGrid.NavigationManager.OpenEditorOnSelection = false;
			if (((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit is FakeBaseEdit)
			{
				TreeHelper.GetChild<FrameworkElement>((DependencyObject)(object)this, (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)((FrameworkElement x) => x.IsEnabled && x.IsVisible && x.IsHitTestVisible && (object)x != this)).Do(delegate(FrameworkElement x)
				{
					x.Focus();
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataValidationErrorChanged(BaseValidationError oldValue)
		{
			UpdateEditValidationError();
		}

		private void UpdateEditValidationError()
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.SetValidationError(DataValidationError);
		}

		protected override void SetEdit(IBaseEdit value)
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).SetEdit(value);
			UpdateEditValidationError();
		}

		protected override void RestoreValidationError()
		{
			BaseValidationError validationErrorFromDataController = GetValidationErrorFromDataController();
			RowData.SetValidationError(validationErrorFromDataController, ValidationErrorSource.Internal);
		}

		private BaseValidationError GetValidationErrorFromDataController()
		{
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IEnumerable<string> validationError = PropertyGrid.DataView.GetValidationError(RowData.Handle);
			if (validationError == null || !validationError.Any())
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new BaseValidationError((object)validationError.First());
		}

		protected override void OnHiddenEditor(bool closeEditor)
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).OnHiddenEditor(closeEditor);
			RowData.UpdateValue();
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			if (propertyGrid != null)
			{
				propertyGrid.UpdateData();
				if (RowData != null)
				{
					propertyGrid.RaiseHiddenEditorEvent(RowData.Handle, base.editCore);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateEditValueCore(IBaseEdit editor)
		{
			isUpdating = true;
			if (editor != null)
			{
				editor.IsValueChanged = false;
				editor.EditValue = ((InplaceEditorBase)this).GetEditableValue();
			}
			isUpdating = false;
		}

		public override bool CanShowEditor()
		{
			return ((InplaceEditorBase)this).RaiseShowingEditor();
		}

		protected override bool ProcessKeyForLookUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!((InplaceEditorBase)this).ProcessKeyForLookUp(e))
			{
				return ShouldActivateEditor(e);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool ShouldActivateEditor(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((((InplaceEditorBase)this).IsEditorVisible || !((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.IsActivatingKey(e)) && !((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.NeedsKey(e))
			{
				if (e.Key != Key.Return && (((InplaceEditorBase)this).IsEditorVisible || e.Key != Key.F2))
				{
					if (((InplaceEditorBase)this).IsEditorVisible)
					{
						return e.Key == Key.Escape;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override HitTestResult HitTestCore(PointHitTestParameters hitTestParameters)
		{
			HitTestResult hitTestResult = ((UIElement)this).HitTestCore(hitTestParameters);
			return hitTestResult ?? new PointHitTestResult((Visual)(object)this, hitTestParameters.HitPoint);
		}

		protected override void OnInnerContentChangedCore()
		{
			if (!((InplaceEditorBase)this).IsEditorVisible)
			{
				((InplaceEditorBase)this).UpdateEditValue(base.editCore);
			}
		}

		public override void ValidateEditorCore()
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).ValidateEditorCore();
			if (base.editCore != null && ((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit != null && !((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.DoValidate())
			{
				BaseValidationError validationError = BaseEditHelper.GetValidationError((DependencyObject)(object)base.editCore);
				OwnerView.SetValidationError(RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Handle), validationError, ValidationErrorSource.Internal);
			}
			else
			{
				OwnerView.ClearValidationError(RowData.With((RowData x) => x.Handle));
			}
		}

		public void SetValidationErrorInternal(BaseValidationError validationError)
		{
			((InplaceEditorBase)this).Edit.SetValidationError(validationError);
		}

		protected override IBaseEdit CreateEditor(BaseEditSettings settings)
		{
			return settings.CreateEditor((IDefaultEditorViewInfo)(object)((InplaceEditorBase)this).EditorColumn, (EditorOptimizationMode)((PropertyGrid != null && PropertyGrid.ActualUseOptimizedEditor) ? 1 : 0));
		}

		protected internal IBaseEdit GetEditCore()
		{
			return base.editCore;
		}

	public class CellEditorOwner : InplaceEditorOwnerBase

				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected override bool EditorSetInactiveAfterClick
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		protected override EditorShowMode EditorShowMode => (EditorShowMode)1;

		protected override Type OwnerBaseType => typeof(PropertyGridView);

		public CellEditorOwner(PropertyGridView owner)
			: base((FrameworkElement)owner, true)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CommitEditing()
		{
			return View.CommitEditing();
		}

		protected override void EnqueueImmediateAction(IAction action)
		{
			View.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction(action);
		}

		protected override string GetDisplayText(InplaceEditorBase inplaceEditor, string originalDisplayText, object value)
		{
			CellEditor cellEditor = inplaceEditor as CellEditor;
			TrimDisplayTextIfNeeded(cellEditor, ref originalDisplayText);
			if (cellEditor != null && cellEditor.RowData != null && View.PropertyGrid != null)
			{
				return View.PropertyGrid.RaiseCustomDisplayText(cellEditor.RowData, originalDisplayText);
			}
			return originalDisplayText;
		}

		protected override bool? GetDisplayText(InplaceEditorBase inplaceEditor, string originalDisplayText, object value, out string displayText)
		{
			displayText = ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).GetDisplayText(inplaceEditor, originalDisplayText, value);
			return displayText != originalDisplayText;
		}

		private void TrimDisplayTextIfNeeded(CellEditor cellEditor, ref string originalDisplayText)
		{
			object obj = cellEditor.With((CellEditor x) => x.RowData).With((RowData x) => ((EditableDataObject)x).Value);
			if (obj != null && View.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.PropertyGrid).If((PropertyGridControl x) => x.TrimDisplayText).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				Type valueType = obj.GetType();
				if (originalDisplayText.If((string x) => x.StartsWith(valueType.FullName)).ReturnSuccess())
				{
					originalDisplayText = originalDisplayText.Replace(valueType.FullName, valueType.Name);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool PerformNavigationOnLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			return View.PerformNavigationOnLeftButtonDown(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject);
		}

		public override void ProcessKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).IsNavigationKey(e.Key) || ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).ActiveEditor == null || !((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).ActiveEditor.NeedsKey(e.Key, ModifierKeysHelper.GetKeyboardModifiers(e)))
			{
				View.ProcessKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		public override bool IsActiveEditorHaveValidationError()
		{
			if (((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).ActiveEditor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IBaseEdit activeEditor = ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).ActiveEditor;
			if (activeEditor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return activeEditor.HasValidationError;
		}

		protected override bool IsActivationAction(ActivationAction action, RoutedEventArgs e, bool defaultValue)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			GetIsEditorActivationActionEventArgs e2 = new GetIsEditorActivationActionEventArgs(action, e, GetEditorChild(), defaultValue, View.PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator, View.PropertyGrid.DataView.CurrentRow);
			View.PropertyGrid.RaiseEvent(e2);
			return e2.IsActivationAction;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldReraiseActivationAction(ActivationAction action, RoutedEventArgs e, bool defaultValue)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ProcessEditorActivationActionEventArgs e2 = new ProcessEditorActivationActionEventArgs(action, e, GetEditorChild(), defaultValue, View.PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator, View.PropertyGrid.DataView.CurrentRow);
			View.PropertyGrid.RaiseEvent(e2);
			return e2.RaiseEventAgain;
		}

		protected override bool NeedsKey(Key key, ModifierKeys modifiers, bool defaultValue)
		{
			if (((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).CurrentCellEditor == null || !((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).CurrentCellEditor.IsEditorVisible)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			GetActiveEditorNeedsKeyEventArgs e = new GetActiveEditorNeedsKeyEventArgs(key, modifiers, GetEditorChild(), defaultValue, View.PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator, View.PropertyGrid.DataView.CurrentRow);
			View.PropertyGrid.RaiseEvent(e);
			return e.NeedsKey;
		}

		private DependencyObject GetEditorChild()
		{
			if (((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).CurrentCellEditor != null && VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount((DependencyObject)(object)((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).CurrentCellEditor) > 0)
			{
				return VisualTreeHelper.GetChild((DependencyObject)(object)((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)this).CurrentCellEditor, 0);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	public class CustomExpandEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs

		protected internal bool ValueAssigned => valueAssigned;

		public bool IsInitializing => isInitializing.Value;

		public bool IsExpanded
		{
			get

				return isExpanded;
			}
			set
			{
				isExpanded = value;
				valueAssigned = true;
			}
		}

		protected internal CustomExpandEventArgs(RowHandle handle, DataViewBase view, RowDataGenerator generator, Func<RowHandle, bool> getDefaultValue)
		{
			isInitializing = new Lazy<bool>(() => !view.IsLoaded(handle));

			row = new Lazy<RowInfo>(() => new RowInfo(view, handle, generator));
		}

		{
			((CustomExpandEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}

			if (data == null)

				return null;

		}

	public class InstanceInitializer : IInstanceInitializer
	{
		public virtual IEnumerable<DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo> Types { get; set; }

		public virtual object CreateInstance(DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo type)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Types")]
	public class XamlInitializer : IInstanceInitializer
	{
		public List<TypeDefinition> Types { get; } = new List<TypeDefinition>();

		IEnumerable<DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo> IInstanceInitializer.Types => Types.Select((TypeDefinition t) => t.GetTypeInfo());

		public event EventHandler<InstanceInitializeEventArgs> Initialize;

		object IInstanceInitializer.CreateInstance(DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo type)
		{
			object instance = null;
			try
			{
				instance = Activator.CreateInstance(type.Type);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				if (this.Initialize == null)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Format("Cannot create an instance of the '{0}' type. Handle the {1}.{2} event to create the instance manually.", type, "XamlInitializer", "Initialize"), innerException);
				}
			}
			InstanceInitializeEventArgs e = new InstanceInitializeEventArgs(type, instance);
			this.Initialize?.Invoke(this, e);
			return e.Instance;
		}

		internal object CreateInstance(DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo type)
		{
			return ((IInstanceInitializer)this).CreateInstance(type);
		}
	}
	public class InstanceInitializeEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo TypeInfo { get; }

		public Type Type => TypeInfo.Type;

		public string Name => TypeInfo.Name;

		public string Description => TypeInfo.Description;

		public dynamic Instance { get; set; }

		internal InstanceInitializeEventArgs(DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo typeInfo, object instance)
		{
			TypeInfo = typeInfo;
			Instance = instance;
		}
	}
	public class TypeDefinition : ISupportInitialize
	{
		public string Name { get; set; }

		public Type Type { get; set; }

		public string Description { get; set; }

		public TypeDefinition()
		{
		}

		internal TypeDefinition(Type type, string name)
		{
			Type = type;
			Name = name;
		}

		internal TypeDefinition(Type type, string name, string description)
			: this(type, name)
		{
			Description = description;
		}

		internal DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo GetTypeInfo()
		{
			return new DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo(Type, Name, Description);
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.BeginInit()
		{
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.EndInit()
		{
			if (Type == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("Type");
			}
		}
	}
