	public class NavigationManager
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty NavigationModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NavigationIndexProperty;

		private bool openEditorOnSelection;

		private Dictionary<NavigationDirection, Func<FrameworkElement, bool>> callbacks;

		private readonly Locker bidirectionalNavigationLocker = new Locker();

		public bool OpenEditorOnSelection
		{
			get
			{
				return openEditorOnSelection;
			}
			protected internal set
			{
				openEditorOnSelection = value;
			}
		}

		public PropertyGridControl Grid { get; set; }

		public PropertyGridView View { get; set; }

		public SelectionStrategy SelectionStrategy => Grid.SelectionStrategy;

		public RowDataGenerator RowDataGenerator => Grid.RowDataGenerator;

		public DataViewBase DataView => Grid.DataView;

		public CellEditorOwner CellEditorOwner => View.CellEditorOwner;

		static NavigationManager()
		{
			NavigationModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("NavigationMode", typeof(NavigationMode), typeof(NavigationManager), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(NavigationMode.None));
			NavigationIndexProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("NavigationIndex", typeof(int), typeof(NavigationManager), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0));
		}

		public NavigationManager()
		{
		}//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown


		public static NavigationMode GetNavigationMode(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return NavigationMode.None;
			}
			return (NavigationMode)obj.GetValue(NavigationModeProperty);
		}

		public static void SetNavigationMode(DependencyObject obj, NavigationMode value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(NavigationModeProperty, value);
		}

		public static int GetNavigationIndex(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return int.MaxValue;
			}
			return (int)obj.GetValue(NavigationIndexProperty);
		}

		public static void SetNavigationIndex(DependencyObject obj, int value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(NavigationIndexProperty, value);
		}

		public NavigationManager(PropertyGridControl owner)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Grid = owner;
			View = owner.View;
			RegisterCallbacks();
		}

		private void RegisterCallbacks()
		{
			callbacks = new Dictionary<NavigationDirection, Func<FrameworkElement, bool>>();
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Left] = NavigateLeft;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Up] = NavigateUp;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Right] = NavigateRight;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Down] = NavigateDown;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Next] = (FrameworkElement fe) => NavigateNext(fe);
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Previous] = (FrameworkElement fe) => NavigatePrevious(fe);
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.PageUp] = OnPageUp;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.PageDown] = OnPageDown;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.First] = SelectFirst;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Last] = SelectLast;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.Enter] = (FrameworkElement fe) => false;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.NextContainer] = SelectNextContainer;
			callbacks[NavigationDirection.PreviousContainer] = SelectPreviousContainer;
		}

		private bool Navigate(NavigationDirection direction, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return callbacks[direction](element);
		}

		public static NavigationDirection GetDirection(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			ModifierKeys keyboardModifiers = ModifierKeysHelper.GetKeyboardModifiers(e);
			if (e.Key == Key.Return && !e.Handled)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.Enter;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Home)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.First;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.End)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.Last;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Left)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.Left;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Right)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.Right;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Up)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.Up;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Down)
			{
				return NavigationDirection.Down;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Tab)
			{
				bool flag = ModifierKeysHelper.IsCtrlPressed(keyboardModifiers);
				if (ModifierKeysHelper.IsShiftPressed(keyboardModifiers))
				{
					if (!flag)
					{
						return NavigationDirection.Previous;
					}
					return NavigationDirection.PreviousContainer;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					return NavigationDirection.Next;
				}
				return NavigationDirection.NextContainer;
			}
			if (object.Equals(e.Key, Key.Prior))
			{
				return NavigationDirection.PageUp;
			}
			if (object.Equals(e.Key, Key.Next))
			{
				return NavigationDirection.PageDown;
			}
			return NavigationDirection.None;
		}

		public void ProcessNavigation(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!Grid.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				return;
			}
			NavigationDirection direction = GetDirection(e);
			if (direction == NavigationDirection.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!View.CheckCanNavigate())
			{
				e.Handled = true;
				return;
			}
			FrameworkElement selectedObject = GetSelectedObject();
			if (selectedObject == null || PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid(selectedObject) != Grid)
			{
				return;
			}
			NavigationMode navigationMode = GetNavigationMode(selectedObject);
			if (navigationMode == NavigationMode.Local)
			{
				return;
			}
			if ((selectedObject as INavigationSupport).If((INavigationSupport x) => x.ProcessNavigation(direction)).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				e.Handled = true;
				return;
			}
			if (GetUseLinearNavigation(selectedObject) || (direction & (NavigationDirection)48) != NavigationDirection.None)
			{
				if ((direction & (NavigationDirection)28) != NavigationDirection.None)
				{
					e.Handled = NavigateNext(selectedObject);
					return;
				}
				if ((direction & (NavigationDirection)35) != NavigationDirection.None)
				{
					e.Handled = NavigatePrevious(selectedObject);
					return;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				switch (direction)
				{
				case NavigationDirection.Left:
				case NavigationDirection.Up:
				case NavigationDirection.Right:
				case NavigationDirection.Down:
					bidirectionalNavigationLocker.Reset();
					e.Handled = Navigate(direction, selectedObject);
					return;
				}
			}
			switch (direction)
			{
			case NavigationDirection.PageUp:
			case NavigationDirection.PageDown:
			case NavigationDirection.First:
			case NavigationDirection.Last:
			case NavigationDirection.PreviousContainer:
			case NavigationDirection.NextContainer:
				e.Handled = Navigate(direction, selectedObject);
				break;
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool SelectPreviousContainer(FrameworkElement arg)
		{
			MoveFocusHelper.MoveFocus((FrameworkElement)View.PropertyGrid, true);
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual bool SelectNextContainer(FrameworkElement arg)
		{
			MoveFocusHelper.MoveFocus((FrameworkElement)View.PropertyGrid, false);
			return true;
		}

		private bool NavigateDown(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			return NavigateBidirectional(selectedObject, NavigationDirection.Down);
		}

		private bool NavigateRight(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			return NavigateBidirectional(selectedObject, NavigationDirection.Right);
		}

		private bool NavigateUp(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			return NavigateBidirectional(selectedObject, NavigationDirection.Up);
		}

		private bool NavigateLeft(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			return NavigateBidirectional(selectedObject, NavigationDirection.Left);
		}

		private bool NavigateBidirectional(FrameworkElement selectedObject, NavigationDirection direction, bool useChildren = false)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (useChildren ? selectedObject : GetParentObject(selectedObject));
			if (frameworkElement == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (GetUseLinearNavigation(frameworkElement))
			{
				if ((direction & (NavigationDirection)28) != NavigationDirection.None)
				{
					bool isLocked = bidirectionalNavigationLocker.IsLocked;
					bidirectionalNavigationLocker.Unlock();
					return NavigateNext(selectedObject, isLocked, direction);
				}
				if ((direction & (NavigationDirection)35) != NavigationDirection.None)
				{
					return NavigatePrevious(selectedObject, direction);
				}
			}
			if (!useChildren && NavigateBidirectional(selectedObject, direction, useChildren: true))
			{
				return true;
			}
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source = from x in GetChildren(frameworkElement)
				where x != selectedObject
				select x;
			if (useChildren && !source.Any())
			{
				return false;
			}
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement2 = GetElementToSelect(frameworkElement, selectedObject, direction);
			if (frameworkElement2 == frameworkElement)
			{
				if (!useChildren)
				{
					bidirectionalNavigationLocker.Lock();
				}
				else
				{
					frameworkElement2 = null;
				}
			}
			if (frameworkElement2 != null)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement3 = frameworkElement2;
				if (!(frameworkElement2 as INavigationSupport).If((INavigationSupport x) => !x.GetSkipNavigation()).ReturnSuccess())
				{
					do
					{
						frameworkElement2 = frameworkElement3;
						frameworkElement3 = GetElementToSelect(frameworkElement3, selectedObject, direction);
					}
					while (frameworkElement3 != frameworkElement2);
				}
				frameworkElement2 = frameworkElement3;
			}
			return Select(frameworkElement2, direction);
		}

		private FrameworkElement GetElementToSelect(FrameworkElement parent, FrameworkElement selectedObject, NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source = from x in GetChildren(parent)
				where x != selectedObject
				select x;
			var source2 = from x in source
				where FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree(x)
				select new
				{
					Element = x,
					Position = x.TransformToVisual(selectedObject).Transform(new Point(0.0, 0.0))
				};
			var source3 = source2.Where(x => x.Position.X < 0.0);
			var source4 = source2.Where(x => x.Position.Y < 0.0);
			var source5 = source2.Where(x => x.Position.X > 0.0);
			var source6 = source2.Where(x => x.Position.Y > 0.0);
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source7 = from x in source3
				orderby x.Position.X descending, Math.Abs(x.Position.Y)
				select x.Element;
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source8 = from x in source5
				orderby x.Position.X, Math.Abs(x.Position.Y)
				select x.Element;
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source9 = from x in source4
				orderby x.Position.Y descending, Math.Abs(x.Position.X)
				select x.Element;
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source10 = from x in source6
				orderby x.Position.Y, Math.Abs(x.Position.X)
				select x.Element;
			return direction switch
			{
				NavigationDirection.Left => source7.FirstOrDefault() ?? parent, 
				NavigationDirection.Up => source9.FirstOrDefault() ?? parent, 
				NavigationDirection.Right => source8.FirstOrDefault() ?? parent, 
				NavigationDirection.Down => source10.FirstOrDefault() ?? parent, 
				_ => parent, 
			};
		}

		private bool NavigatePrevious(FrameworkElement selectedObject, NavigationDirection direction = NavigationDirection.Previous)
		{
			FrameworkElement parentObject = GetParentObject(selectedObject);
			if (parentObject == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> elements = from x in GetChildren(parentObject)
				orderby GetNavigationIndex(x)
				where GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None
				select x;
			FrameworkElement previous = GetPrevious(elements, selectedObject);
			if (previous != null)
			{
				return SelectSelfOrLastChild(previous);
			}
			return Select(parentObject, direction);
		}

		private bool SelectSelfOrLastChild(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source = from x in GetChildren(element)
				where GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None
				select x;
			if (!source.Any())
			{
				return Select(element, NavigationDirection.Previous);
			}
			return SelectSelfOrLastChild(source.LastOrDefault());
		}

		private bool NavigateNext(FrameworkElement selectedObject, bool skipChildren = false, NavigationDirection direction = NavigationDirection.Next)
		{
			IOrderedEnumerable<FrameworkElement> orderedEnumerable = (skipChildren ? null : (from x in GetChildren(selectedObject)
				orderby GetNavigationIndex(x)
				select x).If((IOrderedEnumerable<FrameworkElement> x) => x.Any()));
			if (orderedEnumerable != null)
			{
				bool flag = Select(orderedEnumerable.ElementAt(0), NavigationDirection.Next);
				if (flag)
				{
					return flag;
				}
			}
			FrameworkElement parentObject = GetParentObject(selectedObject);
			if (parentObject == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> elements = from x in GetChildren(parentObject)
				orderby GetNavigationIndex(x)
				where GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None
				select x;
			FrameworkElement next = GetNext(elements, selectedObject);
			if (next == null)
			{
				return NavigateNext(parentObject, skipChildren: true);
			}
			return Select(next, direction);
		}

		private T GetNext<T>(IEnumerable<T> elements, T current) where T : class
		{
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (T element in elements)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					return element;
				}
				flag = CheckEquals(current, element);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private T GetPrevious<T>(IEnumerable<T> elements, T current) where T : class
		{
			T result = null;
			foreach (T element in elements)
			{
				if (CheckEquals(current, element))
				{
					return result;
				}
				result = element;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool CheckEquals(object first, object second)
		{
			FakeRowControl fakeRowControl = first as FakeRowControl;
			FakeRowControl fakeRowControl2 = second as FakeRowControl;
			if (fakeRowControl != null && fakeRowControl2 != null)
			{
				return fakeRowControl.Index == fakeRowControl2.Index;
			}
			return first == second;
		}

		protected internal bool OnPageDown(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			OnPage(down: true);
			return true;
		}

		protected internal bool OnPageUp(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			OnPage(down: false);
			return true;
		}

		private bool SelectLast(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			FrameworkElement input = selectedObject.With(GetParentObject).With(GetChildren).With((IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> x) => x.LastOrDefault());
			return input.Return((FrameworkElement x) => Select(x, NavigationDirection.Last), () => false);
		}

		private bool SelectFirst(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			FrameworkElement input = selectedObject.With(GetParentObject).With(GetChildren).With((IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> x) => x.FirstOrDefault());
			return input.Return((FrameworkElement x) => Select(x, NavigationDirection.First), () => false);
		}

		public void SelectFirstElement()
		{
			NavigateNext(View);
		}

		public void SelectLastElement()
		{
			SelectLast(View);
		}

		private FrameworkElement GetSelectedObject()
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = null;
			RowControlBase rowControlBase = Grid.SelectedPropertyPath.With((string x) => RowDataGenerator.RowDataFromHandle(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(x))).With((RowData x) => View.GetRowControl(x)) ?? View.SelectedItem.With((RowData x) => View.GetRowControl(x));
			if (rowControlBase == null || (rowControlBase.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && !rowControlBase.IsKeyboardFocused) || !View.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				frameworkElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement as FrameworkElement;
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement2 = null;
				if (GetNavigationMode(frameworkElement) != NavigationMode.None)
				{
					frameworkElement2 = frameworkElement;
				}
				frameworkElement = (VisualTreeExtensions.VisualParents((DependencyObject)frameworkElement, false).FirstOrDefault((DependencyObject x) => GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None && !GetSkipNavigation(x)) as FrameworkElement) ?? frameworkElement2;
			}
			if (frameworkElement == null)
			{
				frameworkElement = rowControlBase;
			}
			return frameworkElement;
		}

		private FrameworkElement GetParentObject(FrameworkElement selectedObject)
		{
			return (selectedObject as INavigationSupport).With((INavigationSupport x) => x.GetParent()) ?? (VisualTreeExtensions.VisualParents((DependencyObject)selectedObject, false).FirstOrDefault((DependencyObject x) => GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None || x is INavigationSupport) as FrameworkElement);
		}

		public static IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GetChildren(FrameworkElement parent)
		{
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> source;
			if (parent is INavigationSupport)
			{
				IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> children = ((INavigationSupport)parent).GetChildren();
				source = children ?? Enumerable.Empty<FrameworkElement>();
			}
			else
			{
				List<FrameworkElement> list = new List<FrameworkElement>();
				ConditionalVisualTreeEnumerator conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator = new ConditionalVisualTreeEnumerator(parent, (DependencyObject x) => GetNavigationMode(x) == NavigationMode.None);
				while (((NestedObjectEnumeratorBase)conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator).MoveNext())
				{
					(((VisualTreeEnumerator)conditionalVisualTreeEnumerator).Current as FrameworkElement).If((FrameworkElement x) => GetNavigationMode(x) != NavigationMode.None && x != parent).Do(delegate(FrameworkElement x)
					{
						list.Add(x);
					});
				}
				source = list;
			}
			return source.Where((FrameworkElement x) => x != null && x.IsEnabled && (!(x is CellEditorPresenter) || ((CellEditorPresenter)x).IsEditorEnabled));
		}

		private bool Select(FrameworkElement element, NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (element is PropertyGridControl && VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(element).With((DependencyObject x) => PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid(x)) == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (element is PropertyGridView && direction == NavigationDirection.Next && (element as PropertyGridView).SelectedItem != null && !GetSkipNavigation(element))
			{
				if (CommitCurrentCellEditorAndCheckCanNavigate(element))
				{
					element.Focus();
				}
				return true;
			}
			PropertyGridControl grid = PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid(element) ?? Grid;
			PropertyGridView view = grid.View;
			NavigationManager navigationManager = grid.NavigationManager;
			SelectionStrategy strategy = grid.SelectionStrategy;
			CellEditorOwner cellEditorOwner = view.CellEditorOwner;
			bool flag = ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)cellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor != null || navigationManager.OpenEditorOnSelection;
			if (!view.CheckCanNavigate())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (grid != Grid && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)CellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor != null)
			{
				grid.View.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)delegate
				{
					grid.View.Focus();
				});
			}
			if (GetSkipNavigation(element))
			{
				navigationManager.OpenEditorOnSelection |= !((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)cellEditorOwner).EditorWasClosed;
				if (direction == NavigationDirection.First)
				{
					direction = NavigationDirection.Next;
				}
				if (direction == NavigationDirection.Last)
				{
					direction = NavigationDirection.Previous;
				}
				return navigationManager.Navigate(direction, element);
			}
			if (element is RowControlBase)
			{
				navigationManager.OpenEditorOnSelection |= !((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)cellEditorOwner).EditorWasClosed && ((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)cellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor != null;
				if (CommitCurrentCellEditorAndCheckCanNavigate(element))
				{
					if (!view.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						view.Focus();
					}
					RowControlBase rowControlBase = element as RowControlBase;
					if (rowControlBase is FakeRowControl fakeRowControl)
					{
						grid.View.VirtualizingPanel.BringIndexIntoView(fakeRowControl.Index);
						if (rowControlBase.RowData == null)
						{
							int index = fakeRowControl.Index;
							RowHandle rowHandle = (RowDataGenerator.ItemsSource.GetItemAt(index) as RowData).With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
							strategy.SelectViaHandle(rowHandle);
							return true;
						}
					}
					strategy.SelectViaHandle(rowControlBase.RowData.Handle);
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (element is CellEditorPresenter)
			{
				if (CommitCurrentCellEditorAndCheckCanNavigate(element))
				{
					strategy.SelectViaHandle(PropertyGridHelper.GetRowData(element).Handle);
					(element as CellEditorPresenter).IsSelected = true;
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (CommitCurrentCellEditorAndCheckCanNavigate(element))
			{
				RowData rowData = PropertyGridHelper.GetRowData(element);
				SelectionStrategy selectionStrategy = rowData.With((RowData x) => x.RowDataGenerator).With((RowDataGenerator x) => x.View).With((PropertyGridView x) => x.SelectionStrategy);
				if (selectionStrategy == strategy)
				{
					rowData.With((RowData x) => x.Handle).Do(delegate(RowHandle x)
					{
						strategy.SelectViaHandle(x);
					});
				}
				navigationManager.OpenEditorOnSelection = flag;
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)cellEditorOwner).EditorWasClosed = true;
				FocusElementOrFirstAvailableChild(element, direction);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static void FocusElementOrFirstAvailableChild(FrameworkElement element, NavigationDirection direction)
		{
			Func<IEnumerable<DependencyObject>, Func<DependencyObject, bool>, DependencyObject> func = (((direction & (NavigationDirection)35) == 0) ? new Func<IEnumerable<DependencyObject>, Func<DependencyObject, bool>, DependencyObject>(Enumerable.FirstOrDefault) : new Func<IEnumerable<DependencyObject>, Func<DependencyObject, bool>, DependencyObject>(Enumerable.Last));
			func(VisualTreeExtensions.VisualChildren((DependencyObject)element, true), (DependencyObject x) => x is FrameworkElement && (x as FrameworkElement).Focusable).Do(delegate(DependencyObject x)
			{
				KeyboardHelper.Focus((UIElement)(FrameworkElement)x);
			});
		}

		public static bool CheckCanSelect(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (GetNavigationMode(element) != NavigationMode.None)
			{
				return !(element as INavigationSupport).If((INavigationSupport x) => x.GetSkipNavigation()).ReturnSuccess();
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool CommitCurrentCellEditorAndCheckCanNavigate(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (PropertyGridView item in VisualTreeExtensions.VisualParents((DependencyObject)element, false).OfType<PropertyGridView>())
			{
				((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)item.CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.Do(delegate(InplaceEditorBase x)
				{
					x.CommitEditor(false);
				});
				flag |= item.HasErrorInActiveEditor;
				if (flag)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return !flag;
		}

		private bool GetUseLinearNavigation(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			if (obj is INavigationSupport)
			{
				return ((INavigationSupport)obj).GetUseLinearNavigation();
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool GetSkipNavigation(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			if (obj is INavigationSupport)
			{
				return (obj as INavigationSupport).GetSkipNavigation();
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnPage(bool down)
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = View.ScrollViewer;
			RowData selectedItem = View.SelectedItem;
			RowControl rowControl = View.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(selectedItem) as RowControl;
			if (selectedItem == null || rowControl == null)
			{
				ScrollOnPage(down);
				return;
			}
			int num = (down ? 1 : (-1));
			RowsCollectionView itemsSource = RowDataGenerator.ItemsSource;
			bool flag = true;
			double y = rowControl.TransformToAncestor(scrollViewer).Transform(default(Point)).Y;
			if (down && y + rowControl.ActualHeight < scrollViewer.ViewportHeight)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (!down && y > 0.0)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				ScrollOnPage(down, updateLayout: true);
			}
			int num2 = itemsSource.IndexOf(selectedItem);
			List<RowControl> list = new List<RowControl>();
			for (int i = num2 + num; i >= 0 && i < itemsSource.Count; i += num)
			{
				if (View.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(i) is RowControl item)
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			RowControl input = null;
			for (int num3 = list.Count - 1; num3 >= 0; num3--)
			{
				RowControl rowControl2 = list[num3];
				if (down)
				{
					if (rowControl2.TransformToAncestor(scrollViewer).Transform(default(Point)).Y <= scrollViewer.ViewportHeight)
					{
						input = rowControl2;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (rowControl2.TransformToAncestor(scrollViewer).Transform(new Point(0.0, rowControl2.ActualHeight)).Y >= 0.0)
				{
					input = rowControl2;
					break;
				}
			}
			RowHandle rowHandle = input.With((RowControl x) => x.RowData).With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
			if (rowHandle != null)
			{
				Grid.SelectionStrategy.SelectViaHandle(rowHandle);
			}
		}

		private void ScrollOnPage(bool down, bool updateLayout = false)
		{
			if (down)
			{
				View.ScrollViewer.PageDown();
			}
			else
			{
				View.ScrollViewer.PageUp();
			}
			if (updateLayout)
			{
				View.ScrollViewer.UpdateLayout();
			}
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridViewPopupMenu : GridPopupMenuBase
	{
		private readonly Locker changeIsOpenLocker = new Locker();

		internal Action<RowControlBase> CreateItemsAction { get; set; }

		internal PropertyGridMenuType MenuType { get; set; }

		internal RowHandle RowHandle { get; set; }

		internal PropertyGridView View => (PropertyGridView)((BarPopupBase)this).Owner;

		protected internal Point Offset { get; set; }

		protected override bool ShouldClearItemsOnClose => true;

		protected internal PropertyGridViewPopupMenu(ILogicalOwner context)
			: base(context)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((Popup)this).HorizontalOffset = -1.0;
			((Popup)this).VerticalOffset = -1.0;
			((BarPopupBase)this).AllowMouseCapturing = true;
			UpdatePlacement();
			BarNameScope.SetIsScopeOwner((DependencyObject)(object)this, true);
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdatePlacement()
		{
			((Popup)this).Placement = PlacementMode.Custom;
		}

		protected override object OnIsOpenCoerce(object value)
		{
			if (changeIsOpenLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				return ((Popup)this).IsOpen;
			}
			return ((PopupMenuBase)this).OnIsOpenCoerce(value);
		}

		protected override bool RaiseShowMenu()
		{
			PropertyGridMenuEventArgs e = new PropertyGridMenuEventArgs(this, MenuType, View.PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator, RowHandle)
			{
				RoutedEvent = PropertyGridControl.MenuOpeningEvent
			};
			View.PropertyGrid.RaiseEvent(e);
			return !e.Handled;
		}

		protected override MenuInfoBase CreateMenuInfo(UIElement placementTarget)
		{
			return (MenuInfoBase)(object)new PropertyGridRowMenuInfo((GridPopupMenuBase)(object)this, CreateItemsAction);
		}
	}
	public enum PropertyBuilderMode
	{
		DefinedOnly,
		AllProperties
	}
	public class PropertyBuilder : FrameworkElement
	{
		private enum DefinitionCheckResult
		{
			Failure,
			Success,
			Continue
		}

		private struct DefinitionObject
		{
			private const int strongPathShift = 18;

			private const int weakPathShift = 0;

			private const int collectionPathShift = 17;

			private const int typeShift = 1;

			private const int strongPathMatch = 262144;

			private const int weakPathMatch = 1;

			private const int collectionMatch = 131072;

			private const int pathMask = 262145;

			private const int typeMask = 131070;

			private PropertyDefinitionBase definition;

			private PropertyGridControl propertyGrid;

			private bool containsCategoryInfoInitialized;

			private bool containsCategoryInfo;

			private List<DefinitionObject> children;

			private int checkAttempt;

			private readonly int maxIterationCount;

			private readonly int[] iterationsResult;

			public PropertyDefinitionBase Definition => definition;

			public PropertyGridControl PropertyGrid => propertyGrid;

			private bool ContainsCategoryInfo
			{
				get
				{
					if (!containsCategoryInfoInitialized)
					{
						return containsCategoryInfo = GetContainsCategoryInfo();
					}
					return containsCategoryInfo;
				}
			}

			public IEnumerable<DefinitionObject> Children
			{
				get
				{
					if (children != null)
					{
						return children;
					}
					children = new List<DefinitionObject>();
					foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinition2 in Definition.PropertyDefinitions)
					{
						children.Add(new DefinitionObject(propertyGrid, propertyDefinition2, maxIterationCount, iterationsResult));
					}
					if (definition is PropertyDefinition { InsertDefinitionsFrom: not null } propertyDefinition)
					{
						PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinitionBase = propertyDefinition.InsertDefinitionsFrom;
						if (propertyDefinitionBase is RootPropertyDefinition)
						{
							propertyDefinitionBase = PropertyGrid.RootPropertyDefinition;
						}
						foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinition3 in propertyDefinitionBase.PropertyDefinitions)
						{
							children.Add(new DefinitionObject(propertyGrid, propertyDefinition3, maxIterationCount, iterationsResult));
						}
					}
					return children;
				}
			}

			private bool GetContainsCategoryInfo()
			{
				if (definition.ParentDefinition != null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (definition.SplittedPath == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (definition.SplittedPath.Length == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				string text = definition.SplittedPath[0];
				if (text.Length == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return text[0] == '<';
			}

			public DefinitionObject(PropertyGridControl propertyGrid, PropertyDefinitionBase definition, int maxIterationCount, int[] initialIterationsResult)
			{
				this.definition = definition;
				this.propertyGrid = propertyGrid;
				this.maxIterationCount = maxIterationCount;
				containsCategoryInfoInitialized = false;
				containsCategoryInfo = false;
				checkAttempt = 0;
				children = null;
				iterationsResult = new int[maxIterationCount];
				if (initialIterationsResult != null)
				{
					Buffer.BlockCopy(initialIterationsResult, 0, iterationsResult, 0, maxIterationCount * 4);
				}
			}

			public DefinitionCheckResult Check(BuilderFieldInfo fieldInfo, int iteration, ApplyingMode mode)
			{
				int num = 0;
				try
				{
					if (!definition.ApplyingMode.HasFlag(mode))
					{
						return DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
					}
					bool flag = definition is CategoryDefinition;
					if (flag != fieldInfo.IsCategory)
					{
						if (!ContainsCategoryInfo)
						{
							checkAttempt--;
						}
						return (!flag) ? DefinitionCheckResult.Continue : DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
					}
					string[] splittedPath = Definition.SplittedPath;
					if (splittedPath != null)
					{
						if (splittedPath.Length <= checkAttempt)
						{
							return DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
						}
						string text = splittedPath[checkAttempt];
						string text2 = fieldInfo.SplittedPath[fieldInfo.SplittedPath.Length - 1];
						if (text != "*")
						{
							if (text != text2)
							{
								return DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
							}
							num |= 0x40000;
						}
						else
						{
							num |= 1;
						}
						if (splittedPath.Length - checkAttempt > 1)
						{
							return DefinitionCheckResult.Continue;
						}
					}
					if (definition is CollectionDefinition && !fieldInfo.IsCollection)
					{
						return DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
					}
					if (definition is CollectionDefinition && fieldInfo.IsCollection)
					{
						num |= 0x20000;
					}
					int num2 = 0;
					if (definition is PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition)
					{
						Type pDefType = propertyDefinition.Type;
						if (pDefType != null)
						{
							Type type = fieldInfo.Type;
							if (propertyDefinition.TypeMatchMode == TypeMatchMode.Direct)
							{
								if (pDefType == type)
								{
									num2 = 0;
								}
								else
								{
									if (!TypeHelper.IsNullableOf(type, pDefType, extended: false))
									{
										return ((num & 0x40001) == 0) ? DefinitionCheckResult.Continue : DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
									}
									num2 = 1;
								}
							}
							if (propertyDefinition.TypeMatchMode == TypeMatchMode.Extended)
							{
								bool isInterface = pDefType.IsInterface;
								while (true)
								{
									if (type == null)
									{
										return ((num & 0x40001) == 0) ? DefinitionCheckResult.Continue : DefinitionCheckResult.Failure;
									}
									if (type == pDefType)
									{
										break;
									}
									if (TypeHelper.IsNullableOf(type, pDefType, extended: true))
									{
										num2++;
										break;
									}
									if (isInterface)
									{
										Type type2 = type.GetInterfaces().FirstOrDefault((Type x) => pDefType.IsAssignableFrom(x));
										if (type2 != null)
										{
											int num3 = 1;
											while (type2 != pDefType && type2 != null)
											{
												num3++;
												type2 = type2.BaseType;
											}
											num2 += num3;
											break;
										}
									}
									num2++;
									type = type.BaseType;
								}
							}
							num |= (~num2 << 1) & 0x1FFFE;
						}
					}
					return DefinitionCheckResult.Success;
				}
				finally
				{
					checkAttempt++;
					iterationsResult[iteration] = num;
				}
			}

			public int GetCheckResult(int iteration)
			{
				return iterationsResult[iteration];
			}
		}

		private struct BuilderFieldInfo
		{
			private Lazy<bool> isCollection;

			public Type Type;

			public string Path;

			public bool IsCategory;

			public string[] SplittedPath;

			public bool IsCollection => isCollection.Value;

			public BuilderFieldInfo(string path, Type type, bool isCategory, Func<RowHandle, bool> isCollection, RowHandle handle)
			{
				Path = path;
				Type = type;
				IsCategory = isCategory;
				this.isCollection = new Lazy<bool>(() => isCollection(handle));
				SplittedPath = FieldNameHelper.GetPath(path);
			}
		}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefinitionsProperty;

		private readonly StandardDefinitionsProvider standardDefinitionsProvider;

		private readonly PropertyGridControl propertyGrid;

		private readonly SimpleDefinitionSearchStrategy simplifiedSearchStrategy;

		private PropertyDefinitionCollection definitions;

		private bool isInitializing;

		private static readonly BuilderFieldInfo[] emptyBuilderFieldInfo;

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase> userDefinitionsTable;

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase> standardDefinitionsTable;

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase> nonLogicalStandardDefinitionsTable;

		public StandardDefinitionsProvider StandardDefinitionsProvider => standardDefinitionsProvider;

		public PropertyGridControl PropertyGrid => propertyGrid;

		public PropertyDefinitionCollection Definitions
		{
			get
			{
				return definitions;
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefinitionsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren => StandardDefinitionsProvider.CreatedDefinitions;

		internal event PropertyBuilderChangedEventHandler Changed;

		static PropertyBuilder()
		{
			emptyBuilderFieldInfo = new BuilderFieldInfo[0];
			DefinitionsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Definitions", typeof(PropertyDefinitionCollection), typeof(PropertyBuilder), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((PropertyBuilder)d).OnDefinitionsChanged((PropertyDefinitionCollection)e.OldValue, (PropertyDefinitionCollection)e.NewValue);
			}));
		}

		public void CheckAddCustomDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null)
			{
				simplifiedSearchStrategy.RegisterDefinition(definition);
			}
		}

		protected internal void InternalAddLogicalChild(object element)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(element);
		}

		protected internal void InternalRemoveLogicalChild(object element)
		{
			RemoveLogicalChild(element);
		}

		public void CheckRemoveCustomDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null)
			{
				simplifiedSearchStrategy.UnregisterDefinition(definition);
			}
		}

		public void OnDefiningPropertyChanged(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null)
			{
				simplifiedSearchStrategy.UpdateRegistration(definition);
			}
		}

		public PropertyBuilder(PropertyGridControl propertyGrid)
		{
			this.propertyGrid = propertyGrid;
			simplifiedSearchStrategy = new SimpleDefinitionSearchStrategy();
			standardDefinitionsProvider = new StandardDefinitionsProvider(this);
			userDefinitionsTable = new Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase>();
			standardDefinitionsTable = new Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase>();
			nonLogicalStandardDefinitionsTable = new Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase>();
		}

		public override void BeginInit()
		{
			isInitializing = true;
			base.BeginInit();
		}

		public override void EndInit()
		{
			base.EndInit();
			UpdateLayout();
			isInitializing = false;
			RaiseChanged();
		}

		internal void RaiseChanged(PropertyDefinitionBase definition = null, PropertyBuilderChangeKind changeKind = PropertyBuilderChangeKind.Reset)
		{
			if (!isInitializing && this.Changed != null)
			{
				HandlesInvalidated(null);
				this.Changed(this, new PropertyBuilderChangedEventArgs(definition, changeKind));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefinitionsChanged(PropertyDefinitionCollection oldValue, PropertyDefinitionCollection newValue)
		{
			definitions = newValue;
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.CollectionChanged -= OnDefinitionsCollectionChanged;
				ClearDefinitions(oldValue);
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.ForEach(delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x)
				{
					x.isRoot = true;
				});
				newValue.CollectionChanged += OnDefinitionsCollectionChanged;
				SetUpDefinitions(newValue);
			}
			RaiseChanged();
		}

		public void ClearUnlinkedDefinitions()
		{
			StandardDefinitionsProvider.ClearUnlinkedDefinitions();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefinitionsCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ClearDefinitions(e.OldItems);
			if (e.OldItems != null)
			{
				foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase oldItem in e.OldItems)
				{
					oldItem.isRoot = false;
				}
			}
			if (e.NewItems != null)
			{
				foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					newItem.isRoot = true;
				}
			}
			SetUpDefinitions(e.NewItems);
			RaiseChanged();
		}

		protected internal void ClearDefinitions(IList collection)
		{
			if (collection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase item in collection.OfType<PropertyDefinitionBase>())
			{
				item.Builder = null;
			}
		}

		protected internal void SetUpDefinitions(IList collection)
		{
			if (collection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase item in collection.OfType<PropertyDefinitionBase>())
			{
				item.Builder = this;
			}
		}

		private BuilderFieldInfo[] GetFieldInfos(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle)
		{
			string[] path = FieldNameHelper.GetPath(view.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle));
			int num = path.Length + ((view is CategoryDataView && !view.IsGroupRowHandle(handle)) ? 1 : 0);
			BuilderFieldInfo[] array = new BuilderFieldInfo[num];
			int num2 = num - 1;
			while (handle != null && !handle.IsRoot)
			{
				array[num2] = new BuilderFieldInfo(view.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle), view.GetPropertyType(handle), view.IsGroupRowHandle(handle), (RowHandle h) => view.CanUseCollectionEditor(h), handle);
				bool isRoot = handle.IsRoot;
				handle = view.GetParent(handle);
				num2--;
				if (handle == null && !isRoot)
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			if (num2 != -1)
			{
				return emptyBuilderFieldInfo;
			}
			return array;
		}

		public void HandlesInvalidated(IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles)
		{
			if (handles == null)
			{
				userDefinitionsTable.Clear();
				standardDefinitionsTable.Clear();
				nonLogicalStandardDefinitionsTable.Clear();
				return;
			}
			foreach (RowHandle handle in handles)
			{
				userDefinitionsTable.Remove(handle);
				standardDefinitionsTable.Remove(handle);
				nonLogicalStandardDefinitionsTable.Remove(handle);
			}
		}

		public virtual PropertyDefinitionBase GetDefinition(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle, bool getStandard = true)
		{
			if (userDefinitionsTable.TryGetValue(handle, out var value))
			{
				if (value is FakePropertyDefinition)
				{
					if (getStandard)
					{
						return GetStandardDefinition(view, handle, addToLogicalTree: true);
					}
					return null;
				}
				return value;
			}
			value = GetDefinitionImpl(view, handle);
			userDefinitionsTable.Add(handle, value ?? new FakePropertyDefinition());
			if (getStandard && value == null)
			{
				PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinitionBase = (userDefinitionsTable[handle] = GetStandardDefinition(view, handle, addToLogicalTree: true));
				value = propertyDefinitionBase;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected internal virtual PropertyDefinitionBase GetStandardDefinition(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle, bool addToLogicalTree)
		{
			return GetStandardDefinitionFrom(addToLogicalTree ? standardDefinitionsTable : nonLogicalStandardDefinitionsTable, view, handle, addToLogicalTree);
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase GetStandardDefinitionFrom(Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase> dictionary, DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle, bool addToLogicalTree)
		{
			if (dictionary.TryGetValue(handle, out var value) && !(value is FakePropertyDefinition))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (value != null)
			{
				dictionary.Remove(handle);
			}
			value = GetStandardDefinitionImpl(view, handle, addToLogicalTree);
			dictionary.Add(handle, value ?? new FakePropertyDefinition());
			return value;
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase GetStandardDefinitionImpl(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle, bool addToLogicalTree)
		{
			return StandardDefinitionsProvider.GetStandardDefinition(view, handle, addToLogicalTree);
		}

		public virtual PropertyDefinitionBase GetDefinitionImpl(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle.IsRoot)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (Definitions.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (simplifiedSearchStrategy.SimplifiedSearchAvailable)
			{
				string fieldNameByHandle = view.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle);
				if (fieldNameByHandle == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				int hashCode = fieldNameByHandle.GetHashCode();
				foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase definition in Definitions)
				{
					if (definition.pathHashCode == hashCode && definition.Path == fieldNameByHandle)
					{
						return definition;
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
			BuilderFieldInfo[] fieldInfos = GetFieldInfos(view, handle);
			if (fieldInfos == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<DefinitionObject> list = new List<DefinitionObject>(Definitions.Count);
			List<DefinitionObject> list2 = new List<DefinitionObject>(Definitions.Count);
			for (int i = 0; i < Definitions.Count; i++)
			{
				list2.Add(new DefinitionObject(PropertyGrid, Definitions[i], fieldInfos.Length, null));
			}
			BuilderFieldInfo builderFieldInfo = fieldInfos[fieldInfos.Length - 1];
			ApplyingMode mode = ((view is CategoryDataView) ? ApplyingMode.WhenGrouping : ApplyingMode.WhenNoGrouping);
			int num = -1;
			BuilderFieldInfo[] array = fieldInfos;
			for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
			{
				BuilderFieldInfo fieldInfo = array[j];
				num++;
				bool flag = fieldInfo.Equals(builderFieldInfo);
				List<DefinitionObject> list3 = list2.ToList();
				list2.Clear();
				foreach (DefinitionObject item in list3)
				{
					DefinitionCheckResult definitionCheckResult = item.Check(fieldInfo, num, mode);
					if (definitionCheckResult == DefinitionCheckResult.Continue)
					{
						list2.Add(item);
					}
					if (definitionCheckResult == DefinitionCheckResult.Success)
					{
						if (flag)
						{
							list.Add(item);
						}
						else
						{
							list2.AddRange(item.Children);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			DefinitionObject definitionObject = default(DefinitionObject);
			long num2 = -1L;
			foreach (DefinitionObject item2 in list)
			{
				long num3 = 0L;
				for (int k = 0; k <= num; k++)
				{
					int checkResult = item2.GetCheckResult(k);
					num3 += checkResult * (num - k + 1) / (num + 1);
				}
				if (num3 > num2)
				{
					num2 = num3;
					definitionObject = item2;
				}
			}
			return definitionObject.Definition;
		}

		public static IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetParentHandles(DataViewBase view, string fieldName)
		{
			return GetParentHandles(view, view.GetHandleByFieldName(fieldName));
		}

		public static IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetParentHandles(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!handle.IsInvalid && !handle.IsRoot)
			{
				RowHandle lhandle = handle;
				while (lhandle != null && lhandle != RowHandle.Root)
				{
					yield return lhandle;
					lhandle = view.GetParent(lhandle);
				}
			}
		}

		public PropertyGridSortMode GetActualSortMode(RowHandle handle, RowDataGenerator generator)
		{
			if (handle == RowHandle.Root)
			{
				return PropertyGridSortMode.Unspecified;
			}
			for (PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinitionBase = generator.RowDataFromHandle(handle).With((RowData x) => x.Definition) ?? GetDefinition(generator.DataView, handle); propertyDefinitionBase != null; propertyDefinitionBase = propertyDefinitionBase.ParentDefinition)
			{
				if (propertyDefinitionBase.ChildrenSortMode != PropertyGridSortMode.Unspecified)
				{
					return propertyDefinitionBase.ChildrenSortMode;
				}
			}
			return PropertyGridSortMode.Unspecified;
		}
	}

	{

		MenuProperties,
		VisualClientProperties,
		Visibility,
		Reset
	}

	{
		public PropertyDefinitionBase Definition { get; set; }

		public PropertyBuilderChangeKind ChangeKind { get; set; }

		public PropertyBuilderChangedEventArgs(PropertyDefinitionBase definition, PropertyBuilderChangeKind kind)
		{
			Definition = definition;
			ChangeKind = kind;
		}
	}
	internal static class TypeHelper
	{
		public static bool IsNullableOf(Type nullable, Type argument, bool extended)
		{
			if (!nullable.IsValueType || argument == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Type underlyingType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(nullable);
			if (!extended)
			{
				return underlyingType == argument;
			}
			return argument.IsAssignableFrom(underlyingType);
		}
	}
	internal class SimpleDefinitionSearchStrategy
	{
		private readonly HashSet<int> registeredNames;

		private readonly Dictionary<int, int> registeredDuplicates;

		private int invalidItemsCount;

		private bool? simplifiedSearchAvailable;

		public bool SimplifiedSearchAvailable => (simplifiedSearchAvailable ?? (simplifiedSearchAvailable = GetSimplifiedSearchAvailable())).Value;

		public SimpleDefinitionSearchStrategy()
		{
			registeredNames = new HashSet<int>();
			registeredDuplicates = new Dictionary<int, int>();
		}

		private bool GetSimplifiedSearchAvailable()
		{
			if (invalidItemsCount == 0)
			{
				return registeredDuplicates.Count == 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void RegisterDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (!IsValid(definition))
			{
				definition.validForSimplifiedSearch = false;
				invalidItemsCount++;
				return;
			}
			definition.validForSimplifiedSearch = true;
			int pathHashCode = definition.pathHashCode;
			if (registeredNames.Contains(pathHashCode))
			{
				int value = 0;
				registeredDuplicates.TryGetValue(pathHashCode, out value);
				registeredDuplicates[pathHashCode] = value + 1;
			}
			registeredNames.Add(pathHashCode);
			simplifiedSearchAvailable = null;
		}

		public void UnregisterDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (!definition.validForSimplifiedSearch)
			{
				invalidItemsCount--;
				return;
			}
			int pathHashCode = definition.pathHashCode;
			int value = 0;
			if (registeredDuplicates.TryGetValue(pathHashCode, out value))
			{
				switch (value)
				{
				case 1:
					registeredDuplicates.Remove(pathHashCode);
					break;
				default:
					registeredDuplicates[pathHashCode] = value - 1;
					break;
				case 0:
					break;
				}
			}
			registeredNames.Remove(pathHashCode);
			simplifiedSearchAvailable = null;
		}

		public void UpdateRegistration(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			UnregisterDefinition(definition);
			RegisterDefinition(definition);
		}

		private bool IsValid(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (!definition.isRoot || definition.scopeHashCode != 0 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(definition.Path) || definition.Path.Contains('*') || definition.ApplyingMode != ApplyingMode.Always)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(definition is PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (propertyDefinition.typeHashCode == 0)
			{
				return propertyDefinition.InsertDefinitionsFrom == null;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public enum TypeMatchMode
	{
		Direct,
		Extended
	}
	public enum ApplyingMode
	{
		WhenGrouping = 1,
		WhenNoGrouping,
		Always
	}
	public enum AllowExpandingMode
	{
		Default,
		Never,
		ForceIfNoTypeConverter,
		Force
	}
	public enum HeaderShowMode
	{
		Left,
		Top,
		Hidden,
		OnlyHeader,
		OnlyExpander
	}
	[Flags]
	public enum HeaderHighlightingMode
	{
		None = 0,
		OnlyHeader = 1,
		OnlyContent = 2,
		HeaderAndContent = 3
	}
	[ContentProperty("PropertyDefinitions")]
	public abstract class PropertyDefinitionBase : FrameworkElement
	{
		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ParentDefinitionPropertyKey;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey BuilderPropertyKey;

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		protected internal static readonly DependencyProperty BuilderProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PathProperty;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey PropertyDefinitionsPropertyKey;

		protected internal DataTemplateSelector ActualHeaderTemplateSelector => actualHeaderTemplateSelector ?? (actualHeaderTemplateSelector = ((HeaderTemplate == null && HeaderTemplateSelector == null) ? null : new PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector
		{
			DefaultTemplate = HeaderTemplate,
			DefaultTemplateSelector = HeaderTemplateSelector
		}));

		private static DataTemplate DefaultPresenterTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				DataTemplate dataTemplate = defaultPresenterTemplate;
				if (dataTemplate == null)
				{
					DataTemplate dataTemplate2 = new DataTemplate();
					dataTemplate2.VisualTree = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(CellEditorPresenter));
					dataTemplate = (defaultPresenterTemplate = dataTemplate2.Do(delegate(DataTemplate x)
					{
						x.Seal();
					}));
				}
				return dataTemplate;
			}
		}

		private static DataTemplateSelector DefaultContentTemplateSelector
		{
			get
			{
				object obj = defaultContentTemplateSelector;
				if (obj == null)
				{
					obj = new PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector
					{
						DefaultTemplate = DefaultPresenterTemplate
					};
					defaultContentTemplateSelector = (DataTemplateSelector)obj;
				}
				return (DataTemplateSelector)obj;
			}
		}

		protected internal DataTemplateSelector ActualContentTemplateSelector => actualContentTemplateSelector ?? (actualContentTemplateSelector = ((ContentTemplate == null && ContentTemplateSelector == null) ? DefaultContentTemplateSelector : new PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector
		{
			DefaultTemplate = ContentTemplate,
			DefaultTemplateSelector = ContentTemplateSelector
		}));

		protected internal bool HasLinks => linkLocker.IsLocked;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren => PropertyDefinitions.GetEnumerator();

		internal event PropertyChangedEventHandler NotifyPropertyChanged;

		internal event EventHandler RequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties;

		static PropertyDefinitionBase()
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(PropertyDefinitionBase);
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<PropertyDefinitionBase>.New().RegisterReadOnly<PropertyDefinitionBase>("ParentDefinition", ref ParentDefinitionPropertyKey, ref ParentDefinitionProperty, (PropertyDefinitionBase)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, PropertyDefinitionBase oldValue, PropertyDefinitionBase newValue)
			{
				x.OnParentDefinitionChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null).RegisterReadOnly<PropertyBuilder>("Builder", ref BuilderPropertyKey, ref BuilderProperty, (PropertyBuilder)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyBuilder, PropertyBuilder>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, PropertyBuilder oldValue, PropertyBuilder newValue)
			{
				x.OnBuilderChanged(oldValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.RegisterReadOnly<PropertyDefinitionCollection>("PropertyDefinitions", ref PropertyDefinitionsPropertyKey, ref PropertyDefinitionsProperty, (PropertyDefinitionCollection)null, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<string>("Path", ref PathProperty, (string)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, string, string>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, string oldValue, string newValue)
				{
					x.OnPathChanged(oldValue);
				}, (Func<PropertyDefinitionBase, string, string>)((PropertyDefinitionBase x, string v) => x.CoercePath(v)), (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplateSelector>("HeaderTemplateSelector", ref HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, (DataTemplateSelector)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, DataTemplateSelector, DataTemplateSelector>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
				{
					x.OnHeaderTemplateSelectorChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplate>("HeaderTemplate", ref HeaderTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
				{
					x.OnHeaderTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<ControlTemplate>("RowTemplate", ref RowTemplateProperty, (ControlTemplate)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, ControlTemplate, ControlTemplate>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, ControlTemplate oldValue, ControlTemplate newValue)
				{
					x.OnRowTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<string>("Header", ref HeaderProperty, (string)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, string, string>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, string oldValue, string newValue)
				{
					x.OnHeaderChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<IInstanceInitializer>("InstanceInitializer", ref InstanceInitializerProperty, (IInstanceInitializer)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, IInstanceInitializer, IInstanceInitializer>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, IInstanceInitializer oldValue, IInstanceInitializer newValue)
				{
					x.RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.VisualClientProperties);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool?>("IsReadOnly", ref IsReadOnlyProperty, (bool?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, bool?, bool?>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
				{
					x.OnIsReadOnlyChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<object>("Description", ref DescriptionProperty, (object)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, object, object>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, object oldValue, object newValue)
				{
					x.OnDescriptionChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<StyleSelector>("DescriptionContainerStyleSelector", ref DescriptionContainerStyleSelectorProperty, (StyleSelector)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, StyleSelector, StyleSelector>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, StyleSelector oldValue, StyleSelector newValue)
				{
					x.DescriptionContainerStyleSelectorChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<Style>("DescriptionContainerStyle", ref DescriptionContainerStyleProperty, (Style)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, Style, Style>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, Style oldValue, Style newValue)
				{
					x.DescriptionContainerStyleChanged(oldValue, newValue);

				.Register<DataTemplateSelector>("DescriptionTemplateSelector", ref DescriptionTemplateSelectorProperty, (DataTemplateSelector)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, DataTemplateSelector, DataTemplateSelector>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
				{
					x.OnDescriptionTemplateSelectorChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplate>("DescriptionTemplate", ref DescriptionTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
				{
					x.OnDescriptionTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);

				.Register<IEnumerable>("PropertyDefinitionsSource", ref PropertyDefinitionsSourceProperty, (IEnumerable)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
				{

				.RegisterAttached<DependencyObject, ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>>("GetPropertyDefinitiosAttachedBehavior", ref PropertyDefinitionsAttachedBehaviorProperty, (ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>)null, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<PropertyGridSortMode>("ChildrenSortMode", ref ChildrenSortModeProperty, PropertyGridSortMode.Unspecified, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyGridSortMode, PropertyGridSortMode>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, PropertyGridSortMode oldValue, PropertyGridSortMode newValue)

				.Register<HeaderShowMode>("HeaderShowMode", ref HeaderShowModeProperty, HeaderShowMode.Left, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)

				.Register<Visibility?>("ExpandButtonVisibility", ref ExpandButtonVisibilityProperty, (Visibility?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, Visibility?, Visibility?>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, Visibility? oldValue, Visibility? newValue)

				{
					x.OnContentTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);

				.Register<DataTemplateSelector>("ContentTemplateSelector", ref ContentTemplateSelectorProperty, (DataTemplateSelector)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, DataTemplateSelector, DataTemplateSelector>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
				{
					x.OnContentTemplateSelectorChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("ShowChildren", ref ShowChildrenProperty, true, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, bool, bool>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, bool oldValue, bool newValue)
				{
					x.OnShowChildrenChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<Thickness?>("Padding", ref PaddingProperty, (Thickness?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, Thickness?, Thickness?>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, Thickness? oldValue, Thickness? newValue)
				{
					x.OnPaddingChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.OverrideMetadata<Visibility>(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, Visibility, Visibility>)delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x, Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
				{
					x.OnVisibilityChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (Func<PropertyDefinitionBase, Visibility, Visibility>)((PropertyDefinitionBase x, Visibility v) => (v != Visibility.Hidden) ? v : Visibility.Collapsed));
		}

		private static ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase> GetPropertyDefinitiosAttachedBehavior(DependencyObject owner)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected void InvalidateSplittedPath()
		{
			splittedPath = null;
		}

		protected virtual string[] GetSplittedPath()
		{
			return Path.WithString(FieldNameHelper.GetPath);
		}

		internal PropertyDefinitionBase()
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			SetValue(PropertyDefinitionsPropertyKey, new PropertyDefinitionCollection(this));
			ownTypeHashCode = GetType().GetHashCode();
		}

		protected virtual string CoercePath(string path)
		{
			return path;
		}

		protected internal virtual void ExecuteMenuCustomizations(DependencyObject context)
		{
		}

		internal void RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.NotifyPropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.NotifyPropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		internal void RaiseRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties()
		{
			if (this.RequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties != null)
			{
				this.RequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateActualHeaderTemplateSelector()
		{
			actualHeaderTemplateSelector = null;
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("ActualHeaderTemplateSelector");
		}

		protected void UpdateActualContentTemplateSelector()
		{
			actualContentTemplateSelector = null;
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("ActualContentTemplateSelector");
		}

		protected virtual void OnHighlightingModeChanged(HeaderHighlightingMode? oldValue, HeaderHighlightingMode? newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("HighlightingMode");
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionTemplateSelectorChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, args, PropertyDefinitionsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionTemplateChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, args, PropertyDefinitionsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionStyleChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, args, PropertyDefinitionsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>.OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, args, PropertyDefinitionsAttachedBehaviorProperty, PropertyDefinitionTemplateProperty, PropertyDefinitionTemplateSelectorProperty, PropertyDefinitionStyleProperty, (Func<PropertyDefinitionBase, IList>)((PropertyDefinitionBase propertyGrid) => propertyGrid.PropertyDefinitions), (Func<PropertyDefinitionBase, PropertyDefinitionBase>)((PropertyDefinitionBase propertyGrid) => new PropertyDefinition()), (Action<int, object>)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase>)null, (ISupportInitialize)null, (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase, object>)null, true, true, (Func<PropertyDefinitionBase, bool>)null, true);
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowChildrenChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPaddingChanged(Thickness? oldValue, Thickness? newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("Padding");
		}

		protected virtual void OnExpandButtonVisibilityPropertyChanged(Visibility? oldValue, Visibility? newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("ExpandButtonVisibility");
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			headerTemplate = newValue;
			UpdateActualHeaderTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
		{
			headerTemplateSelector = newValue;
			UpdateActualHeaderTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
		{
			contentTemplateSelector = newValue;
			UpdateActualContentTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			contentTemplate = newValue;
			UpdateActualContentTemplateSelector();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDescriptionTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDescriptionTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void DescriptionContainerStyleChanged(Style oldValue, Style newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void DescriptionContainerStyleSelectorChanged(StyleSelector oldValue, StyleSelector newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnApplyingModeChanged(ApplyingMode oldValue, ApplyingMode newValue)
		{
			applyingMode = newValue;
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
			OnDefiningPropertyChange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.Visibility);
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowTemplateChanged(ControlTemplate oldValue, ControlTemplate newValue)
		{
			rowTemplate = newValue;
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("RowTemplate");
		}

		protected virtual void OnDescriptionChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsReadOnlyChanged(bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
		}

		public void OnHideCollectionButtonChanged(bool? eOldValue, bool? eNewValue)
		{
			RaiseRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnPropertyDefinitionsCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ClearDefinitions(e.OldItems);
			SetUpDefinitions(PropertyDefinitions);
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderChanged(string oldValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.VisualClientProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPathChanged(string oldValue)
		{
			InvalidateSplittedPath();
			path = (string)GetValue(PathProperty);
			pathHashCode = ((path != null) ? path.GetHashCode() : 0);
			OnDefiningPropertyChange();
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected void OnDefiningPropertyChange()
		{
			if (Builder != null)
			{
				Builder.OnDefiningPropertyChanged(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnParentDefinitionChanged(PropertyDefinitionBase oldValue, PropertyDefinitionBase newValue)
		{
			parentDefinition = newValue;
			oldValue?.CheckRemoveCustomDefinition(this);
			newValue?.CheckAddCustomDefinition(this);
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnBuilderChanged(PropertyBuilder oldValue)
		{
			if (!isStandardDefinition)
			{
				ActOnDefinitions(PropertyDefinitions, delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase element)
				{
					element.Builder = Builder;
				});
				oldValue?.CheckRemoveCustomDefinition(this);
				if (Builder != null)
				{
					Builder.CheckAddCustomDefinition(this);
				}
				RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSortModeChanged(PropertyGridSortMode oldValue, PropertyGridSortMode newValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void ClearDefinitions(IList collection)
		{
			ActOnDefinitions(collection, delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase element)
			{
				element.Builder = null;
			}, delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase element)
			{
				element.ParentDefinition = null;
			});
		}

		protected virtual void SetUpDefinitions(IList collection)
		{
			ActOnDefinitions(collection, delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase element)
			{
				element.Builder = Builder;
			}, delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase element)
			{
				element.ParentDefinition = this;
			});
		}

		protected internal void ActOnDefinitions(IList collection, params Action<PropertyDefinitionBase>[] actions)
		{
			if (collection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PropertyDefinitionBase item in collection.OfType<PropertyDefinitionBase>())
			{
				foreach (Action<PropertyDefinitionBase> action in actions)
				{
					action(item);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal void Apply(RowData data, bool mergeWithStandard = true)
		{
			MergeDefaultProperties(data);
			PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinitionBase = data.StandardDefinition;
			if (propertyDefinitionBase == null)
			{
				PropertyDefinitionBase definition = data.Definition;
				if (definition != null && definition.isStandardDefinition)
				{
					propertyDefinitionBase = data.Definition;
				}
			}
			MergeWithDefinition(data, propertyDefinitionBase, mergeWithStandard);
			if (data.Definition != propertyDefinitionBase && data.Definition.Style == null && propertyDefinitionBase != null)
			{
				data.Definition.Style = propertyDefinitionBase.Style;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void MergeWithDefinition(RowData data, PropertyDefinitionBase standard, bool mergeWithStandard)
		{
			MergeDefaultPropertiesFromStandardDefinition(data, standard);
			if (mergeWithStandard)
			{
				MergeOtherPropertiesFromStandardDefinition(data, standard);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void MergeDefaultPropertiesFromStandardDefinition(RowData data, PropertyDefinitionBase standard)
		{
			DataViewBase dataView = data.RowDataGenerator.DataView;
			data.Header = GetMergedHeader(dataView, standard, data.Handle);
			data.Description = GetMergedDescription(dataView, standard, data.Handle);
			data.IsReadOnly = GetMergedValue(IsReadOnlyProperty, standard, () => GetDefaultIsReadOnly(data, dataView)) | dataView.IsExactlyReadOnly(data.Handle);
			data.RenderReadOnly = GetMergedValue(IsReadOnlyProperty, null, () => data.RowDataGenerator.View.PropertyGrid.ReadOnly) || (data.IsReadOnly && dataView.ShouldRenderReadOnly(data.Handle));
		}

		protected virtual bool GetDefaultIsReadOnly(RowData data, DataViewBase dataView)
		{
			if (data.RowDataGenerator.View.PropertyGrid.ReadOnly)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (this is PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition)
			{
				if (propertyDefinition.CellTemplate != null || propertyDefinition.CellTemplateSelector != null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if ((!propertyDefinition.isStandardDefinition || propertyDefinition.isResourceGeneratedDefinition) && propertyDefinition.EditSettings != null)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return dataView.IsReadOnly(data.Handle);
		}

		public string GetMergedHeader(DataViewBase dataView, PropertyDefinitionBase standard, RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetMergedValue(HeaderProperty, standard, () => dataView.GetDisplayName(handle));
		}

		public object GetMergedDescription(DataViewBase dataView, PropertyDefinitionBase standard, RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetMergedValue(DescriptionProperty, standard, (Func<object>)(() => dataView.GetDescription(handle).WithString((string x) => x)));
		}

		protected virtual void MergeOtherPropertiesFromStandardDefinition(RowData data, PropertyDefinitionBase standard)
		{
		}

		private T GetMergedValue<T>(DependencyProperty property, PropertyDefinitionBase standard, Func<T> getBaseValueHandler)
		{
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertySet((DependencyObject)this, property))
			{
				object value = GetValue(property);
				if (value is T)
				{
					return (T)value;
				}
			}
			if (standard != null && DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertySet((DependencyObject)standard, property))
			{
				object value2 = standard.GetValue(property);
				if (value2 is T)
				{
					return (T)value2;
				}
			}
			return getBaseValueHandler();
		}

		protected virtual void MergeDefaultProperties(RowData data)
		{
			if (data != null)
			{
				data.CanExpand &= ShowChildren;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void CheckAddCustomDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null && PropertyDefinitions.Return((PropertyDefinitionCollection x) => x.Contains(definition), () => false))
			{
				AddLogicalChild(definition);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void CheckRemoveCustomDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null && definition.Parent == this)
			{
				RemoveLogicalChild(definition);
			}
		}

		public override void BeginInit()
		{
			isInitializing = true;
			base.BeginInit();
		}

		public override void EndInit()
		{
			base.EndInit();
			isInitializing = false;
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.Reset);
		}

		internal void RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind propertyBuilderChangeKind)
		{
			if (Builder != null && !isInitializing)
			{
				Builder.RaiseChanged(this, propertyBuilderChangeKind);
			}
		}

		internal static int GetIndexInCollection(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition == null || definition.isStandardDefinition)
			{
				return int.MaxValue;
			}
			if (definition.ParentDefinition != null)
			{
				if (definition.ParentDefinition.PropertyDefinitions.Contains(definition))
				{
					return definition.ParentDefinition.PropertyDefinitions.IndexOf(definition);
				}
			}
			else if (definition.Builder != null && definition.Builder.Definitions.Contains(definition))
			{
				return definition.Builder.Definitions.IndexOf(definition);
			}
			return int.MaxValue;
		}
	}

				if (EditSettings != null)

				}
				return list.GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		public event ValidateCellEventHandler ValidateCell
		{
			add

			}
			remove
