				RemoveHandler(ValidateCellEvent, value);
			}
		}

		internal event EventHandler ShowMenuButtonChanged;

		static PropertyDefinition()
		{
			ValidateCellEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ValidateCell", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(ValidateCellEventHandler), typeof(PropertyDefinition));
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(PropertyDefinition);
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<PropertyDefinition>.New().Register<System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?>("EditorHorizontalAlignment", ref EditorHorizontalAlignmentProperty, (System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment? oldValue, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment? newValue)
			{
				x.OnEditorHorizontalAlignmentChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null).Register<DataTemplate>("CellTemplate", ref CellTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
			{
				x.OnCellTemplateChanged(oldValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplateSelector>("CellTemplateSelector", ref CellTemplateSelectorProperty, (DataTemplateSelector)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, DataTemplateSelector, DataTemplateSelector>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, DataTemplateSelector oldValue, DataTemplateSelector newValue)
				{
					x.OnCellTemplateSelectorChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<BaseEditSettings>("EditSettings", ref EditSettingsProperty, (BaseEditSettings)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, BaseEditSettings, BaseEditSettings>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, BaseEditSettings oldValue, BaseEditSettings newValue)
				{
					x.OnEditSettingsChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<Type>("Type", ref TypeProperty, (Type)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, Type, Type>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, Type oldValue, Type newValue)
				{
					x.OnTypeChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<PropertyDefinition>("InsertDefinitionsFrom", ref InsertDefinitionsFromProperty, (PropertyDefinition)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, PropertyDefinition, PropertyDefinition>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, PropertyDefinition oldValue, PropertyDefinition newValue)
				{
					x.OnInsertDefinitionsFromChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<string>("Scope", ref ScopeProperty, (string)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, string, string>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, string oldValue, string newValue)
				{
					x.OnScopeChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool?>("AllowInstanceInitializer", ref AllowInstanceInitializerProperty, (bool?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, bool?, bool?>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
				{
					x.OnAllowInstanceInitializerChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("PostOnEditValueChanged", ref PostOnEditValueChangedProperty, false, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<AllowExpandingMode?>("AllowExpanding", ref AllowExpandingProperty, (AllowExpandingMode?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, AllowExpandingMode?, AllowExpandingMode?>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, AllowExpandingMode? oldValue, AllowExpandingMode? newValue)
				{
					x.OnAllowExpandingChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<object>("CommandParameter", ref CommandParameterProperty, (object)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, object, object>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, object oldValue, object newValue)
				{
					x.OnCommandParameterChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<ICommand>("Command", ref CommandProperty, (ICommand)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, ICommand, ICommand>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, ICommand oldValue, ICommand newValue)
				{
					x.OnCommandChanged(oldValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool?>("ShowMenuButton", ref ShowMenuButtonProperty, (bool?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, bool?, bool?>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
				{
					x.OnShowMenuButtonChanged();
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("UseTypeConverterToStringConversion", ref UseTypeConverterToStringConversionProperty, false, (Action<PropertyDefinition, bool, bool>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, bool oldValue, bool newValue)
				{
					x.OnUseTypeConverterToStringConversionChanged();
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<TypeMatchMode>("TypeMatchMode", ref TypeMatchModeProperty, TypeMatchMode.Direct, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool?>("ShowEditorButtons", ref ShowEditorButtonsProperty, (bool?)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, bool?, bool?>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
				{
					x.OnShowEditorButtonsChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplate>("MenuButtonTemplate", ref MenuButtonTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<PropertyDefinition, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(PropertyDefinition x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
				{
					x.OnMenuButtonTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null);
		}

		protected internal override void ExecuteMenuCustomizations(DependencyObject context)
		{
			menuCustomizations.Execute(context);
		}

		protected override string[] GetSplittedPath()
		{
			if (Scope == null && base.Path == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Scope))
			{
				return base.GetSplittedPath();
			}
			return FieldNameHelper.GetPath(string.Format("{0}.{1}", Scope, base.Path.IfNot(string.IsNullOrEmpty) ?? "*"));
		}

		public PropertyDefinition()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
			menuCustomizations = new CustomizationActionsField((DependencyObject)this, false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowMenuButtonChanged()
		{
			if (this.ShowMenuButtonChanged != null)
			{
				this.ShowMenuButtonChanged(this, null);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMenuButtonTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("MenuButtonTemplate");
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellTemplateSelectorChanged(DataTemplateSelector oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowExpandingChanged(AllowExpandingMode? oldValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.VisualClientProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnEditorHorizontalAlignmentChanged(System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment? oldValue, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment? newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("EditorHorizontalAlignment");
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandParameterChanged(object oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandChanged(ICommand oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnUseTypeConverterToStringConversionChanged()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowEditorButtonsChanged(bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
		{
			RaiseNotifyPropertyChanged("ShowEditorButtons");
		}

		protected virtual void OnEditSettingsChanged(BaseEditSettings oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				RemoveLogicalChild(oldValue);
			}
			if (EditSettings != null)
			{
				AddLogicalChild(EditSettings);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnTypeChanged(Type oldValue)
		{
			type = (Type)GetValue(TypeProperty);
			typeHashCode = ((!(type == null)) ? type.GetHashCode() : 0);
			OnDefiningPropertyChange();
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnInsertDefinitionsFromChanged(PropertyDefinition oldValue, PropertyDefinition newValue)
		{
			OnDefiningPropertyChange();
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnScopeChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			InvalidateSplittedPath();
			scope = (string)GetValue(ScopeProperty);
			scopeHashCode = ((scope != null) ? scope.GetHashCode() : 0);
			OnDefiningPropertyChange();
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowInstanceInitializerChanged(bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.VisualClientProperties);
		}

		protected override void MergeDefaultProperties(RowData data)
		{
			base.MergeDefaultProperties(data);
			if (data != null)
			{
				data.Command = Command;
				data.CommandParameter = CommandParameter;
				data.IsReadOnly |= PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.GetIsReadOnly((DependencyObject)(object)data.EditSettings);
			}
		}

		protected override void MergeOtherPropertiesFromStandardDefinition(RowData data, PropertyDefinitionBase standardDefinition)
		{
			base.MergeOtherPropertiesFromStandardDefinition(data, standardDefinition);
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = standardDefinition as PropertyDefinition;
			if (propertyDefinition != null)
			{
				MergeSettings(data, propertyDefinition);
			}
			MergeCellTemplateAndSelector(data, propertyDefinition);
		}

		private void MergeCellTemplateAndSelector(RowData data, PropertyDefinition standardDefinition)
		{
			PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector prioritizedDataTemplateSelector = new PrioritizedDataTemplateSelector
			{
				CustomTemplate = CellTemplate,
				CustomTemplateSelector = CellTemplateSelector
			};
			if (standardDefinition != null)
			{
				prioritizedDataTemplateSelector.DefaultTemplate = standardDefinition.CellTemplate;
				prioritizedDataTemplateSelector.DefaultTemplateSelector = standardDefinition.CellTemplateSelector;
			}
			data.CellTemplateSelector = prioritizedDataTemplateSelector;
		}

		private void MergeSettings(RowData data, PropertyDefinition standardDefinition)
		{
			BaseEditSettings val = EditSettings;
			if (val == null)
			{
				val = standardDefinition.With((PropertyDefinition x) => x.EditSettings);
			}
			PreprocessEditSettings(val);
			data.EditSettings = val;
		}

		protected virtual void PreprocessEditSettings(BaseEditSettings settings)
		{
			((PopupBaseEditSettings)(object)((settings is PopupBaseEditSettings) ? settings : null)).Do(delegate(PopupBaseEditSettings x)
			{
				PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.SetPostOnPopupClosed((BaseEditSettings)(object)x, value: true);
			});
			((CheckEditSettings)(object)((settings is CheckEditSettings) ? settings : null)).Do(delegate(CheckEditSettings x)
			{
				PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.SetPostOnEditValueChanged((BaseEditSettings)(object)x, value: true);
			});
		}
	}
	public class RootPropertyDefinitionExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return new RootPropertyDefinition();
		}
	}
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[Browsable(false)]
	public sealed class RootPropertyDefinition : PropertyDefinition
	{
		internal RootPropertyDefinition()
		{
		}
	}
	public class CategoryDefinition : PropertyDefinitionBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty GlyphProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColorizeGlyphProperty;

		protected internal sealed override bool IsCategory => true;

		[TypeConverter(typeof(SvgImageSourceConverter))]
		public ImageSource Glyph
		{
			get
			{
				return (ImageSource)GetValue(GlyphProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GlyphProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ColorizeGlyph
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ColorizeGlyphProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ColorizeGlyphProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static CategoryDefinition()
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(CategoryDefinition);
			GlyphProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Glyph", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(CategoryDefinition), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ColorizeGlyphProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColorizeGlyph", typeof(bool), typeof(CategoryDefinition), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			PropertyDefinitionBase.HeaderShowModeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CategoryDefinition), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader));
		}

		protected override string CoercePath(string path)
		{
			if (path == null || path == "*")
			{
				return path;
			}
			if (path.StartsWith("<") && path.EndsWith(">"))
			{
				return path;
			}
			return $"<{path}>";
		}

		protected override void CheckAddCustomDefinition(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			if (definition != null && base.PropertyDefinitions.Contains(definition))
			{
				AddLogicalChild(definition);
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("PropertyDefinitions")]
	public class CollectionDefinition : PropertyDefinition
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty NewItemInitializerProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty UseCollectionEditorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowNewItemInitializerProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowRemoveItemsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowAddItemsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HideCollectionButtonProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializerProperty;

		public IInstanceInitializer NewItemInitializer
		{
			get
			{
				return (IInstanceInitializer)GetValue(NewItemInitializerProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NewItemInitializerProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool AllowRemoveItems
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AllowRemoveItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowRemoveItemsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool AllowAddItems
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AllowAddItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowAddItemsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? UseCollectionEditor
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(UseCollectionEditorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(UseCollectionEditorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? AllowNewItemInitializer
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(AllowNewItemInitializerProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowNewItemInitializerProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializer
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializerProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializerProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static CollectionDefinition()
		{
			AllowRemoveItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowRemoveItems", typeof(bool), typeof(CollectionDefinition), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollectionDefinition)d).OnAllowRemoveItemsChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
			}));
			AllowAddItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowAddItems", typeof(bool), typeof(CollectionDefinition), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollectionDefinition)d).OnAllowAddItemsChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
			}));
			HideCollectionButtonProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HideCollectionButton", typeof(bool?), typeof(CollectionDefinition), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((PropertyDefinitionBase)d).OnHideCollectionButtonChanged((bool?)e.OldValue, (bool?)e.NewValue);
			}));
			NewItemInitializerProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NewItemInitializer", typeof(IInstanceInitializer), typeof(CollectionDefinition), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollectionDefinition)d).RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.VisualClientProperties);
			}));
			UseCollectionEditorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("UseCollectionEditor", typeof(bool?), typeof(CollectionDefinition), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollectionDefinition)d).OnUseCollectionEditorChanged((bool?)e.OldValue, (bool?)e.NewValue);
			}));
			AllowNewItemInitializerProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AllowNewItemInitializer", typeof(bool?), typeof(CollectionDefinition), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollectionDefinition)d).OnAllowNewItemInitializerChanged((bool?)e.OldValue, (bool?)e.NewValue);
			}));
			AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializerProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializer", typeof(bool), typeof(CollectionDefinition), new PropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollectionDefinition)d).OnAllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializerChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
			}));
		}

		public static void SetHideCollectionButton(DependencyObject element, bool? value)
		{
			element.SetValue(HideCollectionButtonProperty, value);
		}

		public static bool? GetHideCollectionButton(DependencyObject element)
		{
			return (bool?)element.GetValue(HideCollectionButtonProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnUseCollectionEditorChanged(bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowNewItemInitializerChanged(bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
		{
			RaiseChanged(PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAllowRemoveItemsWithoutNewItemInitializerChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			RaiseRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAllowRemoveItemsChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			RaiseRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAllowAddItemsChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			RaiseRequestUpdateCollectionButtonProperties();
		}
	}
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[Browsable(false)]
	public class PropertyDefinitionCollection : ObservableCollection<PropertyDefinitionBase>
	{
		private readonly PropertyDefinitionBase owner;

		public PropertyDefinitionCollection(PropertyDefinitionBase owner)
		{
			this.owner = owner;
		}

		protected override void InsertItem(int index, PropertyDefinitionBase item)
		{
			base.InsertItem(index, item);
			item.ParentDefinition = owner;
		}

		protected override void RemoveItem(int index)
		{
			PropertyDefinitionBase propertyDefinitionBase = base[index];
			propertyDefinitionBase.ParentDefinition = null;
			base.RemoveItem(index);
		}

		protected override void ClearItems()
		{
			while (base.Count != 0)
			{
				RemoveAt(0);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (owner != null)
			{
				owner.OnPropertyDefinitionsCollectionChanged(this, e);
			}
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
		}
	}
	internal class FakePropertyDefinition : PropertyDefinitionBase
	{
	}
	public class RenderCollectionButton : RenderButton
	{
		protected override FrameworkRenderElementContext CreateContextInstance()
		{
			return (FrameworkRenderElementContext)(object)new RenderCollectionButtonContext(this);
		}

		protected override void InitializeContext(FrameworkRenderElementContext context)

		}

	public class RenderCollectionButtonContext : RenderButtonContext
	{
		private RowControl rowControl;

		private CollectionButtonKind kind;

		protected RowControl RowControl => rowControl;

		public CollectionButtonKind Kind
		{
			get
			{
				return kind;
			}
			set
			{
				if (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).SetProperty<CollectionButtonKind>(ref kind, value, (FREInvalidateOptions)1))
				{
					OnKindChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public RenderCollectionButtonContext(RenderCollectionButton button)
			: base((RenderButton)(object)button)
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((RenderControlContext)this).RenderTemplateSelector = null;
			((RenderButtonContext)this).Click += new RenderEventHandler(OnClick);
		}

		protected virtual void OnClick(IFrameworkRenderElementContext sender, RenderEventArgsBase args)
		{
			if (RowControl != null)
			{
				RowControl.OnCollectionButtonClick(this);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Attach()
		{
			rowControl = (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent as RowControl) ?? (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent.With(PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl) as RowControl);
			if (rowControl != null)
			{
				rowControl.RowDataChanged += OnRowDataChanged;
				rowControl.RowDataIsCollectionRowChanged += OnRowDataChanged;
				OnRowDataChanged(RowControl, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowDataChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (RowControl == null || RowControl.RowData == null)
			{
				Kind = CollectionButtonKind.Remove;
				return;
			}
			Kind = ((!RowControl.RowData.IsCollectionRow) ? CollectionButtonKind.Remove : CollectionButtonKind.Add);
			((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).IsEnabled = RowControl.GetCollectionButtonIsEnabled();
		}

		public override void Release()
		{
			if (rowControl != null)
			{
				rowControl.RowDataIsCollectionRowChanged -= OnRowDataChanged;
				rowControl.RowDataChanged -= OnRowDataChanged;
				rowControl = null;
				OnRowDataChanged(RowControl, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((RenderButtonContext)this).Release();
		}

		public override void UpdateStates()
		{
			((RenderButtonContext)this).UpdateStates();
			UpdateKindState();
		}

		private void OnKindChanged()
		{
			UpdateKindState();
		}

		private void UpdateKindState()
		{
			((RenderControlContext)this).GoToState(Kind.ToString());
		}
	}
	public class RenderCommandButton : RenderButton
	{
		protected override FrameworkRenderElementContext CreateContextInstance()
		{
			return (FrameworkRenderElementContext)(object)new RenderCommandButtonContext(this);
		}

		protected override void InitializeContext(FrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			((RenderControl)this).InitializeContext(context);
			((RenderCommandButtonContext)(object)context).Attach();
		}
	}
	public class RenderCommandButtonContext : RenderButtonContext
	{
		private RowControl rowControl;

		private CommandSourceHelper commandHelper;

		protected RowControl RowControl => rowControl;

		public RenderCommandButtonContext(RenderCommandButton factory)
			: base((RenderButton)(object)factory)
		{
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((RenderButtonContext)this).Click += new RenderEventHandler(OnClick);
		}

		protected virtual void OnClick(IFrameworkRenderElementContext sender, RenderEventArgsBase args)
		{
			if (commandHelper != null && commandHelper.HasCommand)
			{
				commandHelper.Execute();
			}
			else if (RowControl != null)
			{
				RowControl.OnCommandButtonClick(this);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Attach()
		{
			rowControl = (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent as RowControl) ?? (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent.With(PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl) as RowControl);
			if (rowControl != null)
			{
				rowControl.RowDataChanged += OnRowDataChanged;
				OnRowDataChanged(RowControl, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public override void Release()
		{
			if (rowControl != null)
			{
				rowControl.RowDataChanged -= OnRowDataChanged;
				rowControl = null;
				OnRowDataChanged(RowControl, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((RenderButtonContext)this).Release();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowDataChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (commandHelper != null)
			{
				commandHelper.CanExecuteChanged -= OnCanExecuteChanged;
			}
			commandHelper = RowControl.With((Func<RowControl, CommandSourceHelper>)((RowControl x) => new CommandSourceHelper((ICommandSource)x)));
			if (commandHelper != null)
			{
				commandHelper.CanExecuteChanged += OnCanExecuteChanged;
			}
			OnCanExecuteChanged(null, null);
		}

		protected virtual void OnCanExecuteChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).IsEnabled = commandHelper == null || commandHelper.CanExecute;
		}
	}

	public class RenderExpanderContext : RenderButtonContext
	{
		private RowControl rowControl;

		protected RowControl RowControl => rowControl;

		public RenderExpanderContext(RenderExpander factory)
			: base((RenderButton)(object)factory)
		{
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((RenderButtonContext)this).Click += new RenderEventHandler(OnClick);
		}

		protected virtual void OnClick(IFrameworkRenderElementContext sender, RenderEventArgsBase args)
		{
			if ((((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).Visibility ?? ((RenderContentControlContext)this).Factory.Return((RenderContentControl x) => ((FrameworkRenderElement)x).Visibility, () => Visibility.Collapsed)) == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				rowControl.IsExpanded = !rowControl.IsExpanded;
			}
		}

		public virtual void Attach()
		{
			rowControl = (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent as RowControl) ?? (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent.With(PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl) as RowControl);
			if (RowControl != null)
			{
				RowControl.IsExpandedChanged += OnRowControlIsExpandedChanged;
				((RenderButtonContext)this).IsChecked = RowControl.IsExpanded;
			}
		}

		public override void Release()
		{
			if (RowControl != null)
			{
				RowControl.IsExpandedChanged -= OnRowControlIsExpandedChanged;
			}
			rowControl = null;
			((RenderButtonContext)this).Release();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowControlIsExpandedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			((RenderButtonContext)this).IsChecked = RowControl.IsExpanded;
		}

		protected override FrameworkRenderElementContextAutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer(bool force)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Expected O, but got Unknown
			return new FrameworkRenderElementContextAutomationPeer((FrameworkRenderElementContext)(object)this);
		}
	}
	public class RenderResizingPanel : RenderPanel
	{
		protected override FrameworkRenderElementContext CreateContextInstance()
		{
			return (FrameworkRenderElementContext)(object)new RenderResizingPanelContext(this);
		}

		protected override void InitializeContext(FrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			((RenderPanel)this).InitializeContext(context);
			((RenderResizingPanelContext)(object)context).Attach();
		}

		private FrameworkRenderElementContext GetHeader(IFrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			return context.GetRenderChild(0);
		}

		private FrameworkRenderElementContext GetSeparator(IFrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			return context.GetRenderChild(2);
		}

		private FrameworkRenderElementContext GetContent(IFrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			return context.GetRenderChild(1);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize, IFrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			RenderResizingPanelContext renderResizingPanelContext = (RenderResizingPanelContext)(object)context;
			RowControl rowControl = renderResizingPanelContext.RowControl;
			if (rowControl == null || availableSize.Width < double.Epsilon || availableSize.Height < double.Epsilon)
			{
				return ((RenderPanel)this).MeasureOverride(availableSize, context);
			}
			HeaderShowMode headerShowMode = rowControl.HeaderShowMode;
			bool showGridLines = rowControl.ShowGridLines;
			bool flag = rowControl.ResizingStrategy.AdjustAvailableSize(ref availableSize);
			FrameworkRenderElementContext header = GetHeader(context);
			FrameworkRenderElementContext separator = GetSeparator(context);
			FrameworkRenderElementContext content = GetContent(context);
			Size size = new Size(availableSize.Width, double.PositiveInfinity);
			Size result;
			switch (headerShowMode)
			{
			case HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader:
				header.Measure(availableSize);
				separator.Measure(default(Size));
				content.Measure(default(Size));
				result = header.DesiredSize;
				break;
			case HeaderShowMode.Left:
			{
				double headerPercent = rowControl.ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercent;
				double num = 1.0 - headerPercent;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				separator.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, availableSize.Height));
				num2 = Math.Min(availableSize.Width, separator.DesiredSize.Width);
				double num3 = availableSize.Width - num2;
				header.Measure(new Size(num3 * headerPercent, availableSize.Height));
				content.Measure(new Size(num3 * num, availableSize.Height));
				double val = Math.Max(header.DesiredSize.Width / headerPercent, content.DesiredSize.Width / num);
				val = Math.Max(header.DesiredSize.Width + content.DesiredSize.Width, val);
				val += num2;
				result = new Size(val, Math.Max(header.DesiredSize.Height, content.DesiredSize.Height));
				break;
			}
			case HeaderShowMode.Hidden:
			case HeaderShowMode.OnlyExpander:
				content.Measure(availableSize);
				if (showGridLines)
				{
					separator.Measure(default(Size));
				}
				header.Measure(default(Size));
				result = content.DesiredSize;
				break;
			case HeaderShowMode.Top:
				header.Measure(size);
				if (showGridLines)
				{
					separator.Measure(size);
				}
				content.Measure(size);
				result = new Size(Math.Max(header.DesiredSize.Width, content.DesiredSize.Width), header.DesiredSize.Height + content.DesiredSize.Height + separator.DesiredSize.Height);
				break;
			default:
				result = default(Size);
				break;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				result.Width = availableSize.Width;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize, IFrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			RenderResizingPanelContext renderResizingPanelContext = (RenderResizingPanelContext)(object)context;
			RowControl rowControl = renderResizingPanelContext.RowControl;
			if (rowControl == null)
			{
				return ((RenderPanel)this).ArrangeOverride(finalSize, context);
			}
			HeaderShowMode headerShowMode = rowControl.HeaderShowMode;
			bool showGridLines = rowControl.ShowGridLines;
			FrameworkRenderElementContext header = GetHeader(context);
			FrameworkRenderElementContext separator = GetSeparator(context);
			FrameworkRenderElementContext content = GetContent(context);
			switch (headerShowMode)
			{
			case HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader:
				ArrangeElement(header, new Rect(default(Point), finalSize));
				ArrangeElement(separator, default(Rect));
				ArrangeElement(content, default(Rect));
				return finalSize;
			case HeaderShowMode.Left:
			{
				double headerPercent = rowControl.ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercent;
				double num2 = 1.0 - headerPercent;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				num3 = Math.Min(finalSize.Width, separator.DesiredSize.Width);
				double num4 = finalSize.Width - num3;
				ArrangeElement(header, new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), new Size(num4 * headerPercent, finalSize.Height)));
				ArrangeElement(separator, new Rect(new Point(num4 * headerPercent, 0.0), new Size(num3, finalSize.Height)));
				ArrangeElement(content, new Rect(new Point(num4 * headerPercent + num3, 0.0), new Size(num4 * num2, finalSize.Height)));
				return finalSize;
			}
			case HeaderShowMode.Hidden:
			case HeaderShowMode.OnlyExpander:
				ArrangeElement(content, new Rect(default(Point), finalSize));
				ArrangeElement(separator, default(Rect));
				ArrangeElement(header, default(Rect));
				return finalSize;
			case HeaderShowMode.Top:
			{
				double num = 0.0;
				ArrangeElement(header, new Rect(default(Point), new Size(finalSize.Width, header.DesiredSize.Height)));
				num = header.DesiredSize.Height;
				ArrangeElement(separator, new Rect(new Point(0.0, num), new Size(finalSize.Width, separator.DesiredSize.Height)));
				num += separator.DesiredSize.Height;
				ArrangeElement(content, new Rect(new Point(0.0, num), new Size(finalSize.Width, Math.Max(0.0, finalSize.Height - num))));
				return finalSize;
			}
			default:
				return default(Size);
			}
		}

		private void ArrangeElement(FrameworkRenderElementContext context, Rect finalRect)
		{
			if (context.PreviousAvailableSize.Width - finalRect.Width > 1.0)
			{
				context.Measure(new Size(finalRect.Width, context.PreviousAvailableSize.Height));
			}
			context.Arrange(finalRect);
		}
	}
	public class RenderResizingPanelContext : RenderPanelContext
	{
		public RowControl RowControl { get; private set; }

		public RenderResizingPanelContext(RenderResizingPanel factory)
			: base((FrameworkRenderElement)(object)factory)
		{
		}

		public virtual void Attach()
		{
			RowControl = (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent as RowControl) ?? (((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).ElementHost.Parent.With(PropertyGridHelper.GetRowControl) as RowControl);
			RowControl.HeaderPercentChanged += OnHeaderPercentChanged;
		}

		public override void Release()
		{
			if (RowControl != null)
			{
				RowControl.HeaderPercentChanged -= OnHeaderPercentChanged;
				RowControl = null;
			}
			((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).Release();
		}

		private void OnHeaderPercentChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			((FrameworkRenderElementContext)this).UpdateLayout((FREInvalidateOptions)26);
		}
	}
	public class RowControl : RowControlBase, IChromeSlaveMaster, IChrome, ICommandSource
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CanExpandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderShowModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShouldHighlightValueProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentTemplateSelectorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HighlightingModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowGridLinesProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentPaddingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderPaddingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EditorHorizontalAlignmentProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CategoriesShowModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowEditorButtonsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ExpandButtonVisibilityProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MenuButtonTemplateProperty;

		private readonly ChromeSlave slave;

		private RenderTemplate renderTemplate;

		private IPropertyGridResizingStrategy resizingStrategy;

		private ICommand command;

		private object commandParameter;

		private PropertyDefinitionBase definition;

		private PropertyDefinitionBase standardDefinition;

		private EventHandler headerPercentChangedHandler;

		private Thickness actualPadding;

		ICommand ICommandSource.Command => command;

		object ICommandSource.CommandParameter => commandParameter ?? this;

		IInputElement ICommandSource.CommandTarget => this;

		public RenderTemplate RenderTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return renderTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				if (renderTemplate != value)
				{
					RenderTemplate oldValue = renderTemplate;
					renderTemplate = value;
					OnRenderTemplateChanged(oldValue, value);
				}
			}
		}

		protected RenderTemplate ActualRenderTemplate { get; set; }

		public FrameworkRenderElementContext Root => slave.Root;

		protected IElementHost ElementHost => slave.ElementHost;

		protected INamescope Namescope => slave.Namescope;

		ChromeSlave IChromeSlaveMaster.Slave => slave;

		private EventHandler HeaderPercentChangedHandler => OnHeaderPercentChanged;

		private Thickness ActualPadding
		{
			get
			{
				return actualPadding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(actualPadding == value))
				{
					actualPadding = value;
					UpdateRealMinHeight();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren => (IEnumerator)new MergedEnumerator(new IEnumerator[2] { base.LogicalChildren, ElementHost.LogicalChildren });

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount => base.VisualChildrenCount + ElementHost.ChildrenCount;

		protected bool UseChrome => slave.IsEnabled == true;

		protected FrameworkRenderElementContext PART_Root { get; private set; }

		public string Header => base.RowData?.Header;

		public IPropertyGridResizingStrategy ResizingStrategy
		{
			get
			{
				return resizingStrategy ?? NullPropertyGridResizingStrategy.Instance;
			}
			set
			{
				resizingStrategy = value;
			}
		}

		public CategoriesShowMode CategoriesShowMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (CategoriesShowMode)GetValue(CategoriesShowModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CategoriesShowModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowGridLines
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowGridLinesProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowGridLinesProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowEditorButtons
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowEditorButtonsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowEditorButtonsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate MenuButtonTemplate
		{
			get

				SetValue(MenuButtonTemplateProperty, value);
			}

			get

		public Thickness HeaderPadding
		{
			get
			{
				return (Thickness)GetValue(HeaderPaddingProperty);
			}
			set

		}

		protected internal override FrameworkElement MenuPlacementTarget => this;

		double IChrome.DpiScale => ScreenHelper.GetScaleX((Visual)this);

		public event EventHandler IsExpandedChanged;

		public event EventHandler HeaderPercentChanged;

		static RowControl()
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(RowControl);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<RowControl>();
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<RowControl>.New().Register<CategoriesShowMode>("CategoriesShowMode", ref CategoriesShowModeProperty, CategoriesShowMode.Visible, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null).Register<System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment>("EditorHorizontalAlignment", ref EditorHorizontalAlignmentProperty, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, (Action<RowControl, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment>)delegate(RowControl x, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment oldValue, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment newValue)
			{
				x.OnEditorHorizontalAlignmentChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplateSelector>("HeaderTemplateSelector", ref HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, (DataTemplateSelector)null, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplateSelector>("ContentTemplateSelector", ref ContentTemplateSelectorProperty, (DataTemplateSelector)null, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("ShouldHighlightValue", ref ShouldHighlightValueProperty, false, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("CanExpand", ref CanExpandProperty, false, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<HeaderShowMode>("HeaderShowMode", ref HeaderShowModeProperty, HeaderShowMode.Left, (Action<RowControl, HeaderShowMode, HeaderShowMode>)delegate(RowControl x, HeaderShowMode oldValue, HeaderShowMode newValue)
				{
					x.OnHeaderShowModeChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<HeaderHighlightingMode>("HighlightingMode", ref HighlightingModeProperty, HeaderHighlightingMode.HeaderAndContent, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("ShowGridLines", ref ShowGridLinesProperty, true, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<Thickness>("HeaderPadding", ref HeaderPaddingProperty, default(Thickness), (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<Thickness>("ContentPadding", ref ContentPaddingProperty, default(Thickness), (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<bool>("ShowEditorButtons", ref ShowEditorButtonsProperty, false, (Action<RowControl, bool, bool>)delegate(RowControl x, bool oldValue, bool newValue)
				{
					x.OnShowEditorButtonsChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplate>("MenuButtonTemplate", ref MenuButtonTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<RowControl, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(RowControl x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
				{
					x.OnMenuButtonTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<Visibility>("ExpandButtonVisibility", ref ExpandButtonVisibilityProperty, Visibility.Visible, (Action<RowControl, Visibility, Visibility>)delegate(RowControl x, Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
				{
					x.OnExpandButtonVisibilityChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null);
			CommonThemeHelper.TreeWalkerOverrideProperty<RowControl>((Action<RowControl, CommonThemeTreeWalker>)delegate(RowControl x, CommonThemeTreeWalker newValue)
			{
				x.OnTreeWalkerChanged(newValue);
			});
			new ChromeMetadataWorker<RowControl>().OverrideCommonProperties().OverrideFontProperties().OverrideSvgProperties();
		}

		public RowControl()
		{
			//IL_004a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0054: Expected O, but got Unknown
			slave = new ChromeSlave((IChrome)(object)this, true, (Func<FrameworkRenderElementContext>)InitializeContext, (Action<FrameworkRenderElementContext>)ReleaseContext, (Func<bool>)ValidateTemplate, (Func<Size, Size>)base.MeasureOverride, (Func<Size, Size>)base.ArrangeOverride, false, (Action<Brush>)null);
			slave.IsEnabled = true;
		}

		protected override void OnTemplateChanged(ControlTemplate oldTemplate, ControlTemplate newTemplate)
		{
			base.OnTemplateChanged(oldTemplate, newTemplate);
			slave.IsEnabled = newTemplate == null;
		}

		protected virtual bool UpdateActualRenderTemplate()
		{
			try
			{
				return object.Equals(renderTemplate, ActualRenderTemplate);
			}
			finally
			{
				ActualRenderTemplate = renderTemplate;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ReleaseContext(FrameworkRenderElementContext obj)
		{
			slave.ReleaseContext(obj);
			OnApplyRenderTemplate();
		}

		protected void InvalidateContext()
		{
			slave.InvalidateContext();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateSubTree()
		{
			slave.UpdateSubTree();
		}

		void IChrome.AddChild(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(element);
			AddVisualChild(element);
		}

		void IChrome.RemoveChild(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			RemoveLogicalChild(element);
			RemoveVisualChild(element);
		}

		void IChrome.InvalidateMeasure()
		{
			slave.InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		void IChrome.InvalidateArrange()
		{
			slave.InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		protected override void OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(UIElement child)
		{
			slave.ChildDesiredSizeChanged(child);
			base.OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(child);
		}

		bool IChrome.CaptureMouse(FrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			return slave.CaptureMouse(context);
		}

		void IChrome.ReleaseMouseCapture(FrameworkRenderElementContext context)
		{
			slave.ReleaseMouseCapture(context);
		}

		void IChrome.GoToState(string stateName)
		{
			slave.GoToState(stateName);
		}

		protected override void OnRowDataChanged(RowData oldValue)
		{
			UpdateCommandAndParameter();
			base.OnRowDataChanged(oldValue);
			DetachFromDefinition(base.RowData == null);
			if (base.RowData != null)
			{
				HeaderShowMode = base.RowData.Definition.Return((PropertyDefinitionBase x) => x.HeaderShowMode, () => HeaderShowMode.Left);
				SetBinding(CanExpandProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Source = base.RowData,
					Path = new PropertyPath(RowData.CanExpandProperty)
				});
				AttachToDefinition();
			}
			InvalidateContext();
		}

		protected override void OnRowDataShouldHighlightValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnRowDataShouldHighlightValueChanged(sender, e);
			if (base.RowData != null)
			{
				ShouldHighlightValue = base.RowData.ShouldHighlightValue ?? false;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateCommandAndParameter()
		{
			command = null;
			commandParameter = null;
			if (base.RowData != null && base.RowData.Definition is PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition)
			{
				command = propertyDefinition.Command;
				commandParameter = propertyDefinition.CommandParameter;
			}
		}

		private void DetachFromDefinition(bool clearProperties)
		{
			if (definition != null)
			{
				definition.NotifyPropertyChanged -= PrivateOnDefinitionPropertyChanged;
				if (clearProperties)
				{
					ClearDefinitionProperties();
				}
				standardDefinition = null;
			}
		}

		private void AttachToDefinition()
		{
			definition = base.RowData.Definition;
			standardDefinition = base.RowData.StandardDefinition;
			if (definition != null)
			{
				definition.NotifyPropertyChanged += PrivateOnDefinitionPropertyChanged;
				AssignDefinitionProperties();
			}
		}

		private void PrivateOnDefinitionPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnDefinitionPropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		protected virtual void AssignDefinitionProperties()
		{
			base.Template = definition.RowTemplate ?? standardDefinition?.RowTemplate;
			HeaderTemplateSelector = definition.ActualHeaderTemplateSelector ?? standardDefinition?.ActualHeaderTemplateSelector;
			ContentTemplateSelector = definition.ActualContentTemplateSelector ?? standardDefinition?.ActualContentTemplateSelector;
			UpdateHighlightingMode();
			UpdateShowEditorButtons();
			UpdateExpandButtonVisibility();
			UpdatePadding();
			if (definition is PropertyDefinition)
			{
				EditorHorizontalAlignment = ((PropertyDefinition)definition).EditorHorizontalAlignment ?? ((PropertyDefinition)standardDefinition)?.EditorHorizontalAlignment ?? System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
			}
			else
			{
				EditorHorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
			}
			UpdateMenuButtonTemplate();
		}

		protected internal void UpdateHighlightingMode()
		{
			HighlightingMode = definition?.HighlightingMode ?? standardDefinition?.HighlightingMode ?? base.OwnerView?.PropertyGrid?.HighlightingMode ?? HeaderHighlightingMode.HeaderAndContent;
		}

		protected internal void UpdateShowEditorButtons()
		{
			ShowEditorButtons = (definition as PropertyDefinition)?.ShowEditorButtons ?? (standardDefinition as PropertyDefinition)?.ShowEditorButtons ?? base.OwnerView?.PropertyGrid?.ShowEditorButtons ?? false;
		}

		protected internal void UpdateMenuButtonTemplate()
		{
			MenuButtonTemplate = (definition as PropertyDefinition)?.MenuButtonTemplate ?? (standardDefinition as PropertyDefinition)?.MenuButtonTemplate;
		}

		protected internal void UpdateExpandButtonVisibility()
		{
			ExpandButtonVisibility = definition?.ExpandButtonVisibility ?? standardDefinition?.ExpandButtonVisibility ?? base.OwnerView?.PropertyGrid?.ExpandButtonsVisibility ?? Visibility.Visible;
		}

		protected virtual void OnExpandButtonVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowEditorButtonsChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			foreach (CellEditorPresenter presenter in GetPresenters())
			{
				presenter.CellEditor.Do(delegate(CellEditor x)
				{
					((InplaceEditorBase)x).UpdateEditorButtonVisibility();
				});
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMenuButtonTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
		}

		private void ClearDefinitionProperties()
		{
			base.Template = null;
			HeaderTemplateSelector = null;
			ContentTemplateSelector = null;
			HighlightingMode = HeaderHighlightingMode.HeaderAndContent;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefinitionPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			switch (propertyName)
			{
			case "RowTemplate":
				base.Template = definition.RowTemplate;
				return;
			case "ActualHeaderTemplateSelector":
				HeaderTemplateSelector = definition.ActualHeaderTemplateSelector;
				return;
			case "ActualContentTemplateSelector":
				ContentTemplateSelector = definition.ActualContentTemplateSelector;
				return;
			case "HighlightingMode":
				UpdateHighlightingMode();
				break;
			}
			if (propertyName == "IsReadOnly")
			{
				base.RowData.Do(delegate(RowData x)
				{
					x.UpdateIsReadOnly();
				});
			}
			if (propertyName == "Padding")
			{
				UpdatePadding();
			}
			if (propertyName == "EditorHorizontalAlignment")
			{
				EditorHorizontalAlignment = (definition as PropertyDefinition).Return((PropertyDefinition x) => x.EditorHorizontalAlignment, () => (System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?)null) ?? System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
			}
			if (propertyName == "ShowEditorButtons")
			{
				UpdateShowEditorButtons();
			}
			if (propertyName == "ExpandButtonVisibility")
			{
				UpdateExpandButtonVisibility();
			}
			if (propertyName == "MenuButtonTemplate")
			{
				UpdateMenuButtonTemplate();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnOwnerViewChanged(PropertyGridView oldValue, PropertyGridView newValue)
		{
			base.OnOwnerViewChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercentChanged -= HeaderPercentChangedHandler;
			ResizingStrategy.ResetCurrentRow(this);
			ResizingStrategy = newValue.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.ResizingStrategy);
			ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercentChanged += HeaderPercentChangedHandler;
			UpdateRenderResourceProviderDependentProperties();
			UpdateShowGridLines();
			UpdateHighlightingMode();
			UpdatePadding();
			UpdateShowEditorButtons();
			UpdateExpandButtonVisibility();
			UpdateMenuButtonTemplate();
		}

		protected internal void UpdateShowGridLines()
		{
			if (base.OwnerView != null)
			{
				ShowGridLines = base.OwnerView.PropertyGrid.ShowGridLines;
			}
			else
			{
				ShowGridLines = true;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderShowModeChanged(HeaderShowMode oldValue, HeaderShowMode newValue)
		{
			UpdatePadding();
		}

		protected virtual void OnEditorHorizontalAlignmentChanged(System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment oldValue, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment newValue)
		{
		}

		protected internal void UpdatePadding()
		{
			ActualPadding = definition?.Padding ?? standardDefinition?.Padding ?? base.OwnerView?.PropertyGrid?.RowPadding ?? default(Thickness);
			switch (HeaderShowMode)
			{
			case HeaderShowMode.Left:
				HeaderPadding = new Thickness(ActualPadding.Left, ActualPadding.Top, 0.0, ActualPadding.Bottom);
				ContentPadding = new Thickness(0.0, ActualPadding.Top, ActualPadding.Right, ActualPadding.Bottom);
				break;
			case HeaderShowMode.Top:
				HeaderPadding = new Thickness(ActualPadding.Left, ActualPadding.Top, ActualPadding.Right, 0.0);
				ContentPadding = new Thickness(ActualPadding.Left, 0.0, ActualPadding.Right, ActualPadding.Bottom);
				break;
			case HeaderShowMode.Hidden:
			case HeaderShowMode.OnlyExpander:
				HeaderPadding = default(Thickness);
				ContentPadding = ActualPadding;
				break;
			case HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader:
				HeaderPadding = ActualPadding;
				ContentPadding = default(Thickness);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void OnHeaderPercentChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.HeaderPercentChanged != null)
			{
				this.HeaderPercentChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			UpdateActualDescription();
		}

		protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			int visualChildrenCount = base.VisualChildrenCount;
			if (visualChildrenCount == 0)
			{
				return ElementHost.GetChild(index);
			}
			if (index >= visualChildrenCount)
			{
				return ElementHost.GetChild(index - visualChildrenCount);
			}
			return base.GetVisualChild(index);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			return slave.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			return slave.ArrangeOverride(arrangeBounds);
		}

		protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			slave.OnRender(drawingContext);
		}

		protected virtual void OnRenderTemplateChanged(RenderTemplate oldValue, RenderTemplate newValue)
		{
			InvalidateContext();
		}

		protected virtual void OnTreeWalkerChanged(CommonThemeTreeWalker newValue)
		{
			ChromeSlave.CoerceValue((IChrome)(object)this, FrameworkRenderElementContext.IsTouchEnabledPropertyKey);
			UpdateRenderResourceProviderDependentProperties();
		}

		protected virtual bool ValidateTemplate()
		{
			if (UpdateActualRenderTemplate())
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkRenderElementContext InitializeContext()
		{
			UpdateActualRenderTemplate();
			FrameworkRenderElementContext result = slave.CreateContext(ActualRenderTemplate, (Action<FrameworkRenderElementContext>)null);
			OnApplyRenderTemplate();
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateRenderResourceProviderDependentProperties()
		{
			if (base.OwnerView != null)
			{
				UpdateRenderTemplate();
				UpdateRealMinHeight();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnApplyRenderTemplate()
		{
			PART_Root = Namescope.GetElement("PART_Root");
			UpdateRenderMinHeight();
		}

		protected override void OnIsExpandedChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			base.OnIsExpandedChanged(oldValue);
			if (this.IsExpandedChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsExpandedChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateRealMinHeight()
		{
			base.MinHeight = base.OwnerView.GetResourceProvider().GetRowMinHeight() + ActualPadding.Top + ActualPadding.Bottom;
			UpdateRenderMinHeight();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			ResizingStrategy.SetCurrentRow(this);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			ResizingStrategy.ResetCurrentRow(this);
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateRenderMinHeight()
		{
			if (PART_Root != null)
			{
				PART_Root.MinHeight = base.MinHeight;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateRenderTemplate()
		{
			RenderTemplate = base.OwnerView.GetResourceProvider().GetRowTemplate();
		}

		public void OnCommandButtonClick(object commandButton)
		{
			if (base.OwnerView != null)
			{
				base.OwnerView.MenuHelper.ShowCommandsMenu(base.RowData, commandButton);
			}
		}

		public void OnCollectionButtonClick(object collectionButton)
		{
			if (base.OwnerView != null)
			{
				Action<object> action = delegate(object item)
				{
					base.OwnerView.CollectionHelper.OnCollectionButtonClick(base.RowData, collectionButton, item);
				};
				CollectionButtonClickEventArgs e = new CollectionButtonClickEventArgs(PropertyGridControl.CollectionButtonClickEvent, base.RowData.Handle, base.RowData.RowDataGenerator, (!base.IsCollectionRow) ? CollectionButtonKind.Remove : CollectionButtonKind.Add, action);
				base.OwnerView.PropertyGrid.RaiseEvent(e);
				if (!e.Handled)
				{
					action(null);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool GetCollectionButtonIsEnabled()
		{
			if (base.OwnerView == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.OwnerView.CollectionHelper.CanModifyCollection(base.RowData, (!base.RowData.IsCollectionRow) ? CollectionButtonKind.Remove : CollectionButtonKind.Add);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator.Create(this);
		}

		[SpecialName]
		bool IChrome.get_IsLoaded()
		{
			return base.IsLoaded;
		}

		void IChrome.InvalidateVisual()
		{
			InvalidateVisual();
		}
	}
	internal class FakeRowControl : RowControl
	{
		public int Index { get; set; }

		public FakeRowControl(int index)
		{
			Index = index;
		}

		protected override void OnRowDataChanged(RowData oldValue)
		{
		}
	}
}
namespace DevExpress.Xpf.PropertyGrid.UIAutomation
{
	public class PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator : ObjectCreator<AutomationPeer>
	{
		private static object olock = new object();

		private static PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator defaultCreator;

		protected internal static PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator Default
		{
			get
			{
				if (defaultCreator == null)
				{
					lock (olock)
					{
						if (defaultCreator == null)
						{
							defaultCreator = new PropertyGridAutomationPeersCreator();
						}
					}
				}
				return defaultCreator;
			}
		}

		protected override void RegisterObjects()
		{
			RegisterInternal<PropertyGridControl, PropertyGridAutomationPeer>((Func<PropertyGridControl, PropertyGridAutomationPeer>)((PropertyGridControl x) => new PropertyGridAutomationPeer(x)));
			RegisterInternal((PropertyGridView x) => new PropertyGridViewAutomationPeer(x));
			RegisterInternal<RowControl, RowControlAutomationPeer>((Func<RowControl, RowControlAutomationPeer>)((RowControl x) => new RowControlAutomationPeer(x)));
			RegisterInternal<PropertyGridViewKindSelectorControl, ViewSelectorAutomationPeer>((Func<PropertyGridViewKindSelectorControl, ViewSelectorAutomationPeer>)((PropertyGridViewKindSelectorControl x) => new ViewSelectorAutomationPeer(x)));
			RegisterInternal<PropertyGridSearchControl, SearchBoxAutomationPeer>((Func<PropertyGridSearchControl, SearchBoxAutomationPeer>)((PropertyGridSearchControl x) => new SearchBoxAutomationPeer(x)));
			RegisterInternal<CellEditor, CellEditorAutomationPeer>((Func<CellEditor, CellEditorAutomationPeer>)((CellEditor x) => new CellEditorAutomationPeer(x)));
		}

		protected void RegisterInternal<TOwner, TPeer>(Func<TOwner, TPeer> createMethod) where TOwner : FrameworkElement where TPeer : AutomationPeer
		{
			base.RegisterObject(typeof(TOwner), new BarItemClassInfo<AutomationPeer>
			{
				ItemType = typeof(TPeer),
				CreateMethod = (object x) => createMethod((TOwner)x)
			});
		}

		public static void Register<TOwner, TPeer>(Func<TOwner, TPeer> createMethod) where TOwner : FrameworkElement where TPeer : AutomationPeer
		{
			Default.RegisterInternal(createMethod);
		}

		public static AutomationPeer Create<TOwner>(TOwner owner) where TOwner : FrameworkElement
		{
			return ((ObjectCreator<AutomationPeer>)Default).Create(typeof(TOwner), (object)owner);
		}
	}
	public abstract class BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer<TOwner> : BaseNavigationAutomationPeer where TOwner : FrameworkElement
	{
		protected TOwner Target => ((UIElementAutomationPeer)this).Owner as TOwner;

		public BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer(TOwner element)
			: base((FrameworkElement)element)
		{
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridAutomationPeer : BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer<PropertyGridControl>
	{
		public PropertyGridAutomationPeer(PropertyGridControl element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (base.Target == null)
			{
				return list;
			}
			if (base.Target.ShowToolPanel && base.Target.ViewKindSelector != null)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(base.Target.ViewKindSelector);
				if (automationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(automationPeer);
				}
			}
			if (base.Target.ShowSearchBox && base.Target.SearchControl != null)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer2 = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)(object)base.Target.SearchControl);
				if (automationPeer2 != null)
				{
					list.Add(automationPeer2);
				}
			}
			if (base.Target.View != null)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer3 = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(base.Target.View);
				if (automationPeer3 != null)
				{
					list.Add(automationPeer3);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "PropertyGridControl";
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridViewAutomationPeer : ItemsControlAutomationPeer, ISelectionProvider, IScrollProvider
	{
		protected PropertyGridView Target => base.Owner as PropertyGridView;

		public double HorizontalScrollPercent { get; }

		public double VerticalScrollPercent { get; }

		public double HorizontalViewSize { get; }

		public double VerticalViewSize { get; }

		public bool HorizontallyScrollable { get; }

		public bool VerticallyScrollable { get; }

		public bool CanSelectMultiple => false;

		public bool IsSelectionRequired => false;

		public PropertyGridViewAutomationPeer(PropertyGridView element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected override ItemAutomationPeer CreateItemAutomationPeer(object item)
		{
			return new PropertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer(item, this);
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "PropertyGridView";
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.List;
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			RowsCollectionView rowsCollectionView = Target.ItemsSource as RowsCollectionView;
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			for (int i = 0; i < rowsCollectionView.Count; i++)
			{
				PropertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer propertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer = CreateItemAutomationPeer(rowsCollectionView.GetItemAt(i)) as PropertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer;
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = propertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer.GetWrapperPeer(propertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer);
				if (automationPeer != null)
				{
					propertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer.EventsSource = automationPeer;
				}
				list.Add(propertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Selection || patternInterface == PatternInterface.Scroll)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		public void Scroll(ScrollAmount horizontalAmount, ScrollAmount verticalAmount)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public void SetScrollPercent(double horizontalPercent, double verticalPercent)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetSelection()
		{
			if (Target != null && Target.SelectedItem != null)
			{
				FrameworkElement input = Target.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(Target.SelectedItem) as FrameworkElement;
				IRawElementProviderSimple rawElementProviderSimple = input.With(UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement).With(base.ProviderFromPeer);
				if (rawElementProviderSimple != null)
				{
					return new IRawElementProviderSimple[1] { rawElementProviderSimple };
				}
			}
			return new IRawElementProviderSimple[0];
		}
	}
	public class SearchBoxAutomationPeer : BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer<PropertyGridSearchControl>
	{
		public SearchBoxAutomationPeer(PropertyGridSearchControl element)
			: base(element)

		}
	}
	public class ViewSelectorAutomationPeer : BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer<PropertyGridViewKindSelectorControl>
	{
		public ViewSelectorAutomationPeer(PropertyGridViewKindSelectorControl element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> peers = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (base.Target == null)
			{
				return peers;
			}
			base.Target.ShowCategoriesButton.Do(delegate(System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton x)
			{
				peers.Add(UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(x));
			});
			base.Target.HideCategoriesButton.Do(delegate(System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton x)
			{
				peers.Add(UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(x));
			});
			return peers;
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer : ItemAutomationPeer, IExpandCollapseProvider, IScrollItemProvider, ISelectionItemProvider
	{
		internal Func<ItemAutomationPeer, AutomationPeer> GetWrapperPeer = ReflectionHelper.CreateInstanceMethodHandler<ItemAutomationPeer, Func<ItemAutomationPeer, AutomationPeer>>((ItemAutomationPeer)null, "GetWrapperPeer", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic, (int?)null, (Type[])null, true);

		private RowData Data => base.Item as RowData;

		public ExpandCollapseState ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Data.CanExpand)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.LeafNode;
				}
				if (!Data.IsExpanded)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected => Data.IsSelected;

		public IRawElementProviderSimple SelectionContainer => UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(Data.RowDataGenerator.View).With(base.ProviderFromPeer);

		public PropertyGridViewItemAutomationPeer(object element, PropertyGridViewAutomationPeer viewPeer)
			: base(element, viewPeer)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "RowControl";
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.ListItem;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.ScrollItem || patternInterface == PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse || patternInterface == PatternInterface.SelectionItem)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		public void Expand()
		{
			Data.IsExpanded = true;
		}

		public void Collapse()
		{
			Data.IsExpanded = false;
		}

		protected override Point GetClickablePointCore()
		{
			PropertyGridView propertyGridView = base.ItemsControlAutomationPeer.Owner as PropertyGridView;
			return new Point(propertyGridView.ResizingStrategy.HeaderPercent * propertyGridView.ActualRowWidth / 2.0, 10.0);
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView()
		{
			Data.RowDataGenerator.View.PropertyGrid.ScrollIntoView(Data.FullPath);
		}

		public void Select()
		{
			Data.RowDataGenerator.View.PropertyGrid.SelectedPropertyPath = Data.FullPath;
		}

		public void AddToSelection()
		{
			Select();
		}

		public void RemoveFromSelection()
		{
		}
	}
	public class RowControlAutomationPeer : BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer<RowControl>, IExpandCollapseProvider, IScrollItemProvider, ISelectionItemProvider
	{
		public virtual ExpandCollapseState ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Target == null || !base.Target.CanExpand)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.LeafNode;
				}
				if (!base.Target.IsExpanded)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected => base.Target.IsSelected;

		public IRawElementProviderSimple SelectionContainer => UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(base.Target.OwnerView).With((Func<AutomationPeer, IRawElementProviderSimple>)((AutomationPeer)(object)this).ProviderFromPeer);

		public RowControlAutomationPeer(RowControl element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			//IL_00dc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00fb: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0107: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0112: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (base.Target == null)
			{
				return list;
			}
			if (base.Target.HasCustomTemplate)
			{
				return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetChildrenCore();
			}
			list.AddRange(from x in (from x in base.Target.GetPresenters()
					where x != null
					select x.CellEditor into x
					where x != null
					select x).Select((Func<CellEditor, AutomationPeer>)UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement)
				where x != null
				select x);
			FrameworkRenderElementContext root = ((IChrome)((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner).Root;
			if (root != null)
			{
				RenderExpanderContext renderExpanderContext = ChromeExtensions.Children<RenderExpanderContext>((IChrome)((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner, (Func<RenderExpanderContext, bool>)null).FirstOrDefault();
				RenderCommandButtonContext renderCommandButtonContext = ChromeExtensions.Children<RenderCommandButtonContext>((IChrome)((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner, (Func<RenderCommandButtonContext, bool>)null).FirstOrDefault();
				if (renderExpanderContext != null)
				{
					list.Add((AutomationPeer)(object)FrameworkRenderElementContextAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((FrameworkRenderElementContext)(object)renderExpanderContext, true));
				}
				if (renderCommandButtonContext != null)
				{
					list.Add((AutomationPeer)(object)FrameworkRenderElementContextAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((FrameworkRenderElementContext)(object)renderCommandButtonContext, true));
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdCore()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.ListItem;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "RowControl";
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			return base.Target.With((RowControl x) => x.Header);
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.ScrollItem || patternInterface == PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse || patternInterface == PatternInterface.SelectionItem)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		public virtual void Expand()
		{
			if (base.Target != null)
			{
				base.Target.RowData.IsExpanded = true;
			}
		}

		public virtual void Collapse()
		{
			if (base.Target != null)
			{
				base.Target.RowData.IsExpanded = false;
			}
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView()
		{
			base.Target.OwnerView.PropertyGrid.ScrollIntoView(base.Target.RowData.Header);
		}

		public void Select()
		{
			base.Target.OwnerView.PropertyGrid.SelectedPropertyPath = base.Target.RowData.Header;
		}

		public void AddToSelection()
		{
			Select();
		}

		public void RemoveFromSelection()
		{
		}
	}
	public class CellEditorAutomationPeer : BasePropertyGridAutomationPeer<CellEditor>, IValueProvider
	{
		public virtual string Value => Convert.ToString(base.Target.GetEditableValueForExternalEditor());

		public virtual bool IsReadOnly => base.Target.RowControl.Return((RowControlBase x) => x.IsReadOnly, () => false);

		public CellEditorAutomationPeer(CellEditor element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Value)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		protected override void SetFocusCore()
		{
			base.Target.RowData.Do(delegate(RowData x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = true;
			});
		}

		protected override bool HasKeyboardFocusCore()
		{
			return base.Target.RowData.Return((RowData x) => x.IsSelected, () => false);
		}

		protected override bool IsKeyboardFocusableCore()
		{
			return base.Target.RowControl.Return((RowControlBase x) => !x.IsReadOnly, () => false);
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if ((ClearAutomationEventsHelper.UITestingEnabled || ValidationService.GetAllowAccessibilityAlerts((DependencyObject)(object)base.Target)) && ((InplaceEditorBase)base.Target).IsEditorVisible && base.Target.GetEditCore() is FrameworkElement element)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(element);
				if (automationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(automationPeer);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Edit;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			return base.Target.With((CellEditor x) => x.RowControl as RowControl).With((RowControl x) => x.Header) ?? ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).GetNameCore();
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "CellEditor";
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
			base.Target.SetEditableValueFromExternalEditor(value);
		}
	}

namespace DevExpress.Xpf.PropertyGrid.Themes

	public class DynamicPropertyGridResourceExtension : ThemeResourceExtensionBase
	{
		private const string assemblyName = "DevExpress.Xpf.PropertyGrid";

		private const string defaultThemeName = "DeepBlue";

		private const string themesPrefix = "DevExpress.Xpf.Themes";

		private string themeName;

		protected sealed override string Namespace
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(themeName))
				{
					return "DevExpress.Xpf.PropertyGrid";
				}
				return string.Format("{0}.{1}", "DevExpress.Xpf.Themes", themeName);
			}
		}

		public DynamicPropertyGridResourceExtension(string resourcePath)
			: base(resourcePath)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetResourcePath(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			themeName = ThemeNameHelper.GetThemeName(serviceProvider);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(themeName))
			{
				return string.Format("Themes/{0}/{1}", "DeepBlue", ((ThemeResourceExtensionBase)this).ResourcePath);
			}
			return string.Format("{0}/{1}/{2}", ((ThemeResourceExtensionBase)this).ShortPaths ? "PropertyGrid" : "DevExpress.Xpf.PropertyGrid", themeName, ((ThemeResourceExtensionBase)this).ResourcePath);
		}
	}
