	public class CategorySelectorControl : Selector
	{
		private static Action<Selector, bool> set_CanSelectMultiple;

		static CategorySelectorControl()
		{
			set_CanSelectMultiple = ReflectionHelper.CreateInstanceMethodHandler<Selector, Action<Selector, bool>>((Selector)null, "set_CanSelectMultiple", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic, (int?)null, (Type[])null, true);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<CategorySelectorControl>();
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<CategorySelectorControl>.New().OverrideMetadata(Selector.IsSynchronizedWithCurrentItemProperty, (object)true, (Action<CategorySelectorControl>)null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.None);
		}

		public CategorySelectorControl()
		{
			set_CanSelectMultiple(this, arg2: false);
		}

		protected override void OnSelectionChanged(SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnSelectionChanged(e);
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new CategoryHeaderRowControl();
		}
	}
	public class CategoryHeaderRowControl : ContentControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsSelectedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GlyphProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GlyphTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty StringTemplateProperty;

		public DataTemplate StringTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(StringTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(StringTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate GlyphTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(GlyphTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GlyphTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(Selector.IsSelectedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(Selector.IsSelectedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ImageSource Glyph
		{
			get
			{
				return (ImageSource)GetValue(GlyphProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GlyphProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static CategoryHeaderRowControl()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<CategoryHeaderRowControl>();
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<CategoryHeaderRowControl>.New().Register<ImageSource>("Glyph", ref GlyphProperty, (ImageSource)null, (Action<CategoryHeaderRowControl, ImageSource, ImageSource>)delegate(CategoryHeaderRowControl x, ImageSource oldValue, ImageSource newValue)
			{
				x.OnGlyphChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null).Register<DataTemplate>("GlyphTemplate", ref GlyphTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<CategoryHeaderRowControl, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(CategoryHeaderRowControl x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
			{
				x.OnGlyphTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.Register<DataTemplate>("StringTemplate", ref StringTemplateProperty, (DataTemplate)null, (Action<CategoryHeaderRowControl, DataTemplate, DataTemplate>)delegate(CategoryHeaderRowControl x, DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
				{
					x.OnStringTemplateChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}, (FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions?)null)
				.AddOwner<bool>(ref IsSelectedProperty, Selector.IsSelectedProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnStringTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			UpdateContentTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnGlyphTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			UpdateContentTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnGlyphChanged(ImageSource oldValue, ImageSource newValue)
		{
			UpdateContentTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateContentTemplate()
		{
			if (Glyph == null)
			{
				base.ContentTemplate = StringTemplate ?? GlyphTemplate;
			}
			else
			{
				base.ContentTemplate = GlyphTemplate ?? StringTemplate;
			}
		}

		public CategoryHeaderRowControl()
		{
			SetBinding(GlyphProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Path = new PropertyPath("(0).(1)", RowData.DefinitionProperty, CategoryDefinition.GlyphProperty)
			});
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs
			{
				RoutedEvent = Selector.SelectedEvent
			});
		}
	}
	public class SelectionStrategy
	{
		private readonly Locker locker = new Locker();

		private bool isValid;

		public bool IsLocked => locker.IsLocked;

		public bool Updating { get; private set; }

		private PropertyGridControl PropertyGrid { get; set; }

		private RowDataGenerator RowDataGenerator => PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator;

		private DataViewBase DataView => PropertyGrid.DataView;

		public SelectionStrategy(PropertyGridControl grid)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			PropertyGrid = grid;
			locker.Lock();
		}

		public void SelectViaPath(string selectedPropertyPath)
		{
			locker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
			{
				DataView.CurrentRow = ((selectedPropertyPath == null) ? null : DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(FieldNameHelper.GetParentFieldName(selectedPropertyPath, 0)));
			});
		}

		public void SelectViaHandle(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			PropertyGrid.DataView.CurrentRow = rowHandle;
		}

		public void Refresh()
		{
			RowDataGenerator.VerifySelection(DataView.CurrentRow);
		}

		public void OnInitialized()
		{
			locker.Unlock();
			SelectViaPath(PropertyGrid.SelectedPropertyPath);
		}

		public void Invalidate()
		{
			if (isValid)
			{
				isValid = false;
				PropertyGrid.View.InvalidateVisual();
			}
		}

		public void UpdateSelection()
		{
			if (PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.ItemsSource.AsyncUpdatePending)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				Updating = true;
				if (!isValid)
				{
					isValid = true;
					Refresh();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				Updating = false;
			}
		}
	}
	public class CollectionHelper
	{
		private Lazy<bool> canRemoveCollectionItem;

		private Lazy<bool> canAddNewItem;

		private Lazy<bool> isReadOnly;

		private Lazy<string> displayMember;

		private Lazy<string> descriptionMember;

		private Lazy<Type> elementType;

		private Lazy<DescriptorContext> newItemContext;

		private Lazy<IInstanceInitializerImplementer> itemInitializer;

		private DataController DataController => CollectionContext.DataController;

		private DescriptorContext CollectionContext { get; set; }

		private DescriptorContext NewItemContext => newItemContext.Value;

		public string DisplayMember => displayMember.Value;

		public string DescriptionMember => descriptionMember.Value;

		public bool CanRemoveCollectionItem => canRemoveCollectionItem.Value;

		public bool CanAddNewItem => canAddNewItem.Value;

		public bool IsReadOnly => isReadOnly.Value;

		public Type ElementType => elementType.Value;

		public virtual object SelectedItem => NewItemContext.Value;

		public IInstanceInitializerImplementer ItemInitializer => itemInitializer.Value;

		public virtual IEnumerable NewItemValues
		{
			get
			{
				if (!CanAddNewItem)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return NewItemContext.TypeInfos;
			}
		}

		protected CollectionHelper()
		{
			canRemoveCollectionItem = new Lazy<bool>(() => GetCanRemoveCollectionItem());
			canAddNewItem = new Lazy<bool>(() => GetCanAddNewItem());
			isReadOnly = new Lazy<bool>(() => !GetCanAddNewItemInternal());
		}

		public CollectionHelper(DescriptorContext context)
			: this()
		{
			Guard.ArgumentNotNull((object)context, "context");
			CollectionContext = context;
			elementType = new Lazy<Type>(() => GetElementType());
			itemInitializer = new Lazy<IInstanceInitializerImplementer>(() => GetItemInitializer());
			newItemContext = new Lazy<DescriptorContext>(() => GetNewItemContext());
			displayMember = ((context.EdmPropertyInfo != null) ? context.DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetItemDisplayMember(context.EdmPropertyInfo) : new Lazy<string>(() => (string)null));
			descriptionMember = ((context.EdmPropertyInfo != null) ? context.DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetItemDescriptionMember(context.EdmPropertyInfo) : new Lazy<string>(() => (string)null));
		}

		public virtual void AddNewItem(object item)
		{
			if (NewItemContext != null)
			{
				NewItemContext.SetValue(item);
				DataController.Invalidate(CollectionContext.RowHandle);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Type GetElementType()
		{
			if (CollectionContext.IsICollection)
			{
				return (from x in CollectionContext.CollectionWrapper.InterfaceTypes
					select CollectionWrapper.GetElementType(x) into x
					where x != null && CanUseListItemInitializer(x)
					select x).FirstOrDefault();
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsIDictionary)
			{
				return CollectionContext.DictionaryWrapper.GetElementType();
			}
			return CollectionWrapper.GetElementType(CollectionContext.Value.GetType());
		}

		protected virtual bool GetCanRemoveCollectionItem()
		{
			IList list = CollectionContext.Value as IList;
			if (CollectionContext.IsIList && (CollectionContext.Value as IList).Return((IList x) => !x.IsFixedSize, () => false))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsICollection && ElementType != null)
			{
				return !(bool)CollectionContext.CollectionWrapper.IsReadOnly(ElementType).GetValue(CollectionContext.Value);
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsIDictionary)
			{
				return !(bool)CollectionContext.DictionaryWrapper.IsReadOnly.GetValue(CollectionContext.Value);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual DescriptorContext GetNewItemContext()
		{
			return new NewListItemDescriptorContext(CollectionContext, CreateNewListItemPropertyDescriptor());
		}

		protected virtual bool GetCanAddNewItem()
		{
			if (GetCanAddNewItemInternal() && CanUseListItemInitializer(ElementType))
			{
				return DataController.VisualClient.AllowListItemInitializer(CollectionContext.RowHandle);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual bool GetCanAddNewItemInternal()
		{
			if (CollectionContext.Value == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (CollectionContext.Value is IList list)
			{
				return !list.IsFixedSize;
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsICollection && ElementType != null)
			{
				return !(bool)CollectionContext.CollectionWrapper.IsReadOnly(ElementType).GetValue(CollectionContext.Value);
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsIDictionary)
			{
				return !(bool)CollectionContext.DictionaryWrapper.IsReadOnly.GetValue(CollectionContext.Value);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool CanUseListItemInitializer(Type elementType)
		{
			if (ItemInitializer != null && ItemInitializer.Types != null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return NewInstanceConverter.CanUseDefaultNewItemInitializer(elementType);
		}

		protected internal virtual PropertyDescriptor CreateNewListItemPropertyDescriptor()
		{
			Type type = CollectionContext.Value.GetType();
			Type type2 = ElementType;
			if (CollectionContext.IsIList)
			{
				return ListConverter.CreateNewListItemPropertyDescriptor(type, type2);
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsIDictionary)
			{
				return ListConverter.CreateNewDictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor(type, type2, CollectionContext.DictionaryWrapper);
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsICollection)
			{
				return ListConverter.CreateNewCollectionItemPropertyDescriptor(type, type2, CollectionContext.CollectionWrapper);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public virtual void RemoveCollectionItem(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (CollectionContext.IsIList)
			{
				if (!(CollectionContext.Value is IList list) || !(DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle) is ListItemDescriptorContext listItemDescriptorContext))
				{
					return;
				}
				list.RemoveAt(listItemDescriptorContext.Index);
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsICollection)
			{
				object value = DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).Value;
				CollectionContext.CollectionWrapper.Remove(ElementType).Invoke(CollectionContext.Value, new object[1] { value });
			}
			if (CollectionContext.IsIDictionary)
			{
				IDictionaryItem dictionaryItem = DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).PropertyDescriptor as IDictionaryItem;
				CollectionContext.DictionaryWrapper.Remove.Invoke(CollectionContext.Value, new object[1] { dictionaryItem.Key });
			}
			DataController.Invalidate(CollectionContext.RowHandle);
		}

		public object GetDescriptionMemberValue(object item)
		{
			return GetMemberValueInternal(item, DescriptionMember);
		}

		public object GetDisplayMemberValue(object item)
		{
			return GetMemberValueInternal(item, DisplayMember);
		}

		private static object GetMemberValueInternal(object item, string memberName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(memberName))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string[] path = FieldNameHelper.GetPath(memberName);
			object obj = item;
			string[] array = path;
			foreach (string name in array)
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(obj)[name];
				if (propertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				obj = propertyDescriptor.GetValue(obj);
				if (obj == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}

		protected IInstanceInitializerImplementer GetItemInitializer()
		{
			IInstanceInitializerImplementer listItemInitializer = DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetListItemInitializer(CollectionContext.EdmPropertyInfo);
			if (listItemInitializer != null)
			{
				return listItemInitializer;
			}
			return DataController.VisualClient.GetListItemInitializer(CollectionContext.RowHandle);
		}
	}
	public class NullCollectionHelper : CollectionHelper
	{
		public static CollectionHelper Instance { get; protected set; }

		public override IEnumerable NewItemValues => null;

		static NullCollectionHelper()
		{
			Instance = new NullCollectionHelper();
		}

		protected NullCollectionHelper()
		{
		}

		public override void AddNewItem(object item)
		{
		}

		public override void RemoveCollectionItem(RowHandle handle)
		{
		}

		protected override bool GetCanRemoveCollectionItem()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override bool GetCanAddNewItem()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override bool GetCanAddNewItemInternal()
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class CollectionWrapper
	{
		private static Type ICollectionType = typeof(ICollection<object>);

		public IEnumerable<Type> InterfaceTypes { get; private set; }

		public MethodInfo Add => InterfaceTypes.FirstOrDefault().GetMethod("Add");

		protected CollectionWrapper(IEnumerable<Type> interfaceTypes)
		{
			InterfaceTypes = interfaceTypes;
		}

		public static CollectionWrapper Wrap(Type type)
		{
			if (type == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			IEnumerable<Type> collectionInterfaces = GetCollectionInterfaces(type);
			if (collectionInterfaces != null && collectionInterfaces.Any())
			{
				return new CollectionWrapper(collectionInterfaces);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public PropertyInfo IsReadOnly(Type valueType)
		{
			return GetInterface(valueType).GetRuntimeProperty("IsReadOnly");
		}

		public MethodInfo Remove(Type valueType)
		{
			return GetInterface(valueType).GetMethod("Remove");
		}

		private Type GetInterface(Type valueType)
		{
			return InterfaceTypes.Where((Type x) => valueType.IsAssignableFrom(GetElementType(x))).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public static IEnumerable<Type> GetCollectionInterfaces(Type collectionType)
		{
			return GetInterfaces(collectionType, ICollectionType);
		}

		public static Type[] GetInterfaces(Type type, Type interfaceType)
		{
			return (from x in type.GetInterfaces()
				where x.Name == interfaceType.Name && x.Assembly == interfaceType.Assembly && x.IsGenericType
				select x).ToArray();
		}

		public static Type GetElementType(Type type)
		{
			if (type == typeof(object))
			{
				return typeof(object);
			}
			if (type.HasElementType)
			{
				return type.GetElementType();
			}
			if (type.IsGenericType && type.GetGenericArguments().Length == 1)
			{
				return type.GetGenericArguments()[0];
			}
			return GetElementType(type.BaseType);
		}
	}
	public class DictionaryWrapper
	{
		private static Type IDictionaryType = typeof(IDictionary);

		private static Type IDictionaryGenericType = typeof(IDictionary<object, object>);

		private Type interfaceType;

		private Type[] elementType;

		public PropertyInfo Keys => interfaceType.GetRuntimeProperty("Keys");

		public PropertyInfo Item => interfaceType.GetRuntimeProperty("Item");

		public PropertyInfo IsReadOnly => GetProperty(interfaceType, "IsReadOnly");

		public MethodInfo Add => interfaceType.GetMethod("Add");

		public MethodInfo Remove => interfaceType.GetMethod("Remove");

		protected DictionaryWrapper(Type interfaceType, Type[] elementType)
		{
			this.interfaceType = interfaceType;
			this.elementType = elementType;
		}

		public static DictionaryWrapper Wrap(Type type)
		{
			if (type == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Type input = GetInterfaceType(type);
			return input.With((Type x) => new DictionaryWrapper(x, GetElementType(type)));
		}

		private static Type[] GetElementType(Type type)
		{
			object obj = (from x in GetDictionaryInterfaces(type)
				select new
				{
					Type = x,
					ElementTypes = GetElementTypes(x, 2)
				} into x
				where x.ElementTypes.Length == 2
				select x.ElementTypes).FirstOrDefault();
			if (obj == null)
			{
				if (!(type == typeof(ResourceDictionary)))
				{
					return null;
				}
				obj = new Type[2]
				{
					typeof(object),
					typeof(object)
				};
			}
			return (Type[])obj;
		}

		private static Type GetInterfaceType(Type type)
		{
			Type type2 = type.GetInterface(IDictionaryType.Name);
			if (type2 != null && !type2.IsGenericType)
			{
				return type2;
			}
			return (from x in GetDictionaryInterfaces(type)
				where GetElementTypes(x, 2).Length == 2
				select x).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		private PropertyInfo GetProperty(Type interfaceType, string name)
		{
			PropertyInfo runtimeProperty = interfaceType.GetRuntimeProperty(name);
			if (runtimeProperty != null)
			{
				return runtimeProperty;
			}
			return (from x in interfaceType.GetInterfaces()
				select GetProperty(x, name) into x
				where x != null
				select x).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public Type GetElementType()
		{
			if (elementType == null)
			{
				return typeof(object);
			}
			return typeof(Tuple<, >).MakeGenericType(elementType);
		}

		public static IEnumerable<Type> GetDictionaryInterfaces(Type collectionType)
		{
			return CollectionWrapper.GetInterfaces(collectionType, IDictionaryGenericType);
		}

		private static Type[] GetElementTypes(Type type, int count)
		{
			if (type == typeof(object))
			{
				return Type.EmptyTypes;
			}
			if (type.IsGenericType && type.GetGenericArguments().Length == count)
			{
				return type.GetGenericArguments();
			}
			return GetElementTypes(type.BaseType, count);
		}
	}

	}
	public class TabbedDataView : CategoryDataView
	{
		private Lazy<IList<RowHandle>> visibleGroupHandles;

		private RowHandle currentGroup = RowHandle.Invalid;

		public override bool AllowCollapseGroupRows => false;

		public IList<RowHandle> VisibleGroupHandles => visibleGroupHandles.Value;

		public RowHandle CurrentGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return GetCurrentGroup();
			}
			set
			{
				RowHandle parent = GetParent(base.CurrentRow);
				if (parent != value)
				{
					base.CurrentRow = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public event NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler VisibleGroupHandlesChanged;

		public event EventHandler CurrentGroupChanged;

		public TabbedDataView(DataController dataController)
			: base(dataController)
		{
			ResetVisibleGroupHandles();
		}

		private void ResetVisibleGroupHandles()
		{
			visibleGroupHandles = new Lazy<IList<RowHandle>>(() => GetGroupHandles());
		}

		protected virtual IList<RowHandle> GetGroupHandles()
		{
			return GetChildren(RowHandle.Root).ToList();
		}

		protected override IList<RowHandle> GetVisibleHandles()
		{
			return GetVisibleHandles(CurrentGroup, cached: false);
		}

		protected override void SourceChangedReset()
		{
			base.SourceChangedReset();
			ResetVisibleGroupHandles();
			RaiseVisibleGroupHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
		}

		protected override void OnCurrentRowChanged()
		{
			base.OnCurrentRowChanged();
			RowHandle rowHandle = CurrentGroup;
			if (currentGroup != rowHandle)
			{
				currentGroup = rowHandle;
				OnCurrentGroupChanged();
			}
		}

		protected override RowHandle GetCurrentRow()
		{
			return base.CurrentRowPath.Return((string[] x) => x.Select((string y) => GetHandleByFieldName(y)).FirstOrDefault((RowHandle h) => h != null && !h.IsInvalid && !h.IsRoot), () => (base.CurrentRowInternal != null) ? base.VisibleHandles.FirstOrDefault() : null);
		}

		private RowHandle GetCurrentGroup()
		{
			if (base.CurrentRow == RowHandle.Invalid || base.CurrentRow == null)
			{
				return VisibleGroupHandles.FirstOrDefault();
			}
			return base.DataController.GetParents(base.CurrentRow, (RowHandle h) => GetParent(h)).FirstOrDefault((RowHandle h) => IsGroupRowHandle(h)) ?? VisibleGroupHandles.FirstOrDefault();
		}

		private void OnCurrentGroupChanged()
		{
			ResetVisibleHandles();
			RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
			RaiseCurrentGroupChanged(() => new EventArgs());
		}

		private void RaiseVisibleGroupHandlesChanged(Func<NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs> getArgs)
		{
			this.VisibleGroupHandlesChanged.Do(delegate(NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler x)
			{
				x(this, getArgs());
			});
		}

		private void RaiseCurrentGroupChanged(Func<EventArgs> getArgs)
		{
			this.CurrentGroupChanged.Do(delegate(EventHandler x)
			{
				x(this, getArgs());
			});
		}
	}
	public class RowHandleChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public RowHandle Handle { get; set; }

		public RowHandleChangeType ChangeType { get; set; }
	}
	public enum RowHandleChangeType
	{
		None,
		Collapse,
		Expand,
		Reset,
		Replace
	}

	public class PopupBrushEditVisualClientOwner : SelectorVisualClientOwner
	{
		private IPopupBrushEdit OwnerEdit
		{
			get
			{
				PopupBaseEdit editor = ((VisualClientOwner)this).Editor;
				return (IPopupBrushEdit)(object)((editor is IPopupBrushEdit) ? editor : null);
			}
		}

		public PropertyGridControl PropertyGrid => ((VisualClientOwner)this).InnerEditor as PropertyGridControl;

		protected internal PopupBrushValue PopupPostValue { get; private set; }

		public PopupBrushEditVisualClientOwner(PopupBrushEdit editor)
			: base((PopupBaseEdit)(object)editor)
		{
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement FindEditor()
		{
			if (LookUpEditHelper.GetPopupContentOwner(((VisualClientOwner)this).Editor).Child == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return LayoutHelper.FindElementByName(LookUpEditHelper.GetPopupContentOwner(((VisualClientOwner)this).Editor).Child, "PART_GridControl");
		}

		protected override void SetupEditor()
		{
			if (((VisualClientOwner)this).IsLoaded)
			{
				PropertyGrid.SortMode = PropertyGridSortMode.Definitions;
				PropertyGrid.UseCollectionEditor = true;
				PropertyGrid.AllowInstanceInitializer = false;
				PropertyGrid.ShowCategories = CategoriesShowMode.Hidden;
				PropertyGrid.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0);
				PropertyGrid.ShowSearchBox = false;
				PropertyGrid.ShowToolPanel = false;
				PropertyGrid.ShowMenuButtonInRows = false;
				PropertyGrid.SetValue(ScrollViewer.VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty, ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled);
				AssignPropertyDefinitions();
				SetupEditorInternal(assignSelectedObject: true);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void AssignPropertyDefinitions()
		{
			PropertyGrid.PropertyDefinitions.Clear();
			PropertyGrid.PropertyDefinitions.Add(CreateBrushSelectorDefinition());
			PropertyGrid.PropertyDefinitions.Add(CreateSolidColorBrushDefinition());
			PropertyGrid.PropertyDefinitions.Add(CreateGradientStopsDefinition());
			PropertyGrid.PropertyDefinitions.Add(CreatePointDefinitions());
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase CreatePointDefinitions()
		{
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = new PropertyDefinition();
			propertyDefinition.Type = typeof(Path);
			propertyDefinition.UseTypeConverterToStringConversion = true;
			return propertyDefinition;
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase CreateSolidColorBrushDefinition()
		{
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = new PropertyDefinition();
			propertyDefinition.PostOnEditValueChanged = true;
			propertyDefinition.Path = "Color";
			propertyDefinition.HeaderShowMode = HeaderShowMode.Hidden;
			propertyDefinition.HighlightingMode = HeaderHighlightingMode.None;
			BrushEditSettings brushEditSettings = new BrushEditSettings();
			((BaseEditSettings)brushEditSettings).StyleSettings = (BaseEditStyleSettings)new SolidColorBrushEditStyleSettings();
			propertyDefinition.EditSettings = (BaseEditSettings)(object)brushEditSettings;
			return propertyDefinition;
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase CreateBrushSelectorDefinition()
		{
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = new PropertyDefinition();
			propertyDefinition.Path = "BrushType";
			propertyDefinition.ShowMenuButton = false;
			propertyDefinition.HeaderShowMode = HeaderShowMode.Hidden;
			propertyDefinition.HighlightingMode = HeaderHighlightingMode.None;
			propertyDefinition.Visibility = ((!OwnerEdit.AllowEditBrushType) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			propertyDefinition.Command = new DelegateCommand<BrushType>(ChangeBrushType);
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = PropertyGrid;
			PopupBrushEditThemeKeyExtension obj = new PopupBrushEditThemeKeyExtension
			{
				ResourceKey = PopupBrushEditThemeKeys.BrushSelectorTemplate
			};
			((ThemeKeyExtensionGeneric)obj).ThemeName = CommonThemeHelper.GetEditorThemeName((DependencyObject)PropertyGrid);
			propertyDefinition.ContentTemplate = (DataTemplate)propertyGrid.FindResource(obj);
			return propertyDefinition;
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase CreateGradientStopsDefinition()
		{
			PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = new PropertyDefinition();
			propertyDefinition.Path = "GradientBrush";
			propertyDefinition.HeaderShowMode = HeaderShowMode.Hidden;
			propertyDefinition.HighlightingMode = HeaderHighlightingMode.None;
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = PropertyGrid;
			PopupBrushEditThemeKeyExtension obj = new PopupBrushEditThemeKeyExtension
			{
				ResourceKey = PopupBrushEditThemeKeys.GradientsTemplate
			};
			((ThemeKeyExtensionGeneric)obj).ThemeName = CommonThemeHelper.GetEditorThemeName((DependencyObject)PropertyGrid);
			propertyDefinition.ContentTemplate = (DataTemplate)propertyGrid.FindResource(obj);
			return propertyDefinition;
		}

		private void ChangeBrushType(BrushType brushType)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			PopupPostValue.BrushType = brushType;
		}

		protected virtual void SetupEditorInternal(bool assignSelectedObject)
		{
			//IL_0016: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (assignSelectedObject)
			{
				UpdateSelectedObject(PopupPostValue, OwnerEdit.GetPopupBrushValue(OwnerEdit.BrushType));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateSelectedObject(PopupBrushValue oldValue, PopupBrushValue newValue)
		{
			oldValue.Do(delegate(PopupBrushValue x)
			{
				x.BrushTypeChanged -= PopupValueBrushTypeChanged;
			});
			newValue.Do(delegate(PopupBrushValue x)
			{
				x.BrushTypeChanged += PopupValueBrushTypeChanged;
			});
			PopupPostValue = newValue;
			PropertyGrid.SelectedObject = PopupPostValue;
		}

		private void PopupValueBrushTypeChanged(object sender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			BrushType brushType = PopupPostValue.BrushType;
			UpdateSelectedObject(PopupPostValue, OwnerEdit.GetPopupBrushValue(brushType));
		}

		public override void SyncProperties(bool syncDataSource)
		{
		}

		protected override bool ProcessPreviewKeyDownInternal(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Handled)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!((VisualClientOwner)this).Editor.IsPopupOpen)
			{
				return true;
			}
			PropertyGridView view = PropertyGrid.View;
			if (view.ShouldPassKeyDownInLookUpMode(e))
			{
				return false;
			}
			view.ProcessKeyDown(e);
			return true;
		}

		protected override bool ProcessKeyDownInternal(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public override object GetSelectedItem()
		{
			return PopupPostValue;
		}

		public override IEnumerable GetSelectedItems()
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class ListItemDescriptorContext : PropertyDescriptorContext
	{
		public int Index
		{
			get
			{
				if (!base.IsMultiSource)
				{
					return PropertyDescriptorHelper.GetIndex(PropertyDescriptor);
				}
				return ((MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor)PropertyDescriptor).Index;
			}
		}

		public override AllowExpandingMode DefaultAllowExpanding => AllowExpandingMode.ForceIfNoTypeConverter;

		public ListItemDescriptorContext(object instance, PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor, IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
			: base(instance, propertyDescriptor, serviceProvider)
		{
		}
	}
	public class IndexedStringConverterExtension : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		[ThreadStatic]
		private static int globalIndex = 0;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GlobalIndexProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("GlobalIndex", typeof(int), typeof(IndexedStringConverterExtension), new PropertyMetadata(0));

		public static void SetGlobalIndex(DependencyObject element, int value)
		{
			element.SetValue(GlobalIndexProperty, value);
		}

		public static int GetGlobalIndex(DependencyObject element)
		{
			return (int)element.GetValue(GlobalIndexProperty);
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = value as DependencyObject;
			string text = parameter as string;
			if (dependencyObject == null || text == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = GetGlobalIndex(dependencyObject);
			if (num == 0)
			{
				globalIndex++;
				if (globalIndex == 0)
				{
					globalIndex++;
				}
				num = globalIndex;
				SetGlobalIndex(dependencyObject, num);
			}
			return text + num;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class CategoryDataCollectionView : CollectionView
	{
		private class CategoryDataCollection : ObservableCollectionConverter<RowHandle, RowData>
		{
			private class IListWrapper<T> : IList<T>, ICollection<T>, IEnumerable<T>, IEnumerable
			{
				private readonly Func<IList<T>> listGetter;

				private IList<T> List => listGetter();

				public int Count => List.Count;

				public bool IsReadOnly => List.IsReadOnly;

				public T this[int index]
				{
					get
					{
						return List[index];
					}
					set
					{
						List[index] = value;
					}
				}

				public IListWrapper(Func<IList<T>> listGetter)
				{
					this.listGetter = listGetter;
				}

				public int IndexOf(T item)
				{
					return List.IndexOf(item);
				}

				public void Insert(int index, T item)
				{
					List.Insert(index, item);
				}

				public void RemoveAt(int index)
				{
					List.RemoveAt(index);
				}

				public void Add(T item)
				{
					List.Add(item);
				}

				public void Clear()
				{
					List.Clear();
				}

				public bool Contains(T item)
				{
					return List.Contains(item);
				}

				public void CopyTo(T[] array, int arrayIndex)
				{
					List.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
				}

				public bool Remove(T item)
				{
					return List.Remove(item);
				}

				public IEnumerator<T> GetEnumerator()
				{
					return List.GetEnumerator();
				}

				IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
				{
					return List.GetEnumerator();
				}
			}

			private TabbedDataView view;

			private readonly RowDataGenerator rowDataGenerator;

			public TabbedDataView View
			{
				get
				{
					return view;
				}
				set
				{
					if (view != value)
					{
						TabbedDataView tabbedDataView = view;
						OnViewChanging();
						view = value;
						OnViewChanged();
					}
				}
			}

			public CategoryDataCollection(RowDataGenerator rowDataGenerator)
			{
				this.rowDataGenerator = rowDataGenerator;
				base.Selector = PrepareRowData;
			}

			protected virtual RowData PrepareRowData(RowHandle handle)
			{
				RowData rowDataForHandle = rowDataGenerator.GetRowDataForHandle(handle);
				rowDataGenerator.PrepareRowDataForHandle(rowDataForHandle, handle);
				return rowDataForHandle;
			}

			protected virtual void OnViewChanged()
			{
				if (View != null && View.VisibleGroupHandles != null)
				{
					base.Source = new IListWrapper<RowHandle>(() => View.VisibleGroupHandles);
				}
			}

			protected virtual void OnViewChanging()
			{
				base.Source = null;
			}

			protected override void UnsubscribeSourceCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler handler)
			{
				if (View != null)
				{
					View.VisibleGroupHandlesChanged -= handler;
				}
			}

			protected override void SubscribeSourceCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler handler)
			{
				if (View != null)
				{
					View.VisibleGroupHandlesChanged += handler;
				}
			}
		}

		private CategoryDataCollection DataCollection => SourceCollection as CategoryDataCollection;

		public TabbedDataView View
		{
			get
			{
				return DataCollection.View;
			}
			set
			{
				DataCollection.View = value;
			}
		}

		public override int Count => ((Collection<RowData>)(object)DataCollection).Count;

		public override object GetItemAt(int index)
		{
			return ((Collection<RowData>)(object)DataCollection)[index];
		}

		public static CategoryDataCollectionView Create(RowDataGenerator generator)
		{
			CategoryDataCollection collection = new CategoryDataCollection(generator);
			return new CategoryDataCollectionView(collection);
		}

		private CategoryDataCollectionView(CategoryDataCollection collection)
			: base((IEnumerable)collection)
		{
		}

		protected override void OnCurrentChanged()
		{
			base.OnCurrentChanged();
			View.CurrentGroup = (CurrentItem as RowData).With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
		}

		protected override void OnCurrentChanging(CurrentChangingEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnCurrentChanging(args);
		}
	}
	public class CategoryDataView : DataViewBase
	{
		public CategoryDataView(DataController dataController)
			: base(dataController)
		{
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetRootRowHandlesInternal()
		{
			return base.DataController.GetCategoryHandles();
		}

		public override RowHandle GetParent(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowHandle parent = base.GetParent(handle);
			DescriptorContext context = base.DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = base.DataController.GetDescriptorContext(parent);
			if (descriptorContext != null && descriptorContext.RowHandle.IsRoot && !base.DataController.IsGroup(context))
			{
				return descriptorContext.CategoryHandles.FirstOrDefault((RowHandle y) => base.DataController.GetDescriptorContext(y).DisplayName == context.CategoryName);
			}
			return descriptorContext.Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.RowHandle, () => (RowHandle)null);
		}
	}
	public class DataControllerSourceChangedEventHandler : WeakEventHandler<DataViewBase, EventArgs, EventHandler>
	{
		private static Action<WeakEventHandler<DataViewBase, EventArgs, EventHandler>, object> onDetachAction = delegate(WeakEventHandler<DataViewBase, EventArgs, EventHandler> h, object o)
		{
			((DataController)o).SourceChanged -= h.Handler;
		};

		private static Func<WeakEventHandler<DataViewBase, EventArgs, EventHandler>, EventHandler> createHandlerFunction = (WeakEventHandler<DataViewBase, EventArgs, EventHandler> h) => h.OnEvent;

		public DataControllerSourceChangedEventHandler(DataViewBase view, Action<DataViewBase, object, EventArgs> onEventAction)
			: base(view, onEventAction, onDetachAction, createHandlerFunction)
		{
		}
	}
	public class PropertyDescriptorValueChangedEventHandler : WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler>
	{
		private WeakReference weakContext;

		private static Action<WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler>, object> onDetachAction = delegate(WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler> h, object o)
		{
			if (((PropertyDescriptorValueChangedEventHandler)h).weakContext.Target is DescriptorContext descriptorContext)
			{
				descriptorContext.UnsubscribeValueChanged();
			}
		};

		private static Func<WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler>, EventHandler> createHandlerFunction = (WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler> h) => h.OnEvent;

		public PropertyDescriptorValueChangedEventHandler(DescriptorContext context, Action<DescriptorContext, object, EventArgs> onEventAction)
			: base(context, onEventAction, onDetachAction, createHandlerFunction)
		{
			weakContext = new WeakReference(context);
		}
	}
	public class INotifyPropertyChangedEventHandler : WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, PropertyChangedEventArgs, PropertyChangedEventHandler>
	{
		private static Action<WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, PropertyChangedEventArgs, PropertyChangedEventHandler>, object> onDetachAction = delegate(WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, PropertyChangedEventArgs, PropertyChangedEventHandler> h, object o)
		{
			(o as INotifyPropertyChanged).Do(delegate(INotifyPropertyChanged x)
			{
				x.PropertyChanged -= h.Handler;
			});
		};

		private static Func<WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, PropertyChangedEventArgs, PropertyChangedEventHandler>, PropertyChangedEventHandler> createHandlerFunction = (WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, PropertyChangedEventArgs, PropertyChangedEventHandler> h) => h.OnEvent;

		public INotifyPropertyChangedEventHandler(DescriptorContext context, Action<DescriptorContext, object, PropertyChangedEventArgs> onEventAction)
			: base(context, onEventAction, onDetachAction, createHandlerFunction)
		{
		}
	}
	public class INotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler : WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler>
	{
		private static Action<WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler>, object> onDetachAction = delegate(WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler> h, object o)
		{

				x.CollectionChanged -= h.Handler;
			});
		};

		public INotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler(DescriptorContext context, Action<DescriptorContext, object, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs> onEventAction)
			: base(context, onEventAction, onDetachAction, createHandlerFunction)
		{
		}
	}
	public class DescriptorContextFactory
	{
		protected internal DescriptorContext Create(object obj, PropertyDescriptor pd, string fieldName, DescriptorContext parentContext, IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = CreateInternal(obj, pd, fieldName, parentContext, serviceProvider);
			descriptorContext.FieldName = fieldName;
			return descriptorContext;
		}

		private DescriptorContext CreateInternal(object obj, PropertyDescriptor pd, string fieldName, DescriptorContext parentContext, IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			if (parentContext == null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(fieldName))
			{
				return new RootDescriptorContext(obj, serviceProvider)
				{
					RowHandle = RowHandle.Root
				};
			}
			if (pd == null)
			{
				pd = (serviceProvider.GetService(typeof(IVisualClient)) as IVisualClient).With((IVisualClient x) => x.GetPropertyDescriptor(obj, fieldName, parentContext));
			}
			if (pd != null && parentContext != null && parentContext.PropertyDescriptor != null && (parentContext.PropertyDescriptor.Converter.GetCreateInstanceSupported() || parentContext.PropertyType.IsValueType))
			{
				return new ImmutableDescriptorContext(obj, pd, serviceProvider)
				{
					RowHandle = CreateRowHandle()
				};
			}
			if (parentContext != null && parentContext.IsItemProperty(pd))
			{
				return new ListItemDescriptorContext(obj, pd, serviceProvider)
				{
					RowHandle = CreateRowHandle()
				};
			}
			if (pd != null)
			{
				return new PropertyDescriptorContext(obj, pd, serviceProvider)
				{
					RowHandle = CreateRowHandle()
				};
			}
			return new PropertyDescriptorContext(null, null, serviceProvider)
			{
				RowHandle = RowHandle.Invalid
			};
		}

		private RowHandle CreateRowHandle()
		{
			return new RowHandle();
		}

		protected internal void AddContext(DescriptorContext context, PGridDataModeHelperContextCache cache, DescriptorContext parentContext)
		{
			cache[context.RowHandle] = context;
			cache[context.FieldName] = context;
			context.ParentContext = parentContext;
		}
	}
	public class DescriptorContextIterator
	{
		public bool IsMultiSource { get; set; }

		private PGridDataModeHelperContextCache ContextCache { get; set; }

		internal DescriptorContextIterator(PGridDataModeHelperContextCache contextCache)
		{
			if (contextCache == null)
			{
				throw new NullReferenceException("Can't create DataContextIterator instance.");
			}
			ContextCache = contextCache;
		}

		public virtual void DoOperation(DescriptorContextOperation operation)
		{
			DoLocalOperation(operation, RowHandle.Root);
		}

		public virtual void DoLocalOperation(DescriptorContextOperation operation, IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles)
		{
			if (handles == null || operation == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				operation.Init();
				if (operation.NeedsFullIteration)
				{
					VisitAllNodes(handles, operation);
				}
				else
				{
					VisitParentNodes(handles, operation);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				operation.Release();
			}
		}

		public virtual void DoLocalOperation(DescriptorContextOperation operation, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == null || operation == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				operation.Init();
				VisitAllNodes(handle, operation);
			}
			finally
			{
				operation.Release();
			}
		}

		public virtual void VisitCategories(DescriptorContextOperation operation, IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles)
		{
			foreach (RowHandle handle in handles)
			{
				DescriptorContext context = GetContext(handle);
				if (!operation.CanContinueIteration(context))
				{
					break;
				}
				operation.Execute(context);
			}
		}

		private DescriptorContext GetContext(RowHandle rowhandle)
		{
			return ContextCache[rowhandle];
		}

		protected virtual void VisitAllNodes(IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles, DescriptorContextOperation operation)
		{
			foreach (RowHandle handle in handles)
			{
				VisitAllNodes(handle, operation);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void VisitAllNodes(RowHandle handle, DescriptorContextOperation operation)
		{
			DescriptorContext context = GetContext(handle);
			if (!operation.CanContinueIteration(context))
			{
				return;
			}
			operation.Execute(context);
			if (operation.CanContinueIteration(context) && HasChildren(context) && NeedsVisitChildren(context, operation))
			{
				VisitAllNodes(context.AllChildHandles, operation);
				if (handle == RowHandle.Root)
				{
					VisitCategories(operation, context.CategoryHandles);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool HasChildren(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			return context.Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsLoaded, () => false);
		}

		protected virtual void VisitParentNodes(IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles, DescriptorContextOperation operation)
		{
			foreach (RowHandle handle in handles)
			{
				DescriptorContext context = GetContext(handle);
				if (!operation.CanContinueIteration(context))
				{
					break;
				}
				if (HasChildren(context))
				{
					operation.Execute(context);
					if (!operation.CanContinueIteration(context))
					{
						break;
					}
					if (NeedsVisitChildren(context, operation))
					{
						VisitParentNodes(context.AllChildHandles, operation);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private bool NeedsVisitChildren(DescriptorContext context, DescriptorContextOperation operation)
		{
			if (operation.NeedsVisitChildren(context))
			{
				if (!context.IsLoaded)
				{
					if (!context.IsLoaded)
					{
						return operation.NeedsVisitUnloadedRows;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class DataView : DataViewBase
	{
		public DataView(DataController dataController)
			: base(dataController)
		{
		}
	}
	public class DataController : IServiceProvider
	{
		public static IVisualClient NullClient = new NullVisualClient();

		private static readonly object NullSource = new object();

		private const string IncorrectHandleString = "Incorrect handle";

		internal static readonly NewInstanceConverter NewInstanceConverter = new NewInstanceConverter();

		private static readonly DescriptorContextFactory DescriptorContextFactory = new DescriptorContextFactory();

		public const string RootPropertyName = "";

		private bool isMultiSource;

		private bool showAttachedProperties = true;

		private PGridDataModeHelperContextCache contextCache;

		private GetDescriptorContextCommand getContextCommand;

		private object source;

		private object sourceInternal;

		private object metadataLocator;

		private IVisualClient visualClient;

		private Attribute[] browsableAttributes = new Attribute[1]
		{
			new BrowsableAttribute(browsable: true)
		};

		internal Dictionary<string, bool> expandedState = new Dictionary<string, bool>();

		private HashSet<RowHandle> hiddenInvalidateList = new HashSet<RowHandle>();

		private HashSet<RowHandle> invalidateList = new HashSet<RowHandle>();

		protected internal PGridDataModeHelperContextCache ContextCache
		{
			get
			{
				if (contextCache == null)
				{
					contextCache = CreateContextCache();
					InitializeContextCache();
				}
				return contextCache;
			}
		}

		protected GetDescriptorContextCommand GetContextCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (getContextCommand == null)
				{
					getContextCommand = new GetDescriptorContextCommand();
				}
				return getContextCommand;
			}
		}

		public object Source
		{
			get
			{
				return source;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Source != value)
				{
					source = value;
					OnSourceChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal object SourceInternal
		{
			get
			{
				if (sourceInternal == NullSource)
				{
					sourceInternal = VisualClient.Helper.GetSource(Source);
				}
				return sourceInternal;
			}
			set
			{
				if (sourceInternal != value)
				{
					sourceInternal = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal object MetadataLocator
		{
			get
			{
				return metadataLocator;
			}
			set
			{
				metadataLocator = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ShowAttachedProperties
		{
			get
			{
				return showAttachedProperties;
			}
			set
			{
				if (showAttachedProperties != value)
				{
					showAttachedProperties = value;
					ShowAttachedPropertiesChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsMultiSource
		{
			get
			{
				return isMultiSource;
			}
			protected internal set
			{
				if (isMultiSource != value)
				{
					isMultiSource = value;
					OnIsMultiSourceChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public IVisualClient VisualClient
		{
			get
			{
				if (visualClient == null)
				{
					visualClient = NullClient;
				}
				return visualClient;
			}
			set
			{
				if (VisualClient != value)
				{
					visualClient = value;
					InvalidateSource();
				}
			}
		}

		internal Attribute[] BrowsableAttributes => browsableAttributes;

		private Locker SetValueLocker { get; set; }

		private bool IsRootInvalid => invalidateList.Contains(RowHandle.Root);

		internal bool IsValid => invalidateList.Count == 0;

		public bool AllowCollapseGroupRows { get; set; }

		internal event EventHandler SourceChanged;

		internal event EventHandler SourceChanging;

		internal event CancelEventHandler AddingToInvalidateList;

		internal event Action<IEnumerable<RowHandle>> RowHandlesInvalidated;

		public DataController()
		{
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			SetValueLocker = new Locker();
		}

		public void UpdateContextTree()
		{
			if (contextCache != null)
			{
				UpdateHandlesOperation updateHandlesOperation = new UpdateHandlesOperation();
				DescriptorContextIterator descriptorContextIterator = new DescriptorContextIterator(ContextCache);
				descriptorContextIterator.DoOperation(updateHandlesOperation);
				CollectExpandedStateOperation collectExpandedStateOperation = new CollectExpandedStateOperation();
				descriptorContextIterator.DoLocalOperation(collectExpandedStateOperation, updateHandlesOperation.InvalidatedHandles);
				expandedState = collectExpandedStateOperation.ExpandedState;
				updateHandlesOperation.InvalidatedHandles.ForEach(delegate(RowHandle h)
				{
					RowHandleChanging(h, h.IsRoot ? RowHandleChangeType.Reset : RowHandleChangeType.Replace);
				});
				List<RowHandle> removedHandles = ContextCache.Keys.OfType<RowHandle>().Except(updateHandlesOperation.newCache.Keys.OfType<RowHandle>()).Union(updateHandlesOperation.InvalidatedHandles)
					.ToList();
				RaiseRowHandlesInvalidated(removedHandles);
				contextCache = updateHandlesOperation.newCache;
				ContextCache[RowHandle.Invalid] = null;
				updateHandlesOperation.InvalidatedHandles.ForEach(delegate(RowHandle h)
				{
					ContextCache[h].Reset();
					RowHandleChanged(h, h.IsRoot ? RowHandleChangeType.Reset : RowHandleChangeType.Replace);
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeContextCache()
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = GetDescriptorContext("");
			ContextCache[RowHandle.Invalid] = null;
		}

		public void ResetCache()
		{
			if (contextCache != null)
			{
				RaiseRowHandlesInvalidated(contextCache.Keys.OfType<RowHandle>());
			}
			contextCache = null;
		}

		private void RaiseRowHandlesInvalidated(IEnumerable<RowHandle> removedHandles)
		{
			if (this.RowHandlesInvalidated != null)
			{
				this.RowHandlesInvalidated(removedHandles);
			}
		}

		private void OnIsMultiSourceChanged()
		{
			InvalidateSource();
		}

		private void InvalidateSource()
		{
			Invalidate(RowHandle.Root);
		}

		private void RowHandleChanged(RowHandle handle, RowHandleChangeType change)
		{
			this.SourceChanged.Do(delegate(EventHandler x)
			{
				x(this, new RowHandleChangedEventArgs
				{
					ChangeType = change,
					Handle = handle
				});
			});
		}

		private void RowHandleChanging(RowHandle handle, RowHandleChangeType change)
		{
			this.SourceChanging.Do(delegate(EventHandler x)
			{
				x(this, new RowHandleChangedEventArgs
				{
					ChangeType = change,
					Handle = handle
				});
			});
		}

		private void OnSourceChanged()
		{
			SourceInternal = NullSource;
			MetadataLocator = VisualClient.Helper.GetMetadataLocator(Source);
			IsMultiSource = GetIsMultiSource();
			InvalidateSource();
		}

		private bool GetIsMultiSource()
		{
			return SourceInternal.With((object x) => x as IEnumerable).ReturnSuccess();
		}

		private void ShowAttachedPropertiesChanged()
		{
			InvalidateSource();
		}

		internal object GetValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.Value, () => (object)null);
		}

		internal Type GetPropertyType(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.PropertyType, () => (Type)null);
		}

		internal bool CanExpand(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.CanExpand, () => false);
		}

		public IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetChildHandles(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.ChildHandles, () => Enumerable.Empty<RowHandle>());
		}

		internal bool ShouldIncludeMultiProperty(DescriptorContext parentContext, PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
		{
			if (!IsMultiSource)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return GetDescriptorContext(parentContext, descriptor).PropertyDescriptor != null;
		}

		internal bool IsGroup(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context != null)
			{
				return context is GroupDescriptorContext;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool IsGroupRowHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return IsGroup(GetDescriptorContext(handle));
		}

		internal bool IsNewInstanceInitializer(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsNewInstanceInitializer, () => false);
		}

		internal IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetCategoryHandles()
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = GetDescriptorContext(RowHandle.Root);
			return descriptorContext.CategoryHandles;
		}

		protected virtual PGridDataModeHelperContextCache CreateContextCache()
		{
			return new PGridDataModeHelperContextCache();
		}

		internal DescriptorContext GetParent(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.ParentContext, () => (DescriptorContext)null);
		}

		internal bool IsExpanded(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsExpanded, () => false);
		}

		internal bool IsLoaded(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsLoaded, () => false);
		}

		internal IEnumerable GetStandardValues(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.StandardValues, () => (ICollection)null);
		}

		internal IEnumerable GetTypeInfos(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.TypeInfos, () => (ICollection)null);
		}

		internal bool GetIsStandardValuesSupported(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsStandardValuesSupported, () => false);
		}

		internal bool CanUseCollectionEditor(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.CanUseCollectionEditor(), () => false);
		}

		internal bool IsCollectionHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).With((DescriptorContext x) => x.Converter as ListConverter).ReturnSuccess();
		}

		internal bool GetIsStandardValuesExclusive(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsStandardValuesExclusive, () => true);
		}

		internal CollectionHelper GetCollectionHelper(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.CollectionHelper, null);
		}

		internal DescriptorContext GetDescriptorContext(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return ContextCache[handle];
		}

		internal DescriptorContext GetDescriptorContext(string fieldName, bool onlyBrowsable = false)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContextCore(fieldName, IsMultiSource, onlyBrowsable);
		}

		internal DescriptorContext GetDescriptorContext(DescriptorContext parentContext, PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContextCore(parentContext, descriptor, parentContext.IsMultiSource);
		}

		private DescriptorContext GetDescriptorContextCore(DescriptorContext parentContext, PropertyDescriptor descriptor, bool isMultiSource)
		{
			GetContextCommand.Initialize(isMultiSource, ContextCache, SourceInternal, this, BrowsableAttributes);
			DescriptorContext result = GetContextCommand.Execute(parentContext, descriptor);
			GetContextCommand.Release();
			return result;
		}

		private DescriptorContext GetDescriptorContextCore(string fieldName, bool isMultiSource, bool onlyBrowsable = false)
		{
			if (fieldName == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			GetContextCommand.Initialize(isMultiSource, ContextCache, SourceInternal, this, BrowsableAttributes);
			GetContextCommand.OnlyBrowsable = onlyBrowsable;
			DescriptorContext result = GetContextCommand.Execute(fieldName);
			GetContextCommand.Release();
			return result;
		}

		internal Exception SetValue(RowHandle handle, object newValue)
		{
			return SetValueInternal(handle, (DescriptorContext context) => context.SetValue(newValue));
		}

		private Exception SetValueInternal(RowHandle handle, Func<DescriptorContext, RowHandle> setValueFunction)
		{
			DescriptorContext context = GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (context == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Exception exception = null;
			RowHandle changedHandle = null;
			SetValueLocker.DoLockedAction((Action)delegate
			{
				try
				{
					changedHandle = setValueFunction(context);
				}
				catch (Exception e)
				{
					exception = DescriptorContext.GetUnwindedException(e);
				}
			});
			if (exception == null)
			{
				Invalidate(changedHandle);
			}
			return exception;
		}

		internal bool AddToInvalidateList(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!IsValidHandle(handle))
			{
				return false;
			}
			RowHandle parentForUpdate = GetParentForUpdate(handle);
			if (ContainsWithParents(parentForUpdate))
			{
				return false;
			}
			CancelEventArgs e = new CancelEventArgs();
			if (this.AddingToInvalidateList != null)
			{
				this.AddingToInvalidateList(parentForUpdate, e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					hiddenInvalidateList.Add(parentForUpdate);
					return false;
				}
			}
			invalidateList.Add(parentForUpdate);
			return true;
		}

		private bool ContainsWithParents(RowHandle changedHandle)
		{
			return !GetParents(changedHandle).All((RowHandle x) => !invalidateList.Contains(x) && !hiddenInvalidateList.Contains(x));
		}

		private RowHandle GetParentForUpdate(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowHandle changed = handle;
			if (handle.IsRoot)
			{
				return handle;
			}
			GetParents(handle).All(delegate(RowHandle x)
			{
				changed = x;
				if (CheckAttributeValue(RefreshPropertiesAttribute.All, x))
				{
					changed = RowHandle.Root;
					return false;
				}
				return CheckAttributeValue(NotifyParentPropertyAttribute.Yes, x) ? true : false;
			});
			return changed;
		}

		internal IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetParents(RowHandle handle, Func<RowHandle, RowHandle> getParent = null, bool includeCurrent = true)
		{
			if (getParent == null)
			{
				getParent = (RowHandle h) => GetParent(h).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.RowHandle, () => (RowHandle)null);
			}
			RowHandle currentHandle = handle;
			do
			{
				if (includeCurrent || (!includeCurrent && currentHandle != handle))
				{
					yield return currentHandle;
				}
				if (currentHandle.IsRoot)
				{
					break;
				}
				currentHandle = getParent(currentHandle);
			}
			while (currentHandle != null && !currentHandle.IsInvalid);
		}

		internal void UpdateHandles()
		{
			if (contextCache == null)
			{
				RowHandleChanging(RowHandle.Root, RowHandleChangeType.Reset);
				RowHandleChanged(RowHandle.Root, RowHandleChangeType.Reset);
			}
			else
			{
				InvalidateChildren(invalidateList.Union(hiddenInvalidateList));
				UpdateContextTree();
			}
			invalidateList.Clear();
			hiddenInvalidateList.Clear();
		}

		private void InvalidateChildren(IEnumerable<RowHandle> handles)
		{
			(from x in handles
				select ContextCache[x] into x
				where x != null
				select x).ToList().ForEach(delegate(DescriptorContext x)
			{
				x.InvalidateChildren();
			});
		}

		internal IEnumerable<string> GetValidationError(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.ValidationError, () => (IEnumerable<string>)null);
		}

		internal void InvalidateClient(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (VisualClient.InvokeRequired)
			{
				VisualClient.Invoke((Action)delegate
				{
					VisualClient.Invalidate(handle);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				VisualClient.Invalidate(handle);
			}
		}

		internal void Invalidate(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (VisualClient.InvokeRequired)
			{
				VisualClient.Invoke(new Action<RowHandle>(InvalidateInternal), handle);
			}
			else
			{
				InvalidateInternal(handle);
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateInternal(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (AddToInvalidateList(handle))
			{
				InvalidateClient(handle);
			}
		}

		public void Update()
		{
			if (!IsValid)
			{
				if (VisualClient.InvokeRequired)
				{
					VisualClient.Invoke(new Action(UpdateHandles));
				}
				else
				{
					UpdateHandles();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool CheckAttributeValue<T>(T value, RowHandle handle)
		{
			return value.Equals(GetDescriptorContext(handle).With((DescriptorContext x) => x.PropertyAttributes).Return((AttributeCollection propertyAttributes) => propertyAttributes[typeof(T)], () => (Attribute)null));
		}

		internal bool IsValidHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == null || handle == RowHandle.Invalid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (handle.IsRoot)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle) != null;
		}

		internal bool CanResetValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.CanResetValue, () => false);
		}

		internal Exception ResetValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return SetValueInternal(handle, (DescriptorContext context) => context.ResetValue());
		}

		internal string GetNameByHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.Name, () => (string)null);
		}

		internal string GetFieldNameByHandle(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(rowHandle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.FieldName, () => (string)null);
		}

		internal RowHandle GetHandleByFieldName(string fieldName)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(fieldName).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.RowHandle, () => RowHandle.Invalid);
		}

		internal string GetDescription(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle)?.PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Description, () => (string)null);
		}

		internal bool IsAttachedProperty(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => VisualClient.Helper.IsAttached(x.PropertyDescriptor), () => false);
		}

		internal string GetDisplayName(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(rowHandle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.DisplayName, () => (string)null);
		}

		internal bool IsExactlyReadOnly(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(rowHandle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsPropertyReadOnly, () => false);
		}

		internal bool IsReadOnly(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return !GetDescriptorContext(rowHandle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.IsValueEditable, () => true);
		}

		internal bool ShouldRenderReadOnly(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(rowHandle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.ShouldRenderReadOnly, () => true);
		}

		internal void SetIsExpanded(RowHandle handle, bool isExpanded)
		{
			bool changed = false;
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (!(descriptorContext is GroupDescriptorContext) || AllowCollapseGroupRows)
			{
				RowHandleChanging(handle, (!isExpanded) ? RowHandleChangeType.Collapse : RowHandleChangeType.Expand);
				descriptorContext.Do(delegate(DescriptorContext x)
				{
					changed = x.SetIsExpanded(isExpanded);
				});
				if (changed)
				{
					RowHandleChanged(handle, (!isExpanded) ? RowHandleChangeType.Collapse : RowHandleChangeType.Expand);
				}
				else
				{
					RowHandleChanged(handle, RowHandleChangeType.None);
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool ShouldSerializeValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.ShouldSerializeValue, () => false);
		}

		object IServiceProvider.GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			if (serviceType == typeof(DescriptorContextFactory))
			{
				return DescriptorContextFactory;
			}
			if (serviceType == typeof(DataController))
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (serviceType == typeof(IVisualClient))
			{
				return VisualClient;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal bool CanConvertToString(RowHandle handle)
		{
			DescriptorContext context = GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (context == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return context.Converter.Return((TypeConverter converter) => converter.CanConvertTo(context, typeof(string)), () => false);
		}

		internal bool CanConvertToType(RowHandle handle)
		{
			DescriptorContext context = GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (context == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (context.ConvertToType == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return context.Converter.Return((TypeConverter converter) => converter.CanConvertTo(context, context.ConvertToType), () => false);
		}

		internal string ConvertToString(RowHandle handle, object value)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (descriptorContext == null || descriptorContext.Converter == null)
			{
				return "Incorrect handle";
			}
			return descriptorContext.Converter.ConvertToString(descriptorContext, value);
		}

		internal object ConvertToType(RowHandle handle, object value)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (descriptorContext == null || descriptorContext.Converter == null)
			{
				return "Incorrect handle";
			}
			return descriptorContext.Converter.ConvertTo(descriptorContext, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, value, descriptorContext.ConvertToType);
		}
	}
	public interface IFilterClient
	{
		object GetObject(RowHandle handle);

		object GetDefaultObject();

		bool GetIsVisible(RowHandle handle);
	}
	public interface ISortClient
	{
		IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetSortedChildren(RowHandle parent, IEnumerable<RowHandle> children);
	}
	public class DataViewBase
	{
		private class ChangeInfo
		{
			public IList<RowHandle> OldHandles;
		}

		private readonly IFilterClient DefaultFilterClient;

		private readonly ISortClient DefaultSortClient;

		private DataController dataController;

		private CriteriaOperator filterCriteria;

		private CriteriaOperator searchCriteria;

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, IEnumerable<RowHandle>> childrenCache;

		private IFilterClient filterClient;

		private ISortClient sortClient;

		private Lazy<IList<RowHandle>> visibleHandles;

		private CriteriaOperator combinedFilterCriteria;

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, bool> searchExpandedState;

		private RowHandle currentRow;

		private Func<object, bool> predicate;

		private bool shouldResetVisibleHandlesCache;

		private List<ChangeInfo> changeInfo = new List<ChangeInfo>();

		private Locker UpdateCurrentRowLocker = new Locker();

		private bool IsDataViewValid { get; set; }

		protected string[] CurrentRowPath { get; set; }

		public RowHandle CurrentRow
		{
			get
			{
				if (currentRow == RowHandle.Invalid)
				{
					UpdateCurrentRow();
				}
				return currentRow;
			}
			set
			{
				if (currentRow != value)
				{
					currentRow = value;
					OnCurrentRowChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected RowHandle CurrentRowInternal => currentRow;

		public IList<RowHandle> VisibleHandles => visibleHandles.Value;

		public virtual bool AllowCollapseGroupRows => true;

		public bool IsKeySensitive { get; set; }

		public CriteriaOperator SearchCriteria
		{
			get
			{
				return searchCriteria;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!object.Equals(searchCriteria, value))
				{
					searchCriteria = value;
					FilterCriteriaChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public CriteriaOperator FilterCriteria
		{
			get
			{
				return filterCriteria;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!object.Equals(filterCriteria, value))
				{
					filterCriteria = value;
					FilterCriteriaChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public IFilterClient FilterClient
		{
			get
			{
				if (filterClient == null)
				{
					filterClient = DefaultFilterClient;
				}
				return filterClient;
			}
			set
			{
				if (FilterClient != value)
				{
					filterClient = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public ISortClient SortClient
		{
			get
			{
				if (sortClient == null)
				{
					sortClient = DefaultSortClient;
				}
				return sortClient;
			}
			set
			{
				if (SortClient != value)
				{
					sortClient = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected DataController DataController => dataController;

		private Func<object, bool> Predicate
		{
			get
			{
				if (predicate == null)
				{
					predicate = CreatePredicate();
				}
				return predicate;
			}
		}

		private bool InFilterMode => !object.Equals(FilterCriteria, null);

		private bool InSearchMode => !object.Equals(SearchCriteria, null);

		private bool CanFilter
		{
			get
			{
				if (!InFilterMode)
				{
					return InSearchMode;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private CriteriaOperator CombinedFilterCriteria
		{
			get
			{
				if (object.Equals(combinedFilterCriteria, null))
				{
					combinedFilterCriteria = GetFilterCriteria();
				}
				return combinedFilterCriteria;
			}
		}

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, IEnumerable<RowHandle>> ChildrenCache
		{
			get
			{
				if (childrenCache == null)
				{
					childrenCache = CreateFilterCache();
				}
				return childrenCache;
			}
		}

		protected Locker UpdateLocker { get; set; }

		public event NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler VisibleHandlesChanged;

		public event NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler InvisibleHandlesChanged;

		public event EventHandler CurrentRowChanged;

		protected DataViewBase(DataController dataController)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0089: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0093: Expected O, but got Unknown
			DefaultFilterClient = new PropertyNameFilterClient(this);
			DefaultSortClient = new SortClient();
			this.dataController = dataController;
			dataController.AllowCollapseGroupRows = AllowCollapseGroupRows;
			dataController.SourceChanged += SourceChanged;
			dataController.SourceChanging += SourceChanging;
			dataController.AddingToInvalidateList += AddingToInvalidateList;
			ResetVisibleHandles();
			ResetSearchExpandedState();
			UpdateLocker = new Locker();
			UpdateLocker.Unlocked += delegate
			{
				OnUpdated();
			};
		}

		protected virtual void OnCurrentRowChanged()
		{
			MakeRowVisible(CurrentRow);
			RaiseCurrentRowChanged();
		}

		public void MakeRowVisible(RowHandle handle, bool withChildren = false)
		{
			if (handle != null && (!VisibleHandles.Contains(handle) || withChildren))
			{
				DataController.GetParents(handle, (RowHandle h) => GetParent(h), withChildren).Reverse().ForEach(delegate(RowHandle h)
				{
					SetIsExpanded(h, isExpanded: true);
				});
			}
		}

		private void RaiseCurrentRowChanged()
		{
			this.CurrentRowChanged.Do(delegate(EventHandler x)
			{
				x(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			});
		}

		private void AddingToInvalidateList(object sender, CancelEventArgs args)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = (RowHandle)sender;
			_ = rowHandle.IsRoot;
		}

		private void SourceChanging(object o, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e is RowHandleChangedEventArgs e2))
			{
				return;
			}
			ChangeInfo changeInfo = new ChangeInfo();
			this.changeInfo.Add(changeInfo);
			if (e2.ChangeType != RowHandleChangeType.Reset && e2.ChangeType != RowHandleChangeType.None)
			{
				if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Collapse || e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Replace)
				{
					changeInfo.OldHandles = GetVisibleHandles(e2.Handle, cached: true);
					shouldResetVisibleHandlesCache = e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Replace;
				}
				if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Expand)
				{
					changeInfo.OldHandles = new List<RowHandle>();
				}
			}
		}

		private void SourceChanged(object o, EventArgs e)
		{
			RowHandleChangedEventArgs e2 = e as RowHandleChangedEventArgs;
			IList<RowHandle> newHandles = null;
			if (e2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ChangeInfo info = changeInfo[0];
			changeInfo.RemoveAt(0);
			if (changeInfo.Count == 0 && !IsDataViewValid)
			{
				e2.ChangeType = RowHandleChangeType.Reset;
			}
			if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.None || e2.Handle == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Reset || !visibleHandles.IsValueCreated)
			{
				UpdateDataView(force: true);
				return;
			}
			if (shouldResetVisibleHandlesCache)
			{
				ResetChildrenCache();
			}
			int startIndex = VisibleHandles.IndexOf(e2.Handle);
			if (startIndex == -1)
			{
				List<RowHandle> invisibleHandles = GetVisibleHandles(e2.Handle, cached: false).ToList();
				invisibleHandles.Insert(0, e2.Handle);
				RaiseInvisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, invisibleHandles, invisibleHandles));
				return;
			}
			List<RowHandle> currentList = new List<RowHandle> { e2.Handle };
			if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Collapse)
			{
				((IEnumerable<RowHandle>)info.OldHandles).ForEach((Action<RowHandle>)delegate
				{
					VisibleHandles.RemoveAt(startIndex + 1);
				});
				RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, currentList.ToList(), currentList.ToList(), startIndex));
				RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove, info.OldHandles.ToList(), startIndex + 1));
			}
			if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Expand || e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Replace)
			{
				((IEnumerable<RowHandle>)info.OldHandles).ForEach((Action<RowHandle>)delegate
				{
					VisibleHandles.RemoveAt(startIndex + 1);
				});
				newHandles = GetVisibleHandles(e2.Handle, cached: false);
				newHandles.Reverse().ForEach(delegate(RowHandle x)
				{
					VisibleHandles.Insert(startIndex + 1, x);
				});
			}
			if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Expand)
			{
				RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, currentList.ToList(), currentList.ToList(), startIndex));
				RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add, newHandles.ToList(), startIndex + 1));
			}
			if (e2.ChangeType == RowHandleChangeType.Replace)
			{
				currentList.AddRange(newHandles);
				info.OldHandles.Insert(0, e2.Handle);
				RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, currentList.ToList(), info.OldHandles.ToList(), startIndex));
			}
		}

		public bool IsLoaded(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.FirstExpanded, () => false);
		}

		protected virtual void SourceChangedReset()
		{
			RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(() => new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
		}

		public IEdmPropertyInfo GetEdmPropertyInfo(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.EdmPropertyInfo, () => (IEdmPropertyInfo)null);
		}

		protected virtual RowHandle GetCurrentRow()
		{
			return CurrentRowPath.Return((string[] x) => x.Select((string y) => GetHandleByFieldName(y)).FirstOrDefault((RowHandle h) => h != null && !h.IsInvalid && !h.IsRoot && VisibleHandles.Contains(h)), () => (CurrentRowInternal != null) ? VisibleHandles.FirstOrDefault() : null);
		}

		protected void ResetVisibleHandles()
		{
			visibleHandles = new Lazy<IList<RowHandle>>(() => GetVisibleHandles());
		}

		protected virtual IList<RowHandle> GetVisibleHandles()
		{
			return GetVisibleHandles(RowHandle.Root, cached: false);
		}

		protected IList<RowHandle> GetVisibleHandles(RowHandle handle, bool cached)
		{
			if (!IsExpanded(handle))
			{
				return new List<RowHandle>();
			}
			if (cached)
			{
				return GetCachedVisibleHandles(handle);
			}
			return GetCurrentVisibleHandles(handle);
		}

		private IList<RowHandle> GetCachedVisibleHandles(RowHandle handle)
		{
			IndexedDictionary<RowHandle> val = new IndexedDictionary<RowHandle>();
			IEnumerable<RowHandle> source = DataController.GetParents(handle, (RowHandle h) => GetParent(h), includeCurrent: false).ToList();
			int num = VisibleHandles.IndexOf(handle);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return new List<RowHandle>();
			}
			for (int num2 = num + 1; num2 < VisibleHandles.Count; num2++)
			{
				RowHandle rowHandle = VisibleHandles[num2];
				if (source.Contains(GetParent(rowHandle)))
				{
					break;
				}
				val.Add(rowHandle);
			}
			return (IList<RowHandle>)val;
		}

		private IList<RowHandle> GetCurrentVisibleHandles(RowHandle handle)
		{
			IndexedDictionary<RowHandle> resultHandles = new IndexedDictionary<RowHandle>();
			IEnumerable<RowHandle> children = GetChildren(handle);
			foreach (RowHandle item in children)
			{
				IList<RowHandle> source = GetVisibleHandles(item, cached: false);
				if (source.Any() || !IsGroupRowHandle(item) || !IsExpanded(item))
				{
					resultHandles.Add(item);
