					source.ForEach(delegate(RowHandle x)
					{
						resultHandles.Add(x);
					});
				}
			}
			return (IList<RowHandle>)resultHandles;
		}

		protected virtual Func<object, bool> CreatePredicate()
		{
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			return CriteriaCompiler.ToUntypedPredicate(CombinedFilterCriteria, (CriteriaCompilerDescriptor)(object)new PropertyGridCriteriaCompilerDescriptor(FilterClient.GetDefaultObject().GetType()), new CriteriaCompilerAuxSettings(IsKeySensitive));
		}

		public void InvalidateDataView()
		{
			IsDataViewValid = false;
			DataController.InvalidateClient(RowHandle.Root);
		}

		private void UpdateDataView(bool force = false)
		{
			if (!IsDataViewValid || force)
			{
				InvalidateFilter();
				ResetVisibleHandles();
				IsDataViewValid = true;
				SourceChangedReset();
			}
		}

		public void Invalidate(RowHandle handle)
		{
			DataController.Invalidate(handle);
		}

		public void Update()
		{
			if (!IsDataViewValid || !DataController.IsValid)
			{
				UpdateLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
				{
					SaveAndResetCurrentRow();
					DataController.Update();
					UpdateDataView();
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnUpdated()
		{
			UpdateCurrentRow();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCurrentRow()
		{
			if (!Locker.op_Implicit(UpdateLocker))
			{
				UpdateCurrentRowLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
				{
					CurrentRow = GetCurrentRow();
					CurrentRowPath = null;
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SaveAndResetCurrentRow()
		{
			if (CurrentRowInternal != RowHandle.Invalid && CurrentRowInternal != null)
			{
				CurrentRowPath = (from h in DataController.GetParents(CurrentRowInternal)
					select GetFieldNameByHandle(h)).ToArray();
			}
		}

		public bool IsExpanded(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (InSearchMode && IsLoadedProperty(handle))
			{
				return SearchIsExpanded(handle);
			}
			return DataController.IsExpanded(handle);
		}

		private void ResetSearchExpandedState()
		{
			searchExpandedState = new Dictionary<RowHandle, bool>();
		}

		public void ResetExpandedState()
		{
			DataController.ResetCache();
			DataController.Invalidate(RowHandle.Root);
		}

		private bool SearchIsExpanded(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!searchExpandedState.TryGetValue(handle, out var value))
			{
				value = true;
				searchExpandedState[handle] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		private void SearchSetIsExpanded(RowHandle handle, bool value)
		{
			bool? flag = DataController.IsExpanded(handle);
			if (SearchIsExpanded(handle) != value)
			{
				SourceChanging(this, new RowHandleChangedEventArgs
				{
					Handle = handle,
					ChangeType = ((!value) ? RowHandleChangeType.Collapse : RowHandleChangeType.Expand)
				});
				searchExpandedState[handle] = value;
				SourceChanged(this, new RowHandleChangedEventArgs
				{
					Handle = handle,
					ChangeType = ((!value) ? RowHandleChangeType.Collapse : RowHandleChangeType.Expand)
				});
				if (!flag.HasValue)
				{
					DataController.SetIsExpanded(handle, isExpanded: false);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return DataController.IsReadOnly(rowHandle);
		}

		public bool IsExactlyReadOnly(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return DataController.IsExactlyReadOnly(rowHandle);
		}

		public bool ShouldRenderReadOnly(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			return DataController.ShouldRenderReadOnly(rowHandle);
		}

		public IEnumerable<string> GetValidationError(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetValidationError(handle);
		}

		public Type GetPropertyType(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetPropertyType(handle);
		}

		public object GetSelectedCollectionNewItem(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetCollectionHelper(handle).Return((CollectionHelper x) => x.SelectedItem, () => (object)null);
		}

		public bool CanAddCollectionNewItem(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetCollectionHelper(handle).Return((CollectionHelper x) => x.CanAddNewItem, () => false);
		}

		public bool IsReadOnlyCollection(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetCollectionHelper(handle).Return((CollectionHelper x) => x.IsReadOnly, () => false);
		}

		public IEnumerable GetCollectionNewItemValues(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetCollectionHelper(handle).Return((CollectionHelper x) => x.NewItemValues, () => (IEnumerable)null);
		}

		public void AddCollectionNewItem(RowHandle handle, object item)
		{
			DataController.GetCollectionHelper(handle).Do(delegate(CollectionHelper x)
			{
				x.AddNewItem(item);
			});
		}

		public Exception SetValue(RowHandle handle, object value)
		{
			return DataController.SetValue(handle, value);
		}

		public bool CanResetValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.CanResetValue(handle);
		}

		public bool CanRemoveCollectionItem(RowHandle handle)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).With((DescriptorContext x) => x.ParentContext);
			if (descriptorContext == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return DataController.GetCollectionHelper(descriptorContext.RowHandle).Return((CollectionHelper x) => x.CanRemoveCollectionItem, () => false);
		}

		public void RemoveCollectionItem(RowHandle handle)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).With((DescriptorContext x) => x.ParentContext);
			if (descriptorContext != null)
			{
				DataController.GetCollectionHelper(descriptorContext.RowHandle).Do(delegate(CollectionHelper x)
				{
					x.RemoveCollectionItem(handle);
				});
			}
		}

		public Exception ResetValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.ResetValue(handle);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.ShouldSerializeValue(handle);
		}

		public void SetIsExpanded(RowHandle handle, bool isExpanded)
		{
			if (InSearchMode)
			{
				SearchSetIsExpanded(handle, isExpanded);
			}
			else
			{
				DataController.SetIsExpanded(handle, isExpanded);
			}
		}

		public bool CanUseCollectionEditor(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.CanUseCollectionEditor(handle);
		}

		internal bool IsCollectionHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.IsCollectionHandle(handle);
		}

		public bool IsGroupRowHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.IsGroupRowHandle(handle);
		}

		public bool IsNewInstanceInitializer(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.IsNewInstanceInitializer(handle);
		}

		public IEnumerable GetStandardValues(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetStandardValues(handle);
		}

		public IEnumerable GetTypeInfos(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetTypeInfos(handle);
		}

		public bool GetIsStandardValuesSupported(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetIsStandardValuesSupported(handle);
		}

		public bool GetIsStandardValuesExclusive(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetIsStandardValuesExclusive(handle);
		}

		public bool CanConvertToType(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.CanConvertToType(handle);
		}

		public object ConvertToType(RowHandle handle, object value)
		{
			object result = null;
			try
			{
				result = DataController.ConvertToType(handle, value);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string TryConvertToString(RowHandle handle, object value)
		{
			string result = null;
			try
			{
				result = (DataController.CanConvertToString(handle) ? DataController.ConvertToString(handle, value) : Convert.ToString(value));
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool CanExpand(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (DataController.CanExpand(handle))
			{
				if (!IsLoadedProperty(handle))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return GetChildren(handle).Any();
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object GetValue(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return dataController.GetValue(handle);
		}

		public string GetNameByHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == RowHandle.Invalid)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return dataController.GetNameByHandle(handle);
		}

		public string GetFieldNameByHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == RowHandle.Invalid || handle == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (handle == RowHandle.Root)
			{
				return "";
			}
			return dataController.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle);
		}

		public RowHandle GetHandleByFieldName(string fieldName)
		{
			if (fieldName == null)
			{
				return RowHandle.Invalid;
			}
			return dataController.GetHandleByFieldName(fieldName).Return((RowHandle x) => x, () => RowHandle.Invalid);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptor GetDescriptor(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return dataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).With((DescriptorContext x) => x.PropertyDescriptor);
		}

		public IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetChildren(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!ChildrenCache.TryGetValue(handle, out var value))
			{
				IEnumerable<RowHandle> children = (CanFilter ? GetFilteredChildren(handle) : GetChildrenInternal(handle));
				value = SortClient.GetSortedChildren(handle, children).ToList();
				ChildrenCache[handle] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public string GetDescription(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetDescription(handle);
		}

		public string GetDisplayName(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetDisplayName(handle);
		}

		public virtual RowHandle GetParent(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == null || handle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return DataController.GetParent(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.RowHandle, () => (RowHandle)null);
		}

		public bool IsAttachedProperty(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.IsAttachedProperty(handle);
		}

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, IEnumerable<RowHandle>> CreateFilterCache()
		{
			return new Dictionary<RowHandle, IEnumerable<RowHandle>>();
		}

		private CriteriaOperator GetFilterCriteria()
		{
			if (InSearchMode && InFilterMode)
			{
				return CriteriaOperator.And(SearchCriteria, FilterCriteria);
			}
			if (InSearchMode)
			{
				return SearchCriteria;
			}
			return FilterCriteria;
		}

		private void FilterCriteriaChanged()
		{
			InvalidateDataView();
			ResetSearchExpandedState();
		}

		protected void RaiseVisibleHandlesChanged(Func<NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs> getArgs)
		{
			this.VisibleHandlesChanged.Do(delegate(NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler h)
			{
				h(this, getArgs());
			});
		}

		protected void RaiseInvisibleHandlesChanged(Func<NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs> getArgs)
		{
			this.InvisibleHandlesChanged.Do(delegate(NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler h)
			{
				h(this, getArgs());
			});
		}

		private void InvalidateFilter()
		{
			ResetChildrenCache();
			predicate = null;
			combinedFilterCriteria = null;
		}

		private void ResetChildrenCache()
		{
			shouldResetVisibleHandlesCache = false;
			childrenCache = null;
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetFilteredChildren(RowHandle handle)
		{
			IEnumerable<RowHandle> enumerable = GetChildrenInternal(handle) ?? Enumerable.Empty<RowHandle>();
			List<RowHandle> list = new List<RowHandle>();
			foreach (RowHandle item in enumerable)
			{
				if (IsVisible(item))
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
				else if (FilterClient.GetIsVisible(item) && HasVisibleChildren(item))
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private bool HasVisibleChildren(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!IsLoadedProperty(handle))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return GetChildren(handle).Any();
		}

		private bool IsLoadedProperty(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (InSearchMode)
			{
				if (!searchExpandedState.ContainsKey(handle))
				{
					return DataController.IsLoaded(handle);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return DataController.IsLoaded(handle);
		}

		private bool IsVisible(RowHandle handle)
		{
			try
			{
				return Predicate(FilterClient.GetObject(handle));
			}
			catch
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetAllChildren(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.AllChildHandles, () => Enumerable.Empty<RowHandle>());
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetChildrenInternal(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (!handle.IsRoot)
			{
				return DataController.GetChildHandles(handle);
			}
			return GetRootRowHandlesInternal();
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<RowHandle> GetRootRowHandlesInternal()
		{
			return DataController.GetChildHandles(RowHandle.Root).ToList();
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			FilterClient = null;
			SortClient = null;
			if (DataController != null)
			{
				DataController.SourceChanged -= SourceChanged;
				DataController.SourceChanging -= SourceChanging;
				DataController.AddingToInvalidateList -= AddingToInvalidateList;
			}
			dataController = null;
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridCriteriaCompilerDescriptor : CriteriaCompilerDescriptor
	{
		private Type type;

		public override Type ObjectType => type;

		public PropertyGridCriteriaCompilerDescriptor(Type type)
		{
			this.type = type;
		}

		public override System.Linq.Expressions.Expression MakePropertyAccess(System.Linq.Expressions.Expression baseExpression, string propertyPath)
		{
			return System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Property(baseExpression, propertyPath);
		}
	}
	public abstract class DescriptorContext : ITypeDescriptorContext, IServiceProvider
	{
		protected static readonly TypeConverter NullConverter = new TypeConverter();

		private static Func<TypeDescriptionProvider, TypeDescriptionProvider> GetProvider;

		private object multiInstance;

		private object value;

		private bool hasValue;

		private RowHandle rowHandle;

		private AttributeCollection propertyAttributes;

		private AttributeCollection typeAttributes;

		private Dictionary<object, PropertyDescriptorCollection> properties;

		private HybridDictionary forcedProperties;

		private PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor;

		private IEnumerable<RowHandle> forcedChildHandles;

		protected IEnumerable<RowHandle> visibleHandles;

		private IEnumerable<RowHandle> categoryHandles;

		private bool? isGetPropertiesSupported;

		private bool? shouldSerializeValue;

		private bool? getCreateInstanceSupported;

		private bool? isExpanded;

		private bool childrenInitialized;

		private TypeConverter converter;

		private TypeConverter baseConverter;

		private bool isValid = true;

		private ICollection standardValues;

		private ICollection typeInfos;

		private bool? isStandardValuesSupported;

		private bool? isStandardValuesExclusive;

		private string displayName;

		private Lazy<IEdmPropertyInfo> edmPropertyInfo;

		private Lazy<string> categoryName;

		private Lazy<IEnumerable<string>> validationError;

		private Lazy<bool> supportDataAnnotation;

		private Lazy<bool> canResetValue;

		private Lazy<Type> convertToType;

		private Lazy<IDataErrorInfo> dataErrorInfo;

		private CollectionHelper collectionHelper;

		private Lazy<DictionaryWrapper> dictionaryWrapper;

		private Lazy<CollectionWrapper> collectionWrapper;

		private Lazy<IInstanceInitializerImplementer> instanceInitializer;

		private Lazy<IPropertyValidatorImplementer> propertyValidator;

		private Lazy<object> propertyOwner;

		private Lazy<bool> treatAsValueType;

		private bool? isValueEditableCore;

		private bool? forceReadOnly;

		private bool? readOnlyEditable;

		private PropertyDescriptorValueChangedEventHandler handler;

		private DescriptorContext parentContext;

		private bool? isMultiSource;

		private Locker visibleHandlesLocker = new Locker();

		public DescriptorContext ParentContext
		{
			get
			{
				return parentContext;
			}
			internal set
			{
				parentContext = value;
				ParentContextChanged();
			}
		}

		public bool CanResetValue => canResetValue.Value;

		public object PropertyOwner => propertyOwner.Value;

		public AttributeCollection PropertyAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				if (propertyAttributes == null)
				{
					propertyAttributes = PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Attributes, null);
				}
				return propertyAttributes;
			}
		}

		public AttributeCollection TypeAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				if (typeAttributes == null)
				{
					typeAttributes = TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(PropertyType);
				}
				return typeAttributes;
			}
		}

		public virtual object MultiInstance => multiInstance;

		public virtual object Instance
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsMultiSource)
				{
					return MultiInstance;
				}
				return GetInstanceFromMultiInstance();
			}
		}

		public CollectionHelper CollectionHelper
		{
			get
			{
				if (collectionHelper == null)
				{
					collectionHelper = GetCollectionHelper();
				}
				return collectionHelper;
			}
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptor PropertyDescriptor
		{
			get
			{
				return propertyDescriptor;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (propertyDescriptor != null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Try to change PropertyDescriptor");
				}
				propertyDescriptor = value;
				PropertyDescriptorChanged();
			}
		}

		public bool FirstExpanded => isExpanded.HasValue;

		public virtual bool IsExpanded => IsExpandedInternal;

		protected bool IsExpandedInternal
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isExpanded.HasValue)
				{
					if (DataController == null)
					{
						isExpanded = false;
					}
					else
					{
						isExpanded = DataController.VisualClient.IsExpanded(RowHandle, () => DataController.expandedState.TryGetValue(FieldName, out var flag) ? new bool?(flag) : ((bool?)null));
					}
				}
				return isExpanded.Value;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CanExpand
		{
			get
			{
				if (Value == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!IsGetPropertiesSupported)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return ChildHandles.Any();
			}
		}

		public RowHandle RowHandle
		{
			get
			{
				return rowHandle;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (rowHandle != value)
				{
					rowHandle = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal TypeInfoImplementer NewListItemValue { get; set; }

		public string FieldName { get; set; }

		public bool TreatAsValueType => treatAsValueType.Value;

		public virtual string CategoryName => categoryName.Value;

		public virtual IPropertyValidatorImplementer EdmValidator => propertyValidator.Value;

		public IEnumerable<string> ValidationError => validationError.Value;

		public virtual string Name => PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Name, () => "");

		public virtual string DisplayName
		{
			get
			{
				if (displayName == null)
				{
					displayName = GetDisplayName();
				}
				return displayName;
			}
		}

		public IInstanceInitializerImplementer InstanceInitializer => instanceInitializer.Value;

		public virtual bool IsLoaded
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsExpanded)
				{
					return visibleHandles != null;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<RowHandle> ChildHandles
		{
			get
			{
				if (visibleHandles == null)
				{
					visibleHandles = CreateChildHandles(() => GetProperties(DataController.BrowsableAttributes).Return((PropertyDescriptorCollection x) => x.Cast<PropertyDescriptor>(), () => Enumerable.Empty<PropertyDescriptor>()), visibleHandlesLocker);
					VisibleHandlesCreated();
				}
				return visibleHandles;
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<RowHandle> ForcedChildHandles
		{
			get
			{
				if (forcedChildHandles == null)
				{
					forcedChildHandles = CreateChildHandles(() => GetForcedProperties());
				}
				return forcedChildHandles;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<RowHandle> AllChildHandles => ChildHandles.Union(ForcedChildHandles);

		public IEnumerable<RowHandle> CategoryHandles
		{
			get
			{
				if (categoryHandles == null)
				{
					categoryHandles = CreateCategoryHandles();
				}
				return categoryHandles;
			}
		}

		public bool IsValid => isValid;

		public bool IsStandardValuesExclusive
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isStandardValuesExclusive.HasValue)
				{
					isStandardValuesExclusive = GetStandardValuesExclusive();
				}
				return isStandardValuesExclusive.Value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsStandardValuesSupported
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isStandardValuesSupported.HasValue)
				{
					isStandardValuesSupported = GetStandardValuesSupported();
				}
				return isStandardValuesSupported.Value;
			}
		}

		public ICollection StandardValues
		{
			get
			{
				if (standardValues == null)
				{
					standardValues = GetStandardValues();
				}
				return standardValues;
			}
		}

		public ICollection TypeInfos
		{
			get
			{
				if (typeInfos == null)
				{
					typeInfos = GetTypeInfos();
				}
				return typeInfos;
			}
		}

		protected internal IServiceProvider ServiceProvider { get; set; }

		public bool IsMultiSource
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isMultiSource.HasValue)
				{
					isMultiSource = GetIsMultiSource();
				}
				return isMultiSource.Value;
			}
		}

		internal DataController DataController => ServiceProvider as DataController;

		protected IEdmPropertyInfo AssignedEdmPropertyInfo { get; set; }

		public IEdmPropertyInfo EdmPropertyInfo => edmPropertyInfo.Value;

		private bool IsDefaultTypeConverter
		{
			get
			{
				if (BaseConverter != null)
				{
					return BaseConverter.GetType() == typeof(TypeConverter);
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public virtual AllowExpandingMode DefaultAllowExpanding => AllowExpandingMode.Default;

		public AllowExpandingMode AllowExpanding => DataController.Return(delegate(DataController x)
		{
			AllowExpandingMode? allowExpandingMode = x.VisualClient.GetAllowExpandingMode(RowHandle);
			return allowExpandingMode.HasValue ? allowExpandingMode.Value : DefaultAllowExpanding;
		}, () => DefaultAllowExpanding);

		public virtual bool IsGetPropertiesSupported
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isGetPropertiesSupported.HasValue)
				{
					isGetPropertiesSupported = GetIsGetPropertiesSupported();
				}
				return isGetPropertiesSupported.Value;
			}
		}

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (!hasValue)
				{
					hasValue = true;
					value = GetValue();
					SubscribeNotifications();
				}
				return value;
			}
		}

		protected virtual object ValueForExtractProperties => Value;

		public TypeConverter Converter
		{
			get
			{
				if (converter == null)
				{
					converter = GetConverter();
				}
				return converter;
			}
		}

		public TypeConverter BaseConverter
		{
			get
			{
				if (baseConverter == null)
				{
					baseConverter = GetBaseConverter();
				}
				return baseConverter;
			}
		}

		public bool GetCreateInstanceSupported
		{
			get
			{
				if (!getCreateInstanceSupported.HasValue)
				{
					getCreateInstanceSupported = GetGetCreateInstanceSupported();
				}
				return getCreateInstanceSupported.Value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsNewInstanceInitializer => Converter is NewInstanceConverter;

		public bool IsCustomTypeDescriptor
		{
			get
			{
				if (Value == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (Value is ICustomTypeDescriptor)
				{
					return true;
				}
				TypeDescriptionProvider provider = TypeDescriptor.GetProvider(Value);
				if (GetProvider == null)
				{
					GetProvider = ReflectionHelper.CreateFieldGetter<TypeDescriptionProvider, TypeDescriptionProvider>(provider.GetType(), "Provider", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
				}
				TypeDescriptionProvider typeDescriptionProvider = GetProvider(provider);
				ICustomTypeDescriptor typeDescriptor = typeDescriptionProvider.GetTypeDescriptor(Value);
				if (typeDescriptor != null)
				{
					return typeDescriptor.GetType().Assembly != typeof(TypeDescriptor).Assembly;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsICollection
		{
			get
			{
				if (CollectionWrapper != null && !IsIDictionary)
				{
					return !IsIList;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsIList
		{
			get
			{
				if (Value == null || !(Value is IList))
				{
					return typeof(IList).IsAssignableFrom(PropertyType);
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool IsIDictionary => DictionaryWrapper != null;

		public CollectionWrapper CollectionWrapper => collectionWrapper.Value;

		public DictionaryWrapper DictionaryWrapper => dictionaryWrapper.Value;

		public bool ShouldSerializeValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!shouldSerializeValue.HasValue)
				{
					shouldSerializeValue = GetShouldSerializeValue();
				}
				return shouldSerializeValue.Value;
			}
		}

		private Dictionary<object, PropertyDescriptorCollection> Properties
		{
			get
			{
				if (properties == null)
				{
					properties = new Dictionary<object, PropertyDescriptorCollection>();
				}
				return properties;
			}
		}

		private HybridDictionary ForcedProperties
		{
			get
			{
				if (forcedProperties == null)
				{
					forcedProperties = new HybridDictionary();
				}
				return forcedProperties;
			}
		}

		private bool IsSerializeContentsProp
		{
			get
			{
				if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return PropertyDescriptor.SerializationVisibility == DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool ShouldRenderReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (ForceReadOnly)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (IsPropertyReadOnly && ReadOnlyEditable && PropertyType != null && (PropertyType.IsArray || PropertyType.IsPrimitive || PropertyType.IsValueType))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (ShouldRenderReadOnlyCore && !IsSerializeContentsProp)
				{
					return !NeedsCustomEditorButton;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool NeedsDropDownButton
		{
			get
			{
				if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (GetEditStyle(this) != UITypeEditorEditStyle.DropDown)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private bool NeedsCustomEditorButtonCore
		{
			get
			{
				if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return GetEditStyle(this) == UITypeEditorEditStyle.Modal;
			}
		}

		internal bool NeedsCustomEditorButton
		{
			get
			{
				if (!NeedsCustomEditorButtonCore)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!IsValueEditable)
				{
					return !ReadOnlyEditable;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldRenderReadOnlyCore
		{
			get
			{
				if (ForceReadOnly)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (!IsValueEditable)
				{
					return !ReadOnlyEditable;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsPropertyReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				ReadOnlyAttribute readOnlyAttribute = (ReadOnlyAttribute)PropertyAttributes.With((AttributeCollection x) => x[typeof(ReadOnlyAttribute)]);
				if (EdmPropertyInfo != null && EdmPropertyInfo.Attributes.IsReadOnly.HasValue && (readOnlyAttribute == null || (readOnlyAttribute != null && EdmPropertyInfo.Attributes.IsReadOnly.Value != readOnlyAttribute.IsReadOnly)))
				{
					return EdmPropertyInfo.Attributes.IsReadOnly.Value;
				}
				return IsPropertyReadOnlyCore;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual bool IsPropertyReadOnlyCore
		{
			get
			{
				if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return PropertyDescriptor.IsReadOnly;
			}
		}

		public bool IsValueEditable
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsPropertyReadOnly)
				{
					return IsValueEditableCore;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsValueEditableCore
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isValueEditableCore.HasValue)
				{
					isValueEditableCore = GetIsValueEditableCore();
				}
				return isValueEditableCore.Value;
			}
		}

		private bool IsTextEditable
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsValueEditable)
				{
					return IsTextEditableCore;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsTextEditableCore
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Converter.CanConvertFrom(this, typeof(string)) || Converter.GetStandardValuesExclusive(this))
				{
					return PropertyType.IsAssignableFrom(typeof(object));
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private bool IsEnumerableCore => Converter.GetStandardValuesSupported(this);

		private bool IsEnumerable
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsEnumerableCore)
				{
					return !IsPropertyReadOnly;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsImmutableObject => TypeAttributes[typeof(ImmutableObjectAttribute)] == ImmutableObjectAttribute.Yes;

		private bool ForceReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (!forceReadOnly.HasValue)
				{
					forceReadOnly = GetForceReadOnly();
				}
				return forceReadOnly.Value;
			}
		}

		private bool ReadOnlyEditable
		{
			get
			{
				if (!readOnlyEditable.HasValue)
				{
					readOnlyEditable = GetReadOnlyEditable();
				}
				return readOnlyEditable.Value;
			}
		}

		public Type PropertyType => PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.PropertyType, null);

		public IContainer Container => null;

		PropertyDescriptor ITypeDescriptorContext.PropertyDescriptor => PropertyDescriptor;

		public Type ConvertToType => convertToType.Value;

		private bool SupportDataAnnotations => supportDataAnnotation.Value;

		private IDataErrorInfo DataErrorInfo => dataErrorInfo.Value;

		public DescriptorContext(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ResetCore();
			ServiceProvider = serviceProvider;
		}

		~DescriptorContext()
		{
			UnsubscribeValueChanged();
		}

		internal void UnsubscribeValueChanged()
		{
			if (handler != null && PropertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				PropertyDescriptor.RemoveValueChanged(PropertyOwner, ((WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler>)handler).Handler);
			}
		}

		internal void SubscribeValueChanged()
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				handler = new PropertyDescriptorValueChangedEventHandler(this, delegate(DescriptorContext d, object o, EventArgs e)
				{
					d.DataController.Invalidate(d.RowHandle);
				});
			}
			PropertyDescriptor.AddValueChanged(PropertyOwner, ((WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, EventArgs, EventHandler>)handler).Handler);
		}

		protected virtual void ParentContextChanged()
		{
			ParentContext.Do(delegate(DescriptorContext x)
			{
				x.AddChildHandle(this);
			});
		}

		protected void AddChildHandle(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			visibleHandlesLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = context.PropertyDescriptor;
				if (propertyDescriptor != null && !ForcedProperties.Contains(propertyDescriptor))
				{
					ForcedProperties.Add(propertyDescriptor, null);
					InvalidateForcedProperties();
				}
			});
		}

		private void InvalidateForcedProperties()
		{
			forcedChildHandles = null;
			childrenInitialized = false;
		}

		protected internal virtual void ContextAdded()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void MultiInstanceChanged()
		{
		}

		private object GetInstanceFromMultiInstance()
		{
			return MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(MultiInstance as IEnumerable).Return((IEnumerable<object> x) => x.FirstOrDefault(), () => (object)null);
		}

		private CollectionHelper GetCollectionHelper()
		{
			if (Converter is ListConverter)
			{
				return new CollectionHelper(this);
			}
			return NullCollectionHelper.Instance;
		}

		protected virtual void VisibleHandlesCreated()
		{
			SetIsExpandedInternal(IsExpanded);
		}

		protected virtual bool GetIsMultiSource()
		{
			return PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x is MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor, () => DataController.IsMultiSource);
		}

		protected internal virtual void SetIsExpandedInternal(bool? isExpanded)
		{
			this.isExpanded = isExpanded;
		}

		public bool SetIsExpanded(bool isExpanded)
		{
			bool result = IsExpanded != isExpanded;
			if (!CanExpand)
			{
				return false;
			}
			SetIsExpandedInternal(isExpanded);
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual string GetCategoryName()
		{
			string result = EdmPropertyInfo.Return((IEdmPropertyInfo x) => x.Attributes.GroupName, () => (string)null);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(result))
			{
				return result;
			}
			return PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Category, () => (string)null);
		}

		protected virtual bool GetStandardValuesExclusive()
		{
			return Converter.Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValuesExclusive(this), () => false);
		}

		protected virtual bool GetStandardValuesSupported()
		{
			return Converter.Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValuesSupported(this), () => false);
		}

		protected virtual ICollection GetStandardValues()
		{
			if (IsStandardValuesSupported)
			{
				return Converter.Return((Func<TypeConverter, ICollection>)((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValues(this)), (Func<ICollection>)(() => new List<object>()));
			}
			return new List<object>();
		}

		protected virtual ICollection GetTypeInfos()
		{
			if (IsNewInstanceInitializer)
			{
				return (Converter as NewInstanceConverter).GetTypeInfos(this);
			}
			return new List<object>();
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<RowHandle> CreateCategoryHandles()
		{
			if (FieldName != "")
			{
				return new List<RowHandle>();
			}
			IList<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (RowHandle childHandle in ChildHandles)
			{
				string item = GetContext(childHandle).CategoryName;
				if (!list.Contains(item))
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			IList<RowHandle> list2 = new List<RowHandle>();
			foreach (string item2 in list)
			{
				RowHandle rowHandle = CreateCategory(item2);
				DescriptorContext descriptorContext = CreateGroupDescriptorContext(MultiInstance, item2, FieldName);
				descriptorContext.RowHandle = rowHandle;
				descriptorContext.ParentContext = this;
				DataController.ContextCache[descriptorContext.FieldName] = descriptorContext;
				DataController.ContextCache[rowHandle] = descriptorContext;
				descriptorContext.ContextAdded();
				list2.Add(rowHandle);
			}
			return list2;
		}

		protected virtual DescriptorContext CreateGroupDescriptorContext(object component, string categoryName, string fieldName)
		{
			return new GroupDescriptorContext(component, categoryName, ServiceProvider, fieldName);
		}

		private void PropertyDescriptorChanged()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor != null && PropertyDescriptor.SupportsChangeEvents)
			{
				SubscribeValueChanged();
			}
		}

		private RowHandle CreateCategory(string categoryName)
		{
			return new RowHandle();
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<RowHandle> CreateChildHandles(Func<IEnumerable<PropertyDescriptor>> getProperties, Locker locker = null)
		{
			if (DataController == null || ValueForExtractProperties == null)
			{
				return Enumerable.Empty<RowHandle>();
			}
			IEnumerable<PropertyDescriptor> enumerable = getProperties().Return((IEnumerable<PropertyDescriptor> x) => x.ToList(), () => Enumerable.Empty<PropertyDescriptor>());
			return (from edm in DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetEdmProperties(enumerable, ValueForExtractProperties.GetType(), DataController.MetadataLocator, locker != null)
				select AddProperty(edm, locker) into h
				where h != null
				select h).ToList();
		}

		private RowHandle AddProperty(IEdmPropertyInfo edmPropertyInfo, Locker locker = null)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor descriptor = (PropertyDescriptor)edmPropertyInfo.ContextObject;
			if (DataController.VisualClient.Helper.HideProperty(edmPropertyInfo, DataController.ShowAttachedProperties))
			{
				return null;
			}
			DescriptorContext context = null;
			Action action = delegate
			{
				context = DataController.GetDescriptorContext(this, descriptor);
				if (IsMultiSource && context != null && context.PropertyDescriptor == null)
				{
					context = null;
				}
			};
			locker.Do(delegate(Locker x)
			{
				x.DoLockedAction(action);
			}).DoIfNull<Locker>(delegate
			{
				action();
			});
			return context.Do(delegate(DescriptorContext x)
			{
				x.AssignedEdmPropertyInfo = edmPropertyInfo;
			}).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.RowHandle, () => (RowHandle)null);
		}

		protected internal virtual string GetFieldNameForChild()
		{
			return FieldName;
		}

		protected virtual PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return GetProperties(Value, attributes);
		}

		public virtual IInstanceInitializerImplementer GetInstanceInitializer()
		{
			IInstanceInitializerImplementer instanceInitializerImplementer = DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetInstanceInitializer(EdmPropertyInfo);
			if (instanceInitializerImplementer != null)
			{
				return instanceInitializerImplementer;
			}
			return DataController.VisualClient.GetInstanceInitializer(RowHandle);
		}

		public RowHandle SetValue(object value)
		{
			object obj = TryConvertFromDifferentType(value);
			DoValidate(obj);
			BeforeSetValue(value, obj);
			return SetValueInternal(obj);
		}

		protected virtual void DoValidate(object settingValue)
		{
			if (!DataController.VisualClient.AllowCommitOnValidationAttributeError() && SupportDataAnnotations)
			{
				string errorText = EdmValidator.GetErrorText(settingValue, Instance);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(errorText))
				{
					throw new Exception(errorText);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void BeforeSetValue(object originalValue, object convertedValue)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual RowHandle SetValueInternal(object value)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor.Do(delegate(PropertyDescriptor x)
			{
				x.SetValue(PropertyOwner, value);
			});
			if (TreatAsValueType)
			{
				return ParentContext.SetValueInternal(Instance);
			}
			return RowHandle;
		}

		private object TryConvertFromDifferentType(object value)
		{
			if (Convert.IsDBNull(value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				if (!PropertyDescriptorHelper.IsTableView(PropertyDescriptor))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return DBNull.Value;
			}
			object result = value;
			Type type = value.GetType();
			if (type != PropertyType || (ConvertToType != null && type == ConvertToType))
			{
				if (Converter.CanConvertFrom(this, type))
				{
					result = Converter.ConvertFrom(this, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, value);
				}
				else if (!PropertyType.IsAssignableFrom(type) && (type.IsPrimitive || value is IConvertible))
				{
					Type propertyType = PropertyType;
					Type underlyingType = GetUnderlyingType(propertyType);
					result = Convert.ChangeType(value, (underlyingType != null) ? underlyingType : propertyType, null);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual bool GetCanResetValue()
		{
			if (IsPropertyReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return PropertyDescriptor.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.CanResetValue(PropertyOwner), () => false);
		}

		public virtual RowHandle ResetValue()
		{
			PropertyDescriptor.Do(delegate(PropertyDescriptor x)
			{
				x.ResetValue(PropertyOwner);
			});
			return RowHandle;
		}

		protected virtual TypeConverter GetBaseConverter()
		{
			if (EdmPropertyInfo == null && PropertyType == null && Value == null)
			{
				return NullConverter;
			}
			return DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetConverter(EdmPropertyInfo, (PropertyType != null) ? PropertyType : Value.GetType(), ExtractConverter()).Return((TypeConverter x) => x, () => NullConverter);
		}

		private TypeConverter ExtractConverter()
		{
			TypeConverter typeConverter = null;
			if (PropertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				typeConverter = PropertyDescriptor.Converter;
			}
			if (typeConverter != null && typeConverter.GetType() != typeof(TypeConverter))
			{
				return typeConverter;
			}
			if (Value != null)
			{
				typeConverter = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(Value);
			}
			if (typeConverter != null && typeConverter.GetType() != typeof(TypeConverter))
			{
				return typeConverter;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public TypeConverter GetConverter()
		{
			TypeConverter converterInternal = GetConverterInternal();
			if (converterInternal != null)
			{
				return converterInternal;
			}
			return BaseConverter;
		}

		protected virtual TypeConverter GetConverterInternal()
		{
			if (DataController == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			bool flag = CanUseInstanceInitializer();
			bool? flag2 = ((!flag) ? new bool?(false) : DataController.VisualClient.AllowInstanceInitializer(RowHandle));
			if (CanUseCollectionEditor() && DataController.VisualClient.AllowCollectionEditor(RowHandle) && Value != null && !IsCustomTypeDescriptor)
			{
				return new ListConverter(flag && (flag2 ?? false));
			}
			if (flag && (flag2 ?? true))
			{
				return DataController.NewInstanceConverter;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public bool CanUseCollectionEditor()
		{
			if (ListConverter.IsNewItemProperty(PropertyDescriptor))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!IsICollection && !IsIList)
			{
				return IsIDictionary;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool CanUseInstanceInitializer()
		{
			if (ListConverter.IsItemProperty(PropertyDescriptor) || IsPropertyReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return DataController.NewInstanceConverter.GetTypeInfos(this).If((TypeConverter.StandardValuesCollection x) => x.Count > 0).ReturnSuccess();
		}

		protected virtual PropertyDescriptorCollection GetPropertiesInternal(object source, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			if (!IsGetPropertiesSupported)
			{
				return null;
			}
			try
			{
				PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptorCollection = Converter.GetProperties(this, source, attributes);
				AllowExpandingMode? allowExpandingMode = AllowExpanding;
				if (allowExpandingMode.HasValue && (allowExpandingMode.Value == AllowExpandingMode.Force || allowExpandingMode.Value == AllowExpandingMode.ForceIfNoTypeConverter) && (propertyDescriptorCollection == null || propertyDescriptorCollection.Count == 0) && CollectionHelper == NullCollectionHelper.Instance)
				{
					propertyDescriptorCollection = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(source, attributes, noCustomTypeDesc: false);
				}
				return propertyDescriptorCollection;
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		public PropertyDescriptor GetProperty(object source, Attribute[] attributes, string propertyName, bool onlyBrowsable = false)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptorCollection = GetProperties(source, attributes);
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = null;
			if (propertyDescriptorCollection != null)
			{
				propertyDescriptor = propertyDescriptorCollection.Find(propertyName, ignoreCase: false);
			}
			if (propertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				return propertyDescriptor;
			}
			if (onlyBrowsable)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(source, null, noCustomTypeDesc: true).Return((PropertyDescriptorCollection x) => x.Find(propertyName, ignoreCase: false), () => (PropertyDescriptor)null);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(object source, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			if (source == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			PropertyDescriptorCollection result = null;
			if (!Properties.TryGetValue(source, out result))
			{
				result = GetPropertiesInternal(source, attributes);
				Properties[source] = result;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private IEnumerable<PropertyDescriptor> GetForcedProperties()
		{
			return ForcedProperties.Keys.Cast<PropertyDescriptor>();
		}

		private bool GetIsValueEditableCore()
		{
			if (!ForceReadOnly)
			{
				if (!IsTextEditableCore)
				{
					return IsEnumerable;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool GetForceReadOnly()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (MultiInstance == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ((ReadOnlyAttribute)TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(MultiInstance)[typeof(ReadOnlyAttribute)])?.IsReadOnly ?? false;
		}

		private bool GetReadOnlyEditable()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!CanExpand)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!ForceReadOnly && !IsTextEditable)
			{
				return !IsImmutableObject;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private UITypeEditorEditStyle GetEditStyle(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			return UITypeEditorEditStyle.None;
		}

		protected virtual bool GetShouldSerializeValue()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor == null || PropertyOwner == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return PropertyDescriptor.ShouldSerializeValue(PropertyOwner);
		}

		private string GetDisplayName()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return "";
			}
			string text = EdmPropertyInfo.With((IEdmPropertyInfo x) => x.DisplayName);
			if (NeedParenthesize(PropertyDescriptor))
			{
				return ParenthesizeCaption(text);
			}
			return text;
		}

		private bool NeedParenthesize(PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor)
		{
			return ((ParenthesizePropertyNameAttribute)propertyDescriptor.Attributes[typeof(ParenthesizePropertyNameAttribute)]).NeedParenthesis;
		}

		private string ParenthesizeCaption(string caption)
		{
			return $"({caption})";
		}

		private void SubscribeNotificationsInternal(object value)
		{
			(value as INotifyPropertyChanged).Do(delegate(INotifyPropertyChanged x)
			{
				x.PropertyChanged += ((WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, PropertyChangedEventArgs, PropertyChangedEventHandler>)new INotifyPropertyChangedEventHandler(this, delegate(DescriptorContext d, object o, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.PropertyName))
					{
						d.DataController.Invalidate(d.RowHandle);
					}
					else
					{
						string fieldName = FieldNameHelper.GetFieldName(d.GetFieldNameForChild(), e.PropertyName);
						DescriptorContext descriptorContext = d.DataController.ContextCache[fieldName];
						if (descriptorContext != null && descriptorContext.PropertyDescriptor != null)
						{
							d.DataController.Invalidate(descriptorContext.RowHandle);
						}
					}
				})).Handler;
			});
			(value as INotifyCollectionChanged).Do(delegate(INotifyCollectionChanged x)
			{
				x.CollectionChanged += ((WeakEventHandler<DescriptorContext, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler>)new INotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler(this, delegate(DescriptorContext d, object o, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
				{
					d.DataController.Invalidate(d.RowHandle);
				})).Handler;
			});
		}

		protected virtual void SubscribeNotifications()
		{
			if (IsMultiSource)
			{
				GetMultiValues().ForEach(delegate(object value)
				{
					SubscribeNotificationsInternal(value);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				SubscribeNotificationsInternal(Value);
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<object> GetOwners()
		{
			return PropertyOwner as IEnumerable<object>;
		}

		protected internal virtual IEnumerable<object> GetMultiValues()
		{
			if (!IsMultiSource)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return (PropertyDescriptor as MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor).Return((MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor x) => x.GetValues(GetOwners()), () => GetOwners());
		}

		protected virtual object GetValue()
		{
			object result = null;
			if (PropertyOwner == null || PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return result;
			}
			try
			{
				return PropertyDescriptor.GetValue(PropertyOwner);
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				return GetUnwindedException(e).Message;
			}
		}

		protected bool GetIsGetPropertiesSupported()
		{
			if (DataController != null)
			{
				switch (AllowExpanding)
				{
				case AllowExpandingMode.Force:
					return true;
				case AllowExpandingMode.Never:
					return false;
				case AllowExpandingMode.ForceIfNoTypeConverter:
					if (IsDefaultTypeConverter)
					{
						return true;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			return Converter.Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetPropertiesSupported(this), () => false);
		}

		protected bool GetGetCreateInstanceSupported()
		{
			return Converter.Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetCreateInstanceSupported(this), () => false);
		}

		internal void InvalidateChildren()
		{
			isValid = false;
		}

		private DescriptorContext GetContext(RowHandle rowhandle)
		{
			return DataController.ContextCache[rowhandle];
		}

		public void OnComponentChanged()
		{
		}

		public bool OnComponentChanging()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private T GetService<T>()
		{
			return (T)GetService(typeof(T));
		}

		public object GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			return ServiceProvider.GetService(serviceType);
		}

		protected virtual void ResetCore()
		{
			isMultiSource = null;
			childrenInitialized = false;
			hasValue = false;
			properties = null;
			forcedProperties = null;
			categoryHandles = null;
			forcedChildHandles = null;
			visibleHandles = null;
			isGetPropertiesSupported = null;
			converter = null;
			baseConverter = null;
			getCreateInstanceSupported = null;
			shouldSerializeValue = null;
			isValid = true;
			standardValues = null;
			typeInfos = null;
			isStandardValuesSupported = null;
			isStandardValuesExclusive = null;
			propertyAttributes = null;
			typeAttributes = null;
			displayName = null;
			collectionHelper = null;
			categoryName = new Lazy<string>(() => GetCategoryName());
			validationError = new Lazy<IEnumerable<string>>(() => GetValidationError());
			supportDataAnnotation = new Lazy<bool>(() => GetSupportDataAnnotation());
			dataErrorInfo = new Lazy<IDataErrorInfo>(() => GetIDataErrorInfo());
			instanceInitializer = new Lazy<IInstanceInitializerImplementer>(() => GetInstanceInitializer());
			propertyValidator = new Lazy<IPropertyValidatorImplementer>(() => GetEdmValidator());
			canResetValue = new Lazy<bool>(() => GetCanResetValue());
			propertyOwner = new Lazy<object>(() => GetPropertyOwner());
			properties = null;
			isValueEditableCore = null;
			forceReadOnly = null;
			readOnlyEditable = null;
			edmPropertyInfo = new Lazy<IEdmPropertyInfo>(() => GetEdmPropertyInfo());
			treatAsValueType = new Lazy<bool>(() => GetTreatAsValueType());
			convertToType = new Lazy<Type>(() => GetConvertToType());
			dictionaryWrapper = new Lazy<DictionaryWrapper>(() => GetDictionaryWrapper());
			collectionWrapper = new Lazy<CollectionWrapper>(() => GetCollectionWrapper());
		}

		protected void SetMultiInstance(object value)
		{
			multiInstance = value;
			MultiInstanceChanged();
		}

		internal void Reset()
		{
			ResetCore();
			AfterReset();
		}

		protected virtual void AfterReset()
		{
		}

		protected virtual Type GetConvertToType()
		{
			if (EdmPropertyInfo == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return DataController.VisualClient.GetConvertToType(RowHandle, DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetConvertToType(EdmPropertyInfo));
		}

		protected virtual IPropertyValidatorImplementer GetEdmValidator()
		{
			return DataController.VisualClient.Helper.GetEdmValidator(PropertyDescriptor, Instance.With((object x) => x.GetType()));
		}

		private static Type GetUnderlyingType(Type nullableType)
		{
			if (!nullableType.IsGenericType)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return nullableType.GetGenericArguments()[0];
		}

		private IDataErrorInfo GetIDataErrorInfo()
		{
			return Instance as IDataErrorInfo;
		}

		private bool GetSupportDataAnnotation()
		{
			return EdmValidator != null;
		}

		protected virtual IEdmPropertyInfo GetEdmPropertyInfo()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			ParentContext.Do(delegate(DescriptorContext x)
			{
				x.InitializeChildren();
			});
			return AssignedEdmPropertyInfo;
		}

		protected virtual bool GetTreatAsValueType()
		{
			return ParentContext.With((DescriptorContext x) => x.PropertyType).Return((Type x) => x.IsValueType || x.IsArray, () => false);
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<string> GetValidationError()
		{
			try
			{
				Func<IEnumerable<string>> func = () => SupportDataAnnotations ? EdmValidator.GetErrors(Value, Instance) : Enumerable.Empty<string>();
				return func().Union(GetIDataErrorInfoError()).ToList();
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				return new List<string> { GetUnwindedException(e).Message };
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetIDataErrorInfoError()
		{
			string text = DataErrorInfo?[Name];
			if (text != null)
			{
				yield return text;
			}
		}

		public void InitializeChildren()
		{
			if (!childrenInitialized)
			{
				childrenInitialized = true;
				InitializeChildrenInternal();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeChildrenInternal()
		{
			AllChildHandles.Count();
		}

		public virtual object GetValues()
		{
			if (PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return PropertyOwner;
			}
			if (!IsMultiSource)
			{
				return Value;
			}
			return GetMultiValues();
		}

		public virtual object GetPropertyOwner()
		{
			if (!IsMultiSource)
			{
				return MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor.GetOwner(PropertyDescriptor, MultiInstance);
			}
			return ((MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor)PropertyDescriptor).Return((MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor x) => x.GetOwners(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(MultiInstance as IEnumerable)), () => MultiInstance);
		}

		private DictionaryWrapper GetDictionaryWrapper()
		{
			return DictionaryWrapper.Wrap(GetValueOrPropertyType());
		}

		private CollectionWrapper GetCollectionWrapper()
		{
			return CollectionWrapper.Wrap(GetValueOrPropertyType());
		}

		private Type GetValueOrPropertyType()
		{
			if (Value != null)
			{
				return Value.GetType();
			}
			return PropertyType;
		}

		public bool IsItemProperty(PropertyDescriptor pd)
		{
			if (pd == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!IsMultiSource)
			{
				return ListConverter.IsItemProperty(pd);
			}
			return ((MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor)pd).IsItemProperty;
		}

		public static Exception GetUnwindedException(Exception e)
		{
			Exception ex = e;
			if (e is TargetInvocationException)
			{
				ex = e.InnerException;
			}
			string message = ex.Message;
			while (string.IsNullOrEmpty(message) && ex.InnerException != null)
			{
				message = ex.InnerException.Message;
				ex = ex.InnerException;
			}
			return ex;
		}
	}
	internal static class MayBe1
	{
		public static TI DoIfNull<TI>(this TI input, Action evaluator) where TI : class
		{
			if (input == null)
			{
				evaluator();
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public static class MultiInstanceHelper
	{
		public static IEnumerable<object> Cast(IEnumerable enumerable)
		{
			return enumerable.Return((IEnumerable x) => x.Cast<object>(), () => (IEnumerable<object>)null);
		}
	}
	public abstract class DescriptorContextOperation
	{
