		internal virtual bool NeedsVisitUnloadedRows => false;

		public abstract void Execute(DescriptorContext context);

		public virtual bool CanContinueIteration(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public virtual bool NeedsVisitChildren(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public virtual void Init()
		{
		}

		public virtual void Release()
		{
		}
	}
	internal static class FieldNameHelper
	{
		private static readonly char[] specialSymbols = new char[4] { '(', ')', '<', '>' };

		private static readonly string anyCharExceptSpecial = "[^\\<\\>\\(\\)\\.]";

		private static readonly string groupFormat = "(?<g>{0})";

		private static readonly string simpleFormat = string.Format("(?:{0})", string.Format(groupFormat, "\\<[^\\<\\>]*\\>") + "|" + string.Format("\\({0}\\)", string.Format(groupFormat, "[^\\(\\)]*")) + "|" + string.Format(groupFormat, anyCharExceptSpecial + "+"));

		private static readonly string leftExtended = string.Format(groupFormat, string.Format("{0}+{1}{0}*", anyCharExceptSpecial, simpleFormat));

		private static readonly string rightExtended = string.Format(groupFormat, string.Format("{0}*{1}{0}+", anyCharExceptSpecial, simpleFormat));

		private static readonly Regex pathExpression = new Regex("(?<=^|\\.)" + leftExtended + "|" + rightExtended + "|" + simpleFormat, RegexOptions.Compiled);

		public const string FieldNameDelimiter = ".";

		public static string GetParentFieldName(string fieldName, int level = 1)
		{
			string[] path = GetPath(fieldName);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (int i = 0; i < path.Length - level; i++)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(GetEscapeString(path[i])).Append(".");
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length >= ".".Length)
			{
				stringBuilder.Length -= ".".Length;
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string[] GetPath(string fieldName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fieldName))
			{
				return new string[0];
			}
			if (fieldName.IndexOfAny(specialSymbols) == -1)
			{
				return fieldName.Split('.');
			}
			return (from x in pathExpression.Matches(fieldName).OfType<Match>()
				where x.Success
				select x.Groups["g"].Value).ToArray();
		}

		public static string GetFieldName(string parentFieldName, string propertyName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				return parentFieldName;
			}
			propertyName = GetEscapeString(propertyName);
			if (parentFieldName == string.Empty)
			{
				return propertyName;
			}
			return parentFieldName + "." + propertyName;
		}

		public static string GetPropertyName(string fieldName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fieldName))
			{
				return fieldName;
			}
			string[] path = GetPath(fieldName);
			return path[path.Length - 1];
		}

		public static string GetCategoryFieldName(string fieldName, string categoryName)
		{
			return fieldName + "<" + categoryName + ">";
		}

		private static string GetEscapeString(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName.Contains(".") && (!propertyName.StartsWith("<") || !propertyName.EndsWith(">")))
			{
				return "(" + propertyName + ")";
			}
			return propertyName;
		}
	}
	public class GetDescriptorContextCommand
	{
		private bool isMultiSource;

		private PGridDataModeHelperContextCache contextCache;

		private object dataSource;

		private IServiceProvider serviceProvider;

		private Attribute[] browsableAttributes;

		private int initializeLockerCount;

		protected PGridDataModeHelperContextCache ContextCache => contextCache;

		protected bool IsMultiSource => isMultiSource;

		protected object DataSource => dataSource;

		protected IServiceProvider ServiceProvider => serviceProvider;

		protected Attribute[] BrowsableAttributes => browsableAttributes;

		public bool OnlyBrowsable { get; set; }

		public void Initialize(bool isMultiSource, PGridDataModeHelperContextCache contextCache, object dataSource, IServiceProvider serviceProvider, Attribute[] browsableAttributes)
		{
			this.isMultiSource = isMultiSource;
			this.contextCache = contextCache;
			this.dataSource = dataSource;
			this.serviceProvider = serviceProvider;
			this.browsableAttributes = browsableAttributes;
			initializeLockerCount++;
		}

		public DescriptorContext Execute(string propertyName)
		{
			if (contextCache == null)
			{
				return CreateCachedDescriptorContext(propertyName, null, null, null);
			}
			return RequestDescriptorContext(propertyName);
		}

		public DescriptorContext Execute(DescriptorContext parentContext, PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
		{
			string fieldName = FieldNameHelper.GetFieldName(parentContext.GetFieldNameForChild(), descriptor.Name);
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = ContextCache[fieldName];
			if (descriptorContext != null)
			{
				return descriptorContext;
			}
			object values = parentContext.GetValues();
			if (values == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (IsMultiSource)
			{
				descriptor = GetNextPropertyDescriptor(descriptor.Name, values, parentContext);
			}
			return CreateCachedDescriptorContext(fieldName, values, descriptor, parentContext);
		}

		public void Release()
		{
			initializeLockerCount--;
			if (initializeLockerCount == 0)
			{
				ReleaseInternal();
			}
		}

		public void ReleaseInternal()
		{
			isMultiSource = false;
			contextCache = null;
			dataSource = null;
			serviceProvider = null;
			browsableAttributes = null;
		}

		private DescriptorContext RequestDescriptorContext(string propertyName)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = ContextCache[propertyName];
			if (descriptorContext != null)
			{
				return descriptorContext;
			}
			if (propertyName == "")
			{
				return CreateCachedDescriptorContext("", DataSource, null, null);
			}
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext2 = RequestDescriptorContext(FieldNameHelper.GetParentFieldName(propertyName));
			object values = descriptorContext2.GetValues();
			if (values == null)
			{
				return CreateCachedDescriptorContext(propertyName, null, null, null);
			}
			PropertyDescriptor nextPropertyDescriptor = GetNextPropertyDescriptor(FieldNameHelper.GetPropertyName(propertyName), values, descriptorContext2);
			return CreateCachedDescriptorContext(propertyName, values, nextPropertyDescriptor, descriptorContext2);
		}

		private object GetValues(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			return context.GetValues();
		}

		private PropertyDescriptor GetNextPropertyDescriptor(string propertyName, object value, DescriptorContext parentContext)
		{
			if (IsMultiSource)
			{
				return MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor.GetMultiProp(parentContext, MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(value as IEnumerable), propertyName, BrowsableAttributes, OnlyBrowsable);
			}
			return parentContext.GetProperty(value, BrowsableAttributes, propertyName, OnlyBrowsable);
		}

		private DescriptorContext CreateCachedDescriptorContext(string fieldName, object obj, PropertyDescriptor pd, DescriptorContext parentContext)
		{
			DescriptorContextFactory descriptorContextFactory = ServiceProvider.With((IServiceProvider x) => (DescriptorContextFactory)x.GetService(typeof(DescriptorContextFactory)));
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = descriptorContextFactory.Create(obj, pd, fieldName, parentContext, ServiceProvider);
			if (descriptorContext == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			descriptorContextFactory.AddContext(descriptorContext, ContextCache, parentContext);
			descriptorContext.ContextAdded();
			return descriptorContext;
		}
	}
	public class GroupDescriptorContext : DescriptorContext
	{
		public override bool IsLoaded => true;

		public override string DisplayName => GroupName;

		public override bool IsExpanded
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.DataController != null && !base.DataController.AllowCollapseGroupRows)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return base.IsExpandedInternal;
			}
		}

		public override bool IsGetPropertiesSupported => true;

		public override PropertyDescriptor PropertyDescriptor
		{
			get
			{
				return null;
			}
			internal set
			{
			}
		}

		private string GroupName { get; set; }

		public override bool CanExpand => true;

		protected string BaseFieldName { get; set; }

		protected override object ValueForExtractProperties => Instance;

		public GroupDescriptorContext(object instance, string name, IServiceProvider serviceProvider, string fieldName)
			: base(serviceProvider)
		{
			SetMultiInstance(instance);
			GroupName = name;
			BaseFieldName = fieldName;
			base.FieldName = FieldNameHelper.GetCategoryFieldName(fieldName, name);
		}

		protected override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = base.DataController.GetDescriptorContext(BaseFieldName);
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection((from h in descriptorContext.ChildHandles
				select base.DataController.GetDescriptorContext(h) into c
				where c.CategoryName == GroupName
				select c.PropertyDescriptor).ToArray());
		}

		protected override void ParentContextChanged()
		{
		}

		protected override void VisibleHandlesCreated()
		{
		}

		protected override object GetValue()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected internal override string GetFieldNameForChild()
		{
			return BaseFieldName;
		}

		public override bool GetCanResetValue()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<string> GetValidationError()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override void SubscribeNotifications()
		{
		}

		protected override TypeConverter GetConverterInternal()
		{
			return DescriptorContext.NullConverter;
		}

		protected override TypeConverter GetBaseConverter()
		{
			return DescriptorContext.NullConverter;
		}
	}
	public class ImmutableDescriptorContext : PropertyDescriptorContext
	{
		protected internal override bool IsPropertyReadOnlyCore => ShouldRenderReadOnly;

		public override bool ShouldRenderReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.ParentContext != null)
				{
					return base.ParentContext.ShouldRenderReadOnly;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public ImmutableDescriptorContext(object instance, PropertyDescriptor descriptor, IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
			: base(instance, descriptor, serviceProvider)
		{
		}

		protected override bool GetShouldSerializeValue()
		{
			if (base.ParentContext == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.ParentContext.ShouldSerializeValue;
		}

		protected internal override RowHandle SetValueInternal(object value)
		{
			if (base.IsMultiSource)
			{
				((MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor)base.ParentContext.PropertyDescriptor).SetValues(base.ParentContext.PropertyOwner as IEnumerable<object>, CreateInstances(value));
				return base.ParentContext.RowHandle;
			}
			return base.ParentContext.SetValueInternal(CreateInstance(value, Name, base.ParentContext, base.ParentContext.Value, base.DataController.BrowsableAttributes));
		}

		private IEnumerable<object> CreateInstances(object settingValue)
		{
			return (base.PropertyOwner as IEnumerable<object>).Select((object parentValue) => CreateInstance(settingValue, Name, base.ParentContext, parentValue, base.DataController.BrowsableAttributes));
		}

		public override RowHandle ResetValue()
		{
			return base.ParentContext.ResetValue();
		}

		public override bool GetCanResetValue()
		{
			return base.ParentContext.CanResetValue;
		}

		public static object CreateInstance(object newValue, string name, DescriptorContext parentContext, object parentValue, Attribute[] browsableAttributes)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptorCollection = parentContext.GetProperties(parentValue, browsableAttributes);
			if (propertyDescriptorCollection == null || propertyDescriptorCollection.Count == 0)
			{
				propertyDescriptorCollection = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(parentValue, browsableAttributes);
			}
			Hashtable sameLevelValues = GetSameLevelValues(propertyDescriptorCollection, parentValue);
			sameLevelValues[name] = newValue;
			if (parentContext.Converter.GetCreateInstanceSupported(parentContext))
			{
				return parentContext.Converter.CreateInstance(parentContext, sameLevelValues);
			}
			return Activator.CreateInstance(parentContext.PropertyType, GetValues(sameLevelValues, propertyDescriptorCollection).ToArray());
		}

		private static IEnumerable<object> GetValues(Hashtable values, PropertyDescriptorCollection properties)
		{
			return from PropertyDescriptor p in properties
				select values[p.Name];
		}

		private static Hashtable GetSameLevelValues(PropertyDescriptorCollection properties, object value)
		{
			Hashtable values = new Hashtable();
			properties.Cast<PropertyDescriptor>().ForEach(delegate(PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
			{
				values[descriptor.Name] = descriptor.GetValue(MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor.GetOwner(descriptor, value));
			});
			return values;
		}
	}
	public interface IListItem
	{
		int Index { get; }
	}
	public interface IDictionaryItem
	{
		object Key { get; }
	}
	public class ListConverter : NewInstanceConverter
	{
		protected class NewListItemPropertyDescriptor : SimplePropertyDescriptor
		{
			private TypeConverter converter;

			public override TypeConverter Converter
			{
				get
				{
					if (converter == null)
					{
						converter = CreateConverter();
					}
					return converter;
				}
			}

			public NewListItemPropertyDescriptor(Type arrayType, Type elementType)
				: base(arrayType, "New", elementType)
			{
			}

			public override void SetValue(object instance, object value)
			{
				IList list = instance as IList;
				list.Add(value);
				OnValueChanged(instance, EventArgs.Empty);
			}

			public override object GetValue(object instance)
			{
				return null;
			}

			private TypeConverter CreateConverter()
			{
				return DataController.NewInstanceConverter;
			}
		}

		protected class NewICollectionItemPropertyDescriptor : NewListItemPropertyDescriptor
		{
			private CollectionWrapper wrapper;

			public NewICollectionItemPropertyDescriptor(Type arrayType, Type elementType, CollectionWrapper wrapper)
				: base(arrayType, elementType)
			{
				this.wrapper = wrapper;
			}

			public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
			{
				wrapper.Add.Invoke(component, new object[1] { value });
				OnValueChanged(component, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected class NewIDictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor : NewListItemPropertyDescriptor
		{
			private DictionaryWrapper wrapper;

			public NewIDictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor(Type arrayType, Type elementType, DictionaryWrapper helper)
				: base(arrayType, elementType)
			{
				wrapper = helper;
			}

			public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
			{
				KeyValuePair<object, object> keyValuePair = (KeyValuePair<object, object>)value;
				wrapper.Add.Invoke(component, new object[2] { keyValuePair.Key, keyValuePair.Value });
				OnValueChanged(component, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected class ListItemPropertyDescriptor : SimplePropertyDescriptor, IListItem
		{
			private int index;

			private string description;

			private string displayName;

			public override string Description => description ?? base.Description;

			public override string DisplayName => displayName ?? base.DisplayName;

			int IListItem.Index => index;

			public ListItemPropertyDescriptor(Type listType, Type elementType, int index, string displayName, string propertyName, Attribute[] attributes, string description)
				: base(listType, propertyName, elementType, attributes)
			{
				this.index = index;
				this.description = description;
				this.displayName = displayName;
			}

			public override object GetValue(object instance)
			{
				if (instance is IList)
				{
					IList list = (IList)instance;
					if (index < list.Count)
					{
						return list[index];
					}
				}
				return null;
			}

			public override void SetValue(object instance, object value)
			{
				if (instance is IList)
				{
					IList list = (IList)instance;
					if (index < list.Count)
					{
						list[index] = value;
					}
					OnValueChanged(instance, EventArgs.Empty);
				}
			}
		}

		protected class CollectionItemPropertyDescriptor : SimplePropertyDescriptor
		{
			private CollectionWrapper wrapper;

			private object value;

			private string description;

			private string displayName;

			public override string Description => description ?? base.Description;

			public override string DisplayName => displayName ?? base.DisplayName;

			public CollectionItemPropertyDescriptor(Type componentType, object value, string displayName, string name, Type propertyType, CollectionWrapper wrapper, Attribute[] attributes, string description)
				: base(componentType, name, propertyType, attributes)
			{
				this.value = value;
				this.wrapper = wrapper;
				this.description = description;
				this.displayName = displayName;
			}

			public override object GetValue(object component)
			{
				return value;
			}

			public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
			{
				wrapper.Remove((this.value == null) ? PropertyType : this.value.GetType()).Invoke(component, new object[1] { this.value });
				wrapper.Add.Invoke(component, new object[1] { value });
				OnValueChanged(component, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected class DictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor : SimplePropertyDescriptor, IDictionaryItem
		{
			private object key;

			private DictionaryWrapper helper;

			private string description;

			private string displayName;

			public override string Description => description ?? base.Description;

			public override string DisplayName => displayName ?? base.DisplayName;

			object IDictionaryItem.Key => key;

			public DictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor(Type componentType, object key, string displayName, string name, Type propertyType, DictionaryWrapper helper, Attribute[] attributes, string description)
				: base(componentType, name, propertyType, attributes)
			{
				this.key = key;
				this.helper = helper;
				this.description = description;
				this.displayName = displayName;
			}

			public override object GetValue(object component)
			{
				return helper.Item.GetValue(component, new object[1] { key });
			}

			public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
			{
				helper.Item.SetValue(component, value, new object[1] { key });
				OnValueChanged(component, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		private bool CanCreateNewInstance { get; set; }

		public ListConverter(bool canCreateNewInstance)
		{
			CanCreateNewInstance = canCreateNewInstance;
		}

		public override bool GetStandardValuesSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (!CanCreateNewInstance)
			{
				return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValuesSupported(context), () => false);
			}
			return base.GetStandardValuesSupported(context);
		}

		public override StandardValuesCollection GetStandardValues(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (!CanCreateNewInstance)
			{
				return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValues(context), () => (StandardValuesCollection)null);
			}
			return base.GetStandardValues(context);
		}

		public override bool GetStandardValuesExclusive(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (!CanCreateNewInstance)
			{
				return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValuesExclusive(context), () => false);
			}
			return base.GetStandardValuesExclusive(context);
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("destinationType");
			}
			return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType), () => "(Collection)");
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object enumerableCandidate, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = (DescriptorContext)context;
			if (enumerableCandidate is IList list2)
			{
				int count = list2.Count;
				Type type = list2.GetType();
				Type baseListElementType = descriptorContext.CollectionHelper.ElementType;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					Type elementType = list2[i].Return((object x) => x.GetType(), () => baseListElementType);
					list.Add(new ListItemPropertyDescriptor(type, elementType, i, GetDisplayPropertyName(list2[i], descriptorContext), GetDefaultPropertyName(i), GetAttributes(list2[i])?.ToArray(), GetDescriptionOverride(list2[i], descriptorContext)));
				}
			}
			else if (descriptorContext.IsIDictionary)
			{
				DictionaryWrapper dictionaryWrapper = descriptorContext.DictionaryWrapper;
				foreach (object item in dictionaryWrapper.Keys.GetValue(enumerableCandidate) as IEnumerable)
				{
					string text = $"{item}";
					if (string.Equals(text, string.Empty))
					{
						text = "Empty";
					}
					KeyValuePair<object, object> keyValuePair = new KeyValuePair<object, object>(item, dictionaryWrapper.Item.GetValue(enumerableCandidate, new object[1] { item }));
					list.Add(new DictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor(enumerableCandidate.GetType(), item, GetDisplayPropertyName(keyValuePair, descriptorContext), text, keyValuePair.Value?.GetType() ?? dictionaryWrapper.Item.PropertyType, dictionaryWrapper, AddNotifyParentPropertyAttribute(GetAttributes(keyValuePair.Value)), GetDescriptionOverride(keyValuePair, descriptorContext)));
				}
			}
			else if (descriptorContext.IsICollection)
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (object item2 in enumerableCandidate as IEnumerable)
				{
					list.Add(new CollectionItemPropertyDescriptor(enumerableCandidate.GetType(), item2, GetDisplayPropertyName(item2, descriptorContext), GetDefaultPropertyName(num), item2.GetType(), descriptorContext.CollectionWrapper, AddNotifyParentPropertyAttribute(GetAttributes(item2)), GetDescriptionOverride(item2, descriptorContext)));
					num++;
				}
			}
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection(list.ToArray());
		}

		private static IEnumerable<Attribute> GetAttributes(object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(obj).Cast<Attribute>();
		}

		private static Attribute[] AddNotifyParentPropertyAttribute(IEnumerable<Attribute> attributes)
		{
			IEnumerable<Attribute> enumerable = ((Attribute)new NotifyParentPropertyAttribute(notifyParent: true)).Yield();
			if (attributes == null)
			{
				return enumerable.ToArray();
			}
			return attributes.Union(enumerable).ToArray();
		}

		private string GetDefaultPropertyName(int index)
		{
			return "[" + index + "]";
		}

		private string GetDisplayPropertyName(object item, DescriptorContext descriptorContext)
		{
			return GetMemberString(() => descriptorContext.CollectionHelper.GetDisplayMemberValue(item));
		}

		private string GetDescriptionOverride(object item, DescriptorContext descriptorContext)
		{
			return GetMemberString(() => descriptorContext.CollectionHelper.GetDescriptionMemberValue(item));
		}

		private static string GetMemberString(Func<object> getMemberValue)
		{
			object obj = getMemberValue();
			if (object.Equals(obj, null))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return $"{obj}";
		}

		public static bool IsNewItemProperty(PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor)
		{
			return propertyDescriptor is NewListItemPropertyDescriptor;
		}

		public static bool IsItemProperty(PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor)
		{
			return propertyDescriptor is SimplePropertyDescriptor;
		}

		public static PropertyDescriptor CreateNewListItemPropertyDescriptor(Type arrayType, Type elementType)
		{
			return new NewListItemPropertyDescriptor(arrayType, elementType);
		}

		public static PropertyDescriptor CreateNewCollectionItemPropertyDescriptor(Type arrayType, Type elementType, CollectionWrapper wrapper)
		{
			return new NewICollectionItemPropertyDescriptor(arrayType, elementType, wrapper);
		}

		public static PropertyDescriptor CreateNewDictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor(Type arrayType, Type elementType, DictionaryWrapper helper)
		{
			return new NewIDictionaryItemPropertyDescriptor(arrayType, elementType, helper);
		}

		public override bool GetPropertiesSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return context.Instance != null;
		}
	}
	internal class MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		private class MultiObjectAttributes : AttributeCollection
		{
			private MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor owner;

			private IEnumerable<AttributeCollection> multiAttributes;

			private Lazy<Attribute[]> _attributes;

			private Hashtable attributes;

			public override Attribute this[Type attributeType]
			{
				get
				{
					if (multiAttributes == null)
					{
						multiAttributes = GetMultiAttributes();
					}
					if (attributes == null)
					{
						attributes = new Hashtable();
					}
					Attribute attribute = (Attribute)attributes[attributeType];
					if (attribute == null)
					{
						attribute = GetAttribute(attributeType);
						attributes[attributeType] = attribute;
					}
					return attribute;
				}
			}

			protected override Attribute[] Attributes => _attributes.Value;

			public MultiObjectAttributes(MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor owner)
				: base((Attribute[])null)
			{
				this.owner = owner;
				_attributes = new Lazy<Attribute[]>(() => GetAttributes());
			}

			private IEnumerable<AttributeCollection> GetMultiAttributes()
			{
				return owner.Descriptors.Select((PropertyDescriptor d) => d.Attributes).ToList();
			}

			private Attribute GetAttribute(Type attributeType)
			{
				return multiAttributes.Select((AttributeCollection x) => x[attributeType]).If((IEnumerable<Attribute> x) => x.First() != null && x.AllEqual()).Return((IEnumerable<Attribute> x) => x.First(), () => GetDefaultAttribute(attributeType));
			}

			private Attribute[] GetAttributes()
			{
				return (from Attribute x in owner.Descriptors.First().Attributes
					select this[x.GetType()] into x
					where x != null
					select x).ToArray();
			}
		}

		protected internal IEnumerable<PropertyDescriptor> Descriptors { get; set; }

		public override string Category => Descriptors.First().Category;

		public override Type ComponentType => Descriptors.First().ComponentType;

		public override bool SupportsChangeEvents => Descriptors.All((PropertyDescriptor p) => p.SupportsChangeEvents);

		public override bool IsReadOnly => Descriptors.All((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.IsReadOnly);

		public override Type PropertyType => Descriptors.First().PropertyType;

		public override TypeConverter Converter => Descriptors.First().Converter;

		public int Index => PropertyDescriptorHelper.GetIndex(Descriptors.First());

		public bool IsItemProperty => ListConverter.IsItemProperty(Descriptors.First());

		public override string DisplayName => Descriptors.First().DisplayName;

		public static PropertyDescriptor GetMultiProp(DescriptorContext context, IEnumerable<object> components, string name, Attribute[] attrs, bool onlyBrowsable)
		{
			return components.Select((object o) => context.GetProperty(o, attrs, name, onlyBrowsable)).ToList().If((List<PropertyDescriptor> x) => (x.IsSingle() && x.First() != null) || x.FirstOrDefault().If((PropertyDescriptor d) => (d.Attributes[typeof(MergablePropertyAttribute)] as MergablePropertyAttribute).Return((MergablePropertyAttribute a) => a.AllowMerge, () => true)).ReturnSuccess())
				.If((List<PropertyDescriptor> x) => x.AllEqual())
				.Return((List<PropertyDescriptor> x) => new MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor(x), () => (MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor)null);
		}

		protected override AttributeCollection CreateAttributeCollection()
		{
			return new MultiObjectAttributes(this);
		}

		protected MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor(IEnumerable<PropertyDescriptor> descriptors)
			: base(descriptors.First().Name, null)
		{
			Descriptors = descriptors.ToList();
		}

		public override object GetEditor(Type editorBaseType)
		{
			return Descriptors.First().GetEditor(editorBaseType);
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			return All(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component as IEnumerable), (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => d.CanResetValue(c));
		}

		public override void AddValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			AddValueChangedCore(component, handler, delegate(PropertyDescriptor d, object o, EventHandler h)
			{
				d.AddValueChanged(o, h);
			});
		}

		public override void RemoveValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			AddValueChangedCore(component, handler, delegate(PropertyDescriptor d, object o, EventHandler h)
			{
				if (o != null && h != null)
				{
					d?.RemoveValueChanged(o, h);
				}
			});
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			return GetValues(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component as IEnumerable)).If((IEnumerable<object> x) => x.AllEqual()).Return((IEnumerable<object> x) => x.First(), () => (object)null);
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
			SetValueCore(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component as IEnumerable), delegate(PropertyDescriptor d, object o)
			{
				d.ResetValue(o);
			});
		}

		public IEnumerable<object> GetValues(IEnumerable<object> components)
		{
			return Descriptors.Zip(components, (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => d.GetValue(c));
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
			SetValueCore(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component as IEnumerable), delegate(PropertyDescriptor d, object o)
			{
				d.SetValue(o, value);
			});
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			return All(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component as IEnumerable), (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => d.ShouldSerializeValue(c));
		}

		public IEnumerable<object> GetOwners(IEnumerable<object> components)
		{
			return Descriptors.Zip(components, (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => GetOwner(d, c)).ToList();
		}

		public void SetValues(IEnumerable<object> components, IEnumerable<object> values)
		{
			Descriptors.Zip(components, (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => new
			{
				Descriptor = d,
				Owner = c
			}).Zip(values, (x, object v) => new
			{
				Descriptor = x.Descriptor,
				Owner = x.Owner,
				Value = v
			}).ForEach(x =>
			{
				x.Descriptor.SetValue(x.Owner, x.Value);
			});
		}

		private void SetValueCore(IEnumerable<object> component, Action<PropertyDescriptor, object> setValueAction)
		{
			Descriptors.Zip(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component), (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => new
			{
				Descriptor = d,
				Owner = c
			}).ForEach(x =>
			{
				setValueAction(x.Descriptor, x.Owner);
			});
		}

		private void AddValueChangedCore(object component, EventHandler handler, Action<PropertyDescriptor, object, EventHandler> addRemoveAction)
		{
			(from x in Descriptors.Zip(MultiInstanceHelper.Cast(component as IEnumerable), (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => new
				{
					Descriptor = d,
					Owner = c
				})
				where x.Descriptor.SupportsChangeEvents
				select x).ForEach(x =>
			{
				addRemoveAction(x.Descriptor, x.Owner, handler);
			});
		}

		public static object GetOwner(PropertyDescriptor descriptor, object component)
		{
			return (component as ICustomTypeDescriptor).Return((ICustomTypeDescriptor x) => x.GetPropertyOwner(descriptor), () => component);
		}

		private bool All(IEnumerable<object> components, Func<PropertyDescriptor, object, bool> predicate)
		{
			return Descriptors.Zip(components, (PropertyDescriptor d, object c) => new
			{
				Descriptor = d,
				Owner = c
			}).All(x => predicate(x.Descriptor, x.Owner));
		}
	}
	internal static class PropertyDescriptorHelper
	{
		private const string DataColumnPropertyDescriptorFullName = "System.Data.DataColumnPropertyDescriptor";

		public static int GetIndex(PropertyDescriptor pd)
		{
			return (pd as IListItem).Return((IListItem x) => x.Index, () => -1);
		}

		public static bool IsTableView(PropertyDescriptor pd)
		{
			return string.Equals(((pd as MultiObjectPropertyDescriptor)?.Descriptors?.FirstOrDefault() ?? pd)?.GetType().FullName, "System.Data.DataColumnPropertyDescriptor", StringComparison.Ordinal);
		}
	}
	public class InstanceInitializerTest : IInstanceInitializerImplementer
	{
		public Func<KeyValuePair<object, object>?> GetCreateInstanceDictionary { get; set; }

		public virtual IEnumerable<ITypeInfoImplementer> Types { get; set; }

		public virtual object CreateInstance(ITypeInfoImplementer type)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual KeyValuePair<object, object>? CreateInstance(ITypeInfoImplementer type, ITypeDescriptorContext context, object dictionary)
		{
			return GetCreateInstanceDictionary.Return((Func<KeyValuePair<object, object>?> x) => x(), () => (KeyValuePair<object, object>?)null);
		}
	}
	public class NewInstanceConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		private static Dictionary<Type, bool> CanCreateInstanceCache = new Dictionary<Type, bool>();

		public StandardValuesCollection GetTypeInfos(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			List<ITypeInfoImplementer> list = new List<ITypeInfoImplementer>();
			if (context == null)
			{
				return new StandardValuesCollection(list);
			}
			IInstanceInitializerImplementer instanceInitializer = context.InstanceInitializer;
			if (instanceInitializer != null && instanceInitializer.Types != null)
			{
				list.AddRange(instanceInitializer.Types);
			}
			else
			{
				Type defaultType = GetDefaultType(context);
				if (defaultType == null)
				{
					return new StandardValuesCollection(list);
				}
				ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfoImplementer = context.DataController.VisualClient.Helper.CreateTypeInfo(defaultType);
				if (CanCreateInstance(context, typeInfoImplementer))
				{
					list.Add(typeInfoImplementer);
				}
			}
			return new StandardValuesCollection(list);
		}

		public override StandardValuesCollection GetStandardValues(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValues(context), () => (StandardValuesCollection)null);
		}

		private Type GetDefaultType(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context == null || context.PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Type propertyType = context.PropertyDescriptor.PropertyType;
			if (ListConverter.IsNewItemProperty(context.PropertyDescriptor))
			{
				if (CanUseDefaultNewItemInitializer(propertyType))
				{
					return propertyType;
				}
				return null;
			}
			if (propertyType != null && !propertyType.IsAbstract && propertyType != typeof(object) && propertyType != typeof(DependencyObject) && !IsSimple(propertyType) && HasDefaultConstructor(propertyType))
			{
				return propertyType;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static bool IsSimple(Type type)
		{
			if (type != null)
			{
				if (!type.IsValueType && !type.IsEnum)
				{
					return type.IsPrimitive;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static bool HasDefaultConstructor(Type type)
		{
			if (type != null)
			{
				return type.GetConstructor(new Type[0]) != null;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool CanUseDefaultNewItemInitializer(Type type)
		{
			if (!(type == null) && !type.IsAbstract && (!type.IsGenericType || !(type.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(KeyValuePair<, >))))
			{
				if (!IsSimple(type) && !(type == typeof(string)))
				{
					if (!IsSimple(type))
					{
						return HasDefaultConstructor(type);
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override bool GetStandardValuesExclusive(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValuesExclusive(context), () => false);
		}

		public override bool GetStandardValuesSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.GetStandardValuesSupported(context), () => false);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType), () => base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType));
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (value is ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfoImplementer)
			{
				return typeInfoImplementer.Name;
			}
			return GetBaseConverter(context).Return((TypeConverter x) => x.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType), () => base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType));
		}

		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			if (sourceType.GetInterfaces().FirstOrDefault((Type x) => x == typeof(ITypeInfoImplementer)) != null)
			{
				return (context as DescriptorContext).Return((DescriptorContext x) => x.TypeInfos.Count > 0, () => false);
			}
			return GetBaseConverter(context)?.CanConvertFrom(context, sourceType) ?? false;
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			if (value != null && value is ITypeInfoImplementer)
			{
				object obj = CreateInstance(context as DescriptorContext, value as ITypeInfoImplementer);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			TypeConverter baseConverter = GetBaseConverter(context);
			if (baseConverter != null)
			{
				return baseConverter.ConvertFrom(context, culture, value);
			}
			return base.ConvertFrom(context, culture, value);
		}

		public override bool GetPropertiesSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return GetBaseConverter(context)?.GetPropertiesSupported(context) ?? false;
		}

		protected TypeConverter GetBaseConverter(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (!(context is DescriptorContext { BaseConverter: var baseConverter }))
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (baseConverter == null || baseConverter.GetType() == typeof(NewInstanceConverter))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return baseConverter;
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object value, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return GetBaseConverter(context)?.GetProperties(context, value, attributes);
		}

		private bool CanCreateInstanceInternal(DescriptorContext descriptorContext, ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfo)
		{
			if (typeInfo != null && HasDefaultConstructor(typeInfo.Type))
			{
				return true;
			}
			try
			{
				CreateInstance(descriptorContext, typeInfo);
			}
			catch
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool CanCreateInstance(DescriptorContext descriptorContext, ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfo)
		{
			if (typeInfo == null || typeInfo.Type == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!CanCreateInstanceCache.TryGetValue(typeInfo.Type, out var value))
			{
				value = CanCreateInstanceInternal(descriptorContext, typeInfo);
				CanCreateInstanceCache.Add(typeInfo.Type, value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		private object CreateInstance(DescriptorContext descriptorContext, ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfo)
		{
			if (typeInfo == null || descriptorContext == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			IInstanceInitializerImplementer instanceInitializer = descriptorContext.InstanceInitializer;
			if (descriptorContext.ParentContext.IsIDictionary && instanceInitializer != null)
			{
				return instanceInitializer.CreateInstance(typeInfo, descriptorContext.ParentContext, descriptorContext.Instance);
			}
			return instanceInitializer.Return((IInstanceInitializerImplementer x) => x.CreateInstance(typeInfo), null) ?? CreateInstanceInternal(typeInfo);
		}

		private static object CreateInstanceInternal(ITypeInfoImplementer typeInfo)
		{
			if (TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(typeInfo).GetCreateInstanceSupported())
			{
				Hashtable sameLevelValues = GetSameLevelValues(TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(typeInfo));
				return TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(typeInfo).CreateInstance(sameLevelValues);
			}
			if (typeInfo.Type == typeof(string))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return TypeDescriptor.CreateInstance(null, typeInfo.Type, null, null);
		}

		private static Hashtable GetSameLevelValues(PropertyDescriptorCollection properties)
		{
			Hashtable hashtable = new Hashtable();
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor property in properties)
			{
				object defaultValue = GetDefaultValue(property);
				hashtable[property.Name] = defaultValue;
			}
			return hashtable;
		}

		private static object GetDefaultValue(PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
		{
			DefaultValueAttribute defaultValueAttribute = (DefaultValueAttribute)TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(descriptor.PropertyType)[typeof(DefaultValueAttribute)];
			if (defaultValueAttribute != null)
			{
				return defaultValueAttribute.Value;
			}
			if (descriptor.PropertyType.IsPrimitive)
			{
				return TypeDescriptor.CreateInstance(null, descriptor.PropertyType, null, null);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class NewListItemDescriptorContext : ListItemDescriptorContext
	{
		public NewListItemDescriptorContext(DescriptorContext parentContext, PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor)
			: base(parentContext.Value, propertyDescriptor, parentContext.ServiceProvider)
		{
			base.ParentContext = parentContext;
		}

		public override IInstanceInitializerImplementer GetInstanceInitializer()
		{
			return base.ParentContext.CollectionHelper.ItemInitializer;
		}

		protected override TypeConverter GetConverterInternal()
		{
			return DataController.NewInstanceConverter;
		}

		public override object GetPropertyOwner()
		{
			return MultiInstance;
		}

		protected internal override RowHandle SetValueInternal(object value)
		{
			base.SetValueInternal(value);
			return base.ParentContext.RowHandle;
		}

		protected override void ParentContextChanged()
		{
		}

		protected override void BeforeSetValue(object originalValue, object convertedValue)
		{
			if (convertedValue != null)
			{
				base.ParentContext.NewListItemValue = originalValue as TypeInfoImplementer;
			}
		}

		protected override object GetValue()
		{
			if (base.ParentContext.NewListItemValue == null)
			{
				return base.TypeInfos.Cast<object>().FirstOrDefault();
			}
			return base.ParentContext.NewListItemValue;
		}

		public override bool GetCanResetValue()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override void SubscribeNotifications()
		{
		}
	}
	public class PGridDataModeHelperContextCache
	{
		private Dictionary<object, DescriptorContext> sourceCache;

		protected Dictionary<object, DescriptorContext> SourceCache
		{
			get
			{
				if (sourceCache == null)
				{
					sourceCache = new Dictionary<object, DescriptorContext>();
				}
				return sourceCache;
			}
		}

		public DescriptorContext this[object propName]
		{
			get
			{
				if (!SourceCache.TryGetValue(propName, out var value))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return value;
			}
			set
			{
				SourceCache[propName] = value;
			}
		}

		internal IEnumerable<object> Keys => SourceCache.Keys;

		public void Clear()
		{
			sourceCache = null;
		}

		public void Remove(object key)
		{
			SourceCache.Remove(key);
		}
	}

	public class UpdateHandlesOperation : DescriptorContextOperation
	{
		private List<RowHandle> invalidatedHandles = new List<RowHandle>();

		public PGridDataModeHelperContextCache newCache = new PGridDataModeHelperContextCache();

		public List<RowHandle> InvalidatedHandles => invalidatedHandles;

		public override void Execute(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context != null)
			{
				AddContext(context);
				if (!context.IsValid)
				{
					InvalidatedHandles.Add(context.RowHandle);
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddContext(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			newCache[context.FieldName] = context;
			newCache[context.RowHandle] = context;
		}

		public override bool NeedsVisitChildren(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context != null && context.IsValid)
			{
				return base.NeedsVisitChildren(context);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class CollectExpandedStateOperation : DescriptorContextOperation
	{
		public Dictionary<string, bool> ExpandedState { get; private set; }

		public CollectExpandedStateOperation()
		{
			ExpandedState = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
		}

		public override void Execute(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context != null && context.FirstExpanded)
			{
				ExpandedState[context.FieldName] = context.IsExpanded;
			}
		}

		public override bool NeedsVisitChildren(DescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context != null && context.FirstExpanded)
			{
				return base.NeedsVisitChildren(context);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class CategoriesShowModeTypeConverter : EnumConverter
	{
		public CategoriesShowModeTypeConverter()
			: base(typeof(CategoriesShowMode))
		{
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			if (value is string && bool.TryParse((string)value, out var result))
			{
				return result ? CategoriesShowMode.Visible : CategoriesShowMode.Hidden;
			}
			return base.ConvertFrom(context, culture, value);
		}
	}
	public class EditableObjectWrapper : ICustomTypeDescriptor
	{
		private PropertyDescriptorCollection internalProperties;

		private string fieldName;

		protected internal object Source { get; private set; }

		protected internal Type SourceType { get; private set; }

		protected internal RowHandle Handle => DataGenerator.DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(fieldName);

		protected internal TypeDescriptionProvider SourceTypeDescriptor { get; private set; }

		protected internal RowDataGenerator DataGenerator { get; private set; }

		private PropertyDescriptorCollection InternalProperties
		{
			get
			{
				if (internalProperties == null)
				{
					internalProperties = Source.Return((object x) => PropertyDescriptorWrapper.GetWrappedProperties(TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(x), this), () => PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty);
				}
				return internalProperties;
			}
			set
			{
				internalProperties = value;
			}
		}

		public EditableObjectWrapper(object source, RowDataGenerator dataGenerator, RowHandle handle)
		{
			Source = source;
			DataGenerator = dataGenerator;
			fieldName = dataGenerator.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle);
			if (source != null)
			{
				SourceType = source.GetType();
			}
		}

		public AttributeCollection GetAttributes()
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(x), () => AttributeCollection.Empty);
		}

		public string GetClassName()
		{
			return SourceType.With((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetClassName(x));
		}

		public string GetComponentName()
		{
			return SourceType.With((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetComponentName(x));
		}

		public TypeConverter GetConverter()
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => new TypeConverterWrapper(TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(x), DataGenerator, Handle), () => (TypeConverterWrapper)null);
		}

		public EventDescriptor GetDefaultEvent()
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetDefaultEvent(x), () => (EventDescriptor)null);
		}

		public object GetEditor(Type editorBaseType)
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetEditor(x, editorBaseType), () => (object)null);
		}

		public EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetEvents(x, attributes), () => (EventDescriptorCollection)null);
		}

		public EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents()
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => TypeDescriptor.GetEvents(x), () => (EventDescriptorCollection)null);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptor GetDefaultProperty()
		{
			return SourceType.Return((Type x) => new PropertyDescriptorWrapper(TypeDescriptor.GetDefaultProperty(x), this), () => (PropertyDescriptorWrapper)null);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection collection = Source.Return((object x) => TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(x, attributes), () => PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty);
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection((from wr in InternalProperties.OfType<PropertyDescriptorWrapper>()
				where collection.Contains(wr.Source)
				select wr).ToArray());
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties()
		{
			return InternalProperties;
		}

		public object GetPropertyOwner(PropertyDescriptor pd)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
	public class PropertyDescriptorWrapper : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		private int nameHash = -1;

		private Dictionary<EventHandler, EventHandler> valueChangedHandlers = new Dictionary<EventHandler, EventHandler>();

		protected internal PropertyDescriptor Source { get; private set; }

		protected internal EditableObjectWrapper ObjectWrapper { get; private set; }

		public RowHandle Handle
		{
			get
			{
				RowHandle rowHandle = null;
				string input = ((ObjectWrapper.Handle == null) ? "" : ObjectWrapper.DataGenerator.DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(ObjectWrapper.Handle));
				string fieldName = input.Return((string x) => x += $".{Name}", () => Name);
				return ObjectWrapper.DataGenerator.DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(fieldName);
			}
		}

		public override AttributeCollection Attributes => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Attributes, () => AttributeCollection.Empty);

		public override string Category => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Category, () => (string)null);

		public override Type ComponentType => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.ComponentType, () => (Type)null);

		public override TypeConverter Converter => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => new TypeConverterWrapper(x.Converter, ObjectWrapper.DataGenerator, Handle), () => (TypeConverterWrapper)null);

		public override string Description => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Description, () => (string)null);

		public override bool DesignTimeOnly => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.DesignTimeOnly, () => false);

		public override string DisplayName => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.DisplayName, () => (string)null);

		public override bool IsBrowsable => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.IsBrowsable, () => true);

		public override bool IsLocalizable => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.IsLocalizable, () => true);

		public override bool IsReadOnly => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.IsReadOnly, () => false);

		public override string Name => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Name, () => (string)null);

		protected override int NameHashCode => nameHash;

		public override Type PropertyType => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.PropertyType, () => (Type)null);

		public override bool SupportsChangeEvents => Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.SupportsChangeEvents, () => false);

		public PropertyDescriptorWrapper(PropertyDescriptor source, EditableObjectWrapper objectWrapper)
			: base(source)
		{
			Source = source;
			ObjectWrapper = objectWrapper;
			nameHash = Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Name.GetHashCode(), () => -1);
		}

		protected PropertyDescriptorWrapper(string name, Attribute[] attrs)
			: base(name, attrs)
		{
		}

		protected PropertyDescriptorWrapper(MemberDescriptor descr)
			: base(descr)
		{
		}

		protected PropertyDescriptorWrapper(MemberDescriptor descr, Attribute[] attrs)
			: base(descr, attrs)
		{
		}

		public override void AddValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			EventHandler customHandler = delegate(object d, EventArgs e)
			{
				handler(ObjectWrapper, e);
			};
			valueChangedHandlers[handler] = customHandler;
			Source.Do(delegate(PropertyDescriptor x)
			{
				x.AddValueChanged(GetComponent(component), customHandler);
			});
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			return Source.CanResetValue(GetComponent(component));
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!base.Equals(obj))
			{
				return Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.Equals(obj), () => false);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override object GetEditor(Type editorBaseType)
		{
			return Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.GetEditor(editorBaseType), () => (object)null);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			return Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => new EditableObjectWrapper(x.GetValue(GetComponent(component)), ObjectWrapper.DataGenerator, Handle), () => (EditableObjectWrapper)null);
		}

		public override void RemoveValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			EventHandler customHandler = valueChangedHandlers[handler];
			Source.Do(delegate(PropertyDescriptor x)
			{
				x.RemoveValueChanged(GetComponent(component), customHandler);
			});
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
			Source.Do(delegate(PropertyDescriptor x)
			{
				x.ResetValue(GetComponent(component));
			});
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			return Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.ShouldSerializeValue(GetComponent(component)), () => false);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => x.ToString(), () => base.ToString());
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetChildProperties(object instance, Attribute[] filter)
		{
			return Source.Return((PropertyDescriptor x) => GetWrappedProperties(x.GetChildProperties(instance, filter), ObjectWrapper), () => PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty);
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
			if (Source != null && ObjectWrapper != null && ObjectWrapper.DataGenerator != null)
			{
				ObjectWrapper.DataGenerator.SetValue(Handle, value);
			}
		}

		public static PropertyDescriptorCollection GetWrappedProperties(PropertyDescriptorCollection source, EditableObjectWrapper objectWrapper)
		{
			if (source == null || source == PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty)
			{
				return PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty;
			}
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection((from x in source.OfType<PropertyDescriptor>()
				select new PropertyDescriptorWrapper(x, objectWrapper)).ToArray());
		}

		protected static object GetComponent(object component)
		{
			if (component is EditableObjectWrapper)
			{
				return ((EditableObjectWrapper)component).Source;
			}
			return component;
		}
	}
	public class TypeConverterWrapper : TypeConverter
	{
		private TypeConverter baseConverter;

		private RowDataGenerator generator;

		private RowHandle handle;

		public TypeConverterWrapper(TypeConverter baseConverter, RowDataGenerator generator, RowHandle handle)
		{
			this.baseConverter = baseConverter;
			this.generator = generator;
			this.handle = handle;
		}

		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			return baseConverter.CanConvertFrom(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), sourceType);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			return baseConverter.CanConvertTo(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), destinationType);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			return baseConverter.ConvertFrom(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), culture, GetActualValue(value));
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			return baseConverter.ConvertTo(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), culture, GetActualValue(value), destinationType);
		}

		public override object CreateInstance(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IDictionary propertyValues)
		{
			return baseConverter.CreateInstance(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), propertyValues);
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			return base.Equals(obj);
		}

		public override bool GetCreateInstanceSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return baseConverter.GetCreateInstanceSupported(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)));
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return baseConverter.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object value, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return baseConverter.GetProperties(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), value, attributes);
		}

		public override bool GetPropertiesSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return baseConverter.GetPropertiesSupported(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)));
		}

		public override StandardValuesCollection GetStandardValues(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return baseConverter.GetStandardValues(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)));
		}

		public override bool GetStandardValuesExclusive(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return baseConverter.GetStandardValuesExclusive(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)));
		}

		public override bool GetStandardValuesSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return baseConverter.GetStandardValuesSupported(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)));
		}

		public override bool IsValid(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object value)
		{
			return baseConverter.IsValid(context.With((ITypeDescriptorContext x) => new TypeDescriptorContextWrapper(x)), value);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return base.ToString();
		}

		private object GetActualValue(object source)
		{
			if (source is EditableObjectWrapper)
			{
				return (source as EditableObjectWrapper).Source;
			}
			return source;
		}

		private EditableObjectWrapper Wrap(object source)
		{
			return new EditableObjectWrapper(source, generator, handle);
		}
	}

	public static class PropertyDefinitionExtensions
	{
		public static T GetValue<T, TDefinition>(this TDefinition d1, TDefinition d2, Func<TDefinition, T> getValue, Func<T, T, T> combineValue) where TDefinition : PropertyDefinition
		{
			return combineValue(getValue(d1), getValue(d2));
		}
	}
	public class AggregateAction : IAggregateAction, IAction
	{
		private Action<AggregateAction> action;

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		protected Func<AggregateAction, IAction, bool> CanAggregateDelegate { get; set; }

		public AggregateAction(Action<AggregateAction> action, Func<AggregateAction, IAction, bool> canAggregateFunc)
		{
			this.action = action;
			CanAggregateDelegate = canAggregateFunc;
		}

		void IAction.Execute()
		{
			if (action != null)
			{
				action(this);
			}
		}

		bool IAggregateAction.CanAggregate(IAction action)
		{
			if (CanAggregateDelegate != null)
			{
				return CanAggregateDelegate(this, action);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class FieldNameAction : AggregateAction
	{
		public string FieldName
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)base.Tag;
			}
			protected set
			{
				base.Tag = value;
			}
		}

		public Func<FieldNameAction, IAction, bool> CanAggregateFunc
		{
			get
			{
				return base.CanAggregateDelegate;
			}
			set
			{
				base.CanAggregateDelegate = (AggregateAction first, IAction second) => value((FieldNameAction)first, second);
			}
		}

		public FieldNameAction(Action<FieldNameAction> action, string fieldName)
			: base(delegate(AggregateAction param)
			{
				action((FieldNameAction)param);
			}, null)
		{
			FieldName = fieldName;
		}
	}
	public class DeferredAction : IAction
	{
		private Action action;

		private Func<bool> canExecute;

		private ImmediateActionsManager manager;

		public DeferredAction(Action action, Func<bool> canExecute, ImmediateActionsManager manager)
		{
			this.action = action;
			this.canExecute = canExecute ?? ((Func<bool>)(() => true));
			this.manager = manager;
		}

		public void Execute()
		{
			if (!canExecute() && manager != null)
			{
				manager.EnqueueAction((IAction)(object)this);
			}
			else
			{
				action();
			}
		}
	}
	public class DoubleClickHelper
	{
		private DispatcherTimer timer;

		private object olock = new object();

		public bool IsClicked { get; private set; }

		public event MouseButtonEventHandler DoubleClick;

		public DoubleClickHelper(FrameworkElement owner)
		{
			IsClicked = false;
			owner.MouseLeftButtonUp += OnOwnerMouseLeftButtonUp;
			timer = new DispatcherTimer
			{
				Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(SystemInformation.DoubleClickTime)
			};
			timer.Tick += delegate
			{
				Reset();
			};
		}

		private void OnOwnerMouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			lock (olock)
			{
				if (IsClicked)
				{
					IsClicked = false;
					timer.Stop();
					OnDoubleClick(sender, e);
				}
				else
				{
					IsClicked = true;
					timer.Start();
				}
			}
		}

		private void Reset()
		{
			timer.Stop();
			lock (olock)
			{
				IsClicked = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.DoubleClick != null)
			{
				this.DoubleClick(sender, e);
			}
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridControlBackgroundToBooleanConverterExtension : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value is Brush objA && !object.Equals(objA, Brushes.Transparent);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	internal class ConditionalVisualTreeEnumerator : VisualTreeEnumerator, IEnumerator<DependencyObject>, IDisposable, IEnumerator
	{
		private Predicate<DependencyObject> predicate;

		DependencyObject IEnumerator<DependencyObject>.Current => ((VisualTreeEnumerator)this).Current;

		public ConditionalVisualTreeEnumerator(DependencyObject obj, Predicate<DependencyObject> predicate)
			: base(obj)
		{
			this.predicate = predicate;
		}

		protected override IEnumerator GetNestedObjects(object obj)
		{
			if (!predicate(obj as DependencyObject))
			{
				return NestedObjectEnumeratorBase.EmptyEnumerator;
			}
			return ((VisualTreeEnumerator)this).GetNestedObjects(obj);
		}

		void IDisposable.Dispose()
		{
		}
	}
	public class ModifyCollectionHelper
	{
		private PropertyGridView view;

		protected PropertyGridView View => view;

		protected PropertyGridControl PropertyGrid => View.PropertyGrid;

		protected DataViewBase DataView => PropertyGrid.DataView;

		public ModifyCollectionHelper(PropertyGridView view)
		{
			this.view = view;
		}

		public void OnCollectionButtonClick(RowData data, object clickSource, object item)
		{
			if (data.IsCollectionRow)
			{
				if (item != null || !View.MenuHelper.ShowCollectionMenu(data, clickSource))
				{
					AddCollectionItem(data, item);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				RemoveCollectionItem(data);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveCollectionItem(RowData rowData)
		{
			if (rowData != null && CanRemoveCollectionItem(rowData))
			{
				if ((((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)View.CellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor as DependencyObject).With((DependencyObject x) => PropertyGridHelper.GetRowData(x)) == rowData)
				{
					((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)View.CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.HideEditor(true);
				}
				DataView.RemoveCollectionItem(rowData.Handle);
			}
		}

		public void AddCollectionItem(RowData rowData, object value = null)
		{
			if (rowData != null && CanAddCollectionItem(rowData, value))
			{
				object item = value ?? DataView.GetSelectedCollectionNewItem(rowData.Handle);
				DataView.AddCollectionNewItem(rowData.Handle, item);
			}
		}

		public bool CanAddCollectionItem(RowData rowData, object value = null)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = rowData?.Handle;
			if (rowHandle == null || rowHandle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				return DataView.CanAddCollectionNewItem(rowHandle);
			}
			return !DataView.IsReadOnlyCollection(rowHandle);
		}

		public bool CanRemoveCollectionItem(RowData rowData)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = rowData.With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
			if (rowHandle != null && !rowHandle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return DataView.CanRemoveCollectionItem(rowHandle);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool CanModifyCollection(RowData rowData, CollectionButtonKind kind)
		{
			if (kind != CollectionButtonKind.Add)
			{
				return CanRemoveCollectionItem(rowData);
			}
			return CanAddCollectionItem(rowData);
		}
	}

		public bool HideProperty(IEdmPropertyInfo edmPropertyInfo, bool showAttachedProperties)

		public bool IsAttached(PropertyDescriptor descriptor)

		}

		}

		public TypeConverter GetConverter(IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo, Type ownerType, TypeConverter baseConverter)
		{
			return GetWrappedTypeConverter(propertyInfo, ownerType, baseConverter ?? GetTypeConverter(propertyInfo, ownerType));
		}

		public Type GetConvertToType(IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo)

		}

		}

		{

		}

		private TypeConverter GetTypeConverter(IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo, Type ownerType)
		{
			if (propertyInfo != null)
			{
				TypeConverter value = propertyInfo.Attributes.ReadAttributeProperty<TypeConverterAttribute, TypeConverter>((Func<TypeConverterAttribute, TypeConverter>)((TypeConverterAttribute x) => CreateTypeConverter(x, propertyInfo.PropertyType)), (TypeConverter)null).Value;
				if (value != null)
				{
					return value;
				}
			}
			DataColumnAttributes attributes = DataColumnAttributesProvider.GetAttributes((PropertyDescriptor)null, ownerType, (IAttributesProvider)null);
			return attributes.ReadAttributeProperty<TypeConverterAttribute, TypeConverter>((Func<TypeConverterAttribute, TypeConverter>)((TypeConverterAttribute x) => CreateTypeConverter(x, ownerType)), (TypeConverter)null).Value;
		}

		private TypeConverter GetWrappedTypeConverter(IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo, Type ownerType, TypeConverter baseConverter)
		{
			if (propertyInfo != null)
			{
				TypeConverter value = propertyInfo.Attributes.ReadAttributeProperty<TypeConverterWrapperAttribute, TypeConverter>((Func<TypeConverterWrapperAttribute, TypeConverter>)((TypeConverterWrapperAttribute x) => x.WrapTypeConverter(baseConverter)), (TypeConverter)null).Value;
				if (value != null)
				{
					return value;
				}
			}
			DataColumnAttributes attributes = DataColumnAttributesProvider.GetAttributes((PropertyDescriptor)null, ownerType, (IAttributesProvider)null);
			return attributes.ReadAttributeProperty<TypeConverterWrapperAttribute, TypeConverter>((Func<TypeConverterWrapperAttribute, TypeConverter>)((TypeConverterWrapperAttribute x) => x.WrapTypeConverter(baseConverter)), baseConverter).Value;
		}

		private TypeConverter CreateTypeConverter(TypeConverterAttribute attribute, Type propertyType)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(attribute.ConverterTypeName))
			{
				return CreateConverter(SafeTypeResolver.GetKnownUserType(attribute.ConverterTypeName, false), propertyType);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private TypeConverter CreateConverter(Type converterType, Type propertyType)
		{
			if (converterType == null || converterType.IsAbstract)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Type[] types = new Type[1] { typeof(Type) };
			ConstructorInfo constructor = converterType.GetConstructor(types);
			if (constructor != null)
			{
				return (TypeConverter)Activator.CreateInstance(converterType, propertyType);
			}
			ConstructorInfo constructor2 = converterType.GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes);
			if (constructor2 != null)
			{
				return Activator.CreateInstance(converterType, null) as TypeConverter;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	public class PropertyGridMenuHelper
	{
		private PropertyGridViewPopupMenu menu;

		private readonly PropertyGridView view;

		protected PropertyGridView View => view;

		protected PropertyGridControl PropertyGrid => view.PropertyGrid;

		protected internal PropertyGridViewPopupMenu Menu => menu ?? (menu = CreateMenu());

		public bool MenuIsOpen => menu.Return((PropertyGridViewPopupMenu x) => ((Popup)(object)x).IsOpen, () => false);

		protected virtual PropertyGridViewPopupMenu CreateMenu()
		{
			PropertyGridViewPopupMenu propertyGridViewPopupMenu = new PropertyGridViewPopupMenu((ILogicalOwner)(object)View)
			{
				DataContext = null
			};
			((Popup)(object)propertyGridViewPopupMenu).Opened += OnMenuOpened;
			((Popup)(object)propertyGridViewPopupMenu).Closed += OnMenuClosed;
			((Popup)(object)propertyGridViewPopupMenu).CustomPopupPlacementCallback = PositionPopup;
			return propertyGridViewPopupMenu;
		}

		public PropertyGridMenuHelper(PropertyGridView view)
		{
			this.view = view;
		}

		protected virtual void OnMenuOpened(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)Menu).Tag is bool && (bool)((FrameworkElement)(object)Menu).Tag)
			{
				object popupContent = ((PopupBase)Menu).PopupContent;
				PopupMenuBarControl val = (PopupMenuBarControl)((popupContent is PopupMenuBarControl) ? popupContent : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					NavigationTree.SelectElement((IBarsNavigationSupport)(object)val);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMenuClosed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (DependencyObject item in ((IEnumerable)((PopupMenu)Menu).Items).OfType<DependencyObject>())
			{
				item.ClearValue(RowData.RowDataProperty);
				item.ClearValue(RowControlBase.RowControlProperty);
			}
			((Collection<IBarItem>)(object)((PopupMenu)Menu).Items).Clear();
			Menu.UpdatePlacement();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)Menu).DataContext = null;
		}

		protected internal virtual bool CloseMenu(RowData data)
		{
			if (Menu == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = ((FrameworkElement)(object)Menu).DataContext == data;
			if (((Popup)(object)Menu).IsOpen)
			{
				((BarPopupBase)Menu).ClosePopup();
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void ProcessKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Apps && !((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)View.CellEditorOwner).CurrentCellEditor.If((InplaceEditorBase x) => x.IsEditorVisible).ReturnSuccess<InplaceEditorBase>() && (PropertyGrid.ShowMenuButtonInRows || PropertyGrid.ShowMenu != ShowMenuMode.OnMenuButtonClick) && Menu != null && View.SelectedItem != null)
			{
				if (((Popup)(object)Menu).IsOpen)
				{
					CloseMenu(View.SelectedItem);
				}
				else
				{
					ShowCommandsMenu(View.SelectedItem);
				}
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		public bool ShowCollectionMenu(RowData data, object relativeTo)
		{
			if (!data.IsCollectionRow)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IEnumerable<object> values = PropertyGrid.DataView.GetCollectionNewItemValues(data.Handle).With((IEnumerable x) => x.OfType<object>());
			if (values == null || !values.Skip(1).Any())
			{
				return false;
			}
			Prepare(data, relativeTo);
			Menu.RowHandle = data.Handle;
			Menu.MenuType = PropertyGridMenuType.NewItem;
			Menu.CreateItemsAction = delegate(RowControlBase rowControl)
			{
				CreateRowCollectionMenu(rowControl, values);
			};
			((Popup)(object)Menu).IsOpen = true;
			return true;
		}

		public void ShowCommandsMenu(RowData data)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)Menu).Tag = true;
			ShowCommandsMenu(data, view.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(data) as RowControl);
		}

		public void ShowCommandsMenu(RowData data, object relativeTo, PlacementMode? placement)
		{
			Prepare(data, relativeTo);
			if (placement.HasValue)
			{
				((Popup)(object)Menu).Placement = placement.Value;
			}
			Menu.RowHandle = data.Handle;
			Menu.MenuType = PropertyGridMenuType.Property;
			Menu.CreateItemsAction = CreateRowMenu;
			((Popup)(object)Menu).IsOpen = true;
		}

		private void CreateRowMenu(RowControlBase rowControl)
		{
			RowData rowData = rowControl.RowData;
			ExecuteMenuCustomization(rowData);
			foreach (DependencyObject item in ((IEnumerable)((PopupMenu)Menu).Items).OfType<DependencyObject>())
			{
				RowData.SetRowData(item, rowData);
				RowControlBase.SetRowControl(item, rowControl);
			}
		}

		private void CreateRowCollectionMenu(RowControlBase rowControl, IEnumerable<object> values)
		{
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0066: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_006e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ItemClickEventHandler val2 = default(ItemClickEventHandler);
			foreach (object value in values)
			{
				BarButtonItem val = new BarButtonItem();
				((BarItem)val).Content = Convert.ToString(value);
				((FrameworkContentElement)(object)val).Tag = value;
				((FrameworkContentElement)(object)val).ToolTip = ((ITypeInfoImplementer)value).Description;
				ItemClickEventHandler obj = val2;
				if (obj == null)
				{
					ItemClickEventHandler val3 = delegate(object o, ItemClickEventArgs e)
					{
						View.CollectionHelper.AddCollectionItem(rowControl.RowData, ((FrameworkContentElement)(object)e.Item).Tag);
					};
					ItemClickEventHandler val4 = val3;
					val2 = val3;
					obj = val4;
				}
				((BarItem)val).ItemClick += obj;
				((Collection<IBarItem>)(object)((PopupMenu)Menu).Items).Add((IBarItem)(object)val);
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteMenuCustomization(RowData data)
		{
			data.ExecuteMenuCustomizations((ILinksHolder)(object)Menu);
			PropertyGrid.ExecuteMenuCustomizations((DependencyObject)(object)Menu);
			data.Definition.ExecuteMenuCustomizations((DependencyObject)(object)Menu);
		}

		public void ShowCommandsMenu(RowData data, object relativeTo)
		{
			ShowCommandsMenu(data, relativeTo, null);
		}

		public void ShowCommandsMenu(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowData rowDataForHandle = PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.GetRowDataForHandle(handle);
			if (rowDataForHandle != null)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = View.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(rowDataForHandle);
				if (dependencyObject != null)
				{
					ShowCommandsMenu(rowDataForHandle, dependencyObject);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ShowCollectionMenu(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowData rowDataForHandle = PropertyGrid.RowDataGenerator.GetRowDataForHandle(handle);
			if (rowDataForHandle != null)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = View.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(rowDataForHandle);
				if (dependencyObject != null)
				{
					ShowCollectionMenu(rowDataForHandle, dependencyObject);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void Prepare(RowData data, object relativeTo)
		{
			Guard.ArgumentNotNull(relativeTo, "relativeTo");
			PrepareRowData(data);
			RowControl placementTarget = View.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(data) as RowControl;
			((Popup)(object)Menu).PlacementTarget = placementTarget;
			PrepareMenu(relativeTo);
		}

		protected void PrepareRowData(RowData data)
		{
			View.SelectionStrategy.SelectViaHandle(data.Handle);
		}

		protected void PrepareMenu(object relativeTo)
		{
			UIElement uIElement = relativeTo as UIElement;
			FrameworkRenderElementContext val = (FrameworkRenderElementContext)((relativeTo is FrameworkRenderElementContext) ? relativeTo : null);
			Point point;
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				point = uIElement.TransformToAncestor(((Popup)(object)Menu).PlacementTarget).Transform(new Point(uIElement.RenderSize.Width, uIElement.RenderSize.Height));
			}
			else
			{
				if (val == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException();
				}
				Point point2 = RenderTreeHelper.TransformToRoot(val).Transform(new Point(val.RenderSize.Width, val.RenderSize.Height));
				point = val.ElementHost.Parent.TransformToAncestor(((Popup)(object)Menu).PlacementTarget).Transform(point2);
			}
			Menu.Offset = ScreenHelper.GetScaledPoint(point, (Visual)((Popup)(object)Menu).PlacementTarget);
		}

		private CustomPopupPlacement[] PositionPopup(Size popupSize, Size targetSize, Point offset)
		{
			Point point = new Point(Menu.Offset.X - popupSize.Width, Menu.Offset.Y + 1.0);
			CustomPopupPlacement customPopupPlacement = new CustomPopupPlacement
			{
				Point = point,
				PrimaryAxis = PopupPrimaryAxis.Horizontal
			};
			return new CustomPopupPlacement[1] { customPopupPlacement };
		}
	}

	public class RowDataGenerator : IFilterClient, ISortClient, ISupportInitialize
	{
		[Flags]
		public enum RowDataProperties
		{
			None = 0,
			Defining = 1,
			Value = 2,

			Definition = 8,
			Miscellaneous = 0x10,
			Children = 0x40,
