			Self = 0x1F,
			All = 0x5F
		}

		private readonly Locker initializationLocker;

		private PropertyGridView view;

		private PropertyBuilder propertyBuilder;

		private DataViewBase dataView;

		private readonly WeakList<FieldInvalidatedEventHandler> fieldInvalidated = new WeakList<FieldInvalidatedEventHandler>();

		private bool isDataViewChangeValid = true;

		private bool shouldBringSelectionIntoView;

		private string currentSelectionPath;

		public DataViewBase DataView
		{
			get
			{
				return dataView;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != dataView)
				{
					DataViewBase oldValue = dataView;
					dataView = value;
					OnDataViewChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool Initializing => initializationLocker.IsLocked;

		public PropertyBuilder PropertyBuilder
		{
			get
			{
				return propertyBuilder;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!object.Equals(value, propertyBuilder))
				{
					PropertyBuilder oldValue = propertyBuilder;
					propertyBuilder = value;
					OnPropertyBuilderChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		public ImmediateActionsManager ImmediateActionsManager => View.With((PropertyGridView x) => x.ImmediateActionsManager);

		public PropertyGridView View
		{
			get
			{
				return view;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!object.Equals(value, view))
				{
					PropertyGridView oldValue = view;
					view = value;
					OnViewChanged(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		private Dictionary<RowHandle, RowData> CreatedItems { get; set; }

		public RowsCollectionView ItemsSource { get; private set; }

		public CategoriesCollectionView CategoriesSource { get; protected set; }

		public event FieldInvalidatedEventHandler FieldInvalidated
		{
			add
			{
				fieldInvalidated.Add(value);
			}
			remove
			{
				fieldInvalidated.Remove(value);
			}
		}

		protected void RaiseFieldInvalidated(FieldInvalidatedEventArgs args)
		{
			foreach (FieldInvalidatedEventHandler item in fieldInvalidated)
			{
				item(this, args);
			}
		}

		protected internal void RaiseFieldInvalidated(string fieldName)
		{
			RaiseFieldInvalidated(new FieldInvalidatedEventArgs(fieldName));
		}

		protected internal void RaiseFieldInvalidated(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RaiseFieldInvalidated(DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle));
		}

		public RowDataGenerator()
		{
			//IL_0030: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			CreatedItems = new Dictionary<RowHandle, RowData>();
			CategoriesSource = new CategoriesCollectionView(this);
			initializationLocker = new Locker();
			initializationLocker.Unlocked += delegate
			{
				OnInitialized();
			};
		}

		protected internal RowData ForceGetRowDataForHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			RowData rowData = RowDataFromHandle(handle);
			if (rowData != null)
			{
				return rowData;
			}
			if (!handle.IsRoot && DataView.GetParent(handle) == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			RowData rowData2 = ForceGetRowDataForHandle(DataView.GetParent(handle));
			RowData rowData3 = GetRowDataForHandle(handle).Do(delegate(RowData x)
			{
				PrepareRowDataForHandle(x, handle);
			});
			if (!handle.IsRoot)
			{
				rowData2.ForcedChildren.Add(rowData3);
			}
			return rowData3;
		}

		public RowData GetRowDataForHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowData rowData = RowDataFromHandle(handle);
			if (rowData == null)
			{
				rowData = CreateRowDataForHandle(handle);
				CreatedItems.Add(handle, rowData);
			}
			return rowData;
		}

		public void PrepareRowDataForHandle(RowData data, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (data != null && !data.IsReady)
			{
				PrepareRowDataForHandleOverride(data, handle, RowDataProperties.All);
				data.IsDirty = false;
				data.IsReady = true;
			}
		}

		protected void IsSelectedChanged(RowHandle handle, bool isSelected)
		{
			if (isSelected)
			{
				ItemsSource.MoveCurrentTo(handle);
			}
		}

		public void ClearRowDataForHandle(RowData data, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (data != null)
			{
				data.IsDirty = true;
				if (data.IsReady)
				{
					ClearRowDataForHandleOverride(data, handle);
					data.IsReady = false;
				}
				CreatedItems.Remove(handle);
			}
		}

		public bool UpdateRowDataForHandle(RowHandle handle, RowDataProperties updateKind)
		{
			RowData rowData = RowDataFromHandle(handle);
			if (rowData == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UpdateRowDataForHandleOverride(rowData, handle, updateKind);
			RaiseFieldInvalidated(new FieldInvalidatedEventArgs(DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle)));
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual RowData CreateRowDataForHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return new RowData
			{
				IsCategory = DataView.IsGroupRowHandle(handle)
			};
		}

		protected virtual void PrepareRowDataForHandleOverride(RowData data, RowHandle handle, RowDataProperties properties)
		{
			data.BeginInit();
			if ((properties & RowDataProperties.Defining) != RowDataProperties.None)
			{
				data.Handle = handle;
				data.RowDataGenerator = this;
				data.FullPath = DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle);
				data.ValueType = DataView.GetPropertyType(handle);
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if ((properties & RowDataProperties.Value) != RowDataProperties.None)
			{
				flag = data.SetValueInternal(DataView.GetValue(handle));
			}
			if ((properties & RowDataProperties.Definition) != RowDataProperties.None)
			{
				PropertyDefinitionBase definition = PropertyBuilder.GetDefinition(DataView, handle);
				data.Definition = definition;
				PropertyDefinitionBase definition2 = data.Definition;
				if (definition2 == null || !definition2.isStandardDefinition)
				{
					data.StandardDefinition = PropertyBuilder.GetStandardDefinition(DataView, handle, addToLogicalTree: true);
				}
			}
			if ((properties & RowDataProperties.Miscellaneous) != RowDataProperties.None)
			{
				data.Level = GetLevel(handle);
				BaseValidationError val = null;
				ValidationErrorSource source = ValidationErrorSource.Unknown;
				if (!flag)
				{
					val = View.GetValidationError(handle, out source);
					if (source != ValidationErrorSource.User)
					{
						val = null;
						source = ValidationErrorSource.Unknown;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					View.ClearNestedValidationErrors(handle);
				}
				val = val ?? CreateValidationError(DataView.GetValidationError(handle).Return((IEnumerable<string> x) => x.FirstOrDefault(), () => (string)null));
				data.SetValidationError(val, source);
				data.ShouldHighlightValue = ((View.PropertyGrid == null || !View.PropertyGrid.HighlightNonDefaultValues) ? ((bool?)null) : new bool?(DataView.ShouldSerializeValue(handle)));
				string description = DataView.GetDescription(handle);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(description))
				{
					data.Description = description;
				}
				data.IsCollectionRow = DataView.IsCollectionHandle(handle);
				if (data.IsCollectionRow)
				{
					data.ActualTypeInfo = DataView.GetSelectedCollectionNewItem(handle) as DevExpress.Mvvm.Native.TypeInfo;
				}
				if (CalcIsModifiableCollectionItem(data, handle))
				{
					data.RecalcCanShowCollectionButton();
				}
				data.SetEditableObject((((EditableDataObject)data).Value == null) ? null : new EditableObjectWrapper(((EditableDataObject)data).Value, this, handle));
				if (DataView.GetIsStandardValuesSupported(handle))
				{
					data.StandardValues = DataView.GetStandardValues(handle);
				}
			}
			if ((properties & RowDataProperties.ExpandSettings) != RowDataProperties.None)
			{
				data.CanExpand = DataView.CanExpand(handle);
				if (data.CanExpand)
				{
					data.IsExpanded = GetExpanded(handle);
				}
			}
			if (data.Definition != null)
			{
				data.Definition.Apply(data, (properties & RowDataProperties.Definition) != 0);
			}
			data.EndInit();
			if (handle == DataView.CurrentRow)
			{
				UpdateSelectedPropertyValue();
			}
		}

		protected virtual int GetLevel(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowHandle parent = DataView.GetParent(handle);
			if (parent == null || parent.IsRoot || DataView.IsGroupRowHandle(parent))
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return GetLevel(parent) + 1;
		}

		private bool CalcIsModifiableCollectionItem(RowData data, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (View.PropertyGrid.ReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!data.IsCollectionRow)
			{
				return DataView.CanRemoveCollectionItem(handle);
			}
			if (!DataView.CanAddCollectionNewItem(handle))
			{
				return !DataView.IsReadOnlyCollection(handle);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void SetViewValidationError(RowHandle handle, BaseValidationError error, ValidationErrorSource source)
		{
			if (View != null && handle != null)
			{
				View.SetValidationError(handle, error, source);
			}
		}

		protected virtual BaseValidationError CreateValidationError(string errorText)
		{
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0011: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(errorText))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new BaseValidationError((object)errorText);
		}

		protected internal PropertyDefinitionBase GetStandardDefinition(RowHandle handle, bool addToLogicalTree)
		{
			return PropertyBuilder.GetStandardDefinition(DataView, handle, addToLogicalTree);
		}

		protected virtual void ClearRowDataForHandleOverride(RowData data, RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (data.Handle == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (View.GetValidationError(handle, out var _) != null)
			{
				View.ClearValidationError(handle);
			}
			foreach (RowData forcedChild in data.ForcedChildren)
			{
				if (!ItemsSource.SourceCollection.OfType<RowHandle>().Contains(forcedChild.Handle))
				{
					ClearRowDataForHandle(forcedChild, forcedChild.Handle);
				}
			}
			data.IsSelected = false;
			data.Definition = null;
			data.StandardDefinition = null;
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateRowDataForHandleOverride(RowData data, RowHandle handle, RowDataProperties updateKind)
		{
			bool flag = object.Equals(data.Handle, handle) && object.Equals(data.FullPath, DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle)) && data.ValueType == DataView.GetPropertyType(handle) && data.Definition != null;
			PrepareRowDataForHandleOverride(data, handle, flag ? RowDataProperties.SelfWithoutDefinition : RowDataProperties.Self);
		}

		protected internal virtual List<RowData> GetMatchingRowDataList(Func<RowData, bool> predicate)
		{
			return CreatedItems.Values.Where(predicate).ToList();
		}

		public bool HasRowDataForHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return CreatedItems.ContainsKey(handle);
		}

		public RowData RowDataFromHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == null || !CreatedItems.TryGetValue(handle, out var value))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected internal bool GetExpanded(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return DataView.IsExpanded(handle);
		}

		public bool SetExpanded(RowHandle handle, bool value)
		{
			if (DataView == null)
			{
				return value;
			}
			bool flag = PropertyBuilder.PropertyGrid.RaiseCustomExpand(handle, (RowHandle h) => value);
			DataView.SetIsExpanded(handle, flag);
			return flag;
		}

		public void SetValue(RowHandle handle, object value)
		{
			if (View != null)
			{
				Exception ex = DataView.SetValue(handle, value);
				if (ex != null)
				{
					View.SetValidationError(handle, (BaseValidationError)(object)new CellValidationError(ex.Message, ex, (ErrorType)1), ValidationErrorSource.Internal);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal void Invalidate(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle == RowHandle.Root)
			{
				ItemsSource.Invalidate();
			}
			else
			{
				if (handle.IsInvalid || ItemsSource.Contains(handle))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (View.PropertyGrid.DataController.IsValid)
				{
					UpdateRowDataForHandle(handle, RowDataProperties.All);
					return;
				}
				string fieldName = DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle);
				View.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)delegate
				{
					DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(fieldName).Do(delegate(RowHandle x)
					{
						if (!UpdateRowDataForHandle(x, RowDataProperties.All))
						{
							RaiseFieldInvalidated(x);
						}
					});
				});
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateDataView()
		{
			DataView.Do(delegate(DataViewBase x)
			{
				x.InvalidateDataView();
			});
		}

		private void InvalidateData()
		{
			initializationLocker.DoIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
			{
				DataView.Do(delegate(DataViewBase x)
				{
					x.Invalidate(RowHandle.Root);
				});
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewChanged(PropertyGridView oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.ItemsSource = null;
				oldValue.CategoriesSource = null;
			}
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.CategoriesSource = CategoriesSource;
			}
			UpdateViewItemsSource();
			InvalidateDataView();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataViewChanged(DataViewBase oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.CurrentRowChanged -= CurrentRowChanged;
				oldValue.Dispose();
			}
			if (DataView != null)
			{
				DataView.FilterClient = this;
				DataView.SortClient = this;
				DataView.CurrentRowChanged += CurrentRowChanged;
			}
			InvalidateDataViewChange();
		}

		protected void CurrentRowChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			currentSelectionPath = DataView.CurrentRow.With(DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(currentSelectionPath))
			{
				shouldBringSelectionIntoView = true;
			}
			View.SelectionStrategy.Invalidate();
			View.PropertyGrid.SelectedPropertyPath = DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(DataView.CurrentRow);
			UpdateSelectedPropertyValue();
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedPropertyValue()
		{
			View.PropertyGrid.SelectedPropertyValue = DataView.CurrentRow.With(DataView.GetValue);
		}

		private void InvalidateDataViewChange()
		{
			InvalidateDataView();
			isDataViewChangeValid = false;
		}

		public void UpdateDataViewChange()
		{
			if (!isDataViewChangeValid)
			{
				isDataViewChangeValid = true;
				ItemsSource.Do(delegate(RowsCollectionView x)
				{
					x.ImmediateInvalidate();
				});
				CategoriesSource.ImmediateInvalidate();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyBuilderChanged(PropertyBuilder oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.Changed -= OnPropertyBuilderDescriptionsChanged;
			}
			if (PropertyBuilder != null)
			{
				PropertyBuilder.Changed += OnPropertyBuilderDescriptionsChanged;
			}
			InvalidateDataView();
		}

		private void OnPropertyBuilderDescriptionsChanged(object sender, PropertyBuilderChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (args.Definition == null || args.ChangeKind == PropertyBuilderChangeKind.Reset)
			{
				InvalidateDataView();
			}
			else
			{
				OnPropertyBuilderDescriptionsChangedImpl(sender, args);
			}
		}

		private void OnPropertyBuilderDescriptionsChangedImpl(object sender, PropertyBuilderChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (!object.Equals(null, args.Definition.Builder))
			{
				switch (args.ChangeKind)
				{
				case PropertyBuilderChangeKind.VisualClientProperties:
					InvalidateDataView();
					break;
				case PropertyBuilderChangeKind.CoreProperties:
					InvalidateData();
					break;
				case PropertyBuilderChangeKind.Visibility:
					InvalidateDataView();
					break;
				case PropertyBuilderChangeKind.MenuProperties:
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		IEnumerable<RowHandle> ISortClient.GetSortedChildren(RowHandle parent, IEnumerable<RowHandle> children)
		{
			PropertyGridSortingEventArgs args = new PropertyGridSortingEventArgs(children, DataView, this);
			View.PropertyGrid.Do(delegate(PropertyGridControl x)
			{
				x.RaiseSortEvent(args, PropertyBuilder.GetActualSortMode(parent, this));
			});
			foreach (RowInfo item in args.ResultCollection ?? args.SourceCollection)
			{
				yield return item.Handle;
			}
		}

		object IFilterClient.GetObject(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return new RowInfo(DataView, handle, this);
		}

		bool IFilterClient.GetIsVisible(RowHandle handle)
		{
			return ((RowInfo)((IFilterClient)this).GetObject(handle)).IsVisible;
		}

		object IFilterClient.GetDefaultObject()
		{
			return new RowInfo();
		}

		public void VerifySelection(RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowData rowData = View.SelectedItem.If((RowData x) => !x.IsDirty);
			RowHandle objB = rowData.With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
			if (rowData != null && ((handle != null && object.Equals(handle, objB)) || (((InplaceEditorOwnerBase)View.CellEditorOwner).ActiveEditor != null && string.Equals(rowData.FullPath, DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle(handle)))))
			{
				UpdateFocus(rowData);
				return;
			}
			RowData rowData2 = handle.With(ForceGetRowDataForHandle);
			rowData.If((RowData x) => x.IsSelected).Do(delegate(RowData x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = false;
			});
			rowData2.If((RowData x) => !x.IsSelected).Do(delegate(RowData x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = true;
			});
			View.SelectedItem = rowData2;
			UpdateFocus(rowData2);
		}

		private void UpdateFocus(RowData currentSelection)
		{
			if (currentSelection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			PGVirtualizingStackPanel pGVirtualizingStackPanel = LayoutHelper.FindElementByType((FrameworkElement)View, typeof(PGVirtualizingStackPanel), (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null) as PGVirtualizingStackPanel;
			string text = currentSelection.Handle.With(DataView.GetFieldNameByHandle);
			if (pGVirtualizingStackPanel != null)
			{
				int num = ItemsSource.IndexOf(currentSelection);
				if (num >= 0 && num < ItemsSource.Count && !ItemsSource.AsyncUpdatePending && shouldBringSelectionIntoView && currentSelectionPath == text)
				{
					pGVirtualizingStackPanel.BringIndexIntoView(num);
					shouldBringSelectionIntoView = false;
				}
			}
			if (!View.IsKeyboardFocusWithin || !currentSelection.IsSelected)
			{
				return;
			}
			RowControlBase rowControl = View.GetRowControl(currentSelection);
			if (rowControl == null || rowControl.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				return;
			}
			CellEditor cellEditor = LayoutHelper.FindElement((FrameworkElement)rowControl, (Predicate<FrameworkElement>)((FrameworkElement x) => x.GetType() == typeof(CellEditor) && PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid(x) == View.PropertyGrid)) as CellEditor;
			PropertyGridControl propertyGridControl = (Keyboard.FocusedElement as DependencyObject).With(PropertyGridHelper.GetPropertyGrid);
			PropertyGridControl propertyGrid = View.PropertyGrid;
			if (propertyGridControl == null || propertyGridControl == propertyGrid || !TreeHelper.GetParents<PropertyGridControl>((DependencyObject)propertyGridControl, true, true).Contains(propertyGrid))
			{
				cellEditor.Do(delegate(CellEditor x)
				{
					((InplaceEditorBase)x).Edit.SetKeyboardFocus();
				});
				if (cellEditor == null)
				{
					View.Focus();
				}
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<RowHandle> IterateBranch(RowHandle rowHandle)
		{
			RowHandle handle = rowHandle;
			while (IsValidHandle(handle))
			{
				yield return handle;
				handle = DataView.GetParent(handle);
			}
		}

		private static bool IsValidHandle(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (handle != null)
			{
				if (!handle.IsInvalid)
				{
					return !handle.IsRoot;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ScrollTo(string path)
		{
			if (View.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == GeneratorStatus.NotStarted)
			{
				View.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)delegate
				{
					ScrollTo(path);
				});
				return;
			}
			DataView.MakeRowVisible(DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(path));
			View.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)delegate
			{
				RowHandle handleByFieldName = DataView.GetHandleByFieldName(path);
				int num = DataView.VisibleHandles.IndexOf(handleByFieldName);
				if (num != -1)
				{
					View.ScrollToIndex(num);
				}
			});
		}

		public void ScrollToAsync(string path)
		{
			View.ImmediateActionsManager.EnqueueAction((Action)delegate
			{
				ScrollTo(path);
			});
		}

		public void BeginInit()
		{
			initializationLocker.Lock();
		}

		public void EndInit()
		{
			initializationLocker.Unlock();
		}

		protected virtual void OnInitialized()
		{
			ItemsSource = new RowsCollectionView(this);
			UpdateViewItemsSource();
			InvalidateDataView();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateViewItemsSource()
		{
			if (View != null && ItemsSource != null)
			{
				View.ItemsSource = ItemsSource;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool ShouldProcessCollectionChangedAsynchronously()
		{
			if (View == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return View.ShouldProcessCollectionChangedAsynchronously();
		}
	}
	public class FieldInvalidatedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public string FullPath { get; private set; }

		public FieldInvalidatedEventArgs(string fullPath)
		{
			FullPath = fullPath;
		}
	}
	public delegate void FieldInvalidatedEventHandler(object source, FieldInvalidatedEventArgs args);
	public enum SelectItemResult
	{
		No,
		Scroll,
		Select
	}
	public static class TreeViewHelper
	{
		public static SelectItemResult SelectItem(this PropertyGridView treeView, object item)
		{
			return ExpandAndSelectItem(treeView, item);
		}

		public static SelectItemResult ScrollTo(this PropertyGridView treeView, object item)
		{
			return ExpandAnd(treeView, item, delegate(RowControl x)
			{
				x.BringIntoView();
			});
		}

		public static bool ScrollToIndex(this PropertyGridView treeView, int index)
		{
			if (!(LayoutHelper.FindElementByType((FrameworkElement)treeView, typeof(PGVirtualizingStackPanel), (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null) is PGVirtualizingStackPanel pGVirtualizingStackPanel))
			{
				return false;
			}
			pGVirtualizingStackPanel.BringIndexIntoView(index);
			return true;
		}

		private static SelectItemResult ExpandAnd(ItemsControl parentContainer, object itemToSelect, Action<RowControl> action)
		{
			if (parentContainer.Items.Count == 0)
			{
				return SelectItemResult.No;
			}
			int num = parentContainer.Items.IndexOf(itemToSelect);
			if (num > -1)
			{
				if (parentContainer.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(itemToSelect) is RowControl obj)
				{
					action(obj);
					return SelectItemResult.Select;
				}
				ScrollInto(parentContainer, itemToSelect);
				return SelectItemResult.Scroll;
			}
			return SelectItemResult.No;
		}

		private static SelectItemResult ExpandAndSelectItem(ItemsControl parentContainer, object itemToSelect)
		{
			if (parentContainer.Items.Count == 0)
			{
				return SelectItemResult.No;
			}
			int num = parentContainer.Items.IndexOf(itemToSelect);
			if (num > -1)
			{
				if (parentContainer.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(itemToSelect) is RowControl rowControl)
				{
					rowControl.IsSelected = true;
					return SelectItemResult.Select;
				}
				ScrollInto(parentContainer, itemToSelect);
				return SelectItemResult.Scroll;
			}
			return SelectItemResult.No;
		}

		private static void ScrollInto(ItemsControl parentContainer, object itemToSelect)
		{
			PGVirtualizingStackPanel input = LayoutHelper.FindElementByType((FrameworkElement)parentContainer, typeof(PGVirtualizingStackPanel), (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null) as PGVirtualizingStackPanel;
			input.Do(delegate(PGVirtualizingStackPanel x)
			{
				x.BringIndexIntoView(parentContainer.Items.IndexOf(itemToSelect));
			});
		}
	}
	public abstract class NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateActionBase : IAggregateAction, IAction
	{
		bool IAggregateAction.CanAggregate(IAction action)
		{
			return CanAggregateOverride(action);
		}

		void IAction.Execute()
		{
			ExecuteOverride();
		}

		protected abstract bool CanAggregateOverride(IAction action);

		protected abstract void ExecuteOverride();
	}
	public class NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateAction<TDataView> : NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateActionBase where TDataView : DataViewBase
	{
		private readonly BaseRowsCollectionView<TDataView> owner;

		public NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateAction(BaseRowsCollectionView<TDataView> owner)
		{
			this.owner = owner;
			this.owner.asyncUpdateLocker.Lock();
		}

		protected override bool CanAggregateOverride(IAction action)
		{
			if (!(action is NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateAction<TDataView>))
			{
				return false;
			}
			owner.asyncUpdateLocker.Unlock();
			return true;
		}

		protected override void ExecuteOverride()
		{
			owner.asyncUpdateLocker.Unlock();
			owner.ProcessPendingActions();
		}
	}
	public abstract class BaseRowsCollectionView<TDataView> : CollectionView where TDataView : DataViewBase
	{
		protected class NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs : NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs
		{
			public NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(IList changedItems)
				: base(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, changedItems, changedItems)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action)
				: base(action)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, object changedItem)
				: base(action, changedItem)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, object changedItem, int index)
				: base(action, changedItem, index)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, IList changedItems)
				: base(action, changedItems)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, IList changedItems, int startingIndex)
				: base(action, changedItems, startingIndex)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, object newItem, object oldItem)
				: base(action, newItem, oldItem)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, object newItem, object oldItem, int index)
				: base(action, newItem, oldItem, index)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, IList newItems, IList oldItems)
				: base(action, newItems, oldItems)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, IList newItems, IList oldItems, int startingIndex)
				: base(action, newItems, oldItems, startingIndex)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, object changedItem, int index, int oldIndex)
				: base(action, changedItem, index, oldIndex)
			{
			}

			private NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction action, IList changedItems, int index, int oldIndex)
				: base(action, changedItems, index, oldIndex)
			{
			}
		}

		private IList<RowHandle> visibleHandles = new List<RowHandle>();

		private readonly RowDataGenerator generator;

		private TDataView dataView;

		internal readonly Locker asyncUpdateLocker = new Locker();

		private readonly Locker processPendingActionsLocker = new Locker();

		private static readonly Action<CollectionView> invalidateEnumerableWrapper = ReflectionHelper.CreateInstanceMethodHandler<CollectionView, Action<CollectionView>>((CollectionView)null, "InvalidateEnumerableWrapper", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic, (int?)null, (Type[])null, true);

		private readonly Queue<NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs> pendingActions;

		private bool rootUpdatePending = true;

		private static readonly RowData fakeData = new RowData();

		public override object CurrentItem => (base.CurrentItem as RowHandle).With(generator.RowDataFromHandle);

		private ImmediateActionsManager ActionsManager => Generator.ImmediateActionsManager;

		protected TDataView DataView
		{
			get
			{
				return dataView;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (dataView != value)
				{
					TDataView oldView = dataView;
					dataView = value;
					DataViewChanged(oldView, dataView);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateVisibleHandles()
		{
			visibleHandles = new List<RowHandle>(GetVisibleHandles());
		}

		protected abstract IList<RowHandle> GetVisibleHandles();

		protected void EnqueueOrExecuteAction(IAction action)
		{
			if (ActionsManager == null)
			{
				action.Execute();
			}
			else
			{
				ActionsManager.EnqueueAction(action);
			}
		}

		protected void ExecuteActions()
		{
			if (ActionsManager != null)
			{
				ActionsManager.ExecuteActions();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DataViewChanged(TDataView oldView, TDataView newView)
		{
			if (oldView != null)
			{
				UnsubscribeVisibleHandlesChanged(oldView, OnCollectionChangedAsync);
				UnsubscribeInvisibleHandlesChanged(oldView, OnInvisibleHandlesChangedAsync);
			}
			if (newView != null)
			{
				SubscribeVisibleHandlesChanged(newView, OnCollectionChangedAsync);
				SubscribeInvisibleHandlesChanged(newView, OnInvisibleHandlesChangedAsync);
			}
			ProcessCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
		}

		protected abstract void UnsubscribeVisibleHandlesChanged(TDataView view, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler handler);

		protected abstract void SubscribeVisibleHandlesChanged(TDataView view, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler handler);

		protected abstract void UnsubscribeInvisibleHandlesChanged(TDataView view, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler handler);

		protected abstract void SubscribeInvisibleHandlesChanged(TDataView view, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler handler);

		protected void OnInvisibleHandlesChangedAsync(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnCollectionChangedAsync(sender, new NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs(args.NewItems));
		}

		protected void OnCollectionChangedAsync(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (processPendingActionsLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				EnqueueActions(args);
			}
			else if (AsyncUpdatePending || Generator.ShouldProcessCollectionChangedAsynchronously())
			{
				EnqueueActions(args);
				EnqueueOrExecuteAction((IAction)(object)new NotifyCollectionChangedAggregateAction<TDataView>(this));
			}
			else
			{
				OnCollectionChanged(sender, args);
			}
		}

		private void EnqueueActions(params NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs[] actions)
		{
			if (actions.Any((NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs x) => x.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset))
			{
				pendingActions.Clear();
				pendingActions.Enqueue(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
			}
			else if (pendingActions.Count == 0 || pendingActions.Peek().Action != NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				foreach (NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs item in actions)
				{
					pendingActions.Enqueue(item);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ProcessPendingActions()
		{
			CheckUpdateRoot();
			if (processPendingActionsLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = processPendingActionsLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				while (pendingActions.Count > 0)
				{
					NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args = pendingActions.Dequeue();
					if (!ProcessNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(args, updateCache: true))
					{
						ProcessCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
					}
				}
				invalidateEnumerableWrapper(this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private bool ProcessNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args, bool updateCache)
		{
			if (args is NotifyInvisibleHandlesChangedEventArgs)
			{
				foreach (RowHandle newItem in args.NewItems)
				{
					Generator.Invalidate(newItem);
				}
				return true;
			}
			switch (args.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				ProcessAdd(args, updateCache);
				return true;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				ProcessRemove(args, updateCache);
				return true;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				ProcessReplace(args, updateCache);
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void CheckUpdateRoot()
		{
			if (rootUpdatePending)
			{
				Generator.ClearRowDataForHandle(Generator.RowDataFromHandle(RowHandle.Root), RowHandle.Root);
				Generator.ForceGetRowDataForHandle(RowHandle.Root);
				rootUpdatePending = false;
			}
		}

		public BaseRowsCollectionView(RowDataGenerator generator)
			: base(Enumerable.Empty<object>())
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Locker val = collectionChangedLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				this.generator = generator;
				pendingActions = new Queue<NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs>();
				ImmediateInvalidate();
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		public void Invalidate()
		{
			if (DataView != Generator.DataView)
			{
				DataView = Generator.DataView as TDataView;
			}
			rootUpdatePending = true;
		}

		protected override void ProcessCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (!collectionChangedLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				CheckUpdateRoot();
				if (args.Action != NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
				{
					UpdateVisibleHandles();
				}
				if (!ProcessNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(args, updateCache: false))
				{
					ProcessReplace(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, new List<RowHandle>(GetVisibleHandles()), new List<RowHandle>(VisibleHandles), 0), updateCache: true);
				}
				invalidateEnumerableWrapper(this);
			}
		}

		protected void ProcessReplace(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args, bool updateCache)
		{
			int num = args.OldStartingIndex;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			IEnumerator enumerator = args.OldItems.GetEnumerator();
			IEnumerator enumerator2 = args.NewItems.GetEnumerator();
			if (args.OldItems.Count > args.NewItems.Count)
			{
				enumerator2 = (from x in Enumerable.Range(0, args.OldItems.Count - args.NewItems.Count)
					select (object)null).Concat(args.NewItems.OfType<object>()).GetEnumerator();
			}
			HashSet<RowHandle> hashSet = new HashSet<RowHandle>(args.NewItems.OfType<RowHandle>().Intersect(args.OldItems.OfType<RowHandle>()));
			while ((flag = enumerator.MoveNext()) | (flag2 = enumerator2.MoveNext()))
			{
				RowHandle rowHandle = (flag ? enumerator.Current : null) as RowHandle;
				RowHandle rowHandle2 = (flag2 ? enumerator2.Current : null) as RowHandle;
				RowData rowData = generator.RowDataFromHandle(rowHandle);
				try
				{
					if (rowHandle == rowHandle2)
					{
						if (rowData != null)
						{
							Generator.UpdateRowDataForHandle(rowHandle2, RowDataGenerator.RowDataProperties.All);
						}
						continue;
					}
					if ((args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace || args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset) && hashSet.Contains(rowHandle))
					{
						Generator.UpdateRowDataForHandle(rowHandle, RowDataGenerator.RowDataProperties.All);
					}
					object obj = ((object)rowData) ?? ((object)rowHandle);
					object obj2 = ((rowData != null && rowHandle2 != null) ? ((object)ForceCreateRowData(num, GetVisibleHandles())) : ((object)rowHandle2));
					if (rowHandle != null && rowHandle2 != null)
					{
						VisibleHandles[num] = rowHandle2;
						OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, obj2, obj, num));
					}
					else if (rowHandle != null)
					{
						VisibleHandles.RemoveAt(num);
						OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove, obj, num));
						num--;
					}
					else if (rowHandle2 != null)
					{
						VisibleHandles.Insert(num, rowHandle2);
						OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add, obj2, num));
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					num++;
				}
				if (!hashSet.Contains(rowHandle))
				{
					generator.ClearRowDataForHandle(rowData, rowHandle);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void ProcessRemove(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args, bool updateCache)
		{
			int index = args.OldStartingIndex + args.OldItems.Count - 1;
			foreach (RowHandle item in args.OldItems.OfType<RowHandle>().Reverse())
			{
				RowData rowData = generator.RowDataFromHandle(item);
				if (updateCache)
				{
					VisibleHandles.RemoveAt(index);
				}
				OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove, rowData, index--));
				generator.ClearRowDataForHandle(rowData, item);
			}
		}

		protected void ProcessAdd(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args, bool updateCache)
		{
			int newStartingIndex = args.NewStartingIndex;
			foreach (RowHandle newItem in args.NewItems)
			{
				if (updateCache)
				{
					VisibleHandles.Insert(newStartingIndex, newItem);
				}
				OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add, fakeData, newStartingIndex++));
			}
		}

		public void ImmediateInvalidate()
		{
			DataView = Generator.DataView as TDataView;
		}

		public override bool Contains(object item)
		{
			return base.Contains(item);
		}

		public override object GetItemAt(int index)
		{
			if (AsyncUpdatePending)
			{
				ExecuteActions();
			}
			return ForceCreateRowData(index, VisibleHandles);
		}

		protected RowData ForceCreateRowData(int index, IList<RowHandle> handleSource)
		{
			if (handleSource.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			RowHandle rowHandle = handleSource[index];
			if (rowHandle.IsInvalid)
			{
				return null;
			}
			RowData rowDataForHandle = generator.GetRowDataForHandle(rowHandle);
			generator.PrepareRowDataForHandle(rowDataForHandle, rowHandle);
			return rowDataForHandle;
		}

		public override int IndexOf(object item)
		{
			RowHandle rowHandle = (item as RowHandle) ?? (item as RowData).With((RowData x) => x.Handle);
			if (rowHandle == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return VisibleHandles.IndexOf(rowHandle);
		}

		protected override IEnumerator GetEnumerator()
		{
			return EnumerateChildren().GetEnumerator();
		}

		protected IEnumerable EnumerateChildren()
		{
			foreach (RowHandle visibleHandle in VisibleHandles)
			{
				yield return generator.RowDataFromHandle(visibleHandle);
			}
		}
	}

				base.DataView.CurrentGroup = rowHandle;
			}
		}

		{
			base.OnCollectionChanged(args);
			UpdateCurrentGroup();
		}

		private void OnCurrentGroupChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateCurrentGroup();
		}

		private void UpdateCurrentGroup()
		{
			if (base.DataView != null)
			{
				if (base.DataView.VisibleGroupHandles.Contains(base.DataView.CurrentGroup))
				{
					MoveCurrentTo(base.DataView.CurrentGroup);
				}
				else
				{
					MoveCurrentToFirst();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class StandardDefinitionsProvider
	{
		private class CacheRecord
		{
			public PropertyDefinitionBase Value { get; private set; }

			public PropertyDefinitionBase ValidValue
			{
				get
				{
					if (!NeedsValidation)
					{
						return Value;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			public bool NeedsValidation { get; set; }

			public CacheRecord(PropertyDefinitionBase value)
			{
				Value = value;
				NeedsValidation = false;
			}

			public bool Validate(PropertyDefinitionBase value)
			{
				NeedsValidation = !Value.Equals(value);
				return !NeedsValidation;
			}
		}

		private readonly PropertyBuilder builder;

		private readonly Dictionary<RowHandle, CacheRecord> cache;

		private readonly Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase> nonLogicalCache;

		private readonly Stack<RowHandle> currentHandle = new Stack<RowHandle>().Do(delegate(Stack<RowHandle> x)
		{
			x.Push(null);
		});

		private readonly PropertyGridControl propertyGrid;

		private readonly HashSet<RowHandle> skipAdditionHandles;

		public RowHandle CurrentHandle
		{
			get
			{
				return currentHandle.Peek();
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != null && nonLogicalCache.ContainsKey(value))
				{
					nonLogicalCache.Remove(value);
				}
				if (value == null && CurrentHandle != null)
				{
					if (nonLogicalCache.ContainsKey(CurrentHandle))
					{
						nonLogicalCache.Remove(CurrentHandle);
					}
					currentHandle.Pop();
				}
				else
				{
					currentHandle.Push(value);
				}
			}
		}

		public PropertyDefinitionBase CurrentDefinition
		{
			get
			{
				if (skipAdditionHandles.Contains(CurrentHandle))
				{
					if (!nonLogicalCache.ContainsKey(CurrentHandle))
					{
						return null;
					}
					return nonLogicalCache[CurrentHandle];
				}
				if (CurrentHandle == null || !cache.ContainsKey(CurrentHandle))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return cache[CurrentHandle].ValidValue;
			}
			set
			{
				if (CurrentHandle == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (skipAdditionHandles.Contains(CurrentHandle))
				{
					nonLogicalCache[CurrentHandle] = value;
				}
				else
				{
					if (cache.TryGetValue(CurrentHandle, out var value2) && value2.Validate(value))
					{
						return;
					}
					if (value2 != null)
					{
						value2.Value.Do(delegate(PropertyDefinitionBase x)
						{
							x.Builder = null;
						});
						RemoveLogicalChild(value2.Value);
					}
					cache[CurrentHandle] = new CacheRecord(value);
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerator<PropertyDefinitionBase> CreatedDefinitions
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (CacheRecord value in cache.Values)
				{
					if (!value.NeedsValidation)
					{
						yield return value.ValidValue;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public StandardDefinitionsProvider(PropertyBuilder builder)
		{
			this.builder = builder;
			propertyGrid = builder.PropertyGrid;
			cache = new Dictionary<RowHandle, CacheRecord>();
			nonLogicalCache = new Dictionary<RowHandle, PropertyDefinitionBase>();
			skipAdditionHandles = new HashSet<RowHandle>();
		}

		public void ChangeHandle(RowHandle oldHandle, RowHandle newHandle)
		{
			CacheRecord value = null;
			if (cache.TryGetValue(oldHandle, out value))
			{
				cache.Remove(oldHandle);
				cache[newHandle] = value;
			}
		}

		public void ClearUnlinkedDefinitions()
		{
			DataViewBase dataViewBase = builder.With((PropertyBuilder x) => x.PropertyGrid.DataView);
			foreach (RowHandle item in cache.Keys.ToList())
			{
				CacheRecord cacheRecord = cache[item];
				if (cacheRecord == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag = true;
				if (dataViewBase != null && dataViewBase.GetFieldNameByHandle(item) == null)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if (cacheRecord.NeedsValidation || !flag)
				{
					PropertyDefinitionBase value = cacheRecord.Value;
					cache.Remove(item);
					if (value != null)
					{
						value.Builder = null;
						RemoveLogicalChild(value);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public PropertyDefinitionBase GetStandardDefinition(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle, bool addToLogicalTree)
		{
			//IL_0062: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0068: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (handle == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			try
			{
				CurrentHandle = handle;
				if (!addToLogicalTree)
				{
					nonLogicalCache[handle] = CurrentDefinition;
					skipAdditionHandles.Add(handle);
				}
				if (view.IsGroupRowHandle(handle))
				{
					return GetStandardDefinitionForGroupHandle(view, handle);
				}
				if (!CacheExists(handle))
				{
					IEdmPropertyInfo edmPropertyInfo = view.GetEdmPropertyInfo(handle);
					if (edmPropertyInfo != null)
					{
						GenerateEditorOptions val = GenerateEditorOptions.ForRuntime();
						RuntimeEditingContext val2 = new RuntimeEditingContext((object)this, (EditingContextTrace)null);
						PropertyDefinitionsGenerator propertyDefinitionsGenerator = new PropertyDefinitionsGenerator(ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)(object)val2), propertyGrid, view, handle, addToLogicalTree);
						EditorsSource.GenerateEditor(edmPropertyInfo, (EditorsGeneratorBase)(object)propertyDefinitionsGenerator, (Func<IEdmPropertyInfo, ForeignKeyInfo>)null, val.CollectionProperties, val.GuessImageProperties, val.GuessDisplayMembers, false);
						CurrentDefinition.isStandardDefinition = true;
						if (addToLogicalTree)
						{
							AddLogicalChild(CurrentDefinition);
							CurrentDefinition.Builder = builder;
						}
					}
				}
				return CurrentDefinition;
			}
			finally
			{
				skipAdditionHandles.Remove(handle);
				CurrentHandle = null;
			}
		}

		private bool CacheExists(RowHandle handle)
		{
			if (cache.ContainsKey(handle))
			{
				return !cache[handle].NeedsValidation;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private RowHandle GetParentHandle(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle)
		{
			RowHandle parent = view.GetParent(handle);
			if (!view.IsGroupRowHandle(handle))
			{
				return parent;
			}
			return view.GetParent(parent);
		}

		private PropertyDefinitionBase GetStandardDefinitionForGroupHandle(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle)
		{
			CategoryDefinition categoryDefinition = new CategoryDefinition();
			categoryDefinition.isStandardDefinition = true;
			categoryDefinition.HeaderShowMode = HeaderShowMode.OnlyHeader;
			cache[handle] = new CacheRecord(categoryDefinition);
			AddLogicalChild(categoryDefinition);
			return categoryDefinition;
		}

		private void AddLogicalChild(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			builder.InternalAddLogicalChild(definition);
		}

		private void RemoveLogicalChild(PropertyDefinitionBase definition)
		{
			builder.InternalRemoveLogicalChild(definition);
		}
	}
	internal sealed class StandardValuesSource : ObservableCollectionConverter<object, object>
	{
		private bool useTypeConverter;

		private readonly DataViewBase view;

		private readonly RowHandle handle;

		public bool UseTypeConverter
		{
			get
			{
				return useTypeConverter;
			}
			set
			{
				useTypeConverter = value;
				UpdateSelector();
			}
		}

		public StandardValuesSource(DataViewBase view, RowHandle handle)
		{
			this.view = view;
			this.handle = handle;
			base.Source = view.GetStandardValues(handle);
			UpdateSelector();
		}

		private void UpdateSelector()
		{
			if (UseTypeConverter)
			{
				base.Selector = (object x) => (object)new CustomItem
				{
					EditValue = x,
					DisplayText = view.TryConvertToString(handle, x)
				};
			}
			else
			{
				base.Selector = (object x) => x;
			}
		}
	}

		private void GenerateEditor(IEdmPropertyInfo property, IModelItem propertyDefinition, IModelItem editSettings, Initializer initializer)
		{
			propertyDefinition = propertyDefinition ?? definitionsProvider.Properties["CurrentDefinition"].If((IModelProperty x) => x.IsSet).With((IModelProperty x) => x.Value);
			propertyDefinition = propertyDefinition ?? definitionsProvider.Context.CreateItem(typeof(PropertyDefinition));
			AttributesApplier.ApplyBaseAttributes(property, (Action<string>)delegate(string x)
			{
				ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(propertyDefinition, PropertyDefinitionBase.PathProperty, (object)x, true);
			}, (Action<string>)delegate(string x)
			{
				ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(propertyDefinition, PropertyDefinitionBase.HeaderProperty, (object)x, true);
			}, (Action<string>)null, (Action<string>)delegate(string x)
			{
				ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(propertyDefinition, PropertyDefinitionBase.DescriptionProperty, (object)x, true);
			}, (Action)null, (Action<bool?>)delegate(bool? x)
			{
				ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(propertyDefinition, PropertyDefinitionBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, (object)(x.HasValue ? new bool?(!x.Value) : x), true);
			}, (Action)null, (Action)null, (Action)null);
			AttributesApplier.ApplyDisplayFormatAttributesForEditSettings(property, (Func<IModelItem>)(() => editSettings ?? (editSettings = propertyDefinition.Context.CreateItem(typeof(TextEditSettings)))), (Action<IModelItem, DependencyProperty, object>)null);
			((Initializer)(ref initializer)).SetContainerProperties(propertyDefinition);
			if (editSettings != null)
			{
				((Initializer)(ref initializer)).SetEditProperties(propertyDefinition, editSettings);
				ModelExtensions.SetValue(propertyDefinition, PropertyDefinition.EditSettingsProperty, (object)editSettings, true);
			}
			definitionsProvider.Properties["CurrentDefinition"].SetValue((object)propertyDefinition);
		}

		protected override void GenerateEditor(IEdmPropertyInfo property, Type editType, Initializer initializer)
		{
			//IL_0140: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			IModelItem val = definitionsProvider.Properties["CurrentDefinition"].If((IModelProperty x) => x.IsSet).With((IModelProperty x) => x.Value);
			IModelItem val2 = null;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val2 = val.Properties["EditSettings"].If((IModelProperty x) => x.IsSet).With((IModelProperty x) => x.Value);
				if (!object.Equals(val2.With((IModelItem x) => x.ItemType), editType ?? typeof(TextEditSettings)))
				{
					val2 = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				val = definitionsProvider.Context.CreateItem(typeof(PropertyDefinition));
			}
			val2 = val2 ?? ((editType != null) ? definitionsProvider.Context.CreateItem(editType) : null);
			GenerateEditor(property, val, val2, initializer);
		}

		private bool InitializeStandardValuesEditor(IEdmPropertyInfo property)
		{
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!addToLogicalTree)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (dataView.GetIsStandardValuesSupported(handle))
			{
				((EditorsGeneratorBase)this).GenerateEditor(property, typeof(ComboBoxEditSettings), StandardValuesEditorInitializer(property).Value);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private Initializer? StandardValuesEditorInitializer(IEdmPropertyInfo property, Action<IModelItem, DependencyProperty, object> setValueMethod = null)
		{
			//IL_007c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!addToLogicalTree)
			{
				return null;
			}
			setValueMethod = setValueMethod ?? ((Action<IModelItem, DependencyProperty, object>)delegate(IModelItem ed, DependencyProperty prop, object value)
			{
				ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(ed, prop, value, false);
			});
			if (dataView.GetIsStandardValuesSupported(handle))
			{
				return new Initializer((Action<IModelItem, IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem container, IModelItem settings)
				{
					setValueMethod(settings, LookUpEditSettingsBase.ItemsSourceProperty, new StandardValuesSource(dataView, handle));
					if (dataView.GetIsStandardValuesExclusive(handle))
					{
						setValueMethod(settings, ButtonEditSettings.IsTextEditableProperty, false);
						setValueMethod(settings, LookUpEditSettingsBase.AllowRejectUnknownValuesProperty, true);
					}
					if (PropertyInfoExtensions.HasNullableType(property))
					{
						setValueMethod(settings, ButtonEditSettings.NullValueButtonPlacementProperty, (object)(EditorPlacement)1);
					}
				}, (Action<IModelItem>)null);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override void Text(IEdmPropertyInfo property, bool multiline)
		{

				((EditorsGeneratorBase)this).Text(property, multiline);
			}
		}

		}

		public override void Object(IEdmPropertyInfo property)
		{
			//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Type type = null;
			if (property.PropertyType.If((Type x) => typeof(Brush).IsAssignableFrom(x)).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				type = typeof(PopupBrushEditSettings);
			}
			else if (property.PropertyType.If((Type x) => typeof(Color).IsAssignableFrom(x)).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				type = typeof(PopupColorEditSettings);
			}
			else if (InitializeStandardValuesEditor(property))
			{
				return;
			}
			((EditorsGeneratorBase)this).GenerateEditor(property, type, ((EditorsGeneratorBase)this).ObjectInitializer(property));
		}

		protected override Initializer ObjectInitializer(IEdmPropertyInfo property)
		{
			//IL_0051: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_00a8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (property.PropertyType.If((Type x) => typeof(Brush).IsAssignableFrom(x)).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				return new Initializer((Action<IModelItem, IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem container, IModelItem edit)
				{
					((DependencyObject)edit.GetCurrentValue()).SetValue(PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.PostOnEditValueChangedProperty, true);
				}, (Action<IModelItem>)null);
			}
			if (property.PropertyType.If((Type x) => typeof(Color).IsAssignableFrom(x)).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				return new Initializer((Action<IModelItem, IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem container, IModelItem edit)
				{
					((DependencyObject)edit.GetCurrentValue()).SetValue(PropertyGridEditSettingsHelper.PostOnEditValueChangedProperty, true);
				}, (Action<IModelItem>)null);
			}
			return ((EditorsGeneratorBase)this).ObjectInitializer(property);
		}

		protected override Initializer EnumInitializer(IEdmPropertyInfo property, Type enumType, bool? useFlags = null, bool? nullable = null)
		{
			//IL_00cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00db: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Initializer? val = null;
			DescriptorContext descriptorContext = owner.DataController.GetDescriptorContext(handle);
			if (descriptorContext == null || descriptorContext.BaseConverter == null || descriptorContext.BaseConverter.GetType() != typeof(EnumConverter))
			{
				val = StandardValuesEditorInitializer(property, delegate(IModelItem ed, DependencyProperty prop, object value)
				{
					ModelExtensions.SetValue(ed, prop, value, false);
				});
			}
			if (val.HasValue)
			{
				Initializer? val2 = val;
				Initializer val3 = default(Initializer);
				((Initializer)(ref val3))..ctor((Action<IModelItem, IModelItem>)null, (Action<IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem x)
				{
					ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(x, PropertyDefinition.UseTypeConverterToStringConversionProperty, (object)true, true);
				});
				val = (val2.HasValue ? new Initializer?(val2.GetValueOrDefault() + val3) : ((Initializer?)null));
			}
			else
			{
				val = Initializer.Default;
			}
			return ((EditorsGeneratorBase)this).EnumInitializer(property, enumType, useFlags, nullable) + val.Value;
		}

		public override void GenerateEditorFromResources(IEdmPropertyInfo property, object resourceKey, Initializer initializer)
		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			DataTemplate resourceTemplate = EditorsGeneratorBase.GetResourceTemplate((FrameworkElement)owner, resourceKey);
			object resourceContent = EditorsGeneratorBase.GetResourceContent<PropertyDefinition, BaseEditSettings, BaseEdit>(resourceTemplate);
			EditorFromResourcesCore(property, resourceContent, resourceTemplate, initializer);
			owner.PropertyBuilder.StandardDefinitionsProvider.CurrentDefinition.isResourceGeneratedDefinition = true;
		}

		private void EditorFromResourcesCore(IEdmPropertyInfo property, object content, DataTemplate template, Initializer initializer)
		{
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0088: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0067: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0067: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (content is PropertyDefinition)
			{
				PropertyDefinition propertyDefinition = content as PropertyDefinition;
				IModelItem editSettings = ((propertyDefinition.EditSettings != null) ? ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)new RuntimeEditingContext((object)propertyDefinition.EditSettings, (EditingContextTrace)null)) : null);
				GenerateEditor(property, ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)new RuntimeEditingContext(content, (EditingContextTrace)null)), editSettings, initializer);
			}
			else if (content is BaseEditSettings)
			{
				GenerateEditor(property, null, ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)new RuntimeEditingContext(content, (EditingContextTrace)null)), initializer);
			}
			else
			{
				Initializer initializer2 = initializer + new Initializer((Action<IModelItem, IModelItem>)null, (Action<IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem container)
				{
					ModelExtensions.SetValueIfNotSet(container, PropertyDefinition.CellTemplateProperty, (object)template, true);
				});
				GenerateEditor(property, null, null, initializer2);
			}
		}
	}
	public interface IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider
	{
		RenderTemplate GetRowTemplate();

		double GetRowMinHeight();

		double GetExpanderWidth();

		double GetSplitterWidth();
	}
	public static class PropertyGridViewExtensions
	{
		private class NullPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider : IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider
		{
			public static readonly NullPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider Instance = new NullPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider();

			double IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetExpanderWidth()
			{
				return 22.0;
			}

			double IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetRowMinHeight()
			{
				return 21.0;
			}

			RenderTemplate IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetRowTemplate()
			{
				return null;
			}

			double IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetSplitterWidth()
			{
				return 5.0;
			}
		}

		public static IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider GetResourceProvider(this PropertyGridView view)
		{
			if (view == null)
			{
				return NullPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.Instance;
			}
			return view.ResourceProvider;
		}
	}
	public class PropertyGridRenderResourceProvider : IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider
	{
		private readonly string themeName;

		private readonly bool isTouch;

		private readonly FrameworkElement target;

		private RenderTemplate rowTemplate;

		private double? rowMinHeight;

		private double? expanderWidth;

		private double? splitterWidth;

		public static PropertyGridRenderResourceProvider From(FrameworkElement dObj)
		{
			CommonThemeTreeWalker treeWalker = CommonThemeHelper.GetTreeWalker((DependencyObject)dObj);
			if (treeWalker == null)
			{
				return new PropertyGridRenderResourceProvider(null, isTouch: false, dObj);
			}
			return new PropertyGridRenderResourceProvider(CommonThemeHelper.GetTreeWalkerThemeName(treeWalker, false), treeWalker.IsTouch, dObj);
		}

		public PropertyGridRenderResourceProvider(string themeName, bool isTouch, FrameworkElement target)
		{
			this.themeName = themeName;
			this.isTouch = isTouch;
			this.target = target;
		}

		protected T GetResource<T>(FrameworkElement element, ref T field, VSViewRenderTemplateThemeKeys key) where T : class
		{
			T val = field;
			if (val == null)
			{
				VSViewRenderTemplateThemeKeyExtension obj = new VSViewRenderTemplateThemeKeyExtension
				{
					ResourceKey = key
				};
				((ThemeKeyExtensionGeneric)obj).ThemeName = themeName;
				val = (field = (T)element.FindResource(obj));
			}
			return val;
		}

		protected TResource GetResource<TResource, TThemeKey, TExtension>(ref TResource field, TThemeKey key) where TResource : class where TExtension : ThemeKeyExtensionBase<TThemeKey>, new()
		{
			TResource val = field;
			if (val == null)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = target;
				TExtension val2 = new TExtension();
				((ThemeKeyExtensionBase<TThemeKey>)val2).ResourceKey = key;
				((ThemeKeyExtensionGeneric)(object)val2).ThemeName = themeName;
				val = (field = (TResource)frameworkElement.FindResource(val2));
			}
			return val;
		}

		protected TResource GetResource<TResource, TThemeKey, TExtension>(ref TResource? field, TThemeKey key) where TResource : struct where TExtension : ThemeKeyExtensionBase<TThemeKey>, new()
		{
			if (!field.HasValue)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = target;
				TExtension val = new TExtension();
				((ThemeKeyExtensionBase<TThemeKey>)val).ResourceKey = key;
				((ThemeKeyExtensionGeneric)(object)val).ThemeName = themeName;
				field = (TResource)frameworkElement.FindResource(val);
			}
			return field.Value;
		}

		RenderTemplate IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetRowTemplate()
		{
			return GetResource<RenderTemplate, VSViewRenderTemplateThemeKeys, VSViewRenderTemplateThemeKeyExtension>(ref rowTemplate, VSViewRenderTemplateThemeKeys.RowControl);
		}

		double IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetRowMinHeight()
		{
			return GetResource<double, PropertyGridViewThemeKeys, PropertyGridViewThemeKeyExtension>(ref rowMinHeight, PropertyGridViewThemeKeys.RowMinHeight);
		}

		double IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetExpanderWidth()
		{
			return GetResource<double, DataRowThemeKeys, DataRowThemeKeyExtension>(ref expanderWidth, DataRowThemeKeys.ExpanderWidth);
		}

		double IPropertyGridRenderResourceProvider.GetSplitterWidth()
		{
			return GetResource<double, DataRowThemeKeys, DataRowThemeKeyExtension>(ref splitterWidth, DataRowThemeKeys.DataRowHeaderThumbWidth);
		}
	}
}
